structure Hypotheses₂ : Prop extends X.HcPublished where
  uo : UnramifiedOrthogonality
  gw : GreenbergWiles

def eqHom {k Γ M : Type} [CommRing k] [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [AddCommGroup M] [Module k M]
    (ρ : Representation k Γ M) (D J : Subgroup Γ) : Submodule k (J → M) where
  carrier := {f | IsLocallyConstant f ∧ (∀ a b, f (a * b) = f a + f b) ∧
    ∀ d ∈ D, ∀ j j' : J, j'.1 = d * j.1 * d⁻¹ → f j' = ρ d (f j)}
  zero_mem' := ⟨IsLocallyConstant.const _, by simp, by simp⟩
  add_mem' {a b} ha hb := ⟨ha.1.add hb.1, fun x y => by simp only [Pi.add_apply, ha.2.1, hb.2.1]; abel,
    fun d hd j j' h => by simp [ha.2.2 d hd j j' h, hb.2.2 d hd j j' h]⟩
  smul_mem' c a ha := ⟨ha.1.comp (c • ·), fun x y => by simp [ha.2.1], fun d hd j j' h => by simp [ha.2.2 d hd j j' h]⟩

variable (F Ω : Type) [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime]

def cycF (k : Type) [Field k] [CharP k l] : G F Ω →* kˣ :=
  (Units.map (ZMod.castHom (dvd_pow_self l one_ne_zero) k).toMonoidHom).comp (cycMod F Ω l 1)

variable {F Ω}

def clRk (U : Subgroup (G F Ω)) : Cardinal := Module.rank (ZMod l) (Kum.XDual 1 U (ZMod l) (ZMod l))

def tameN (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))) (K : Subgroup (G F Ω)) : Subgroup (G F Ω) :=
  (Subgroup.normalClosure (⋃ v ∈ Sᶜ, ⋃ A ∈ {A : ValuationSubring Ω | IsPlaceOver A v},
    ((Ine (F := F) A ⊓ K : Subgroup (G F Ω)) : Set (G F Ω)))).topologicalClosure

structure GLink (S : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))) (K : Subgroup (G F Ω)) {Γ : Type} [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ]
    (π : G F Ω →* Γ) : Prop where
  odd : l ≠ 2
  above : ContainsAbove S l
  normal : K.Normal
  isOpen : IsOpen (K : Set (G F Ω))
  idx : ¬ l ∣ K.index
  cont : Continuous π
  onto : Function.Surjective π
  ker : π.ker = tameN (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))) K

structure LLink {V : Type} {Gv : V → Type} [∀ v, Group (Gv v)] [∀ v, TopologicalSpace (Gv v)] (ι : ∀ v, Gv v →* G F Ω)
    (A : V → ValuationSubring Ω) (pl : V → HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F)) : Prop where
  isOver : ∀ v, IsPlaceOver (A v) (pl v)
  inj : Function.Injective pl
  mem : ∀ w, w ∈ Set.range pl ↔ ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 F) ∈ w.asIdeal
  cont : ∀ v, Continuous (ι v)
  ι_inj : ∀ v, Function.Injective (ι v)
  range : ∀ v, (ι v).range = Dec (F := F) (A v)

structure FLink {k : Type} [Field k] (Φ : Fr.Frame l k) (S : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))) (K : Subgroup (G F Ω))
    (π : G F Ω →* Φ.Γ) (ι : ∀ v, Φ.Gv v →* G F Ω) (A : Φ.V → ValuationSubring Ω) (pl : Φ.V → HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))
    (e : Φ.Inf ≃ InfinitePlace F) (c : Φ.Inf → G F Ω) : Prop where
  real : IsTotallyReal F
  g : GLink l S K π
  only : ∀ w ∈ S, ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 F) ∈ w.asIdeal
  loc : LLink l ι A pl
  loc_eq : ∀ v s, Φ.loc v s = π (ι v s)
  ine : ∀ v, Φ.Iv v = (Ine (F := F) (A v)).comap (ι v)
  dv : ∀ v, Φ.dv v = locDeg (pl v)
  eps : ∀ g, Φ.eps (π g) = cycZ F Ω l g
  cc : ∀ w, IsCCAt (e w) (c w)
  cw : ∀ w, Φ.cw w = π (c w)
  del_open : IsOpen (Φ.del.ker : Set Φ.Γ)
  del_c : Φ.del Φ.c = -1
  K_del : ∀ g ∈ K, Φ.del (π g) = 1
  K_Ψ : ∀ g ∈ K, Φ.Ψ (π g) = 1

theorem gLink_quot (hl : l ≠ 2) {S : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))} (hS : ContainsAbove S l) {K : Subgroup (G F Ω)}
    (h1 : K.Normal) (h2 : IsOpen (K : Set (G F Ω))) (h3 : ¬ l ∣ K.index)
    [(tameN (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))) K).Normal] :
    GLink l S K (QuotientGroup.mk' (tameN (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))) K)) := by
  refine ⟨hl, hS, h1, h2, h3, ?_, QuotientGroup.mk'_surjective _, QuotientGroup.ker_mk' _⟩
  exact continuous_quot_mk

end UpperTheory.V.GC

end

https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/ContinuousCoboundaries2.lean#L1-L216

section Cone

variable {k G W V : Type} [CommRing k] [Group G] [TopologicalSpace G] [AddCommGroup W] [Module k W]
  {Gv : V → Type} [∀ v, Group (Gv v)] [∀ v, TopologicalSpace (Gv v)]
  (loc : ∀ v, Gv v →* G) (ρ : Representation k G W) (b : V → Submodule k W)

def coneH0 : Submodule k W where
  carrier := {X | (∀ g, ρ g X = X) ∧ ∀ v, X ∈ b v}
  add_mem' := by
    rintro x y ⟨a1, a2⟩ ⟨b1, b2⟩
    exact ⟨fun g => by rw [map_add, a1, b1], fun v => add_mem (a2 v) (b2 v)⟩
  zero_mem' := ⟨fun g => map_zero _, fun v => zero_mem _⟩
  smul_mem' := by
    rintro c x ⟨a1, a2⟩
    exact ⟨fun g => by rw [map_smul, a1], fun v => Submodule.smul_mem _ c (a2 v)⟩

def Z1C : Submodule k ((G → W) × (V → W)) where
  carrier := {x | IsLocallyConstant x.1 ∧ (∀ s t, x.1 (s * t) = x.1 s + ρ s (x.1 t)) ∧
    ∀ v s, x.1 (loc v s) - (ρ (loc v s) (x.2 v) - x.2 v) ∈ b v}
  add_mem' := by
    rintro x y ⟨a1, a2, a3⟩ ⟨b1, b2, b3⟩
    refine ⟨a1.add b1, fun s t => ?_, fun v s => ?_⟩
    · simp only [Prod.fst_add, Pi.add_apply, a2, b2, map_add]; abel
    · convert add_mem (a3 v s) (b3 v s) using 1
      simp only [Prod.fst_add, Prod.snd_add, Pi.add_apply, map_add]; abel
  zero_mem' := ⟨IsLocallyConstant.const 0, by simp, by simp⟩
  smul_mem' := by
    rintro c x ⟨a1, a2, a3⟩
    refine ⟨a1.comp (c • ·), fun s t => by simp [a2], fun v s => ?_⟩
    convert Submodule.smul_mem _ c (a3 v s) using 1
    simp [smul_sub]

def continuousCoboundaries1 : Submodule k ((G → W) × (V → W)) where
  carrier := {x | ∃ X : W, (∀ s, x.1 s = ρ s X - X) ∧ ∀ v, x.2 v - X ∈ b v}
  add_mem' := by
    rintro x y ⟨X, a1, a2⟩ ⟨Y, b1, b2⟩
    refine ⟨X + Y, fun s => ?_, fun v => ?_⟩
    · simp only [Prod.fst_add, Pi.add_apply, a1, b1, map_add]; abel
    · convert add_mem (a2 v) (b2 v) using 1
      simp only [Prod.snd_add, Pi.add_apply]; abel
  zero_mem' := ⟨0, by simp, by simp⟩
  smul_mem' := by
    rintro c x ⟨X, a1, a2⟩
    refine ⟨c • X, fun s => by simp [a1, smul_sub], fun v => ?_⟩
    convert Submodule.smul_mem _ c (a2 v) using 1
    simp [smul_sub]

abbrev H1C : Type := Z1C loc ρ b ⧸ (continuousCoboundaries1 ρ b).comap (Z1C loc ρ b).subtype

def continuousCocycles2 : Submodule k ((G × G → W) × (∀ v, Gv v → W)) where
  carrier := {x | IsLocallyConstant x.1 ∧ (∀ v, IsLocallyConstant (x.2 v)) ∧
    (∀ s t u, ρ s (x.1 (t, u)) + x.1 (s, t * u) = x.1 (s * t, u) + x.1 (s, t)) ∧
    ∀ v s t, x.1 (loc v s, loc v t) - dOne (ρ.comp (loc v)) (x.2 v) (s, t) ∈ b v}
  add_mem' := by
    rintro x y ⟨a1, a2, a3, a4⟩ ⟨b1, b2, b3, b4⟩
    refine ⟨a1.add b1, fun v => (a2 v).add (b2 v), fun s t u => ?_, fun v s t => ?_⟩
    · simp only [Prod.fst_add, Pi.add_apply, map_add]
      rw [add_add_add_comm, a3, b3, add_add_add_comm]
    · convert add_mem (a4 v s t) (b4 v s t) using 1
      simp only [Prod.fst_add, Prod.snd_add, Pi.add_apply, dOne, map_add]; abel
  zero_mem' := ⟨IsLocallyConstant.const 0, fun _ => IsLocallyConstant.const 0, by simp, by simp [dOne]⟩
  smul_mem' := by
    rintro c x ⟨a1, a2, a3, a4⟩
    refine ⟨a1.comp (c • ·), fun v => (a2 v).comp (c • ·), fun s t u => ?_, fun v s t => ?_⟩
    · simp only [Prod.smul_fst, Pi.smul_apply, map_smul, ← smul_add, a3]
    · convert Submodule.smul_mem _ c (a4 v s t) using 1
      simp [dOne, smul_sub]

def continuousCoboundaries2 : Submodule k ((G × G → W) × (∀ v, Gv v → W)) where
  carrier := {x | ∃ (β : G → W) (Y : V → W), IsLocallyConstant β ∧ x.1 = dOne ρ β ∧
    ∀ v s, x.2 v s - (β (loc v s) - (ρ (loc v s) (Y v) - Y v)) ∈ b v}
  add_mem' := by
    rintro x y ⟨β, Y, a1, a2, a3⟩ ⟨β', Y', b1, b2, b3⟩
    refine ⟨β + β', Y + Y', a1.add b1, ?_, fun v s => ?_⟩
    · funext p
      simp only [Prod.fst_add, Pi.add_apply, a2, b2, dOne, map_add]; abel
    · convert add_mem (a3 v s) (b3 v s) using 1
      simp only [Prod.snd_add, Pi.add_apply, map_add]; abel
  zero_mem' := ⟨0, 0, IsLocallyConstant.const 0, by funext p; simp [dOne], by simp⟩
  smul_mem' := by
    rintro c x ⟨β, Y, a1, a2, a3⟩
    refine ⟨c • β, c • Y, a1.comp (c • ·), ?_, fun v s => ?_⟩
    · funext p
      simp [a2, dOne, smul_sub]
    · convert Submodule.smul_mem _ c (a3 v s) using 1
      simp [smul_sub]

abbrev coneH2 : Type := continuousCocycles2 loc ρ b ⧸ (continuousCoboundaries2 loc ρ b).comap (continuousCocycles2 loc ρ b).subtype

end Cone

def chRep {k G : Type} [CommRing k] [Group G] (χ : G →* kˣ) : Representation k G k where
  toFun g := (χ g : k) • LinearMap.id
  map_one' := by ext; simp
  map_mul' g h := by ext; simp [mul_comm]

@[simp] theorem chRep_apply {k G : Type} [CommRing k] [Group G] (χ : G →* kˣ) (g : G) (x : k) :
    chRep χ g x = χ g * x := rfl

structure Frame (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (k : Type) [Field k] : Type 1 where
  Γ : Type
  [grp : Group Γ]
  [top : TopologicalSpace Γ]
  [tg : IsTopologicalGroup Γ]
  [cpt : CompactSpace Γ]
  [t2 : T2Space Γ]
  [td : TotallyDisconnectedSpace Γ]
  del : Γ →* ℤˣ
  c : Γ
  V : Type
  [fV : Fintype V]
  Gv : V → Type
  [gv : ∀ v, Group (Gv v)]
  [tv : ∀ v, TopologicalSpace (Gv v)]
  [tgv : ∀ v, IsTopologicalGroup (Gv v)]
  [cv : ∀ v, CompactSpace (Gv v)]
  [t2v : ∀ v, T2Space (Gv v)]
  [tdv : ∀ v, TotallyDisconnectedSpace (Gv v)]
  loc0 : ∀ v, Gv v →* del.ker
  Iv : ∀ v, Subgroup (Gv v)
  dv : V → ℕ
  Inf : Type
  [fI : Fintype Inf]
  cw : Inf → Γ
  eps : Γ →* ℤ_[l]ˣ
  Ψ : Γ →* kˣ
  g0 : del.ker

attribute [instance] Frame.grp Frame.top Frame.tg Frame.cpt Frame.t2 Frame.td Frame.fV Frame.gv Frame.tv Frame.tgv
  Frame.cv Frame.t2v Frame.tdv Frame.fI

namespace Frame

variable {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {k : Type} [Field k] (F : Frame l k)

abbrev G0 : Type := F.del.ker

def loc (v : F.V) : F.Gv v →* F.Γ := F.del.ker.subtype.comp (F.loc0 v)

def conj : F.G0 →* F.G0 := (MulAut.conjNormal F.c : MulAut F.G0).toMonoidHom

def star (g : F.G0) : F.G0 := F.conj g⁻¹

def loc2 (p : F.V × Bool) : F.Gv p.1 →* F.G0 := if p.2 then F.conj.comp (F.loc0 p.1) else F.loc0 p.1

def d : ℕ := ∑ v, F.dv v

def om (K : Type) [CommRing K] [CharP K l] : F.Γ →* Kˣ :=
  (Units.map ((ZMod.castHom (dvd_refl l) K).comp PadicInt.toZMod).toMonoidHom).comp F.eps

def delK (K : Type) [CommRing K] : F.Γ →* Kˣ := (Units.map (Int.castRingHom K).toMonoidHom).comp F.del

def Nv (v : F.V) : Subgroup (F.Gv v) := F.Iv v ⊓ (F.eps.comp (F.loc v)).ker

def ext {k' : Type} [Field k'] (f : k →+* k') : Frame l k' :=
  { F with Ψ := (Units.map f.toMonoidHom).comp F.Ψ }

def EC : Prop :=
  ∀ (K W : Type) [Field K] [Finite K] [CharP K l] [AddCommGroup W] [Module K W] [Module.Finite K W]
    (ρ : Representation K F.Γ W) (b : F.V → Submodule K W), IsOpen (ρ.ker : Set F.Γ) →
    (∀ v s, ∀ X ∈ b v, ρ (F.loc v s) X ∈ b v) →
    (∀ f : W →ₗ[K] K, (∀ g X, f (ρ g X) = F.om K g * f X) → f = 0) →
    Module.Finite K (coneH2 F.loc ρ b) ∧
    Module.finrank K (coneH2 F.loc ρ b) + Module.finrank K (coneH0 ρ b) + ∑ v, F.dv v * Module.finrank K (b v) ≤
      Module.finrank K (H1C F.loc ρ b) + ∑ w, Module.finrank K (LinearMap.ker (ρ (F.cw w) - 1))

structure Ok [CharP k l] (C₀ : ℕ) : Prop where
  l_odd : l ≠ 2
  fin : Finite k
  del_open : IsOpen (F.del.ker : Set F.Γ)
  c_sq : F.c * F.c = 1
  del_c : F.del F.c = -1
  loc_cont : ∀ v, Continuous (F.loc0 v)
  eps_cont : Continuous F.eps
  Ψ_open : IsOpen (F.Ψ.ker : Set F.Γ)
  Ψ_c : F.Ψ F.c = 1
  baseSubgroup_spec : F.Ψ F.g0 ^ 2 ≠ 1
  phi : PhiCond F.Γ l
  card_inf : Fintype.card F.Inf = F.d
  cw_sq : ∀ w, F.cw w * F.cw w = 1
  del_cw : ∀ w, F.del (F.cw w) = -1
  f2a : ∃ g : F.G0, F.om k g ≠ 1
  f2b : ∀ j : ℤ, j = 1 ∨ j = -1 → ∃ g : F.G0, F.om k g * F.Ψ g ^ j ≠ 1
  f2c : ∀ j : ℤ, j = 2 ∨ j = -2 → ∃ g, F.om k g * F.Ψ g ^ j * F.delK k g ≠ 1
  f3 : ∀ v, ∃ g, F.Ψ (F.loc v g) ^ 2 ≠ 1
  f4 : Nontrivial (H1C F.loc (chRep (F.Ψ⁻¹ * F.delK k)) fun _ => ⊥)
  f4' : Nontrivial (H1C F.loc (chRep (F.Ψ⁻¹ ^ 2 * F.delK k)) fun _ => ⊥)
  f5 : ¬ ∃ j : ℤ, ∀ g : F.G0, F.om k g = F.Ψ g ^ j
  Ψ_N : ∀ v, ∀ g ∈ F.Nv v, F.Ψ (F.loc v g) = 1

  gen : ∀ v, ∃ (u : Fin (F.dv v) → F.Gv v) (a₀ : Fin (F.dv v)), (∀ a, u a ∈ F.Iv v ∧ F.Ψ (F.loc v (u a)) = 1) ∧
    (∀ a, a ≠ a₀ → u a ∈ F.Nv v) ∧
    ∀ (A : Type) [CommGroup A] [Finite A], IsPGroup l A → ∀ χ : F.Gv v →* A, IsOpen (χ.ker : Set (F.Gv v)) →
      (∀ a, χ (u a) = 1) → ∀ g ∈ F.Iv v, χ g = 1

  cl : ∃ h : ℕ, h ≠ 0 ∧ ∀ (A : Type) [CommGroup A] [Finite A], IsPGroup l A → ∀ χ : F.G0 →* A,
    IsOpen (χ.ker : Set F.G0) → (∀ p : F.V × Bool, ∀ g ∈ F.Iv p.1, χ (F.loc2 p g) = 1) → ∀ g, χ g ^ h = 1
  ec : F.EC
  b1p : Module.rank k (H1C F.loc (chRep (F.Ψ⁻¹ ^ 2 * F.delK k)) fun _ => ⊥) ≤ C₀
  b1 : Module.rank k (H1C F.loc2 (chRep (F.Ψ⁻¹.comp F.del.ker.subtype)) fun _ => ⊥) ≤ C₀
  h1 : Module.rank k (H1C F.loc (chRep (1 : F.Γ →* kˣ)) fun _ => ⊤) ≤ 1 + C₀

def Big (C₀ : ℕ) : Prop := 3 * C₀ + 5 + 2 < F.d ∧ ∀ v, 5 < F.dv v

end Frame

end UpperTheory.V.Fr


[LFC_SRC_GEN_DEFS] Full Gen, UFacts, Linked and HasFrameW

import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisCochains.GLinkQuot

open NumberField Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal IsLocalRing UpperTheory.V.HV UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.V.Pr
open scoped Pointwise

namespace UpperTheory.V.Fr

variable {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O]
  [CharP (ResidueField O) l]

variable (Ω O) in

abbrev omO : G ℚ Ω →* (ResidueField O)ˣ := GC.cycF ℚ Ω l (ResidueField O)

structure Gen (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ) (σ : G ℚ Ω) (B : ValuationSubring Ω) (M : ℕ) (Hb : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)) : Prop where
  odd : l ≠ 2
  fin : Finite (ResidueField O)
  M_ne : M ≠ 0
  l_dvd : l ∣ M
  two_dvd : 2 ∣ M
  cc : IsCC σ
  over_l : B.valuation (l : Ω) < 1
  quad : ∃ (D : ℕ) (δ : Ω), 0 < D ∧ (∀ q : ℕ, q.Prime → q ∣ D → q ∣ M) ∧ δ ^ 2 = -(D : Ω) ∧ ∀ s : G ℚ Ω, s δ = (η s : ℤ) • δ
  cyc : ∃ a : ℕ, Af.KillsMu (M ^ a) η ∧ Af.KillsMu (M ^ a) (barChar O Ψ)
  η_l : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → Ine (F := ℚ) B' ≤ η.ker
  even : barChar O Ψ σ = 1
  Ψ_l : ∃ j : ℕ, ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → ∀ s ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) B', barChar O Ψ s = omO Ω l O s ^ j
  g4 : ¬ (omO Ω l O).ker ⊓ (barChar O Ψ).ker ≤ η.ker
  g5 : ∃ s ∈ Dec (F := ℚ) B, η s = 1 ∧ barChar O Ψ s ^ 2 ≠ 1
  Hb_open : IsOpen (Hb : Set (G ℚ Ω))
  Hb_ab : commutator (G ℚ Ω) ≤ Hb
  Hb_idx : ∃ a : ℕ, Hb.index = l ^ a
  Hb_l : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → Ine (F := ℚ) B' ≤ Hb
  teich : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬ B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → ∀ s ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) B' ⊓ Hb, barChar O Ψ s = 1 → Ψ s = 1

variable (Ω) in

def Hth (w : ℕ) : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω) where
  carrier := {s | ∃ a : ℕ, ∀ ζ : Ω, ζ ^ w = 1 → s ζ = ζ ^ a ^ l} ∩ {s | ∃ a : ℕ, ∀ ζ : Ω, ζ ^ w = 1 → s⁻¹ ζ = ζ ^ a ^ l}
  one_mem' := ⟨⟨1, by simp⟩, ⟨1, by simp⟩⟩
  mul_mem' := by
    rintro s t ⟨⟨a, ha⟩, ⟨a', ha'⟩⟩ ⟨⟨b, hb⟩, ⟨b', hb'⟩⟩
    refine ⟨⟨a * b, fun ζ h => ?_⟩, ⟨b' * a', fun ζ h => ?_⟩⟩
    · rw [AlgEquiv.mul_apply, hb ζ h, map_pow, ha ζ h, ← pow_mul, mul_pow]
    · rw [mul_inv_rev, AlgEquiv.mul_apply, ha' ζ h, map_pow, hb' ζ h, ← pow_mul, mul_pow]
  inv_mem' := by
    rintro s ⟨h1, h2⟩
    exact ⟨h2, by show ∃ a : ℕ, ∀ ζ : Ω, ζ ^ w = 1 → s⁻¹⁻¹ ζ = ζ ^ a ^ l; rw [inv_inv]; exact h1⟩

variable (Ω) in

def Hlay (m : ℕ) : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω) := (cycMod ℚ Ω l (m + 1) ^ (l - 1)).ker

structure FrobeniusSplitPrimeDatum (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (M : ℕ) (H₀ : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)) : Prop where
  isOpen : IsOpen (H₀ : Set (G ℚ Ω))
  idx : H₀.index = 2
  real : ∀ τ : G ℚ Ω, IsCC τ → τ ∈ H₀
  loc : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, B'.valuation (M : Ω) < 1 → Dec (F := ℚ) B' ⊓ H₀ = Dec (F := ℚ) B' ⊓ η.ker
  ram : ∃ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬ B'.valuation (M : Ω) < 1 ∧ ¬ Ine (F := ℚ) B' ≤ H₀

structure FrobeniusWitnessPrime (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ) (M w : ℕ) : Prop where
  prime : w.Prime
  cop : ¬ w ∣ M
  one : w ≡ 1 [MOD l]
  split : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, B'.valuation (w : Ω) < 1 →
    Dec (F := ℚ) B' ≤ η.ker ⊓ (barChar O Ψ).ker ⊓ (omO Ω l O).ker

def Upl (Hb H₀ : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)) (w m : ℕ) : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω) := H₀ ⊓ Hth Ω l w ⊓ Hb ⊓ Hlay Ω l m

structure UFacts (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ) (M m : ℕ) (U : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)) : Prop where
  isOpen : IsOpen (U : Set (G ℚ Ω))
  ab : commutator (G ℚ Ω) ≤ U
  real : ∀ τ : G ℚ Ω, IsCC τ → τ ∈ U
  unr : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, Ine (F := ℚ) B' ⊓ U ≤ η.ker
  split : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, B'.valuation (M : Ω) < 1 → Dec (F := ℚ) B' ⊓ U ≤ η.ker
  teich : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬ B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → ∀ s ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) B' ⊓ U, barChar O Ψ s = 1 → Ψ s = 1
  mu : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → ∃ s ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) B' ⊓ U, cycMod ℚ Ω l 1 s ≠ 1
  deg : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → l ^ m ≤ U.relIndex (Dec (F := ℚ) B')
  f2b : ∀ j : ℤ, j = 1 ∨ j = -1 → ∃ s ∈ U ⊓ η.ker, omO Ω l O s * barChar O Ψ s ^ j ≠ 1
  f2c : ∀ j : ℤ, j = 2 ∨ j = -2 → ∃ s ∈ U ⊓ η.ker, omO Ω l O s * barChar O Ψ s ^ j ≠ 1
  f3 : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → ∃ s ∈ Dec (F := ℚ) B' ⊓ U, barChar O Ψ s ^ 2 ≠ 1
  f5 : ¬ ∃ j : ℤ, ∀ s ∈ U ⊓ η.ker, omO Ω l O s = barChar O Ψ s ^ j

abbrev etaO (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) : G ℚ Ω →* (ResidueField O)ˣ :=
  (Units.map (Int.castRingHom (ResidueField O)).toMonoidHom).comp η

def StrictCl (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ) (κ : G ℚ Ω →* (ResidueField O)ˣ) (U : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)) : Prop :=
  ∃ y : G ℚ Ω → ResidueField O, IsLocallyConstant y ∧ (∀ s t, y (s * t) = y s + κ s * y t) ∧
    (¬ ∃ b, ∀ s ∈ U, y s = κ s * b - b) ∧
    (∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → ∃ b, ∀ s ∈ Dec (F := ℚ) B', y s = κ s * b - b) ∧
    ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬ B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → ∀ s ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) B' ⊓ U, η s = 1 → barChar O Ψ s = 1 → y s = 0

def Linked (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ) (σ g₁ : G ℚ Ω) (B : ValuationSubring Ω) (M : ℕ)
    (L : Type) [Field L] [NumberField L] [IsCMField L] [Algebra L Ω] (Φ : Frame l (ResidueField O)) : Prop :=
  ∃ (k : GalLink η Ψ σ g₁ B M L Φ), ∃ S π ι pl e c, GC.FLink (F := W.Lp L) (Ω := Ω) l Φ S
      ((η.ker ⊓ (barChar O Ψ).ker).comap (galEmb ℚ (W.Lp L) Ω)) π ι k.pl pl e c ∧
      (∀ (g : G (W.Lp L) Ω) (h : galEmb ℚ (W.Lp L) Ω g ∈ upl Ω L), π g = k.p ⟨_, h⟩) ∧
      ∀ v s, galEmb ℚ (W.Lp L) Ω (ι v s) = k.j v s

def HasFrameW (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ) (σ : G ℚ Ω) (B : ValuationSubring Ω) (M Q : ℕ) : Prop :=
  ∃ (L : Type) (_ : Field L) (_ : NumberField L) (_ : IsCMField L) (_ : Algebra L Ω) (g₁ : G ℚ Ω)
    (Φ : Frame l (ResidueField O)) (C₀ w : ℕ), Linked l η Ψ σ g₁ B M L Φ ∧ Φ.Ok C₀ ∧ Φ.Big C₀ ∧
      FrobeniusWitnessPrime l η Ψ M w ∧ w ≡ 1 [MOD Q] ∧ upl Ω L ≤ Hth Ω l w

end UpperTheory.V.Fr

end


[LFC_SRC_HAS_FRAME] GalLink, HasFrame, SeedClause and separate named-data endpoints

open scoped Pointwise

namespace UpperTheory.V.Pr

variable (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω]

def upl (L : Type) [Field L] [NumberField L] [Algebra L Ω] : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω) where
  carrier := {s | ∀ x ∈ maximalRealSubfield L, s (algebraMap L Ω x) = algebraMap L Ω x}
  one_mem' _ _ := rfl
  mul_mem' {a b} ha hb x hx := by rw [AlgEquiv.mul_apply, hb x hx, ha x hx]
  inv_mem' {a} ha x hx := a.symm_apply_eq.2 (ha x hx).symm

variable {Ω} {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O]

structure GalLink (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ) (σ g₁ : G ℚ Ω) (B : ValuationSubring Ω) (M : ℕ)
    (L : Type) [Field L] [NumberField L] [Algebra L Ω] (Φ : Fr.Frame l (ResidueField O)) where
  ab : IsAbelianGalois ℚ L
  gal : galOf ℚ L Ω = upl Ω L ⊓ η.ker
  unr : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, Ine (F := ℚ) B' ⊓ upl Ω L ≤ η.ker
  split : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, B'.valuation (M : Ω) < 1 → Dec (F := ℚ) B' ⊓ upl Ω L ≤ η.ker
  teich : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬ B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → ∀ s ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) B' ⊓ upl Ω L,
    barChar O Ψ s = 1 → Ψ s = 1
  p : upl Ω L →* Φ.Γ
  p_cont : Continuous p
  p_surj : Function.Surjective p
  p_ker : p.ker = (Subgroup.normalClosure {s : upl Ω L | ∃ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬ B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 ∧
    s.1 ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) B' ∧ η s = 1 ∧ barChar O Ψ s = 1}).topologicalClosure
  del : ∀ s, Φ.del (p s) = η s
  eps : ∀ s, Φ.eps (p s) = cycZ ℚ Ω l s
  psi : ∀ s, Φ.Ψ (p s) = barChar O Ψ s
  c : ∃ h : σ ∈ upl Ω L, p ⟨σ, h⟩ = Φ.c
  g0 : ∃ h : g₁ ∈ upl Ω L, p ⟨g₁, h⟩ = Φ.g0
  pl : Φ.V → ValuationSubring Ω
  pl_conj : ∀ v, ∃ γ : G ℚ Ω, η γ = 1 ∧ pl v = γ • B
  pl_all : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → ∃! v, ∃ u ∈ upl Ω L, B' = u • pl v
  j : ∀ v, Φ.Gv v →* upl Ω L
  j_cont : ∀ v, Continuous (j v)
  j_inj : ∀ v, Function.Injective (j v)
  j_range : ∀ v (s : upl Ω L), s ∈ (j v).range ↔ s.1 ∈ Dec (F := ℚ) (pl v)
  j_loc : ∀ v g, Φ.loc v g = p (j v g)
  j_ine : ∀ v g, g ∈ Φ.Iv v ↔ (j v g).1 ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) (pl v)
  dv : ∀ v, Φ.dv v = (upl Ω L).relIndex (Dec (F := ℚ) (pl v))
  mu : ∀ v, ∃ g, cycMod ℚ Ω l 1 (j v g) ≠ 1

def GalLink.p0 {η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ} {Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ} {σ g₁ : G ℚ Ω} {B : ValuationSubring Ω} {M : ℕ}
    {L : Type} [Field L] [NumberField L] [Algebra L Ω] {Φ : Fr.Frame l (ResidueField O)} (k : GalLink η Ψ σ g₁ B M L Φ)
    (s : G L Ω) : Φ.G0 :=
  have h : galEmb ℚ L Ω s ∈ upl Ω L ⊓ η.ker := k.gal ▸ galEmb_mem ℚ L Ω s
  ⟨k.p ⟨galEmb ℚ L Ω s, h.1⟩, (k.del _).trans h.2⟩

def SeedClause (c : Coef l) (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* c.Oˣ) (σ : G ℚ Ω) (L : Type) [Field L] [NumberField L] [Algebra L Ω] : Prop :=
  ∃ (K : Type) (_ : Field K) (_ : NumberField K) (_ : Algebra K Ω) (N w : ℕ) (χ' η' θ : G ℚ Ω →* (Qbar l)ˣ) (j : ℕ),
    Sd.FieldPairCharacterTripleConditions K L σ N w χ' η' θ j ∧ χ' ≠ η' ∧ ¬ l ∣ orderOf χ' ∧ (∀ s, (η' s).val = (((η s).val : ℤ) : Qbar l)) ∧
    (∀ s, ‖((cycBar ℚ Ω l ^ j * χ') s).val - (Ψ s).val.1‖ < 1) ∧ ¬ (l : ℤ) ∣ discr K

def HasFrame (c : Coef l) [CharP (ResidueField c.O) l] (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* c.Oˣ) (σ : G ℚ Ω) (B : ValuationSubring Ω)
    (M : ℕ) : Prop :=
  ∃ (L : Type) (_ : Field L) (_ : NumberField L) (_ : IsCMField L) (_ : Algebra L Ω) (g₁ : G ℚ Ω)
    (Φ : Fr.Frame l (ResidueField c.O)) (C₀ : ℕ), Nonempty (GalLink η Ψ σ g₁ B M L Φ) ∧ Φ.Ok C₀ ∧ Φ.Big C₀ ∧
      SeedClause c η Ψ σ L

def HostFrameEx : Prop :=
  ∀ (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) [CharP (ResidueField c.O) l]
    (N : ℕ) [NeZero N] (e : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N) (o₁ : ℕ), UnitaryThreeInertia.HostData l e ψ o₁ →
  ∀ σ : G ℚ Ω, IsCC σ → ∀ B : ValuationSubring Ω, B.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 →
    HasFrame c (UnitaryThreeInertia.eta4 Ω) (Chr.Psi l e (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ)) σ B (l * N)

def GdFrameEx : Prop :=
  ∀ (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (K : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] [IsCMField K] [Algebra K Ω]
    (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) [CharP (ResidueField c.O) l] (N : ℕ) [NeZero N] (e : ℕ)
    (ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N) (D : Dat Ω K c e ψ) (B : ValuationSubring Ω), B.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 →
    HasFrame c D.η D.Ψ D.cc B (l * N * (discr K).natAbs)

end UpperTheory.V.Pr

end

namespace UpperTheory.V.X

section Frame

variable {k Γ M : Type} [CommRing k] [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [AddCommGroup M] [Module k M] [TopologicalSpace M]
  [DiscreteTopology M] (F : Alg.Frame Γ) (ρ : Representation k Γ M)

def selF : Submodule k (H1 ρ) :=
  (⨅ v, LinearMap.ker (resH1 ρ (F.φ v) (F.hφ v))) ⊓
    ⨅ q, (H1ur (ρ.comp (F.ψ q)) (F.N q)).comap (resH1 ρ (F.ψ q) (F.hψ q))

def shaF : Submodule k (H2 ρ) :=
  (⨅ v, LinearMap.ker (resH2 ρ (F.φ v) (F.hφ v))) ⊓ ⨅ q, LinearMap.ker (resH2 ρ (F.ψ q) (F.hψ q))

end Frame

structure HcPublished : Prop where
  lf : LocalFiniteness
  lt : LocalTateDuality
  le : LocalEulerChar
  gf : GlobalFiniteness
  ge : GlobalEulerChar
  pt : GlobalTheory.Published.PoitouTateNormalised

section Gal

variable (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [Algebra ℚ Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω]

def cycK (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (k : Type) [Field k] [CharP k l] : G ℚ Ω →* kˣ :=
  (Units.map (ZMod.castHom (dvd_pow_self l one_ne_zero) k).toMonoidHom).comp (cycMod ℚ Ω l 1)

variable {Ω}

def galFrame (SP : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 ℚ))) (T Q : Finset ℕ) (v0 : T) (pl : ℕ → ValuationSubring Ω)
    (K : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)) : Alg.Frame (GS Ω SP) where
  T := T
  v0 := v0
  Gv v := Dec (F := ℚ) (pl v)
  φ v := locHom SP (pl v)
  hφ v := continuous_locHom SP (pl v)
  Q := Q
  Gq q := Dec (F := ℚ) (pl q)
  ψ q := locHom SP (pl q)
  hψ q := continuous_locHom SP (pl q)
  N q := (Ine (F := ℚ) (pl q) ⊓ K).subgroupOf (Dec (F := ℚ) (pl q))

structure GalData (l : ℕ) (SP : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 ℚ))) (T Q : Finset ℕ) (pl : ℕ → ValuationSubring Ω)
    (K : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)) : Prop where
  l_odd : l ≠ 2
  prime : ∀ p, p ∈ T ∨ p ∈ Q → p.Prime
  disj : Disjoint T Q
  l_mem : l ∈ T
  mem : ∀ v, v ∈ SP ↔ ∃ p, (p ∈ T ∨ p ∈ Q) ∧ ((p : ℕ) : 𝓞 ℚ) ∈ v.asIdeal
  isOver : ∀ p, p ∈ T ∨ p ∈ Q → (pl p).valuation (p : Ω) < 1
  idx : ∀ q ∈ Q, ¬ l ∣ K.relIndex (Ine (F := ℚ) (pl q))

def Van {k : Type} [Field k] (T : Finset ℕ) (pl : ℕ → ValuationSubring Ω) (K : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)) (γ : G ℚ Ω →* kˣ) : Prop :=
  ∀ z : G ℚ Ω → k, IsLocallyConstant z → (∀ g h, z (g * h) = z g + (γ g : k) * z h) →
    (∀ A : ValuationSubring Ω, (∀ p ∈ T, ¬ A.valuation (p : Ω) < 1) → ∀ g ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) A, g ∈ K → z g = 0) →
    (∀ v ∈ T, ∃ m, ∀ g ∈ Dec (F := ℚ) (pl v), z g = (γ g : k) * m - m) → ∃ m, ∀ g, z g = (γ g : k) * m - m

example (l : ℕ) (hl : l.Prime) (h2 : l ≠ 2) (A : ValuationSubring Ω) (hA : A.valuation (l : Ω) < 1) :
    GalData l {v | ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 ℚ) ∈ v.asIdeal} {l} ∅ (fun _ => A) ⊤ :=
  ⟨h2, by simpa using hl, by simp, by simp, by simp, by simpa using hA, by simp⟩

end Gal

end UpperTheory.V.X

[LFC_SRC_PHI_DEF] Literal PhiCond over every open subgroup
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CommutativeAlgebra/ResMapBijective.lean#L1-L342
import Mathlib

noncomputable section

open IsLocalRing MvPowerSeries

namespace UpperTheory.V.Alg

class IsCO (O R : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] : Prop
    extends IsLocalRing R, IsNoetherianRing R where
  complete : IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal R) R
  isLocalHom : IsLocalHom (algebraMap O R)
  res_surj : ∀ x : R, ∃ a : O, x - algebraMap O R a ∈ maximalIdeal R

attribute [instance] IsCO.complete IsCO.isLocalHom

instance IsCO.self (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [IsNoetherianRing O]
    [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] : IsCO O O where
  complete := inferInstance
  isLocalHom := ⟨fun _ h => h⟩
  res_surj x := ⟨x, by simp⟩

section Res

variable (O R : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R]

theorem resMap_bijective : Function.Bijective (ResidueField.map (algebraMap O R)) := by
  refine ⟨(ResidueField.map (algebraMap O R)).injective, fun y => ?_⟩
  obtain ⟨x, rfl⟩ := residue_surjective y
  obtain ⟨a, ha⟩ := IsCO.res_surj (O := O) x
  refine ⟨residue O a, ?_⟩
  rw [ResidueField.map_residue]
  exact ((Ideal.Quotient.eq).2 ha).symm

def res : R →ₐ[O] ResidueField O :=
  { ((RingEquiv.ofBijective _ (resMap_bijective O R)).symm.toRingHom.comp (residue R)) with
    commutes' := fun a => by
      show (RingEquiv.ofBijective _ (resMap_bijective O R)).symm (residue R (algebraMap O R a)) = residue O a
      rw [RingEquiv.symm_apply_eq]
      exact (ResidueField.map_residue _ _).symm }

theorem res_eq_zero_iff (x : R) : res O R x = 0 ↔ x ∈ maximalIdeal R := by
  show (RingEquiv.ofBijective _ (resMap_bijective O R)).symm (residue R x) = 0 ↔ _
  rw [map_eq_zero_iff _ (RingEquiv.injective _), residue_eq_zero_iff]

variable {O R} in

theorem res_map {R' : Type} [CommRing R'] [Algebra O R'] [IsCO O R'] (f : R →ₐ[O] R') (x : R) :
    res O R' (f x) = res O R x := by
  obtain ⟨a, ha⟩ := IsCO.res_surj (O := O) x
  have h1 : res O R x = algebraMap O _ a := by
    have := (res_eq_zero_iff O R _).2 ha
    rwa [map_sub, AlgHom.commutes, sub_eq_zero] at this
  have hm : ∀ y ∈ maximalIdeal R, f y ∈ maximalIdeal R' := fun y hy => by
    obtain ⟨b, hb⟩ := IsCO.res_surj (O := O) (f y)
    by_cases hu : IsUnit b
    · exfalso
      have h2 : IsUnit (y - algebraMap O R b) := by
        by_contra hn
        have h3 : y - algebraMap O R b ∈ maximalIdeal R := hn
        have h4 : algebraMap O R b ∈ maximalIdeal R := by simpa using Ideal.sub_mem _ hy h3
        exact h4 (hu.map _)
      have h5 := h2.map f
      rw [map_sub, AlgHom.commutes] at h5
      exact hb h5
    · have h6 : algebraMap O R' b ∈ maximalIdeal R' := fun h => hu (IsLocalHom.map_nonunit b h)
      simpa using Ideal.add_mem _ hb h6
  have h2 := (res_eq_zero_iff O R' _).2 (hm _ ha)
  rw [map_sub, map_sub, AlgHom.commutes, AlgHom.commutes, sub_eq_zero] at h2
  rw [h2, h1]

end Res

def AdicCont {Γ R : Type} [TopologicalSpace Γ] [CommRing R] (I : Ideal R) (f : Γ → R) : Prop :=
  ∀ N : ℕ, IsLocallyConstant fun g => Ideal.Quotient.mk (I ^ N) (f g)

section Weights

variable (O : Type) [CommRing O] {σ X : Type} [AddCommGroup X] (wt : σ → X)

def wtPart (j : X) : Submodule O (MvPowerSeries σ O) where
  carrier := {f | ∀ n, Finsupp.weight wt n ≠ j → coeff n f = 0}
  add_mem' ha hb n hn := by rw [map_add, ha n hn, hb n hn, add_zero]
  zero_mem' n _ := map_zero _
  smul_mem' c f hf n hn := by rw [map_smul, hf n hn, smul_zero]

open Classical in

def wcomp (j : X) : MvPowerSeries σ O →ₗ[O] MvPowerSeries σ O where
  toFun f := fun n => if Finsupp.weight wt n = j then coeff n f else 0
  map_add' f g := funext fun n => by
    show (if _ then _ else _) = (if _ then _ else _) + (if _ then _ else (0 : O))
    split_ifs <;> simp
  map_smul' c f := funext fun n => by
    show (if _ then _ else _) = c • (if _ then _ else (0 : O))
    split_ifs <;> simp

open Classical in
theorem coeff_wcomp (j : X) (f : MvPowerSeries σ O) (n : σ →₀ ℕ) :
    coeff n (wcomp O wt j f) = if Finsupp.weight wt n = j then coeff n f else 0 := rfl

theorem wcomp_mem (j : X) (f : MvPowerSeries σ O) : wcomp O wt j f ∈ wtPart O wt j :=
  fun n hn => by rw [coeff_wcomp, if_neg hn]

theorem mul_mem_wtPart {i j : X} {f g : MvPowerSeries σ O} (hf : f ∈ wtPart O wt i) (hg : g ∈ wtPart O wt j) :
    f * g ∈ wtPart O wt (i + j) := fun n hn => by
  classical
  rw [coeff_mul]
  refine Finset.sum_eq_zero fun p hp => ?_
  by_cases h1 : Finsupp.weight wt p.1 = i
  · have h2 : Finsupp.weight wt p.2 ≠ j := fun h2 => hn (by
      rw [← Finset.HasAntidiagonal.mem_antidiagonal.1 hp, map_add, h1, h2])
    rw [hg _ h2, mul_zero]
  · rw [hf _ h1, zero_mul]

theorem one_mem_wtPart : (1 : MvPowerSeries σ O) ∈ wtPart O wt 0 := fun n hn => by
  classical
  rw [coeff_one, if_neg]
  rintro rfl
  exact hn (map_zero _)

theorem X_mem_wtPart (i : σ) : (MvPowerSeries.X i : MvPowerSeries σ O) ∈ wtPart O wt (wt i) := fun n hn => by
  classical
  rw [coeff_X, if_neg]
  rintro rfl
  exact hn (by simp [Finsupp.weight_single])

instance : SetLike.GradedMonoid (wtPart O wt) where
  one_mem := one_mem_wtPart O wt
  mul_mem _ _ _ _ := mul_mem_wtPart O wt

def IsHomog (J : Ideal (MvPowerSeries σ O)) : Prop := ∀ f ∈ J, ∀ j, wcomp O wt j f ∈ J

end Weights

def gradedSub {O R X : Type} [CommRing O] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [AddCommGroup X] (𝒜 : X → Submodule O R)
    [SetLike.GradedMonoid 𝒜] : Subalgebra O R :=
  (⨆ j, 𝒜 j).toSubalgebra (le_iSup 𝒜 0 (SetLike.GradedOne.one_mem)) fun x y hx hy => by
    refine Submodule.iSup_induction 𝒜 (motive := fun x => x * y ∈ ⨆ j, 𝒜 j) hx (fun i a ha => ?_) (by simp)
      (fun a b ha hb => by rw [add_mul]; exact add_mem ha hb)
    exact Submodule.iSup_induction 𝒜 (motive := fun y => a * y ∈ ⨆ j, 𝒜 j) hy
      (fun j b hb => le_iSup 𝒜 (i + j) (SetLike.GradedMul.mul_mem ha hb)) (by simp)
      (fun b c hb hc => by rw [mul_add]; exact add_mem hb hc)

structure WPres (O X R : Type) [CommRing O] [AddCommGroup X] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] where
  σ : Type
  [fin : Fintype σ]
  wt : σ → X
  π : MvPowerSeries σ O →ₐ[O] R
  surj : Function.Surjective π
  homog : IsHomog O wt (RingHom.ker π)

attribute [instance] WPres.fin

namespace WPres

variable {O X R : Type} [CommRing O] [AddCommGroup X] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] (W : WPres O X R)

def free (O : Type) [CommRing O] {σ : Type} [Fintype σ] (wt : σ → X) : WPres O X (MvPowerSeries σ O) where
  σ := σ
  wt := wt
  π := AlgHom.id O _
  surj := Function.surjective_id
  homog f hf j := by
    have : f = 0 := hf
    subst this
    simp

def z (i : W.σ) : R := W.π (MvPowerSeries.X i)

def part (j : X) : Submodule O R := (wtPart O W.wt j).map W.π.toLinearMap

instance : SetLike.GradedMonoid W.part where
  one_mem := ⟨1, one_mem_wtPart O W.wt, map_one W.π⟩
  mul_mem := by
    rintro i j _ _ ⟨f, hf, rfl⟩ ⟨g, hg, rfl⟩
    exact ⟨f * g, mul_mem_wtPart O W.wt hf hg, map_mul W.π f g⟩

theorem z_mem (i : W.σ) : W.z i ∈ W.part (W.wt i) := ⟨_, X_mem_wtPart O W.wt i, rfl⟩

def A : Subalgebra O R := gradedSub W.part

def gr (j : X) : Submodule O W.A := (W.part j).comap W.A.val.toLinearMap

instance : SetLike.GradedMonoid W.gr where
  one_mem := (SetLike.GradedOne.one_mem : (1 : R) ∈ W.part 0)
  mul_mem _ _ _ _ ha hb := (SetLike.GradedMul.mul_mem ha hb : _ ∈ W.part _)

theorem part_le_A {j : X} {x : R} (hx : x ∈ W.part j) : x ∈ W.A := le_iSup W.part j hx

def zA (i : W.σ) : W.A := ⟨W.z i, W.part_le_A (W.z_mem i)⟩

def HasOrigin : Prop := ∀ f, W.π f = 0 → constantCoeff f = 0

def origin (h : W.HasOrigin) : R →ₐ[O] O :=
  (Ideal.Quotient.liftₐ (RingHom.ker W.π)
    ({ (constantCoeff : MvPowerSeries W.σ O →+* O) with commutes' := fun a => by simp [MvPowerSeries.algebraMap_apply] } : MvPowerSeries W.σ O →ₐ[O] O)
    h).comp (Ideal.quotientKerAlgEquivOfSurjective W.surj).symm.toAlgHom

theorem origin_π (h : W.HasOrigin) (f : MvPowerSeries W.σ O) : W.origin h (W.π f) = constantCoeff f := by
  have : (Ideal.quotientKerAlgEquivOfSurjective W.surj).symm (W.π f) = Ideal.Quotient.mk _ f := by
    rw [AlgEquiv.symm_apply_eq]; rfl
  simp only [origin, AlgHom.comp_apply, AlgEquiv.coe_algHom]
  erw [this]
  rfl

end WPres

section Points

variable {O A X K : Type} [CommRing O] [CommRing A] [Algebra O A] [AddCommGroup X] [DecidableEq X]
  (𝒜 : X → Submodule O A) [GradedAlgebra 𝒜] [CommRing K] [Algebra O K]

def act (t : Multiplicative X →* Kˣ) (ξ : A →ₐ[O] K) : A →ₐ[O] K :=
  (DirectSum.toAlgebra O (fun j => 𝒜 j)
    (fun j => ((t (Multiplicative.ofAdd j) : Kˣ) : K) • (ξ.toLinearMap ∘ₗ (𝒜 j).subtype))
    (by simp) (fun {i j} a b => by
      simp only [LinearMap.smul_apply, LinearMap.comp_apply, Submodule.subtype_apply, AlgHom.toLinearMap_apply,
        SetLike.coe_gMul, map_mul, ofAdd_add, Units.val_mul, smul_eq_mul]
      ring)).comp (DirectSum.decomposeAlgEquiv 𝒜).toAlgHom

theorem act_of_mem (t : Multiplicative X →* Kˣ) (ξ : A →ₐ[O] K) {j : X} {f : A} (hf : f ∈ 𝒜 j) :
    act 𝒜 t ξ f = t (Multiplicative.ofAdd j) * ξ f := by
  have h : DirectSum.decompose 𝒜 f = DirectSum.of (fun j => 𝒜 j) j ⟨f, hf⟩ := DirectSum.decompose_of_mem 𝒜 hf
  simp [act, h, DirectSum.toAlgebra]
  erw [DirectSum.toSemiring_of]
  rfl

end Points

def PtDer {O A B : Type} [CommRing O] [CommRing A] [Algebra O A] [CommRing B] [Algebra O B] (x : A →ₐ[O] B)
    (Q : Type) [AddCommGroup Q] [Module O Q] [Module B Q] [SMulCommClass O B Q] : Submodule B (A →ₗ[O] Q) where
  carrier := {D | ∀ f g, D (f * g) = x f • D g + x g • D f}
  add_mem' {D D'} h h' f g := by simp only [LinearMap.add_apply, h f g, h' f g, smul_add]; abel
  zero_mem' f g := by simp
  smul_mem' c D h f g := by simp only [LinearMap.smul_apply, h f g, smul_add, smul_smul, mul_comm]

def HasWt {O A X Q : Type} [CommRing O] [CommRing A] [Algebra O A] [AddCommGroup Q] [Module O Q]
    (𝒜 : X → Submodule O A) (j : X) (D : A →ₗ[O] Q) : Prop := ∀ i, i ≠ j → ∀ f ∈ 𝒜 i, D f = 0

section Cot

variable {O A X : Type} [CommRing O] [CommRing A] [Algebra O A] (x : A →ₐ[O] O)

def satSq [IsDomain O] : Submodule O A where
  carrier := {f | ∃ c : O, c ≠ 0 ∧ c • f ∈ RingHom.ker x ^ 2}
  add_mem' := by
    rintro f g ⟨c, hc, hf⟩ ⟨d, hd, hg⟩
    refine ⟨c * d, mul_ne_zero hc hd, ?_⟩
    rw [smul_add, mul_comm c d, mul_smul, mul_comm d c, mul_smul]
    exact add_mem (Submodule.smul_of_tower_mem _ d hf) (Submodule.smul_of_tower_mem _ c hg)
  zero_mem' := ⟨1, one_ne_zero, by simp⟩
  smul_mem' a f := by
    rintro ⟨c, hc, hf⟩
    exact ⟨c, hc, by rw [smul_comm]; exact Submodule.smul_of_tower_mem _ a hf⟩

def cotLat [IsDomain O] (𝒜 : X → Submodule O A) (j : X) : Submodule O (A ⧸ satSq x) :=
  (𝒜 j ⊓ (RingHom.ker x).restrictScalars O).map (satSq x).mkQ

def cotSpan [IsDomain O] {ι : Type} (F : ι → A) : Submodule O (A ⧸ satSq x) :=
  Submodule.span O (Set.range fun i => (satSq x).mkQ (F i))

def SpansCot [IsDomain O] (𝒜 : X → Submodule O A) (j : X) {ι : Type} (F : ι → A) : Prop :=
  ∀ p ∈ cotLat x 𝒜 j, ∃ c : O, c ≠ 0 ∧ c • p ∈ cotSpan x F

def cotLen [IsDomain O] (𝒜 : X → Submodule O A) (j : X) {ι : Type} (F : ι → A) : ℕ∞ :=
  Module.length O (cotLat x 𝒜 j ⧸ (cotSpan x F).comap (cotLat x 𝒜 j).subtype)

end Cot

section Lifts

variable (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] {Γ ι : Type} [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [Fintype ι]
  [DecidableEq ι] (rbar : Γ →* Matrix ι ι (ResidueField O))

@[ext] structure Lift (R : Type) [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R] where
  hom : Γ →* Matrix ι ι R
  cont : ∀ a b, AdicCont (maximalIdeal R) fun g => hom g a b
  red : ∀ g a b, res O R (hom g a b) = rbar g a b

variable {O rbar} {R R' : Type} [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R] [CommRing R'] [Algebra O R'] [IsCO O R']

def Lift.map (f : R →ₐ[O] R') (r : Lift O rbar R) : Lift O rbar R' where
  hom := (f.mapMatrix : Matrix ι ι R →ₐ[O] Matrix ι ι R').toMonoidHom.comp r.hom
  cont a b N := by
    have hle : maximalIdeal R ^ N ≤ (maximalIdeal R' ^ N).comap (f : R →+* R') := by
      refine le_trans (Ideal.pow_right_mono (fun y hy => ?_) N) (Ideal.le_comap_pow _ N)
      show f y ∈ maximalIdeal R'
      rw [← res_eq_zero_iff O, res_map, res_eq_zero_iff]
      exact hy
    exact (r.cont a b N).comp (Ideal.quotientMap _ (f : R →+* R') hle)
  red g a b := (res_map f _).trans (r.red g a b)

@[simp] theorem Lift.map_hom (f : R →ₐ[O] R') (r : Lift O rbar R) (g : Γ) (a b : ι) :
    (r.map f).hom g a b = f (r.hom g a b) := rfl

def Lift.comap {H : Type} [Group H] [TopologicalSpace H] (φ : H →* Γ) (hφ : Continuous φ) (r : Lift O rbar R) :
    Lift O (rbar.comp φ) R where
  hom := r.hom.comp φ
  cont a b N := (r.cont a b N).comp_continuous hφ
  red g a b := r.red (φ g) a b

variable (O rbar) in

def Cond : Type 1 := ∀ (R : Type) [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R], Lift O rbar R → Prop

def polyCond (C : Set (MvPolynomial (Γ × ι × ι) O)) : Cond O rbar :=
  fun _ _ _ _ r => ∀ p ∈ C, MvPolynomial.aeval (fun s : Γ × ι × ι => r.hom s.1 s.2.1 s.2.2) p = 0

def Represents (P : Cond O rbar) (r : Lift O rbar R) : Prop :=
  ∀ (R' : Type) [CommRing R'] [Algebra O R'] [IsCO O R'],
    (∀ f : R →ₐ[O] R', P R' (r.map f)) ∧ ∀ r' : Lift O rbar R', P R' r' → ∃! f : R →ₐ[O] R', r.map f = r'

def Lift.Generates (r : Lift O rbar R) : Prop :=
  ∀ (R' : Type) [CommRing R'] [Algebra O R'] [IsCO O R'] (f f' : R →ₐ[O] R'), r.map f = r.map f' → f = f'

variable {X : Type} [AddCommGroup X] (w : ι → X)

variable (rbar) in

def Centralises : Prop := ∀ g a b, w a ≠ w b → rbar g a b = 0

def IsTorusConj (t : Multiplicative X →* Rˣ) (r r' : Lift O rbar R) : Prop :=
  ∀ g a b, r'.hom g a b = t (Multiplicative.ofAdd (w a - w b)) * r.hom g a b

def Lift.TStable (r : Lift O rbar R) : Prop :=
  ∀ (R' : Type) [CommRing R'] [Algebra O R'] [IsCO O R'] (f : R →ₐ[O] R') (t : Multiplicative X →* R'ˣ),
    (∀ j, res O R' (t j) = 1) → ∃ f' : R →ₐ[O] R', IsTorusConj w t (r.map f) (r.map f')

def Lift.Adapted (r : Lift O rbar R) (W : WPres O X R) : Prop :=
  (∀ g a b, r.hom g a b ∈ W.part (w a - w b)) ∧
    ∀ i, ∃ g a b, ∃ c : O, W.z i = r.hom g a b - algebraMap O R c ∧ W.wt i = w a - w b ∧ (w a ≠ w b → c = 0)

variable (Γ) in

def PhiCond (l : ℕ) : Prop :=
  ∀ H : Subgroup Γ, IsOpen (H : Set Γ) →
    Finite {φ : H →* Multiplicative (ZMod l) // IsOpen (φ.ker : Set H)}

end Lifts

end UpperTheory.V.Alg


[LFC_SRC_FIN_CHAR] Finite Kummer characters with fixed ramification
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/GaloisLibrary/KummerTheory/FinChar.lean#L1-L127
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.KummerTheory.RadExists
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.KummerTheory.DvdOfIne
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.KummerTheory.PlaceDict
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.KummerTheory.CardSel
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.KummerTheory.CardSUnit

open NumberField IsDedekindDomain Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal Formalism.GaloisCohomology UpperTheory.V UpperTheory.V.LDataCardLeFinrank UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.V.Kum

private theorem exists_valuationSubring_isPlaceOver (K Ω : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] [Field Ω] [Algebra K Ω]
    (v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 K)) : ∃ A : ValuationSubring Ω, IsPlaceOver A v := by
  let f : 𝓞 K →+* Ω := (algebraMap K Ω).comp (algebraMap (𝓞 K) K)
  have hf : Function.Injective f :=
    (algebraMap K Ω).injective.comp (IsFractionRing.injective (𝓞 K) K)
  let e : 𝓞 K ≃+* f.range := RingEquiv.ofBijective f.rangeRestrict
    ⟨fun x y h => hf (congrArg Subtype.val h), f.rangeRestrict_surjective⟩
  let I : Ideal f.range := v.asIdeal.map e
  have hI : I ≠ ⊤ := by
    haveI : (v.asIdeal.map e).IsPrime := Ideal.map_isPrime_of_equiv e
    exact Ideal.IsPrime.ne_top this
  obtain ⟨V, hRV, hV⟩ := Ideal.image_subset_nonunits_valuationSubring I hI
  have hlt : ∀ y ∈ v.asIdeal, V.valuation (f y) < 1 := by
    intro y hy
    have h1 : f y ∈ V.nonunits := hV ⟨e y, Ideal.mem_map_of_mem e hy, rfl⟩
    exact (ValuationSubring.mem_nonunits_iff V).1 h1
  have hle : ∀ y : 𝓞 K, V.valuation (f y) ≤ 1 := by
    intro y
    exact (ValuationSubring.valuation_le_one_iff V _).2 (hRV (RingHom.mem_range_self f y))
  refine ⟨V, fun x => ⟨fun hx => ?_, fun hx => hlt x hx⟩⟩
  by_contra hxv
  obtain ⟨r, y, hy, hry⟩ := v.isMaximal.exists_inv hxv
  have h2 : V.valuation (f (r * x)) < 1 := by
    rw [map_mul, Valuation.map_mul]
    exact lt_of_le_of_lt (mul_le_of_le_one_left' (hle r)) hx
  have h3 : V.valuation (f (r * x + y)) < 1 := by
    rw [map_add]
    exact Valuation.map_add_lt _ h2 (hlt y hy)
  rw [hry, map_one, Valuation.map_one] at h3
  exact lt_irrefl _ h3

private theorem finite_above_of_ne_zero (K : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] (N : ℕ) (hN : N ≠ 0) :
    (Above K N).Finite := by
  have h0 : Ideal.span {((N : ℕ) : 𝓞 K)} ≠ 0 := by
    rw [Ne, Ideal.zero_eq_bot, Ideal.span_singleton_eq_bot]
    exact_mod_cast hN
  refine (Ideal.finite_factors h0).subset ?_
  intro v hv
  exact Ideal.dvd_span_singleton.2 hv

theorem UpperTheory.V.Kum.finChar :
    ∀ (K Ω : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] [Field Ω] [Algebra K Ω] [IsAlgClosure K Ω] (n N : ℕ) (ζ : K), n ≠ 0 → N ≠ 0 → IsPrimitiveRoot ζ n → {φ : G K Ω → ZMod n | IsLocallyConstant φ ∧ (∀ a b, φ (a * b) = φ a + φ b) ∧ ∀ A : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬ A.valuation (N : Ω) < 1 → ∀ g ∈ Ine (F := K) A, φ g = 0}.Finite := by
  intro K Ω _ _ _ _ _ n N ζ hn hN hζ
  haveI : NeZero n := ⟨hn⟩
  let ζΩ := algebraMap K Ω ζ
  have hζΩ : IsPrimitiveRoot ζΩ n := hζ.map_of_injective (algebraMap K Ω).injective
  have hfix : ∀ (g : G K Ω) (x : K), g (algebraMap K Ω x) = algebraMap K Ω x :=
    fun g x => AlgEquiv.commutes g x
  have hη : ∀ g : G K Ω, g ζΩ = ζΩ ^ ((fun _ => (1 : ZMod n)) g).val := by
    intro g
    rw [hfix g ζ]
    rcases Nat.lt_or_ge 1 n with h1 | h1
    · rw [ZMod.val_one_eq_one_mod, Nat.mod_eq_of_lt h1, pow_one]
    · have hn1 : n = 1 := by omega
      subst hn1
      have : ζΩ = 1 := IsPrimitiveRoot.one_right_iff.1 hζΩ
      show ζΩ = ζΩ ^ _
      rw [this, one_pow]
  have key : ∀ φ ∈ {φ : G K Ω → ZMod n | IsLocallyConstant φ ∧ (∀ a b, φ (a * b) = φ a + φ b) ∧
      ∀ A : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬ A.valuation (N : Ω) < 1 → ∀ g ∈ Ine (F := K) A, φ g = 0},
      ∃ β : Ω, ∃ a : Kˣ, a ∈ SelK (Above K N) n ∧ β ^ n = algebraMap K Ω a ∧
        ∀ g : G K Ω, g β = ζΩ ^ (φ g).val * β := by
    rintro φ ⟨hlc, hadd, hine⟩
    obtain ⟨β, a, hβ, hg⟩ := radExists K Ω n ζΩ (fun _ => 1) φ hζΩ hη hlc
      (fun g h => by rw [hadd, one_mul])
    refine ⟨β, a, ?_, hβ, hg⟩
    intro v hv
    obtain ⟨A, hA⟩ := exists_valuationSubring_isPlaceOver K Ω v
    have hnot : ¬ A.valuation (N : Ω) < 1 := fun h => hv (((placeDict K Ω).2.2.2 A v N hA).1 h)
    exact dvdOfIne K Ω n A v β a hA hβ
      (fun g hg' => by rw [hg, hine A hnot g hg', ZMod.val_zero, pow_zero, one_mul])
  choose! β a ha hβ hg using key
  haveI hQ : Finite (↥(SelK (Above K N) n) ⧸ (powK K n).subgroupOf (SelK (Above K N) n)) := by
    apply Nat.finite_of_card_ne_zero
    rw [cardSel K _ n hn, cardSUnit K _ n (finite_above_of_ne_zero K N hN)]
    have h1 : Nat.card ↥(rootsOfUnity n K) ≠ 0 := Nat.card_pos.ne'
    have h2 : Nat.card {c : ClS (Above K N) // c ^ n = 1} ≠ 0 := by
      haveI : Nonempty {c : ClS (Above K N) // c ^ n = 1} := ⟨⟨1, one_pow _⟩⟩
      exact Nat.card_pos.ne'
    exact mul_ne_zero (mul_ne_zero (pow_ne_zero _ hn) h1) h2
  rw [← Set.finite_coe_iff]
  refine Finite.of_injective (β := ↥(SelK (Above K N) n) ⧸ (powK K n).subgroupOf (SelK (Above K N) n))
    (fun φ => QuotientGroup.mk ⟨a φ.1, ha φ.1 φ.2⟩) ?_
  rintro ⟨φ, hφ⟩ ⟨ψ, hψ⟩ h
  simp only at h
  rw [QuotientGroup.eq, Subgroup.mem_subgroupOf] at h
  obtain ⟨c, hc⟩ := h
  simp only [powMonoidHom_apply, Subgroup.coe_mul, Subgroup.coe_inv] at hc
  have hcu : a φ * c ^ n = a ψ := by rw [hc, mul_inv_cancel_left]
  have hne : ∀ θ ∈ _, β θ ≠ 0 := fun θ hθ h0 => by
    have := hβ θ hθ
    rw [h0, zero_pow hn] at this
    exact (Units.ne_zero (a θ)) ((algebraMap K Ω).injective (this.symm.trans (map_zero _).symm))
  refine Subtype.ext (funext fun g => ?_)
  have hφ0 := hne φ hφ
  have hcΩ : algebraMap K Ω (c : K) ≠ 0 := by
    rw [Ne, map_eq_zero]
    exact c.ne_zero
  set ω := β ψ / (β φ * algebraMap K Ω (c : K)) with hωdef
  have hωn : ω ^ n = 1 := by
    rw [hωdef, div_pow, mul_pow, hβ ψ hψ, hβ φ hφ, ← map_pow, ← map_mul, ← hcu]
    push_cast
    exact div_self (by simp)
  obtain ⟨i, -, hi⟩ := hζΩ.eq_pow_of_pow_eq_one hωn
  have hψeq : β ψ = ω * (β φ * algebraMap K Ω (c : K)) := by
    rw [hωdef, div_mul_cancel₀ _ (mul_ne_zero hφ0 hcΩ)]
  have hgω : g ω = ω := by
    rw [← hi, map_pow]
    exact congrArg (· ^ i) (hfix g ζ)
  have hω0 : ω ≠ 0 := by
    rw [← hi]
    exact pow_ne_zero _ (hζΩ.ne_zero hn)
  have e1 := hg ψ hψ g
  rw [hψeq, map_mul, map_mul, hgω, hg φ hφ g, hfix] at e1
  have e2 : ζΩ ^ (φ g).val = ζΩ ^ (ψ g).val := by
    have h3 := mul_ne_zero hω0 (mul_ne_zero hφ0 hcΩ)
    apply mul_right_cancel₀ h3
    linear_combination e1
  exact ZMod.val_injective _ (hζΩ.pow_inj (ZMod.val_lt _) (ZMod.val_lt _) e2)


[LFC_SRC_RAD_EXISTS] Kummer eigenvector construction
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/GaloisLibrary/KummerTheory/RadExists.lean#L1-L24
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.KummerTheory.Mk0IdMapCongr
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.KummerTheory.Imports

open NumberField IsDedekindDomain Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal Formalism.GaloisCohomology UpperTheory.V UpperTheory.V.LDataCardLeFinrank UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.V.Kum

theorem UpperTheory.V.Kum.radExists :
    ∀ (K Ω : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] [Field Ω] [Algebra K Ω] [IsAlgClosure K Ω] (n : ℕ) [NeZero n] (ζ : Ω) (η z : G K Ω → ZMod n), IsPrimitiveRoot ζ n → (∀ g : G K Ω, g ζ = ζ ^ (η g).val) → IsLocallyConstant z → (∀ g h, z (g * h) = z g + η g * z h) →
    ∃ (β : Ω) (a : Kˣ), β ^ n = algebraMap K Ω a ∧ ∀ g : G K Ω, g β = ζ ^ (z g).val * β := by
  intro K Ω _ _ _ _ _ n _ ζ η z hζ hη hz hc
  have hmod : ∀ m : ℕ, ζ ^ (m % n) = ζ ^ m := fun m => (pow_eq_pow_mod m hζ.pow_eq_one).symm
  have hζ0 : ζ ≠ 0 := hζ.ne_zero (NeZero.ne n)
  obtain ⟨β, hβ0, hβ⟩ := UpperTheory.V.Kum.h90 K Ω (fun g => ζ ^ (z g).val)
    (hz.comp fun x : ZMod n => ζ ^ x.val) (fun g => pow_ne_zero _ hζ0) (fun g h => by
      show ζ ^ (z (g * h)).val = ζ ^ (z g).val * g (ζ ^ (z h).val)
      rw [hc, ZMod.val_add, hmod, pow_add, ZMod.val_mul, hmod, map_pow, hη, ← pow_mul])
  haveI : IsGalois K Ω := {}
  have hfix : ∀ g : Ω ≃ₐ[K] Ω, g (β ^ n) = β ^ n := fun g => by
    rw [map_pow, hβ g, mul_pow, ← pow_mul, mul_comm _ n, pow_mul, hζ.pow_eq_one, one_pow, one_mul]
  obtain ⟨a, ha⟩ := (InfiniteGalois.mem_range_algebraMap_iff_fixed (β ^ n)).2 hfix
  have ha0 : a ≠ 0 := by
    rintro rfl
    rw [map_zero] at ha
    exact pow_ne_zero n hβ0 ha.symm
  exact ⟨β, Units.mk0 a ha0, ha.symm, hβ⟩


[LFC_SRC_CARD_SEL] Ideal-class exact-sequence calculation
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/GaloisLibrary/KummerTheory/CardSel.lean#L1-L44
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.KummerTheory.Mk0IdMapCongr
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.KummerTheory.RadClMul
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.KummerTheory.RadClSurj
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.KummerTheory.RadClKer

open NumberField IsDedekindDomain Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal Formalism.GaloisCohomology UpperTheory.V UpperTheory.V.LDataCardLeFinrank UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.V.Kum

private theorem card_parity_sign_length_of_eq {G C : Type} [CommGroup G] [Group C] (A U P : Subgroup G) (hU : U ≤ A) (hP : P ≤ A)
    (f : A →* C) (hf : f.ker = (U ⊔ P).subgroupOf A) :
    Nat.card (A ⧸ P.subgroupOf A) = Nat.card (U ⧸ P.subgroupOf U) * Nat.card f.range := by
  have hQ : U ⊔ P ≤ A := sup_le hU hP
  have hPQ : P.subgroupOf A ≤ (U ⊔ P).subgroupOf A := fun x hx => (le_sup_right : P ≤ U ⊔ P) hx
  change (P.subgroupOf A).index = (P.subgroupOf U).index * _
  rw [← Subgroup.relIndex_mul_index hPQ, Subgroup.relIndex_subgroupOf hQ, Subgroup.relIndex_sup_right, ← hf]
  congr 1
  exact Nat.card_congr (QuotientGroup.quotientKerEquivRange f).toEquiv

theorem UpperTheory.V.Kum.cardSel :
    ∀ (K : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 K))) (n : ℕ), n ≠ 0 → Nat.card (SelK S n ⧸ (powK K n).subgroupOf (SelK S n)) = Nat.card (S.unit K ⧸ (powK K n).subgroupOf (S.unit K)) * Nat.card {c : ClS S // c ^ n = 1} := by
  intro K _ _ S n hn
  obtain ⟨hmul, hpow, -⟩ := radClMul K S n hn
  let f : SelK S n →* ClS S :=
    MonoidHom.mk' (fun a => (QuotientGroup.mk (radCl n a.1) : ClS S)) fun a b => hmul a.1 a.2 b.1 b.2
  have hU : S.unit K ≤ SelK S n := by
    intro a ha v hv
    have h : v.valuation K (a : K) = 1 := ha v hv
    have h' : v.valuationOfNeZero a = 1 := by
      rw [← HeightOneSpectrum.valuationOfNeZero_eq] at h
      exact_mod_cast h
    simp [ordv, h']
  have hP : powK K n ≤ SelK S n := by
    rintro _ ⟨b, rfl⟩ v _
    simp [ordv]
  have hf : f.ker = (S.unit K ⊔ powK K n).subgroupOf (SelK S n) := by
    ext a
    rw [MonoidHom.mem_ker, Subgroup.mem_subgroupOf, ← radClKer K S n hn a.1 a.2]
    exact QuotientGroup.eq_one_iff _
  rw [card_parity_sign_length_of_eq _ _ _ hU hP f hf]
  congr 1
  refine Nat.card_congr (Equiv.subtypeEquivRight fun c => ⟨?_, fun hc => ?_⟩)
  · rintro ⟨a, rfl⟩
    exact hpow a.1 a.2
  · obtain ⟨a, ha, h⟩ := radClSurj K S n hn c hc
    exact ⟨⟨a, ha⟩, h⟩


[LFC_SRC_CARD_SUNIT] S-unit finiteness input
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/GaloisLibrary/KummerTheory/CardSUnit.lean#L1-L68
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.KummerTheory.Mk0IdMapCongr
import GroundTheory.GlobalFields.ExistenceTheorem.PowerIndex

open NumberField IsDedekindDomain Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal Formalism.GaloisCohomology UpperTheory.V UpperTheory.V.LDataCardLeFinrank UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.V.Kum

private theorem index_range_eq_card_ker {T : Type*} [CommGroup T] [Finite T] (f : T →* T) :
    f.range.index = Nat.card f.ker := by
  have h1 := Subgroup.card_mul_index f.range
  have h2 := Subgroup.card_mul_index f.ker
  have hk : f.ker.index = Nat.card f.range := by
    show Nat.card (T ⧸ f.ker) = _
    exact Nat.card_congr (QuotientGroup.quotientKerEquivRange f).toEquiv
  rw [hk] at h2
  have hpos : 0 < Nat.card f.range := Nat.card_pos
  have h3 : Nat.card f.range * f.range.index = Nat.card f.range * Nat.card f.ker := by
    rw [h1, ← h2, mul_comm]
  exact Nat.eq_of_mul_eq_mul_left hpos h3

private theorem mem_unit_of_pow_mem {K : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] {S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 K))} {p : ℕ}
    (hp : p ≠ 0) {b : Kˣ} (hb : b ^ p ∈ S.unit K) : b ∈ S.unit K := by
  intro v hv
  have h := hb v hv
  rw [Units.val_pow_eq_pow_val, map_pow] at h
  simpa [hp] using h

private theorem powK_subgroupOf {K : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 K))) {p : ℕ}
    (hp : p ≠ 0) : (powK K p).subgroupOf (S.unit K) = (powMonoidHom p : S.unit K →* S.unit K).range := by
  ext x
  rw [Subgroup.mem_subgroupOf, powK.eq_1, MonoidHom.mem_range, MonoidHom.mem_range]
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨b, hb⟩
    rw [powMonoidHom_apply] at hb
    have hbS : b ∈ S.unit K := mem_unit_of_pow_mem hp (by rw [hb]; exact x.2)
    refine ⟨⟨b, hbS⟩, ?_⟩
    apply Subtype.ext
    simpa using hb
  · rintro ⟨c, hc⟩
    refine ⟨c.1, ?_⟩
    rw [← hc]
    simp

private theorem card_ker_eq {K : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 K))) (n : ℕ) [NeZero n] :
    Nat.card (powMonoidHom n : CommGroup.torsion (S.unit K) →* CommGroup.torsion (S.unit K)).ker =
      Nat.card (rootsOfUnity n K) := by
  have hfin : ∀ ζ : rootsOfUnity n K, IsOfFinOrder (ζ : Kˣ) := fun ζ =>
    isOfFinOrder_iff_pow_eq_one.mpr ⟨n, NeZero.pos n, (mem_rootsOfUnity _ _).mp ζ.2⟩
  have hS : ∀ ζ : rootsOfUnity n K, (ζ : Kˣ) ∈ S.unit K := fun ζ v _ =>
    GroundTheory.Global.SUnit.valuation_eq_one_of_isOfFinOrder (hfin ζ) v
  refine Nat.card_congr
    { toFun := fun t => ⟨t.1.1.1, (mem_rootsOfUnity _ _).mpr (by
        have h := congrArg (fun y : CommGroup.torsion (S.unit K) => ((y : S.unit K) : Kˣ))
          (MonoidHom.mem_ker.mp t.2)
        simpa using h)⟩
      invFun := fun ζ => ⟨⟨⟨ζ.1, hS ζ⟩, (CommGroup.mem_torsion _).mpr
          (isOfFinOrder_iff_pow_eq_one.mpr ⟨n, NeZero.pos n, Subtype.ext (by
            simpa using (mem_rootsOfUnity _ _).mp ζ.2)⟩)⟩,
        MonoidHom.mem_ker.mpr (Subtype.ext (Subtype.ext (by
            simpa using (mem_rootsOfUnity _ _).mp ζ.2)))⟩
      left_inv := fun _ => rfl
      right_inv := fun _ => rfl }

theorem UpperTheory.V.Kum.cardSUnit :
    ∀ (K : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 K))) (n : ℕ) [NeZero n], S.Finite → Nat.card (S.unit K ⧸ (powK K n).subgroupOf (S.unit K)) = n ^ (InfinitePlace.nrRealPlaces K + InfinitePlace.nrComplexPlaces K - 1 + Nat.card S) * Nat.card (rootsOfUnity n K) := by
  intro K _ _ S n _ hS
  haveI : Finite S := hS
  rw [← Subgroup.index_eq_card, powK_subgroupOf S (NeZero.ne n),
    GroundTheory.Global.index_range_powMonoidHom_eq_pow_mul (NeZero.ne n), GroundTheory.Global.SUnit.finrank_quotient_torsion,
    index_range_eq_card_ker, card_ker_eq, Units.rank, InfinitePlace.card_eq_nrRealPlaces_add_nrComplexPlaces]


[LFC_SRC_DVD_INE] Unramified radical implies divisible valuations
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/GaloisLibrary/KummerTheory/DvdOfIne.lean#L1-L90
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.KummerTheory.Mk0IdMapCongr
import CompanionTheory.AutomorphicTheory.HeckeStandardModules.ExistsCen

open NumberField IsDedekindDomain Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal Formalism.GaloisCohomology UpperTheory.V UpperTheory.V.LDataCardLeFinrank UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.V.Kum

private theorem ram_one {K E : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] [Field E] [NumberField E] [Algebra K E] [IsGalois K E]
    (v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 K)) (P : Ideal (𝓞 E)) [P.IsPrime] [P.LiesOver v.asIdeal]
    (L : IntermediateField K E) (h : P.inertia (E ≃ₐ[K] E) ≤ L.fixingSubgroup) :
    (P.under (𝓞 L)).ramificationIdx (𝓞 K) = 1 := by
  have hb := v.ne_bot
  have hPb : P ≠ ⊥ := Ideal.ne_bot_of_liesOver_of_ne_bot hb P
  have hqb : P.under (𝓞 L) ≠ ⊥ := Ideal.under_ne_bot _ hPb
  have : IsGaloisGroup L.fixingSubgroup L E :=
    .of_mulEquiv_algEquiv (IntermediateField.fixingSubgroupEquiv L) fun _ _ ↦ rfl
  have : IsGaloisGroup L.fixingSubgroup (𝓞 L) (𝓞 E) :=
    IsGaloisGroup.of_isFractionRing _ _ _ L E
  have := v.isMaximal
  have : (P.under (𝓞 L)).IsMaximal := Ideal.IsPrime.isMaximal inferInstance hqb
  have : Finite (𝓞 K ⧸ v.asIdeal) := Ideal.finiteQuotientOfFreeOfNeBot _ hb
  have : Finite (𝓞 L ⧸ P.under (𝓞 L)) := Ideal.finiteQuotientOfFreeOfNeBot _ hqb
  have h1 := Ideal.card_inertia_eq_ramificationIdxIn (G := E ≃ₐ[K] E) v.asIdeal P
  have h2 := Ideal.card_inertia_eq_ramificationIdxIn (G := L.fixingSubgroup) (P.under (𝓞 L)) P
  rw [Ideal.ramificationIdxIn_eq_ramificationIdx _ P (E ≃ₐ[K] E)] at h1
  rw [Ideal.ramificationIdxIn_eq_ramificationIdx _ P L.fixingSubgroup] at h2
  have h3 := Ideal.ramificationIdx_tower (R := 𝓞 K) (P.under (𝓞 L)) P
  have h4 : Nat.card (P.inertia L.fixingSubgroup) =
      Nat.card ↥(P.inertia (E ≃ₐ[K] E) ⊓ L.fixingSubgroup) := by
    rw [← AddSubgroup.inertia_map_subtype, Subgroup.card_subtype]
  rw [← h1, ← h2, h4] at h3
  have hpos : 0 < Nat.card ↥(P.inertia (E ≃ₐ[K] E) ⊓ L.fixingSubgroup) := Nat.card_pos
  rw [inf_eq_left.mpr h] at h3 hpos
  exact Nat.eq_of_mul_eq_mul_right hpos (h3.symm.trans (one_mul _).symm)

private theorem dvd_of_ram {K L : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] [Field L] [NumberField L] [Algebra K L]
    (v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 K)) (Q : Ideal (𝓞 L)) [Q.IsPrime] [Q.LiesOver v.asIdeal]
    (h : Q.ramificationIdx (𝓞 K) = 1) (n : ℕ) (b : L) (a : Kˣ) (hb : b ^ n = algebraMap K L a) :
    (n : ℤ) ∣ ordv v a := by
  let w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 L) := ⟨Q, inferInstance, Ideal.ne_bot_of_liesOver_of_ne_bot v.ne_bot Q⟩
  have : w.asIdeal.LiesOver v.asIdeal := (inferInstance : Q.LiesOver v.asIdeal)
  have h1 := HeightOneSpectrum.valuation_liesOver L v w (a : K)
  rw [show v.asIdeal.ramificationIdx' w.asIdeal = 1 from
    (Ideal.ramificationIdx'_eq_ramificationIdx v.asIdeal Q v.ne_bot).trans h, pow_one, ← hb, map_pow,
    ← HeightOneSpectrum.valuationOfNeZero_eq] at h1
  rcases Nat.eq_zero_or_pos n with rfl | hn
  · rw [pow_zero, ← WithZero.coe_one, WithZero.coe_inj] at h1
    simp [ordv, h1]
  · have h0 : w.valuation L b ≠ 0 := fun e => by
      rw [e, zero_pow hn.ne'] at h1
      exact WithZero.coe_ne_zero h1
    obtain ⟨m, hm⟩ := WithZero.ne_zero_iff_exists.1 h0
    rw [← hm, ← WithZero.coe_pow, WithZero.coe_inj] at h1
    rw [ordv, h1, toAdd_pow, dvd_neg, nsmul_eq_mul]
    exact dvd_mul_right _ _

private theorem dvd_fin {K E : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] [Field E] [NumberField E] [Algebra K E] [IsGalois K E]
    (v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 K)) (P : Ideal (𝓞 E)) [P.IsPrime] [P.LiesOver v.asIdeal] (n : ℕ) (b : E) (a : Kˣ)
    (hb : b ^ n = algebraMap K E a) (h : ∀ τ ∈ P.inertia (E ≃ₐ[K] E), τ b = b) : (n : ℤ) ∣ ordv v a := by
  let L : IntermediateField K E := IntermediateField.fixedField (P.inertia (E ≃ₐ[K] E))
  have hle : P.inertia (E ≃ₐ[K] E) ≤ L.fixingSubgroup := (IntermediateField.le_iff_le _ _).1 le_rfl
  have hbL : b ∈ L := (IntermediateField.mem_fixedField_iff _ _).2 h
  have : (P.under (𝓞 L)).LiesOver v.asIdeal := ⟨by rw [Ideal.under_under]; exact Ideal.LiesOver.over⟩
  exact dvd_of_ram v (P.under (𝓞 L)) (ram_one v P L hle) n ⟨b, hbL⟩ a (Subtype.ext hb)

theorem UpperTheory.V.Kum.dvdOfIne :
    ∀ (K Ω : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] [Field Ω] [Algebra K Ω] [IsAlgClosure K Ω] (n : ℕ) (A : ValuationSubring Ω) (v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 K)) (β : Ω) (a : Kˣ), IsPlaceOver A v → β ^ n = algebraMap K Ω a → (∀ g ∈ Ine (F := K) A, g β = β) → (n : ℤ) ∣ ordv v a := by
  intro K Ω _ _ _ _ _ n A v β a hA hβ hI
  have : IsGalois K Ω := IsAlgClosure.isGalois K Ω
  have : CharZero Ω := charZero_of_injective_algebraMap (algebraMap K Ω).injective
  let L : IntermediateField K Ω := (FiniteGaloisIntermediateField.adjoin K ({β} : Set Ω)).toIntermediateField
  have hβL : β ∈ L := FiniteGaloisIntermediateField.subset_adjoin K _ rfl
  have : NumberField L := NumberField.of_module_finite K _
  have hv : (CompanionTheory.PlaceDict.cen A).under (𝓞 K) = v.asIdeal := Ideal.ext fun x => hA x
  have : Algebra.IsIntegral (𝓞 K) (𝓞 Ω) := ⟨fun b => (RingOfIntegers.isIntegral b).tower_top⟩
  have hm : (CompanionTheory.PlaceDict.cen A).IsMaximal :=
    Ideal.isMaximal_of_isIntegral_of_isMaximal_comap (R := 𝓞 K) _ (by
      have := v.isMaximal
      rwa [← hv] at this)
  let P : Ideal (𝓞 L) := (CompanionTheory.PlaceDict.cen A).under (𝓞 L)
  have : P.LiesOver v.asIdeal := ⟨by rw [← hv]; exact (Ideal.under_under _).symm⟩
  refine dvd_fin v P n ⟨β, hβL⟩ a (Subtype.ext hβ) fun τ hτ => ?_
  obtain ⟨φ, h1, h2⟩ := CompanionTheory.PlaceDict.lift L (CompanionTheory.PlaceDict.cen A) τ (RingHom.id _) fun x => by
    rw [RingHom.id_apply, Ideal.Quotient.eq, ← map_sub, ← Ideal.mem_comap]
    exact hτ x
  obtain ⟨h3, h4⟩ := CompanionTheory.PlaceDict.of_cong A φ one_ne_zero fun b => by
    rw [pow_one]
    exact Ideal.Quotient.eq.1 (h2 b)
  have h5 : φ ∈ Ine (F := K) A := ⟨h3, fun x hx => by
    simpa using (A.mem_nonunits_iff).1 (h4 x ((A.valuation_le_one_iff _).1 hx))⟩
  rw [← h1]
  exact Subtype.ext ((AlgEquiv.restrictNormal_commutes φ L ⟨β, hβL⟩).trans (hI φ h5))


[LFC_SRC_PHI] Full phiGam
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/GaloisCochains/PhiGam.lean#L1-L93
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisCochains.GLinkQuot
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.KummerTheory.FinChar
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.SelmerTransport.GalEmbTop

open NumberField IsDedekindDomain Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal Module UpperTheory.V UpperTheory.V.Fr UpperTheory.V.GC

private theorem pG_nat {F : Type} [Field F] [NumberField F] (S : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))) :
    ∃ N : ℕ, N ≠ 0 ∧ ∀ v ∈ S, ((N : ℕ) : 𝓞 F) ∈ v.asIdeal := by
  refine ⟨∏ v ∈ S, Ideal.absNorm v.asIdeal, Finset.prod_ne_zero_iff.2 fun v _ h => v.ne_bot (Ideal.absNorm_eq_zero_iff.1 h),
    fun v hv => ?_⟩
  obtain ⟨c, hc⟩ := Finset.dvd_prod_of_mem (fun v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F) => Ideal.absNorm v.asIdeal) hv
  rw [hc, Nat.cast_mul]
  exact Ideal.mul_mem_right _ _ (Ideal.absNorm_mem _)

private theorem pG_lc {H M : Type} [Group H] [TopologicalSpace H] [IsTopologicalGroup H] [Group M]
    (φ : H →* M) (h : IsOpen (φ.ker : Set H)) : IsLocallyConstant φ := by
  rw [IsLocallyConstant.iff_eventually_eq]
  intro x
  have ho : IsOpen ((fun y => x⁻¹ * y) ⁻¹' (φ.ker : Set H)) := h.preimage (by fun_prop)
  filter_upwards [ho.mem_nhds (show x ∈ (fun y => x⁻¹ * y) ⁻¹' (φ.ker : Set H) by simp)] with y hy
  have : φ (x⁻¹ * y) = 1 := hy
  rw [map_mul, map_inv, inv_mul_eq_one] at this
  exact this.symm

theorem UpperTheory.V.GC.phiGam :
    ∀ (F Ω : Type) [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] [IsAlgClosure F Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (S : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))) (K : Subgroup (G F Ω)) (Γ : Type) [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [IsTopologicalGroup Γ] [CompactSpace Γ] [T2Space Γ] [TotallyDisconnectedSpace Γ] (π : G F Ω →* Γ), GLink l S K π → Alg.PhiCond Γ l := by
  intro F Ω _ _ _ _ _ l hl S K Γ _ _ _ _ _ _ π hL H hH
  haveI : IsGalois F Ω := {}
  haveI : IsAlgClosed Ω := IsAlgClosure.isAlgClosed F
  haveI : CharZero Ω := charZero_of_injective_algebraMap (algebraMap F Ω).injective
  haveI : NeZero l := ⟨hl.out.ne_zero⟩
  obtain ⟨ζ, hζ⟩ := HasEnoughRootsOfUnity.exists_primitiveRoot Ω l
  obtain ⟨N, hN, hNS⟩ := pG_nat S
  let V : Subgroup (G F Ω) := (H.comap π ⊓ K) ⊓ MulAction.stabilizer (G F Ω) ζ
  have hVo : IsOpen (V : Set (G F Ω)) :=
    ((hH.preimage hL.cont).inter hL.isOpen).inter (stabilizer_isOpen_of_isIntegral ζ)
  let E : IntermediateField F Ω := IntermediateField.fixedField V
  have hE : E.fixingSubgroup = V :=
    InfiniteGalois.fixingSubgroup_fixedField ⟨V, V.isClosed_of_isOpen hVo⟩
  haveI : FiniteDimensional F E := (InfiniteGalois.isOpen_iff_finite E).1 (by rw [← hE] at hVo; exact hVo)
  haveI : NumberField E := NumberField.of_module_finite F E
  have hmem : ∀ σ : G E Ω, Base.galEmb F E Ω σ ∈ V := fun σ => by
    rw [← hE, IntermediateField.mem_fixingSubgroup_iff]
    exact fun x hx => σ.commutes ⟨x, hx⟩
  have hgal : V ≤ Base.galOf F E Ω := fun u hu x => x.2 ⟨u, hu⟩
  have hζE : ζ ∈ E := fun v => v.2.2
  have hζ' : IsPrimitiveRoot (⟨ζ, hζE⟩ : E) l :=
    IsPrimitiveRoot.of_map_of_injective (f := algebraMap E Ω) hζ (algebraMap E Ω).injective
  haveI := (Kum.finChar E Ω l N _ hl.out.ne_zero hN hζ').to_subtype
  let e : G E Ω →* H := (π.comp (Base.galEmb F E Ω)).codRestrict H fun σ => (hmem σ).1.1
  have he : Continuous e := (hL.cont.comp (Base.galEmbTop F E Ω).1.continuous).subtype_mk _
  have hker : ∀ A : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬ A.valuation (N : Ω) < 1 → ∀ g ∈ Ine (F := E) A, e g = 1 := by
    intro A hA g hg
    by_cases hT : A = ⊤
    · subst hT
      rw [(Kum.placeDict E Ω).1, Subgroup.mem_bot] at hg
      rw [hg, map_one]
    · obtain ⟨v, hv⟩ := (Kum.placeDict F Ω).2.1 A hT
      have hvS : v ∉ S := fun h => hA (((Kum.placeDict F Ω).2.2.2 A v N hv).2 (hNS v h))
      have hI : Base.galEmb F E Ω g ∈ Ine (F := F) A := ⟨hg.1, hg.2⟩
      have : Base.galEmb F E Ω g ∈ π.ker := by
        rw [hL.ker]
        refine Subgroup.le_topologicalClosure _ (Subgroup.subset_normalClosure ?_)
        simp only [Set.mem_iUnion]
        exact ⟨v, hvS, A, hv, hI, (hmem g).1.2⟩
      exact Subtype.ext this
  haveI : Finite (G F Ω ⧸ V) := V.quotient_finite_of_isOpen hVo
  have hfin : Finite (H ⧸ e.range) := by
    haveI : V.FiniteIndex := Subgroup.finiteIndex_of_finite_quotient
    have h1 : (V.map π).FiniteIndex :=
      ⟨fun h => Subgroup.FiniteIndex.index_ne_zero (H := V)
        (Nat.eq_zero_of_zero_dvd (h ▸ Subgroup.index_map_dvd V hL.onto))⟩
    have h2 : (V.map π).subgroupOf H ≤ e.range := by
      rintro x ⟨u, hu, hx⟩
      exact ⟨Kum.toGal E hgal ⟨u, hu⟩, Subtype.ext hx⟩
    haveI := Subgroup.finiteIndex_of_le h2
    infer_instance
  refine Finite.of_injective (β := {φ : G E Ω → ZMod l | IsLocallyConstant φ ∧ (∀ a b, φ (a * b) = φ a + φ b) ∧
      ∀ A : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬ A.valuation (N : Ω) < 1 → ∀ g ∈ Ine (F := E) A, φ g = 0} ×
      (H ⧸ e.range → Multiplicative (ZMod l)))
    (fun φ => (⟨fun g => Multiplicative.toAdd (φ.1 (e g)), ((pG_lc φ.1 φ.2).comp_continuous he).comp _,
      fun a b => by simp only [map_mul, toAdd_mul], fun A hA g hg => by
        show Multiplicative.toAdd (φ.1 (e g)) = 0
        rw [hker A hA g hg, map_one, toAdd_one]⟩, fun q => φ.1 q.out)) ?_
  intro φ ψ h
  have h1 : ∀ g, φ.1 (e g) = ψ.1 (e g) := fun g =>
    Multiplicative.toAdd.injective (congrFun (congrArg Subtype.val (congrArg Prod.fst h)) g)
  have h2 : ∀ q : H ⧸ e.range, φ.1 q.out = ψ.1 q.out := congrFun (congrArg Prod.snd h)
  refine Subtype.ext (MonoidHom.ext fun x => ?_)
  obtain ⟨⟨_, g, rfl⟩, hg⟩ := QuotientGroup.mk_out_eq_mul e.range x
  have h3 := h2 (QuotientGroup.mk x)
  rw [hg, map_mul, map_mul, h1] at h3
  exact mul_right_cancel h3

theorem UpperTheory.V.GC.phi :
    ∀ (F Ω : Type) [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] [IsAlgClosure F Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (k : Type) [Field k] [CharP k l] (Φ : Fr.Frame l k) (S : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))) (K : Subgroup (G F Ω)) (π : G F Ω →* Φ.Γ) (ι : ∀ v, Φ.Gv v →* G F Ω) (A : Φ.V → ValuationSubring Ω) (pl : Φ.V → HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F)) (e : Φ.Inf ≃ InfinitePlace F) (c : Φ.Inf → G F Ω), FLink l Φ S K π ι A pl e c → Alg.PhiCond Φ.Γ l := by
  intro F Ω _ _ _ _ _ l _ k _ _ Φ S K π ι A pl e c h
  exact UpperTheory.V.GC.phiGam F Ω l S K Φ.Γ π h.g


[LFC_SRC_CL] Full GC.cl, uniform exponent before all finite groups
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/GaloisCochains/EqOneOfPow.lean#L1-L252
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisCochains.GLinkQuot
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.KummerTheory.ArtKer
