open NumberField IsDedekindDomain Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal Module UpperTheory.V UpperTheory.V.Fr UpperTheory.V.GC

open scoped Pointwise in
private theorem galEmb_mem_inertia (k E Ω : Type) [Field k] [NumberField k] [Field E] [NumberField E] [Field Ω]
    [Algebra k Ω] [Algebra E Ω] [Algebra k E] [IsScalarTower k E Ω] (Av : ValuationSubring Ω)
    (σ : G E Ω) (hσ : σ ∈ Ine Av) : UpperTheory.V.Base.galEmb k E Ω σ ∈ Ine Av := by
  rw [mem_Ine] at hσ ⊢
  obtain ⟨hd, hv⟩ := hσ
  refine ⟨?_, fun x hx => by rw [UpperTheory.V.Base.galEmb_apply]; exact hv x hx⟩
  rw [MulAction.mem_stabilizer_iff] at hd ⊢
  conv_rhs => rw [← hd]
  ext x
  rw [ValuationSubring.mem_pointwise_smul_iff_inv_smul_mem,
    ValuationSubring.mem_pointwise_smul_iff_inv_smul_mem, ← map_inv, AlgEquiv.smul_def,
    AlgEquiv.smul_def, UpperTheory.V.Base.galEmb_apply]

private theorem valuation_lt_one_iff_zero_or_inv_not_mem {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] (Av : ValuationSubring Ω) (x : Ω) :
    Av.valuation x < 1 ↔ (x = 0 ∨ x⁻¹ ∉ Av) := by
  by_cases hx : x = 0
  · simp [hx]
  · rw [← ValuationSubring.valuation_le_one_iff, not_le, map_inv₀]
    simp only [hx, false_or]
    rw [← map_inv₀, Valuation.one_lt_val_iff _ (inv_ne_zero hx), inv_inv]

open scoped Pointwise in
private theorem conj_mem_inertia_of_mem_inertia_smul {F Ω : Type} [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω]
    (Av : ValuationSubring Ω) (σ τ : G F Ω) (hτ : τ ∈ Ine (σ • Av)) : σ⁻¹ * τ * σ ∈ Ine Av := by
  rw [mem_Ine] at hτ ⊢
  obtain ⟨hd, hv⟩ := hτ
  rw [MulAction.mem_stabilizer_iff] at hd
  refine ⟨?_, fun x hx => ?_⟩
  · rw [MulAction.mem_stabilizer_iff, mul_smul, mul_smul, hd, inv_smul_smul]
  · have hx' : σ x ∈ σ • Av := ValuationSubring.smul_mem_pointwise_smul σ x Av
      ((ValuationSubring.valuation_le_one_iff _ _).1 hx)
    have h := hv (σ x) ((ValuationSubring.valuation_le_one_iff _ _).2 hx')
    rw [valuation_lt_one_iff_zero_or_inv_not_mem] at h ⊢
    have he : (σ⁻¹ * τ * σ) x - x = σ⁻¹ (τ (σ x) - σ x) := by
      simp [map_sub]
    rw [he]
    rcases h with h | h
    · left; rw [h, map_zero]
    · right
      rw [← map_inv₀]
      intro hm
      apply h
      rw [ValuationSubring.mem_pointwise_smul_iff_inv_smul_mem, AlgEquiv.smul_def]
      exact hm

private theorem eq_one_of_pow_coprime_in_pGroup {B : Type} [CommGroup B] {l : ℕ} (hl : l.Prime) (hB : IsPGroup l B)
    (y : B) (n : ℕ) (hn : ¬ l ∣ n) (hy : y ^ n = 1) : y = 1 := by
  obtain ⟨m, hm⟩ := hB y
  have h1 := orderOf_dvd_of_pow_eq_one hm
  have h2 := orderOf_dvd_of_pow_eq_one hy
  have hc : Nat.Coprime (l ^ m) n := Nat.Coprime.pow_left m ((Nat.Prime.coprime_iff_not_dvd hl).2 hn)
  have : orderOf y ∣ 1 := by
    rw [← hc]
    exact Nat.dvd_gcd h1 h2
  exact orderOf_eq_one_iff.1 (Nat.dvd_one.1 this)

open UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.V.Kum in
theorem UpperTheory.V.GC.cl :
    ∀ (F Ω : Type) [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] [IsAlgClosure F Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (k : Type) [Field k] [CharP k l] (Φ : Fr.Frame l k) (S : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))) (K : Subgroup (G F Ω)) (π : G F Ω →* Φ.Γ) (ι : ∀ v, Φ.Gv v →* G F Ω) (A : Φ.V → ValuationSubring Ω) (pl : Φ.V → HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F)) (e : Φ.Inf ≃ InfinitePlace F) (c : Φ.Inf → G F Ω), FLink l Φ S K π ι A pl e c → ∃ h : ℕ, h ≠ 0 ∧ ∀ (B : Type) [CommGroup B] [Finite B], IsPGroup l B → ∀ χ : Φ.G0 →* B,
    IsOpen (χ.ker : Set Φ.G0) → (∀ p : Φ.V × Bool, ∀ g ∈ Φ.Iv p.1, χ (Φ.loc2 p g) = 1) → ∀ g, χ g ^ h = 1 := by
  intro F Ω _ _ _ _ _ l _ k _ _ Φ S K π ι A pl e c hL
  classical
  set U : Subgroup (G F Ω) := Φ.del.ker.comap π with hU
  have hUo : IsOpen (U : Set (G F Ω)) := hL.g.cont.isOpen_preimage _ hL.del_open
  let Uc : ClosedSubgroup (G F Ω) := ⟨U, U.isClosed_of_isOpen hUo⟩
  set E : IntermediateField F Ω := IntermediateField.fixedField (Uc : Subgroup (G F Ω)) with hE
  have hEU : E.fixingSubgroup = U := InfiniteGalois.fixingSubgroup_fixedField Uc
  haveI : FiniteDimensional F E := (InfiniteGalois.isOpen_iff_finite E).1 (by rw [hEU]; exact hUo)
  haveI : NumberField E := inferInstance
  haveI : IsAlgClosure E Ω := ⟨IsAlgClosure.isAlgClosed F, Algebra.IsAlgebraic.tower_top (K := F) E⟩
  have hgal : UpperTheory.V.Base.galOf F E Ω = U := by
    rw [← hEU]
    ext σ
    simp [UpperTheory.V.Base.galOf, IntermediateField.mem_fixingSubgroup_iff]
  refine ⟨2 * Nat.card (ClassGroup (𝓞 E)), ?_, ?_⟩
  · have : 0 < Nat.card (ClassGroup (𝓞 E)) := Nat.card_pos
    omega
  intro B _ _ hB χ hχo hχI g
  have hmemU : ∀ σ : G E Ω, π (UpperTheory.V.Base.galEmb F E Ω σ) ∈ Φ.del.ker := fun σ => by
    have := UpperTheory.V.Base.galEmb_mem F E Ω σ
    rw [hgal] at this
    exact this
  let f : G E Ω →* Φ.G0 := (π.comp (UpperTheory.V.Base.galEmb F E Ω)).codRestrict Φ.del.ker hmemU
  let ψ : G E Ω →* B := (χ.comp f) ^ 2
  have key : ∀ (Av : ValuationSubring Ω) (τ : G F Ω), τ ∈ Ine Av → ∀ hτ : π τ ∈ Φ.del.ker, χ ⟨π τ, hτ⟩ = 1 := by
    intro Av τ hτA hτ
    have hZ : ∀ y : Ω, IsIntegral ℤ y → y ∈ Av := by
      intro y hy
      have hy' : IsIntegral Av y := hy.tower_top
      obtain ⟨a, ha⟩ := IsIntegrallyClosed.isIntegral_iff.1 hy'
      rw [← ha]
      exact a.2
    let φ : 𝓞 F →+* Av := ((algebraMap F Ω).comp (algebraMap (𝓞 F) F)).codRestrict Av
      (fun x => hZ _ ((x.2).map (IsScalarTower.toAlgHom ℤ F Ω)))
    let P : Ideal (𝓞 F) := (IsLocalRing.maximalIdeal Av).comap φ
    have hP : ∀ x : 𝓞 F, x ∈ P ↔ Av.valuation (algebraMap F Ω x) < 1 := by
      intro x
      exact (Av.valuation_lt_one_iff (φ x))
    by_cases hPb : P = ⊥
    · have hall : ∀ y : Ω, y ∈ Av := by
        intro y
        haveI : CharZero Ω := charZero_of_injective_algebraMap (algebraMap F Ω).injective
        haveI : Algebra.IsAlgebraic F Ω := IsAlgClosure.isAlgebraic
        haveI : Algebra.IsAlgebraic ℚ Ω := Algebra.IsAlgebraic.trans ℚ F Ω
        have halg : IsAlgebraic ℤ y := by
          rw [IsFractionRing.isAlgebraic_iff ℤ ℚ Ω]
          exact Algebra.IsAlgebraic.isAlgebraic y
        obtain ⟨m, hm0, hm⟩ := halg.exists_integral_multiple
        have h1 : m • y ∈ Av := hZ _ hm
        have hcast : algebraMap F Ω ((m : 𝓞 F) : F) = (m : Ω) := by simp
        have h2 : Av.valuation (m : Ω) = 1 := by
          have hnot : (m : 𝓞 F) ∉ P := by
            rw [hPb, Ideal.mem_bot]
            exact_mod_cast hm0
          rw [hP, hcast] at hnot
          have hle : Av.valuation (m : Ω) ≤ 1 := (Av.valuation_le_one_iff _).2 (intCast_mem Av m)
          exact le_antisymm hle (not_lt.1 hnot)
        rw [← Av.valuation_le_one_iff] at h1 ⊢
        rw [zsmul_eq_mul, map_mul, h2, one_mul] at h1
        exact h1
      have hτ1 : τ = 1 := by
        ext x
        rw [mem_Ine] at hτA
        have h := hτA.2 x ((Av.valuation_le_one_iff x).2 (hall x))
        rw [valuation_lt_one_iff_zero_or_inv_not_mem] at h
        rcases h with h | h
        · simpa [sub_eq_zero] using h
        · exact absurd (hall _) h
      have : (⟨π τ, hτ⟩ : Φ.G0) = 1 := by
        apply Subtype.ext
        simp [hτ1]
      rw [this, map_one]
    · haveI : P.IsPrime := Ideal.comap_isPrime φ (IsLocalRing.maximalIdeal Av)
      let v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F) := ⟨P, inferInstance, hPb⟩
      have hA : IsPlaceOver Av v := fun x => (hP x).symm
      by_cases hvS : v ∈ S
      · have hlv : (l : 𝓞 F) ∈ v.asIdeal := hL.only v hvS
        obtain ⟨u, hu⟩ := (hL.loc.mem v).2 hlv
        have hAu : IsPlaceOver Av (pl u) := hu ▸ hA
        obtain ⟨σ, hσ⟩ := exists_smul_eq_of_isPlaceOver (hL.loc.isOver u) hAu
        rw [← hσ] at hτA
        have hc := conj_mem_inertia_of_mem_inertia_smul (A u) σ τ hτA
        have hdec : σ⁻¹ * τ * σ ∈ (ι u).range := by
          rw [hL.loc.range u]
          exact Ine_le_Dec _ hc
        obtain ⟨s, hs⟩ := hdec
        have hsI : s ∈ Φ.Iv u := by
          rw [hL.ine u, Subgroup.mem_comap, hs]
          exact hc
        have hπτ : π τ = π σ * ((Φ.loc0 u s : Φ.G0) : Φ.Γ) * (π σ)⁻¹ := by
          have h1 : ((Φ.loc0 u s : Φ.G0) : Φ.Γ) = π (ι u s) := hL.loc_eq u s
          rw [h1, hs, map_mul, map_mul, map_inv]
          group
        rcases Int.units_eq_one_or (Φ.del (π σ)) with hd | hd
        · have ha : π σ ∈ Φ.del.ker := hd
          have : (⟨π τ, hτ⟩ : Φ.G0) = ⟨π σ, ha⟩ * Φ.loc0 u s * (⟨π σ, ha⟩ : Φ.G0)⁻¹ := by
            apply Subtype.ext
            simp [hπτ]
          rw [this, map_mul, map_mul, map_inv, mul_comm (χ _), mul_inv_cancel_right]
          have h := hχI (u, false) s hsI
          simpa [UpperTheory.V.Fr.Frame.loc2] using h
        · have ha : Φ.c⁻¹ * π σ ∈ Φ.del.ker := by
            rw [MonoidHom.mem_ker, map_mul, map_inv, hd, hL.del_c]
            rfl
          have : (⟨π τ, hτ⟩ : Φ.G0) =
              Φ.conj (⟨Φ.c⁻¹ * π σ, ha⟩ * Φ.loc0 u s * (⟨Φ.c⁻¹ * π σ, ha⟩ : Φ.G0)⁻¹) := by
            apply Subtype.ext
            simp only [UpperTheory.V.Fr.Frame.conj, MulEquiv.coe_toMonoidHom, MulAut.conjNormal_apply]
            simp [hπτ]
            group
          rw [this, map_mul, map_mul, map_inv, map_mul, map_mul, map_inv, mul_comm (χ _),
            mul_inv_cancel_right]
          have h := hχI (u, true) s hsI
          simpa [UpperTheory.V.Fr.Frame.loc2] using h
      · haveI := hL.g.normal
        set n := K.index with hn
        have hK : τ ^ n ∈ K := K.pow_index_mem τ
        have hI : τ ^ n ∈ Ine Av := (Ine Av).pow_mem hτA n
        have hT : τ ^ n ∈ tameN (↑S) K := by
          apply Subgroup.le_topologicalClosure
          apply Subgroup.subset_normalClosure
          refine Set.mem_iUnion₂.2 ⟨v, by simpa using hvS, Set.mem_iUnion₂.2 ⟨Av, hA, ?_⟩⟩
          exact Subgroup.mem_inf.2 ⟨hI, hK⟩
        have hπ : π (τ ^ n) = 1 := by
          rw [← MonoidHom.mem_ker, hL.g.ker]
          exact hT
        apply eq_one_of_pow_coprime_in_pGroup (Fact.out : l.Prime) hB _ n hL.g.idx
        rw [← map_pow]
        have : (⟨π τ, hτ⟩ : Φ.G0) ^ n = 1 := by
          apply Subtype.ext
          simp [← map_pow, hπ]
        rw [this, map_one]
  have hopen : IsOpen (ψ.ker : Set (G E Ω)) := by
    have hc : Continuous f := by
      have h1 : Continuous (UpperTheory.V.Base.galEmb F E Ω) :=
        (UpperTheory.V.Base.galEmbTop F E Ω).1.continuous
      exact (hL.g.cont.comp h1).subtype_mk _
    have h2 : IsOpen ((χ.comp f).ker : Set (G E Ω)) := hχo.preimage hc
    refine Subgroup.isOpen_mono ?_ h2
    intro x hx
    simp only [MonoidHom.mem_ker] at hx ⊢
    simp [ψ, hx]
  have hine : ∀ Av : ValuationSubring Ω, Ine Av ≤ ψ.ker := by
    intro Av σ hσ
    have h := key Av _ (galEmb_mem_inertia F E Ω Av σ hσ) (hmemU σ)
    rw [MonoidHom.mem_ker]
    have : ψ σ = χ ⟨π (UpperTheory.V.Base.galEmb F E Ω σ), hmemU σ⟩ ^ 2 := rfl
    rw [this, h, one_pow]
  have hconj : ∀ σ : G E Ω, IsConjOm σ.toRingEquiv → ψ σ = 1 := by
    intro σ ⟨j, hj⟩
    have hsq : σ * σ = 1 := by
      ext x
      apply j.injective
      simp only [AlgEquiv.mul_apply, AlgEquiv.one_apply]
      have := hj (σ x)
      simp only [AlgEquiv.toRingEquiv_eq_coe, RingEquiv.coe_toEquiv, AlgEquiv.coe_ringEquiv] at this hj
      rw [this, hj, Complex.conj_conj]
    have : ψ σ = χ (f (σ * σ)) := by
      simp [ψ, pow_two]
    rw [this, hsq, map_one, map_one]
  have hker := UpperTheory.V.Kum.artKer E Ω B ψ hopen hine hconj
  obtain ⟨τ, hτ⟩ := hL.g.onto (g : Φ.Γ)
  have hτU : τ ∈ UpperTheory.V.Base.galOf F E Ω := by
    rw [hgal]
    show π τ ∈ Φ.del.ker
    rw [hτ]
    exact g.2
  let σ : G E Ω := { τ with commutes' := fun x => hτU x }
  have hσ : f σ = g := by
    apply Subtype.ext
    show π (UpperTheory.V.Base.galEmb F E Ω σ) = g
    rw [← hτ]
    congr 1
  have h1 : ψ (σ ^ Nat.card (ClassGroup (𝓞 E))) = 1 := by
    apply hker
    rw [MonoidHom.mem_ker, map_pow]
    exact pow_card_eq_one'
  rw [map_pow] at h1
  have h2 : ψ σ = χ g ^ 2 := by
    simp [ψ, hσ]
  rw [h2, ← pow_mul] at h1
  exact h1


[LFC_SRC_DEC_ZHAT] Residue Frobenius and procyclic decomposition quotient
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/GaloisCochains/DecZhat.lean#L1-L139
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisCochains.GLinkQuot
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.CharacterLevelBounds.ClsFacts
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.KummerTheory.DecGen

section SlopeTestRemainingChoices

open Formalism.GaloisCohomology UpperTheory.V.Base

private theorem dz_locConst {Γ A : Type} [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [IsTopologicalGroup Γ] [Group A] (ψ : Γ →* A)
    (h : IsOpen (ψ.ker : Set Γ)) : IsLocallyConstant ψ :=
  _root_.UpperTheory.Reformulated.B.LocallyConstantOfKernel.lc_of_ker ψ h

private theorem dz_exists_prime {F : Type} [Field F] [NumberField F] (v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F)) (n : ℕ) (hn : n ≠ 0) (h : (n : 𝓞 F) ∈ v.asIdeal) :
    ∃ p : ℕ, p.Prime ∧ p ∣ n ∧ (p : 𝓞 F) ∈ v.asIdeal :=
  _root_.UpperTheory.Reformulated.B.Qk5.dz_exists_prime v n hn h

private theorem dz_not_both {F : Type} [Field F] [NumberField F] (v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F)) {a b : ℕ} (hab : a.Coprime b) (ha : (a : 𝓞 F) ∈ v.asIdeal)
    (hb : (b : 𝓞 F) ∈ v.asIdeal) : False := by
  obtain ⟨x, y, h⟩ : IsCoprime (a : 𝓞 F) (b : 𝓞 F) := hab.cast
  exact v.isPrime.ne_top ((Ideal.eq_top_iff_one _).2
    (h ▸ v.asIdeal.add_mem (v.asIdeal.mul_mem_left _ ha) (v.asIdeal.mul_mem_left _ hb)))

private theorem dz_val {F Ω : Type} [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] {A : ValuationSubring Ω} {v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F)} (hv : IsPlaceOver A v) (n : ℕ) :
    A.valuation (n : Ω) < 1 ↔ ((n : ℕ) : 𝓞 F) ∈ v.asIdeal := by
  have h := hv ((n : ℕ) : 𝓞 F)
  have e : algebraMap F Ω (((n : ℕ) : 𝓞 F) : F) = (n : Ω) := by simp
  rw [e] at h
  exact h

private theorem dz_order {q n m : ℕ} (hq : 1 < q) (hn : 0 < n) (hm : m + 1 = q ^ n) (u : (ZMod m)ˣ)
    (hu : (u : ZMod m) = q) : orderOf u = n := by
  rw [orderOf_eq_iff hn]
  refine ⟨Units.ext ?_, fun k hk hk0 h => ?_⟩
  · rw [Units.val_pow_eq_pow_val, hu, ← Nat.cast_pow, ← hm]
    simp
  · have h1 : ((q ^ k : ℕ) : ZMod m) = ((1 : ℕ) : ZMod m) := by
      rw [Nat.cast_pow, ← hu, ← Units.val_pow_eq_pow_val, h]
      simp
    have h2 : 1 < q ^ k := Nat.one_lt_pow hk0.ne' hq
    have h3 : q ^ k < q ^ n := Nat.pow_lt_pow_right hq hk
    have h4 := (Nat.modEq_iff_dvd' h2.le).1 ((ZMod.natCast_eq_natCast_iff _ _ _).1 h1).symm
    have h5 := Nat.le_of_dvd (by omega) h4
    omega

private theorem dz_frob (F Ω : Type) [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] [IsAlgClosure F Ω] (A : ValuationSubring Ω) (v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))
    (hv : IsPlaceOver A v) (D I : Subgroup (G F Ω)) (hD : D = Dec (F := F) A) (hI : I = Ine (F := F) A) :
    ∃ φ : D, IsFrob (F := F) A (normV v) φ.1 ∧ (I.subgroupOf D ⊔ Subgroup.zpowers φ).topologicalClosure = ⊤ ∧
      ∀ n : ℕ, 0 < n → ∃ d : D →* Multiplicative (ZMod n), IsLocallyConstant d ∧ (∀ s ∈ I.subgroupOf D, d s = 1) ∧
        d φ = Multiplicative.ofAdd 1 := by
  subst hD hI
  haveI : CharZero Ω := charZero_of_injective_algebraMap (algebraMap F Ω).injective
  haveI : IsGalois F Ω := {}
  obtain ⟨γ, hγ⟩ := exists_isFrob hv
  refine ⟨⟨γ, hγ.1⟩, hγ, ?_, fun n hn => ?_⟩
  · have key := ProfiniteGrp.closedSubgroup_eq_sInf_open (G := G F Ω)
      ⟨(Ine (F := F) A ⊔ Subgroup.zpowers γ).topologicalClosure, Subgroup.isClosed_topologicalClosure _⟩
    have h1 : Dec (F := F) A ≤ (Ine (F := F) A ⊔ Subgroup.zpowers γ).topologicalClosure := by
      refine le_trans (le_sInf ?_) key.ge
      rintro N ⟨hNo, hN⟩
      have h := (Subgroup.le_topologicalClosure _).trans hN
      exact Kum.decGen F Ω A _ γ N hv hγ hNo (le_sup_left.trans h)
        (h (Subgroup.mem_sup_right (Subgroup.mem_zpowers γ)))
    have h2 : ((Ine (F := F) A).subgroupOf (Dec (F := F) A) ⊔
        Subgroup.zpowers (⟨γ, hγ.1⟩ : Dec (F := F) A)).map (Dec (F := F) A).subtype =
        Ine (F := F) A ⊔ Subgroup.zpowers γ := by
      rw [Subgroup.map_sup, Subgroup.subgroupOf_map_subtype, MonoidHom.map_zpowers, inf_eq_left.2 (Ine_le_Dec A)]
      rfl
    rw [eq_top_iff]
    intro x _
    have h3 := h1 x.2
    rw [← h2] at h3
    exact closure_subtype.2 h3
  · have hq : normV v = Ideal.absNorm v.asIdeal := by
      rw [Ideal.absNorm_apply, Submodule.cardQuot_apply]; rfl
    have hq1 : 1 < normV v := by
      rw [hq]
      have h0 : Ideal.absNorm v.asIdeal ≠ 0 := fun h => v.ne_bot (Ideal.absNorm_eq_zero_iff.1 h)
      have h1 : Ideal.absNorm v.asIdeal ≠ 1 := fun h => v.isPrime.ne_top (Ideal.absNorm_eq_one_iff.1 h)
      omega
    have hqv : ((normV v : ℕ) : 𝓞 F) ∈ v.asIdeal := hq ▸ Ideal.absNorm_mem _
    obtain ⟨p, hp, hpq, hpv⟩ := dz_exists_prime v _ (by omega) hqv
    haveI : Fact p.Prime := ⟨hp⟩
    have h1 : 1 < normV v ^ n := Nat.one_lt_pow hn.ne' hq1
    obtain ⟨m, hm⟩ : ∃ m, m + 1 = normV v ^ n := ⟨normV v ^ n - 1, by omega⟩
    haveI : NeZero m := ⟨by omega⟩
    have hmp : m.Coprime p := by
      refine ((Nat.Prime.coprime_iff_not_dvd hp).2 fun h => ?_).symm
      have h2 : p ∣ m + 1 := hm ▸ hpq.trans (dvd_pow_self _ hn.ne')
      exact hp.one_lt.ne' (Nat.dvd_one.1 ((Nat.dvd_add_right h).1 h2))
    have hA := (dz_val hv p).2 hpv
    obtain ⟨hopen, -, -, -, hIne⟩ := Chr.clsFacts F Ω m
    have hI := hIne A fun h => dz_not_both v hmp ((dz_val hv m).1 h) hpv
    have hu : ((Chr.cls F Ω m γ : (ZMod m)ˣ) : ZMod m) = normV v := by
      haveI : IsAlgClosed Ω := IsAlgClosure.isAlgClosed F
      obtain ⟨ζ, hζ⟩ := HasEnoughRootsOfUnity.exists_primitiveRoot Ω m
      have hv1 : A.valuation ζ ≤ 1 := by
        have h1 : A.valuation ζ ^ m = 1 := by rw [← map_pow, hζ.pow_eq_one, map_one]
        exact ((pow_eq_one_iff_left (NeZero.ne m)).1 h1).le
      have h2 : γ ζ = ζ ^ normV v := by
        refine (LDataCardLeFinrank.ineFixRoots F Ω p A hA m hmp (ζ ^ normV v) ?_).1 (γ ζ) ?_ (hγ.2 ζ hv1)
        · rw [← pow_mul, mul_comm, pow_mul, hζ.pow_eq_one, one_pow]
        · rw [← map_pow, hζ.pow_eq_one, map_one]
      rw [Chr.cls_spec m γ ζ hζ.pow_eq_one, pow_eq_pow_mod (normV v) hζ.pow_eq_one] at h2
      have h3 := hζ.pow_inj (ZMod.val_lt _) (Nat.mod_lt _ (NeZero.pos m)) h2
      rw [← ZMod.natCast_zmod_val ((Chr.cls F Ω m γ : (ZMod m)ˣ) : ZMod m), h3, ZMod.natCast_mod]
    have hord := dz_order hq1 hn hm _ hu
    have hle : Dec (F := F) A ≤ (Subgroup.zpowers (Chr.cls F Ω m γ)).comap (Chr.cls F Ω m) := by
      have hk : (Chr.cls F Ω m).ker ≤ (Subgroup.zpowers (Chr.cls F Ω m γ)).comap (Chr.cls F Ω m) :=
        Subgroup.ker_le_comap _ _
      exact Kum.decGen F Ω A _ γ _ hv hγ (Subgroup.isOpen_mono hk hopen) (hI.trans hk) (Subgroup.mem_zpowers _)
    let f : Dec (F := F) A →* Subgroup.zpowers (Chr.cls F Ω m γ) :=
      ((Chr.cls F Ω m).comp (Dec (F := F) A).subtype).codRestrict _ fun x => hle x.2
    have hg : ∀ x : Subgroup.zpowers (Chr.cls F Ω m γ), x ∈ Subgroup.zpowers (f ⟨γ, hγ.1⟩) := by
      rintro ⟨_, k, rfl⟩
      exact ⟨k, rfl⟩
    have hc : Nat.card (Subgroup.zpowers (Chr.cls F Ω m γ)) = n := by rw [Nat.card_zpowers, hord]
    have hf1 : ∀ s : Dec (F := F) A, Chr.cls F Ω m s = 1 → f s = 1 := fun s hs => Subtype.ext hs
    refine ⟨(zmodMulEquivOfGenerator hg hc).symm.toMonoidHom.comp f, dz_locConst _ ?_, fun s hs => ?_, ?_⟩
    · refine Subgroup.isOpen_mono (H₁ := (Chr.cls F Ω m).ker.subgroupOf _) (fun s hs => ?_)
        (hopen.preimage continuous_subtype_val)
      rw [MonoidHom.mem_ker, MonoidHom.comp_apply, hf1 s hs, map_one]
    · rw [MonoidHom.comp_apply, hf1 s (hI hs), map_one]
    · exact zmodMulEquivOfGenerator_symm_apply_generator hg hc

end SlopeTestRemainingChoices

theorem UpperTheory.V.GC.decZhat :
    ∀ (F Ω : Type) [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] [IsAlgClosure F Ω] (v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F)) (A : ValuationSubring Ω), IsPlaceOver A v →
    ∃ φ : Dec (F := F) A, IsFrob (F := F) A (normV v) φ.1 ∧ ((Ine (F := F) A).subgroupOf (Dec (F := F) A) ⊔ Subgroup.zpowers φ).topologicalClosure = ⊤ ∧
    ∀ n : ℕ, 0 < n → ∃ d : Dec (F := F) A →* Multiplicative (ZMod n), IsLocallyConstant d ∧ (∀ s ∈ (Ine (F := F) A).subgroupOf (Dec (F := F) A), d s = 1) ∧ d φ = Multiplicative.ofAdd 1 := by
  intro F Ω _ _ _ _ _ v A hv
  exact dz_frob F Ω A v hv _ _ rfl rfl


[LFC_SRC_GEN_DEC] Full local finite-abelian-group detector theorem
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/GaloisCochains/GenDec.lean#L1-L276
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisCochains.LocEuler
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisCochains.DecZhat
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.IntegralCasts.CapelliIdentities.ChoiceFunction

open NumberField IsDedekindDomain Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal Module UpperTheory.V UpperTheory.V.Fr UpperTheory.V.GC

private theorem pick {k D : Type} [Field k] (P : Set D) {p0 : D} (hp0 : p0 ∈ P) (W : Submodule k (D → k))
    (hW : ∀ f, f ∈ W ↔ ∀ p ∈ P, f p = 0) : ∀ (n : ℕ) (Y : Submodule k (D → k)) [FiniteDimensional k Y],
    finrank k Y ≤ n + finrank k (Y ⊓ W : Submodule k (D → k)) →
    ∃ u : Fin n → D, (∀ a, u a ∈ P) ∧ ∀ f ∈ Y, (∀ a, f (u a) = 0) → f ∈ W :=
  _root_.UpperTheory.Reformulated.B.InterpolationPointsOfFinrankBound.pick P hp0 W hW

private theorem cyc1 {D : Type} [Group D] [TopologicalSpace D] [IsTopologicalGroup D] [CompactSpace D]
    {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] (hl : l ≠ 2) (J : Subgroup D) (hJ : IsClosed (J : Set D)) (e : D →* ℤ_[l]ˣ)
    (he : Continuous e) (N : Set D) (hN : IsOpen N) (hJN : ∀ g ∈ J, e g = 1 → g ∈ N) :
    ∃ u ∈ J, ∀ g ∈ J, ∃ m : ℕ, g⁻¹ * u ^ m ∈ N := by
  let C : ℕ → Set D := fun n => (J : Set D) ∩ Nᶜ ∩ {g | ‖(e g : ℤ_[l]) - 1‖ ≤ (l : ℝ) ^ (-(n : ℤ))}
  have hC : ∀ n, IsClosed (C n) := fun n =>
    (hJ.inter hN.isClosed_compl).inter
      (isClosed_le ((Units.continuous_val.comp he).sub continuous_const).norm continuous_const)
  have hiff : ∀ (n : ℕ) (x : ℤ_[l]), ‖x - 1‖ ≤ (l : ℝ) ^ (-(n : ℤ)) ↔ PadicInt.toZModPow n x = 1 := by
    intro n x
    rw [PadicInt.norm_le_pow_iff_mem_span_pow, ← PadicInt.ker_toZModPow, RingHom.mem_ker, map_sub, map_one, sub_eq_zero]
  have hex : ∃ n, C n = ∅ := by
    by_contra hne
    push Not at hne
    have hanti : ∀ n, C (n + 1) ⊆ C n := fun n g hg => ⟨hg.1, le_trans (b := (l : ℝ) ^ (-((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℤ))) hg.2
      (zpow_le_zpow_right₀ (by exact_mod_cast (Fact.out : l.Prime).one_le) (by push_cast; omega))⟩
    obtain ⟨g, hg⟩ := IsCompact.nonempty_iInter_of_sequence_nonempty_isCompact_isClosed C hanti hne
      (hC 0).isCompact hC
    rw [Set.mem_iInter] at hg
    refine (hg 0).1.2 (hJN g (hg 0).1.1 ?_)
    ext1
    refine PadicInt.ext_of_toZModPow.mp fun n => ?_
    rw [(hiff n _).mp (hg n).2]
    simp
  obtain ⟨n, hn⟩ := hex
  let en : D →* (ZMod (l ^ n))ˣ := (Units.map (PadicInt.toZModPow n : ℤ_[l] →+* ZMod (l ^ n)).toMonoidHom).comp e
  haveI : IsCyclic (ZMod (l ^ n))ˣ := ZMod.isCyclic_units_of_prime_pow l Fact.out hl n
  haveI : NeZero (l ^ n) := ⟨pow_ne_zero _ (Fact.out : l.Prime).ne_zero⟩
  obtain ⟨⟨_, u, hu, rfl⟩, hgen⟩ := IsCyclic.exists_monoid_generator (α := J.map en)
  refine ⟨u, hu, fun g hg => ?_⟩
  obtain ⟨m, hm⟩ := hgen ⟨en g, g, hg, rfl⟩
  have hm' : en u ^ m = en g := congrArg Subtype.val hm
  refine ⟨m, ?_⟩
  by_contra hN'
  have : g⁻¹ * u ^ m ∈ C n := by
    refine ⟨⟨J.mul_mem (J.inv_mem hg) (J.pow_mem hu m), hN'⟩, (hiff n _).mpr ?_⟩
    have h1 : en (g⁻¹ * u ^ m) = 1 := by rw [map_mul, map_inv, map_pow, hm', inv_mul_cancel]
    exact congrArg Units.val h1
  rw [hn] at this
  exact this

private theorem exChar {B : Type} [CommGroup B] [Finite B] {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] (hB : IsPGroup l B)
    (H : Subgroup B) (hH : H ≠ ⊤) :
    ∃ θ : B →* Multiplicative (ZMod l), (∀ x ∈ H, θ x = 1) ∧ ∃ x, θ x ≠ 1 := by
  obtain ⟨M, hM, hHM⟩ := (eq_top_or_exists_le_coatom H).resolve_left hH
  haveI : Nontrivial (B ⧸ M) := QuotientGroup.nontrivial_iff.mpr hM.1
  haveI : IsSimpleGroup (B ⧸ M) := by
    refine ⟨fun N _ => ?_⟩
    have h1 : M ≤ N.comap (QuotientGroup.mk' M) := fun x hx => by
      rw [Subgroup.mem_comap, show QuotientGroup.mk' M x = 1 from (QuotientGroup.eq_one_iff x).mpr hx]
      exact N.one_mem
    have h2 := Subgroup.map_comap_eq_self_of_surjective (QuotientGroup.mk'_surjective M) N
    rcases hM.le_iff.mp h1 with h | h
    · right
      rw [← h2, h]
      exact Subgroup.map_top_of_surjective _ (QuotientGroup.mk'_surjective M)
    · left
      rw [← h2, h, Subgroup.map_eq_bot_iff, QuotientGroup.ker_mk']
  have hp : (Nat.card (B ⧸ M)).Prime := IsSimpleGroup.prime_card
  have hq : IsPGroup l (B ⧸ M) := hB.to_quotient M
  have hc : Nat.card (B ⧸ M) = l := by
    obtain ⟨n, hn⟩ := IsPGroup.iff_card.mp hq
    have : Nat.card (B ⧸ M) ∣ l := hp.dvd_of_dvd_pow (n := n) (by rw [hn])
    exact (Nat.prime_dvd_prime_iff_eq hp Fact.out).mp this
  let ε : (B ⧸ M) ≃* Multiplicative (ZMod l) := mulEquivOfPrimeCardEq hc (by simp)
  refine ⟨ε.toMonoidHom.comp (QuotientGroup.mk' M), fun x hx => ?_, ?_⟩
  · have : (QuotientGroup.mk' M x) = 1 := (QuotientGroup.eq_one_iff x).mpr (hHM hx)
    simp only [MonoidHom.comp_apply, this, map_one]
  · obtain ⟨x, hx⟩ : ∃ x, x ∉ M := by
      by_contra h; push Not at h
      exact hM.1 (eq_top_iff.mpr fun x _ => h x)
    refine ⟨x, fun h => hx ?_⟩
    have : (QuotientGroup.mk' M x) = 1 := ε.injective (by simpa using h)
    exact (QuotientGroup.eq_one_iff x).mp this

private theorem fratt {D B : Type} [Group D] [TopologicalSpace D] [IsTopologicalGroup D] [CommGroup B] [Finite B]
    {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] (hB : IsPGroup l B) (χ : D →* B) (hχ : IsOpen (χ.ker : Set D)) (S : Set D)
    (hS : ∀ f : D → ZMod l, Continuous f → (∀ g h, f (g * h) = f g + f h) → (∀ s ∈ S, f s = 0) → f = 0)
    (H : Subgroup B) (hH : ∀ s ∈ S, χ s ∈ H) (g : D) : χ g ∈ H := by
  by_contra hg
  have hne : H.subgroupOf χ.range ≠ ⊤ := fun e => hg (by
    have : (⟨χ g, g, rfl⟩ : χ.range) ∈ H.subgroupOf χ.range := e ▸ Subgroup.mem_top _
    exact this)
  obtain ⟨θ, h1, ⟨_, x, rfl⟩, h2⟩ := exChar (hB.to_subgroup χ.range) _ hne
  let ψ : D →* Multiplicative (ZMod l) := θ.comp χ.rangeRestrict
  have hψ : IsOpen (ψ.ker : Set D) := Subgroup.isOpen_mono (fun y hy => by
    have : χ.rangeRestrict y = 1 := Subtype.ext hy
    simp only [MonoidHom.mem_ker, ψ, MonoidHom.comp_apply, this, map_one]) hχ
  have hc : Continuous ψ := by
    refine continuous_of_continuousAt_one ψ ?_
    rw [ContinuousAt, map_one]
    exact tendsto_const_nhds.congr' (Filter.eventually_of_mem (hψ.mem_nhds ψ.ker.one_mem) fun y hy => Eq.symm hy)
  have := hS (fun y => (ψ y).toAdd) (continuous_toAdd.comp hc) (fun a b => by simp)
    (fun s hs => by
      have : ψ s = 1 := h1 _ (hH s hs)
      simp [this])
  exact h2 (by
    have h3 := congrFun this x
    exact h3)

private theorem lastStep {D B : Type} [Group D] [TopologicalSpace D] [IsTopologicalGroup D] [CommGroup B] [Finite B]
    {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] (hB : IsPGroup l B) (χ : D →* B) (hχ : IsOpen (χ.ker : Set D)) (I : Subgroup D) (φ : D)
    (hd : ∀ n : ℕ, 0 < n → ∃ d : D →* Multiplicative (ZMod n), IsLocallyConstant d ∧ (∀ s ∈ I, d s = 1) ∧
      d φ = Multiplicative.ofAdd 1) (S : Set D) (hSI : ∀ s ∈ S, s ∈ I)
    (hS : ∀ f : D → ZMod l, Continuous f → (∀ g h, f (g * h) = f g + f h) → f φ = 0 → (∀ s ∈ S, f s = 0) → f = 0)
    (h0 : ∀ s ∈ S, χ s = 1) (g : D) (hg : g ∈ I) : χ g = 1 := by
  obtain ⟨n, hn⟩ := IsPGroup.iff_card.mp hB
  have hpos : 0 < l ^ n := pow_pos (Fact.out : l.Prime).pos n
  haveI : NeZero (l ^ n) := ⟨hpos.ne'⟩
  obtain ⟨d, hd1, hd2, hd3⟩ := hd (l ^ n) hpos
  have hB' : IsPGroup l (B × Multiplicative (ZMod (l ^ n))) :=
    IsPGroup.of_card (n := n + n) (by rw [Nat.card_prod, hn, pow_add]; simp)
  have hker : IsOpen (((χ.prod d).ker : Subgroup D) : Set D) := by
    rw [MonoidHom.ker_prod]
    exact hχ.inter (hd1.isOpen_fiber 1)
  have := fratt hB' (χ.prod d) hker (insert φ S)
    (fun f h1 h2 h3 => hS f h1 h2 (h3 φ (Set.mem_insert _ _)) fun s hs => h3 s (Set.mem_insert_of_mem _ hs))
    (Subgroup.zpowers ((χ.prod d) φ)) (by
      rintro s (rfl | hs)
      · exact Subgroup.mem_zpowers _
      · have : (χ.prod d) s = 1 := Prod.ext (h0 s hs) (hd2 s (hSI s hs))
        rw [this]
        exact Subgroup.one_mem _) g
  obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ := this
  have k1 : χ φ ^ k = χ g := congrArg Prod.fst hk
  have k2 : d φ ^ k = d g := congrArg Prod.snd hk
  rw [hd2 g hg, hd3, ← ofAdd_zsmul, ofAdd_eq_one, zsmul_eq_mul, mul_one, ZMod.intCast_zmod_eq_zero_iff_dvd] at k2
  obtain ⟨c, rfl⟩ := k2
  rw [← k1, zpow_mul, zpow_natCast, ← hn, pow_card_eq_one', one_zpow]

private theorem core {D : Type} [Group D] [TopologicalSpace D] [IsTopologicalGroup D] [CompactSpace D]
    {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] (hl : l ≠ 2) (I J : Subgroup D) (hJ : IsClosed (J : Set D)) (e : D →* ℤ_[l]ˣ)
    (he : Continuous e) (r : ℕ) (hr : ¬ l ∣ r) (hIJ : ∀ g ∈ I, g ^ r ∈ J) (φ : D)
    (hφ : (I ⊔ Subgroup.zpowers φ).topologicalClosure = ⊤)
    (X : Submodule (ZMod l) (D → ZMod l)) (hX : ∀ f, f ∈ X ↔ Continuous f ∧ ∀ g h, f (g * h) = f g + f h)
    [FiniteDimensional (ZMod l) X] (n : ℕ) (hn : finrank (ZMod l) X ≤ n + 2) (f0 : D → ZMod l) (hf0 : f0 ∈ X)
    (hf0' : f0 φ ≠ 0) :
    ∃ u : Fin (n + 1) → D, (∀ a, u a ∈ J) ∧ (∀ a, a ≠ 0 → e (u a) = 1) ∧
      ∀ f : D → ZMod l, Continuous f → (∀ g h, f (g * h) = f g + f h) → f φ = 0 → (∀ a, f (u a) = 0) → f = 0 := by
  let P : Set D := {g | g ∈ J ∧ e g = 1}
  have hP : (1 : D) ∈ P := ⟨J.one_mem, map_one e⟩
  let W : Submodule (ZMod l) (D → ZMod l) := ⨅ p ∈ P, LinearMap.ker (LinearMap.proj p)
  have hW : ∀ f, f ∈ W ↔ ∀ p ∈ P, f p = 0 := fun f => by simp [W, Submodule.mem_iInf]
  let Y : Submodule (ZMod l) (D → ZMod l) := X ⊓ LinearMap.ker (LinearMap.proj φ)
  have hlt : Y < X := lt_of_le_of_ne inf_le_left fun h => hf0' (by
    have : f0 ∈ Y := h ▸ hf0
    exact this.2)
  haveI : FiniteDimensional (ZMod l) Y := Submodule.finiteDimensional_of_le hlt.le
  haveI : FiniteDimensional (ZMod l) (Y ⊓ W : Submodule (ZMod l) (D → ZMod l)) :=
    Submodule.finiteDimensional_of_le inf_le_left
  have h1 := Submodule.finrank_lt_finrank_of_lt hlt
  by_cases hYW : Y ⊓ W = ⊥
  · obtain ⟨u, hu, hu'⟩ := pick P hP W hW (n + 1) Y (by omega)
    refine ⟨u, fun a => (hu a).1, fun a _ => (hu a).2, fun f c1 c2 c3 c4 => ?_⟩
    have hf : f ∈ Y := ⟨(hX f).mpr ⟨c1, c2⟩, c3⟩
    have : f ∈ Y ⊓ W := ⟨hf, hu' f hf c4⟩
    rwa [hYW] at this
  · have h2 : finrank (ZMod l) (Y ⊓ W : Submodule (ZMod l) (D → ZMod l)) ≠ 0 := fun h =>
      hYW (Submodule.finrank_eq_zero.mp h)
    obtain ⟨u, hu, hu'⟩ := pick P hP W hW n Y (by omega)
    haveI : Finite (Y ⊓ W : Submodule (ZMod l) (D → ZMod l)) := Module.finite_of_finite (ZMod l)
    let N : Set D := ⋂ f : (Y ⊓ W : Submodule (ZMod l) (D → ZMod l)), f.1 ⁻¹' {0}
    have hN : IsOpen N := isOpen_iInter_of_finite fun f =>
      (isOpen_discrete _).preimage ((hX f.1).mp f.2.1.1).1
    obtain ⟨u0, hu0, hgen⟩ := cyc1 hl J hJ e he N hN fun g hg hg' =>
      Set.mem_iInter.mpr fun f => (hW f.1).mp f.2.2 g ⟨hg, hg'⟩
    refine ⟨Fin.cons u0 u, fun a => ?_, fun a => ?_, fun f c1 c2 c3 c4 => ?_⟩
    · refine Fin.cases ?_ (fun a => ?_) a
      · exact hu0
      · exact (hu a).1
    · refine Fin.cases ?_ (fun a => ?_) a
      · exact fun h => absurd rfl h
      · exact fun _ => (hu a).2
    · have hf : f ∈ Y := ⟨(hX f).mpr ⟨c1, c2⟩, c3⟩
      have hfW : f ∈ Y ⊓ W := ⟨hf, hu' f hf fun a => c4 a.succ⟩
      let ψ : D →* Multiplicative (ZMod l) := MonoidHom.mk' (fun g => Multiplicative.ofAdd (f g)) fun a b => by
        rw [c2, ofAdd_add]
      have p0 : ψ u0 = 1 := congrArg Multiplicative.ofAdd (c4 0)
      have pJ : ∀ g ∈ J, ψ g = 1 := fun g hg => by
        obtain ⟨m, hm⟩ := hgen g hg
        have : ψ (g⁻¹ * u0 ^ m) = 1 := congrArg Multiplicative.ofAdd (Set.mem_iInter.mp hm ⟨f, hfW⟩)
        rwa [map_mul, map_pow, p0, one_pow, mul_one, map_inv, inv_eq_one] at this
      have pI : I ≤ ψ.ker := fun g hg => by
        have h3 := pJ _ (hIJ g hg)
        rw [map_pow] at h3
        have h4 : (r : ZMod l) * f g = 0 := by
          have := congrArg Multiplicative.toAdd h3
          rw [toAdd_pow, nsmul_eq_mul] at this
          exact this
        have h5 : (r : ZMod l) ≠ 0 := by rwa [Ne, ZMod.natCast_eq_zero_iff]
        exact congrArg Multiplicative.ofAdd ((mul_eq_zero.mp h4).resolve_left h5)
      have hcl : IsClosed (ψ.ker : Set D) := (isClosed_discrete ({0} : Set (ZMod l))).preimage c1
      have htop : ⊤ ≤ ψ.ker := by
        rw [← hφ]
        refine Subgroup.topologicalClosure_minimal _ (sup_le pI ?_) hcl
        rw [Subgroup.zpowers_le]
        exact congrArg Multiplicative.ofAdd c3
      funext g
      exact congrArg Multiplicative.toAdd (htop (Subgroup.mem_top g))

private theorem finrank_cocycles_trivial_mod_l {D : Type} [Group D] [TopologicalSpace D] {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] (d : ℕ) (ω : D →* (ZMod l)ˣ)
    (hω : ∃ g, ω g ≠ 1) (h1 : Module.Finite (ZMod l) (H1 (Representation.trivial (ZMod l) D (ZMod l))))
    (h2 : finrank (ZMod l) (H1 (Representation.trivial (ZMod l) D (ZMod l))) =
      finrank (ZMod l) (H0 (Representation.trivial (ZMod l) D (ZMod l))) +
      finrank (ZMod l) (Loc.H0dual ω (Representation.trivial (ZMod l) D (ZMod l))) + d * finrank (ZMod l) (ZMod l)) :
    FiniteDimensional (ZMod l) (Z1 (Representation.trivial (ZMod l) D (ZMod l))) ∧
      finrank (ZMod l) (Z1 (Representation.trivial (ZMod l) D (ZMod l))) ≤ d + 1 := by
  have hb : (B1 (Representation.trivial (ZMod l) D (ZMod l))).comap
      (Z1 (Representation.trivial (ZMod l) D (ZMod l))).subtype = ⊥ := by
    rw [eq_bot_iff]
    intro f hf
    obtain ⟨m, hm⟩ := (mem_coboundaries1 _ _).mp hf
    exact Subtype.ext (funext fun g => by simpa using hm g)
  let ε := Submodule.quotEquivOfEqBot _ hb
  have h3 : Loc.H0dual ω (Representation.trivial (ZMod l) D (ZMod l)) = ⊥ := by
    rw [eq_bot_iff]
    intro f hf
    obtain ⟨g, hg⟩ := hω
    refine LinearMap.ext fun x => ?_
    have h4 : f x = (ω g : ZMod l) * f x := hf g x
    by_contra h5
    have h6 : f x ≠ 0 := h5
    exact hg (Units.ext ((mul_eq_right₀ h6).mp h4.symm))
  have h4 := Submodule.finrank_le (H0 (Representation.trivial (ZMod l) D (ZMod l)))
  rw [h3, finrank_bot, finrank_self, ε.finrank_eq] at h2
  rw [finrank_self] at h4
  exact ⟨Module.Finite.equiv ε, by omega⟩

theorem UpperTheory.V.GC.genDec :
    LocalFiniteness → LocalTateDuality → LocalEulerChar → ∀ (F Ω : Type) [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] [IsAlgClosure F Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime], l ≠ 2 → ∀ (v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F)) (A : ValuationSubring Ω), IsPlaceOver A v → ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 F) ∈ v.asIdeal → (∃ g ∈ Dec (F := F) A, cycMod F Ω l 1 g ≠ 1) →
    ∀ K : Subgroup (G F Ω), K.Normal → IsOpen (K : Set (G F Ω)) → ¬ l ∣ K.index →
    ∃ (u : Fin (locDeg v) → Dec (F := F) A) (a₀ : Fin (locDeg v)), (∀ a, (u a).1 ∈ Ine (F := F) A ∧ (u a).1 ∈ K) ∧ (∀ a, a ≠ a₀ → cycZ F Ω l (u a).1 = 1) ∧
    ∀ (B : Type) [CommGroup B] [Finite B], IsPGroup l B → ∀ χ : Dec (F := F) A →* B, IsOpen (χ.ker : Set (Dec (F := F) A)) → (∀ a, χ (u a) = 1) → ∀ g : Dec (F := F) A, g.1 ∈ Ine (F := F) A → χ g = 1 := by
  intro p1 p2 p3 F Ω _ _ _ _ _ l _ hl v A hA hv hω K hK hKo hKi
  obtain ⟨φ, -, hφ, hd⟩ := decZhat F Ω v A hA
  obtain ⟨e1, -, e2, -, e3⟩ := locEuler p1 p2 p3 F Ω l v A hA (ZMod l) (ZMod l)
    (Representation.trivial (ZMod l) (Dec (F := F) A) (ZMod l)) continuous_snd
  have hω' : ∃ g, ((cycF F Ω l (ZMod l)).comp (Dec (F := F) A).subtype) g ≠ 1 := by
    obtain ⟨g, hg, hg'⟩ := hω
    refine ⟨⟨g, hg⟩, fun h => hg' (Units.ext ?_)⟩
    have h1 : ZMod.castHom (dvd_pow_self l one_ne_zero) (ZMod l) (cycMod F Ω l 1 g : ZMod (l ^ 1)) = 1 :=
      congrArg Units.val h
    haveI : Fact (l ^ 1).Prime := ⟨by rw [pow_one]; exact Fact.out⟩
    exact (ZMod.castHom (dvd_pow_self l one_ne_zero) (ZMod l)).injective (by rw [h1]; simp)
  obtain ⟨f1, f2⟩ := finrank_cocycles_trivial_mod_l (locDeg v) _ hω' e1 (by rw [e2 hv, e3])
  obtain ⟨n, hn⟩ : ∃ n, locDeg v = n + 1 := by
    have := v.isPrime
    have h1 := Ideal.ramificationIdx_pos v.asIdeal ℤ
    have h2 := Ideal.inertiaDeg_pos v.asIdeal ℤ
    exact Nat.exists_eq_succ_of_ne_zero (Nat.mul_ne_zero h1.ne' h2.ne')
  rw [hn] at f2 ⊢
  haveI : IsGalois F Ω := {}
  haveI : CompactSpace (Dec (F := F) A) := isCompact_iff_compactSpace.mp (isClosed_Dec A).isCompact
  obtain ⟨d, d1, -, d3⟩ := hd l (Fact.out : l.Prime).pos
  obtain ⟨u, u1, u2, u3⟩ := core hl ((Ine (F := F) A).subgroupOf (Dec (F := F) A))
    ((Ine (F := F) A ⊓ K).subgroupOf (Dec (F := F) A))
    (((isClosed_Ine A).inter (K.isClosed_of_isOpen hKo)).preimage continuous_subtype_val)
    ((cycZ F Ω l).comp (Dec (F := F) A).subtype) ((continuous_cycZ F Ω l).comp continuous_subtype_val)
    K.index hKi (fun g hg => ⟨(Ine (F := F) A).pow_mem hg _, K.pow_index_mem g.1⟩) φ hφ
    (Z1 (Representation.trivial (ZMod l) (Dec (F := F) A) (ZMod l))) (fun f => Iff.rfl) n f2
    (fun g => (d g).toAdd) ⟨continuous_toAdd.comp d1.continuous, fun g h => by simp⟩ (by simp [d3])
  refine ⟨u, 0, u1, u2, fun B _ _ hB χ hχ h0 g hg => ?_⟩
  exact lastStep hB χ hχ _ φ hd (Set.range u) (by rintro _ ⟨a, rfl⟩; exact (u1 a).1)
    (fun f c1 c2 c3 c4 => u3 f c1 c2 c3 fun a => c4 _ ⟨a, rfl⟩) (by rintro _ ⟨a, rfl⟩; exact h0 a) g hg


[LFC_SRC_GEN] Full GC.gen and cyclotomic reduction convention
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/GaloisCochains/ToZModEqOne.lean#L1-L68

private theorem toZMod_cycZ_eq_one_of_cycMod_eq_one (F Ω : Type) [Field F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime]
    (g : G F Ω) (h : cycMod F Ω l 1 g = 1) :
    PadicInt.toZMod ((cycZ F Ω l g : ℤ_[l]ˣ) : ℤ_[l]) = 1 := by
  have h1 := congrArg Units.val h
  simp only [cycMod, MonoidHom.comp_apply, Units.coe_map, Units.val_one] at h1
  have hk : ((cycZ F Ω l g : ℤ_[l]ˣ) : ℤ_[l]) - 1 ∈
      RingHom.ker (PadicInt.toZModPow 1 : ℤ_[l] →+* ZMod (l ^ 1)) := by
    rw [RingHom.mem_ker, map_sub, map_one, sub_eq_zero]
    exact h1
  rw [PadicInt.ker_toZModPow, pow_one, ← PadicInt.maximalIdeal_eq_span_p, ← PadicInt.ker_toZMod,
    RingHom.mem_ker, map_sub, map_one, sub_eq_zero] at hk
  exact hk

theorem UpperTheory.V.GC.gen :
    Hypotheses₂ → ∀ (F Ω : Type) [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] [IsAlgClosure F Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (k : Type) [Field k] [CharP k l] (Φ : Fr.Frame l k) (S : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))) (K : Subgroup (G F Ω)) (π : G F Ω →* Φ.Γ) (ι : ∀ v, Φ.Gv v →* G F Ω) (A : Φ.V → ValuationSubring Ω) (pl : Φ.V → HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F)) (e : Φ.Inf ≃ InfinitePlace F) (c : Φ.Inf → G F Ω), FLink l Φ S K π ι A pl e c → (∀ v, ∃ s, Φ.om k (Φ.loc v s) ≠ 1) → ∀ v, ∃ (u : Fin (Φ.dv v) → Φ.Gv v) (a₀ : Fin (Φ.dv v)), (∀ a, u a ∈ Φ.Iv v ∧ Φ.Ψ (Φ.loc v (u a)) = 1) ∧ (∀ a, a ≠ a₀ → u a ∈ Φ.Nv v) ∧
    ∀ (B : Type) [CommGroup B] [Finite B], IsPGroup l B → ∀ χ : Φ.Gv v →* B, IsOpen (χ.ker : Set (Φ.Gv v)) → (∀ a, χ (u a) = 1) → ∀ g ∈ Φ.Iv v, χ g = 1 := by
  intro hP F Ω _ _ _ _ _ l _ k _ _ Φ S K π ι A pl e c hL hom v
  have hdv : Φ.dv v = locDeg (pl v) := hL.dv v
  have hmemv : ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 F) ∈ (pl v).asIdeal := (hL.loc.mem (pl v)).1 ⟨v, rfl⟩
  have hcyc : ∃ g ∈ Dec (A v), cycMod F Ω l 1 g ≠ 1 := by
    obtain ⟨s, hs⟩ := hom v
    refine ⟨ι v s, ?_, fun h1 => hs ?_⟩
    · rw [← hL.loc.range v]
      exact ⟨s, rfl⟩
    · have h2 := toZMod_cycZ_eq_one_of_cycMod_eq_one F Ω l _ h1
      apply Units.ext
      simp [Fr.Frame.om, hL.loc_eq, hL.eps, h2]
  obtain ⟨u', a₀', hu1, hu2, hu3⟩ := genDec hP.lf hP.lt hP.le F Ω l hL.g.odd (pl v) (A v)
    (hL.loc.isOver v) hmemv hcyc K hL.g.normal hL.g.isOpen hL.g.idx
  let eq : Φ.Gv v ≃* Dec (A v) :=
    (MonoidHom.ofInjective (hL.loc.ι_inj v)).trans (MulEquiv.subgroupCongr (hL.loc.range v))
  have heq : ∀ x, ((eq x : Dec (A v)) : G F Ω) = ι v x := fun x => rfl
  have hcont : Continuous eq := by
    apply continuous_induced_rng.2
    exact hL.loc.cont v
  let h' := hcont.homeoOfEquivCompactToT2 (f := eq.toEquiv)
  refine ⟨fun a => eq.symm (u' (Fin.cast hdv a)), Fin.cast hdv.symm a₀', fun a => ⟨?_, ?_⟩,
    fun a ha => ?_, ?_⟩
  · rw [hL.ine v, Subgroup.mem_comap, ← heq, MulEquiv.apply_symm_apply]
    exact (hu1 _).1
  · rw [hL.loc_eq, ← heq, MulEquiv.apply_symm_apply]
    exact hL.K_Ψ _ (hu1 _).2
  · refine Subgroup.mem_inf.2 ⟨?_, ?_⟩
    · rw [hL.ine v, Subgroup.mem_comap, ← heq, MulEquiv.apply_symm_apply]
      exact (hu1 _).1
    · rw [MonoidHom.mem_ker, MonoidHom.comp_apply, hL.loc_eq, hL.eps, ← heq,
        MulEquiv.apply_symm_apply]
      refine hu2 _ fun h => ha ?_
      rw [← h]
      ext
      simp
  · intro B _ _ hB χ hχ hu g hg
    let χ' : Dec (A v) →* B := χ.comp eq.symm.toMonoidHom
    have hopen : IsOpen (SetLike.coe χ'.ker) := by
      have hs : SetLike.coe χ'.ker = h'.symm ⁻¹' (SetLike.coe χ.ker) := rfl
      rw [hs]
      exact hχ.preimage h'.symm.continuous
    have hg' : ((eq g : Dec (A v)) : G F Ω) ∈ Ine (A v) := by
      rw [heq]
      rw [hL.ine v] at hg
      exact hg
    have := hu3 B hB χ' hopen (fun a => by simpa [χ'] using hu (Fin.cast hdv.symm a)) (eq g) hg'
    simpa [χ'] using this


[LFC_SRC_XDUAL] Literal XDual and its inertia and decomposition clauses
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/GaloisLibrary/KummerTheory/Mk0IdMapCongr.lean#L1-L237
import Mathlib

open NumberField IsDedekindDomain Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal Formalism.GaloisCohomology UpperTheory.V.LDataCardLeFinrank UpperTheory.V.Base
open scoped nonZeroDivisors

namespace UpperTheory.V.Kum

section Cl

variable {K K' : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] [Field K'] [NumberField K']

def primeCl (v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 K)) : ClassGroup (𝓞 K) :=
  ClassGroup.mk0 ⟨v.asIdeal, mem_nonZeroDivisors_of_ne_zero v.ne_bot⟩

def clSub (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 K))) : Subgroup (ClassGroup (𝓞 K)) := Subgroup.closure (primeCl '' S)

abbrev ClS (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 K))) : Type := ClassGroup (𝓞 K) ⧸ clSub S

def idMap (τ : K ≃+* K') : (Ideal (𝓞 K))⁰ →* (Ideal (𝓞 K'))⁰ where
  toFun I := ⟨I.1.map (RingOfIntegers.mapRingEquiv τ), mem_nonZeroDivisors_of_ne_zero fun h =>
    nonZeroDivisors.ne_zero I.2 ((Ideal.map_eq_bot_iff_of_injective (RingOfIntegers.mapRingEquiv τ).injective).1 h)⟩
  map_one' := Subtype.ext (by simp [Ideal.map_top])
  map_mul' I J := Subtype.ext (Ideal.map_mul _ _ _)

theorem mk0_idMap_congr (τ : K ≃+* K') {I J : (Ideal (𝓞 K))⁰} (h : ClassGroup.mk0 I = ClassGroup.mk0 J) :
    ClassGroup.mk0 (idMap τ I) = ClassGroup.mk0 (idMap τ J) := by
  rw [ClassGroup.mk0_eq_mk0_iff] at h ⊢
  obtain ⟨x, y, hx, hy, h⟩ := h
  refine ⟨RingOfIntegers.mapRingEquiv τ x, RingOfIntegers.mapRingEquiv τ y, by simpa using hx, by simpa using hy, ?_⟩
  have := congrArg (Ideal.map (RingOfIntegers.mapRingEquiv τ)) h
  rw [Ideal.map_mul, Ideal.map_mul, Ideal.map_span, Ideal.map_span, Set.image_singleton, Set.image_singleton] at this
  exact this

private def clFun (τ : K ≃+* K') (c : ClassGroup (𝓞 K)) : ClassGroup (𝓞 K') :=
  ClassGroup.mk0 (idMap τ (ClassGroup.mk0_surjective c).choose)

private theorem class_fun_mk0 (τ : K ≃+* K') (I : (Ideal (𝓞 K))⁰) :
    clFun τ (ClassGroup.mk0 I) = ClassGroup.mk0 (idMap τ I) :=
  mk0_idMap_congr τ (ClassGroup.mk0_surjective _).choose_spec

def clMap (τ : K ≃+* K') : ClassGroup (𝓞 K) →* ClassGroup (𝓞 K') :=
  MonoidHom.mk' (clFun τ) fun a b => by
    obtain ⟨I, rfl⟩ := ClassGroup.mk0_surjective a
    obtain ⟨J, rfl⟩ := ClassGroup.mk0_surjective b
    rw [← map_mul, class_fun_mk0, class_fun_mk0, class_fun_mk0, map_mul, map_mul]

theorem class_map_mk0 (τ : K ≃+* K') (I : (Ideal (𝓞 K))⁰) : clMap τ (ClassGroup.mk0 I) = ClassGroup.mk0 (idMap τ I) :=
  class_fun_mk0 τ I

variable (K) in

def ClDual (N : ℕ) (R M : Type) [CommRing R] [AddCommGroup M] [Module R M] : Submodule R (ClassGroup (𝓞 K) → M) where
  carrier := {θ | (∀ a b, θ (a * b) = θ a + θ b) ∧ ∀ v ∈ Above K N, θ (primeCl v) = 0}
  zero_mem' := ⟨by simp, by simp⟩
  add_mem' {a b} ha hb := ⟨fun x y => by simp only [Pi.add_apply, ha.1, hb.1]; abel,
    fun v hv => by simp [ha.2 v hv, hb.2 v hv]⟩
  smul_mem' c a ha := ⟨fun x y => by simp [ha.1], fun v hv => by simp [ha.2 v hv]⟩

def ordv (v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 K)) (a : Kˣ) : ℤ := -Multiplicative.toAdd (v.valuationOfNeZero a)

theorem ordv_mul (v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 K)) (a b : Kˣ) : ordv v (a * b) = ordv v a + ordv v b := by
  simp [ordv, add_comm]

theorem ordv_one (v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 K)) : ordv v (1 : Kˣ) = 0 := by simp [ordv]

theorem ordv_inv (v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 K)) (a : Kˣ) : ordv v a⁻¹ = -ordv v a := by simp [ordv]

def SelK (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 K))) (n : ℕ) : Subgroup Kˣ where
  carrier := {a | ∀ v ∉ S, (n : ℤ) ∣ ordv v a}
  one_mem' v _ := by rw [ordv_one]; exact dvd_zero _
  mul_mem' {a b} ha hb v hv := by rw [ordv_mul]; exact dvd_add (ha v hv) (hb v hv)
  inv_mem' {a} ha v hv := by rw [ordv_inv]; exact (ha v hv).neg_right

variable (K) in

def powK (n : ℕ) : Subgroup Kˣ := (powMonoidHom n : Kˣ →* Kˣ).range

def radCl (n : ℕ) (a : Kˣ) : ClassGroup (𝓞 K) := ∏ᶠ v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 K), primeCl v ^ (ordv v a / n)

end Cl

section X

variable {F Ω : Type} [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω]

def XDual (N : ℕ) (U : Subgroup (G F Ω)) (R M : Type) [CommRing R] [AddCommGroup M] [Module R M] : Submodule R (U → M) where
  carrier := {φ | IsLocallyConstant φ ∧ (∀ a b, φ (a * b) = φ a + φ b) ∧
    (∀ (A : ValuationSubring Ω) (u : U), u.1 ∈ Ine (F := F) A → φ u = 0) ∧
    ∀ A : ValuationSubring Ω, A.valuation (N : Ω) < 1 → ∀ u : U, u.1 ∈ Dec (F := F) A → φ u = 0}
  zero_mem' := ⟨IsLocallyConstant.const _, by simp, by simp, by simp⟩
  add_mem' {a b} ha hb := ⟨ha.1.add hb.1, fun x y => by simp only [Pi.add_apply, ha.2.1, hb.2.1]; abel,
    fun A u h => by simp [ha.2.2.1 A u h, hb.2.2.1 A u h],
    fun A hA u h => by simp [ha.2.2.2 A hA u h, hb.2.2.2 A hA u h]⟩
  smul_mem' c a ha := ⟨ha.1.comp (c • ·), fun x y => by simp [ha.2.1], fun A u h => by simp [ha.2.2.1 A u h],
    fun A hA u h => by simp [ha.2.2.2 A hA u h]⟩

variable {R M : Type} [CommRing R] [AddCommGroup M] [Module R M]

def conjCond (U : Subgroup (G F Ω)) (σ : Ω ≃+* Ω) (T : M →ₗ[R] M) : Submodule R (U → M) where
  carrier := {φ | ∀ u u' : U, (∀ x, u'.1 (σ x) = σ (u.1 x)) → φ u' = T (φ u)}
  zero_mem' := by intro u u' _; simp
  add_mem' {a b} ha hb u u' h := by simp [ha u u' h, hb u u' h]
  smul_mem' c a ha u u' h := by simp [ha u u' h]

def EqHom (N : ℕ) (U : Subgroup (G F Ω)) (ρ : Representation R (G F Ω) M) : Submodule R (U → M) :=
  XDual N U R M ⊓ ⨅ g : G F Ω, conjCond U g.toRingEquiv (ρ g)

def EqEig (N : ℕ) (U : Subgroup (G F Ω)) (ρ : Representation R (G F Ω) M) (σ : Ω ≃+* Ω) (s : R) : Submodule R (U → M) :=
  EqHom N U ρ ⊓ conjCond U σ (s • LinearMap.id)

variable (R M) in

def restr {U V : Subgroup (G F Ω)} (h : U ≤ V) : (V → M) →ₗ[R] (U → M) := LinearMap.funLeft R M (Subgroup.inclusion h)

variable (R M) in

def funRep (U : Subgroup (G F Ω)) [U.Normal] : Representation R (G F Ω) (U → M) where
  toFun g := LinearMap.funLeft R M (conjHom U g)
  map_one' := LinearMap.ext fun φ => funext fun u => congrArg φ (Subtype.ext (by simp [conjHom]))
  map_mul' g h := LinearMap.ext fun φ => funext fun u => congrArg φ (Subtype.ext (by simp [conjHom, mul_assoc]))

def toGal (K : Type) [Field K] [Algebra K Ω] {U : Subgroup (G F Ω)} (hU : U ≤ galOf F K Ω) : U →* G K Ω where
  toFun u := AlgEquiv.ofRingEquiv (f := u.1.toRingEquiv) (hU u.2)
  map_one' := rfl
  map_mul' _ _ := rfl

def clCond (K : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω] (σ : Ω ≃+* Ω) (T : M →ₗ[R] M) :
    Submodule R (ClassGroup (𝓞 K) → M) where
  carrier := {θ | ∀ τ : K ≃+* K, IsLift τ σ → ∀ c, θ (clMap τ c) = T (θ c)}
  zero_mem' := by intro τ _ c; simp
  add_mem' {a b} ha hb τ h c := by simp [ha τ h c, hb τ h c]
  smul_mem' r a ha τ h c := by simp [ha τ h c]

def ClEq (K : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω] (N : ℕ) (ρ : Representation R (G F Ω) M) :
    Submodule R (ClassGroup (𝓞 K) → M) :=
  ClDual K N R M ⊓ ⨅ g : G F Ω, clCond K g.toRingEquiv (ρ g)

def ClEig (K : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω] (N : ℕ) (ρ : Representation R (G F Ω) M) (σ : Ω ≃+* Ω) (s : R) :
    Submodule R (ClassGroup (𝓞 K) → M) :=
  ClEq K N ρ ⊓ clCond K σ (s • LinearMap.id)

def ClTw (K : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω] (n : ℕ) (η : G F Ω →* (ZMod n)ˣ) : Subgroup (ClassGroup (𝓞 K)) where
  carrier := {c | c ^ n = 1 ∧ ∀ (g : G F Ω) (τ : K ≃+* K), IsLift τ g.toRingEquiv → clMap τ c = c ^ (η g : ZMod n).val}
  one_mem' := ⟨one_pow _, fun g τ _ => by simp⟩
  mul_mem' {a b} ha hb := ⟨by rw [mul_pow, ha.1, hb.1, one_mul], fun g τ h => by
    rw [map_mul, ha.2 g τ h, hb.2 g τ h, mul_pow]⟩
  inv_mem' {a} ha := ⟨by rw [inv_pow, ha.1, inv_one], fun g τ h => by rw [map_inv, ha.2 g τ h, inv_pow]⟩

def ClMinus {K : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] (τ : K ≃+* K) : Subgroup (ClassGroup (𝓞 K)) :=
  (clMap τ * MonoidHom.id _).ker

def IsRad {n : ℕ} (ζ : Ω) (U : Subgroup (G F Ω)) (φ : U → ZMod n) (β : Ω) : Prop :=
  β ≠ 0 ∧ ∀ u : U, u.1 β = ζ ^ (φ u).val * β

variable [IsAlgClosure F Ω]

instance isAlgClosure_sub (K : IntermediateField F Ω) : IsAlgClosure K Ω :=
  ⟨IsAlgClosure.isAlgClosed F, Algebra.IsAlgebraic.tower_top (K := F) K⟩

end X

section Tower

variable (F Ω : Type) [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] [IsAlgClosure F Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime]

abbrev Uinf : Subgroup (G F Ω) := cycU l ⊤

def Fn (n : ℕ) : IntermediateField F Ω :=
  IntermediateField.adjoin F ((Polynomial.X ^ l ^ n - 1 : Polynomial F).rootSet Ω)

instance isSplittingField_Fn (n : ℕ) : Polynomial.IsSplittingField F (Fn F Ω l n) (Polynomial.X ^ l ^ n - 1) :=
  haveI : IsAlgClosed Ω := IsAlgClosure.isAlgClosed F
  IntermediateField.adjoin_rootSet_isSplittingField (IsAlgClosed.splits _)

instance (n : ℕ) : FiniteDimensional F (Fn F Ω l n) :=
  Polynomial.IsSplittingField.finiteDimensional _ (Polynomial.X ^ l ^ n - 1)

instance (n : ℕ) : Normal F (Fn F Ω l n) := Normal.of_isSplittingField (Polynomial.X ^ l ^ n - 1)

instance (n : ℕ) : NumberField (Fn F Ω l n) := NumberField.of_module_finite F _

abbrev Un (n : ℕ) : Subgroup (G F Ω) := levU l ⊤ n

def cycR (r : ℕ) : G F Ω →* (ZMod (l ^ r))ˣ :=
  (Units.map (PadicInt.toZModPow r : ℤ_[l] →+* ZMod (l ^ r)).toMonoidHom).comp (cycZ F Ω l)

def Procyc : Prop := l ≠ 2 ∨ ∀ g, cycR F Ω l 2 g = 1

variable {F Ω}

abbrev Dinf (N : ℕ) (U : Subgroup (G F Ω)) : Submodule ℤ_[l] (cycU l U → QZ l) := XDual N (cycU l U) ℤ_[l] (QZ l)

abbrev Xinf (N : ℕ) (U : Subgroup (G F Ω)) : Type := Dinf l N U →ₗ[ℤ_[l]] QZ l

end Tower

def errTwo (l : ℕ) : ℕ∞ := if l = 2 then 1 else 0

section Res

def resTriv {R Γ M U : Type} [CommRing R] [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [AddCommGroup M] [Module R M]
    [TopologicalSpace M] [IsTopologicalAddGroup M] [ContinuousConstSMul R M] (ρ : Representation R Γ M) (φ : U → Γ)
    (h : ∀ u m, ρ (φ u) m = m) : H1 ρ →ₗ[R] (U → M) :=
  Submodule.liftQ _ ((LinearMap.funLeft R M φ).comp (Z1 ρ).subtype) fun z hz => by
    obtain ⟨m, hm⟩ := (mem_coboundaries1 _ _).1 hz
    exact funext fun u => (hm (φ u)).trans (by rw [h, sub_self]; rfl)

variable {F Ω : Type} [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] [IsAlgClosure F Ω] {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime]

def resInf (D : LData F Ω l) (e : ℤ) : H1 (qzTw D e) →ₗ[ℤ_[l]] (Uinf F Ω l → QZ l) :=
  resTriv (qzTw D e) (fun u => toGS Ω _ u.1) fun u m => by
    have h : D.chi (toGS Ω _ u.1) = 1 := (DFunLike.congr_fun D.hchi u.1).trans u.2.2
    rw [qzTw_apply, h, one_zpow, Units.val_one, one_smul]

theorem resInf_mk (D : LData F Ω l) (e : ℤ) (z : Z1 (qzTw D e)) (u : Uinf F Ω l) :
    resInf D e (Submodule.Quotient.mk z) u = z.1 (toGS Ω _ u.1) := rfl

def zmTw (D : LData F Ω l) (r : ℕ) (e : ℤ) :
    Representation (ZMod (l ^ r)) (GS Ω (D.S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F)))) (ZMod (l ^ r)) :=
  scalRep (ZMod (l ^ r))
    (((Units.map (PadicInt.toZModPow r : ℤ_[l] →+* ZMod (l ^ r)).toMonoidHom).comp D.chi) ^ (-e))

def ShaZm (D : LData F Ω l) (r : ℕ) (e : ℤ) : Submodule (ZMod (l ^ r)) (H1 (zmTw D r e)) :=
  Sha1 (zmTw D r e) (locFam D.S D.A) (continuous_locFam D.S D.A)

def resZm (D : LData F Ω l) (r : ℕ) : H1 (zmTw D r 0) →ₗ[ZMod (l ^ r)] (G F Ω → ZMod (l ^ r)) :=
  resTriv (zmTw D r 0) (toGS Ω _) fun u m => by simp [zmTw]

end Res

end UpperTheory.V.Kum

end


[LFC_SRC_CL_PULL] Finite class-field character dictionary
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/GaloisLibrary/KummerTheory/PullOnto.lean#L1-L57
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.KummerTheory.ClPullApply
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.KummerTheory.ArtKer
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.KummerTheory.ArtFrob
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.KummerTheory.ArtSurj
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.KummerTheory.PullMem
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.KummerTheory.ToGalDict
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.KummerTheory.PlaceDict

open NumberField IsDedekindDomain Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal Formalism.GaloisCohomology UpperTheory.V UpperTheory.V.LDataCardLeFinrank UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.V.Kum

theorem UpperTheory.V.Kum.pullOnto :
    ∀ (F Ω : Type) [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] [IsAlgClosure F Ω] (K : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra F K] [Algebra K Ω] [IsScalarTower F K Ω] [IsAlgClosure K Ω] (U : Subgroup (G F Ω)) (hU : U ≤ galOf F K Ω) (N : ℕ) (R M : Type) [CommRing R] [AddCommGroup M] [Module R M], N ≠ 0 → galOf F K Ω ≤ U → (IsTotallyComplex K ∨ ∀ m : M, 2 • m = 0 → m = 0) → (ClDual K N R M).map (clPull K hU R M) = XDual N U R M := by
  intro F Ω _ _ _ _ _ K _ _ _ _ _ _ U hU N R M _ _ _ hN hU' hK
  refine le_antisymm (pullMem F Ω K U hU N R M hN).1 fun φ hφ => ?_
  obtain ⟨hlc, hadd, hI, hD⟩ := hφ
  obtain ⟨hDec, hIne, -, -⟩ := toGalDict F Ω K U hU
  obtain ⟨hE, -, -⟩ := galEmbTop F K Ω
  let ι : G K Ω →* U := (galEmb F K Ω).codRestrict U fun σ => hU' (galEmb_mem F K Ω σ)
  have hι : Continuous ι := hE.continuous.subtype_mk _
  have h1 : ∀ σ, toGal K hU (ι σ) = σ := fun σ => AlgEquiv.ext fun x => rfl
  have h2 : ∀ u, ι (toGal K hU u) = u := fun u => Subtype.ext (AlgEquiv.ext fun x => rfl)
  let φ' : U →* Multiplicative M := MonoidHom.mk' (fun u => Multiplicative.ofAdd (φ u)) fun a b => by
    rw [hadd, ofAdd_add]
  let χ : G K Ω →* Multiplicative M := φ'.comp ι
  have hχ : ∀ σ, χ σ = 1 ↔ φ (ι σ) = 0 := fun σ => Iff.rfl
  have hker : (artCl K Ω).ker ≤ χ.ker := by
    refine artKer K Ω (Multiplicative M) χ ?_ (fun A σ hσ => ?_) fun c hc => ?_
    · exact (hlc.comp_continuous hι).isOpen_fiber 0
    · exact (hχ σ).2 (hI A _ ((hIne A _).1 ((h1 σ).symm ▸ hσ)))
    · obtain ⟨j, hj⟩ := hc
      rcases hK with hK | hK
      · refine absurd ?_ (InfinitePlace.not_isReal_iff_isComplex.2
          (hK.isComplex (InfinitePlace.mk (j.comp (algebraMap K Ω)))))
        rw [InfinitePlace.isReal_mk_iff, ComplexEmbedding.isReal_iff]
        ext x
        have := hj (algebraMap K Ω x)
        rw [show c.toRingEquiv (algebraMap K Ω x) = algebraMap K Ω x from c.commutes x] at this
        exact this.symm
      · have hcc : c * c = 1 := AlgEquiv.ext fun x => j.injective (by
          have e1 := hj (c x)
          have e2 := hj x
          exact e1.trans ((congrArg _ e2).trans (Complex.conj_conj _)))
        have e : χ c * χ c = 1 := by rw [← map_mul, hcc, map_one]
        refine (hχ c).2 (hK _ ?_)
        rw [two_nsmul]
        exact e
  let θ := MonoidHom.liftOfSurjective (artCl K Ω) (artSurj K Ω).1 ⟨χ, hker⟩
  have hθ : ∀ σ, θ (artCl K Ω σ) = χ σ := fun σ =>
    MonoidHom.liftOfRightInverse_comp_apply _ _ _ ⟨χ, hker⟩ σ
  refine ⟨fun c => Multiplicative.toAdd (θ c), ⟨fun a b => congrArg Multiplicative.toAdd (θ.map_mul a b), fun v hv => ?_⟩, funext fun u => ?_⟩
  · obtain ⟨A, hA⟩ := exists_isPlaceOver Ω v
    obtain ⟨σ, hσ⟩ := exists_isFrob hA
    show Multiplicative.toAdd (θ (primeCl v)) = 0
    rw [← artFrob K Ω A v σ hA hσ, hθ]
    exact hD A (((placeDict K Ω).2.2.2 A v N hA).2 hv) _ ((hDec A _).1 ((h1 σ).symm ▸ hσ.1))
  · rw [clPull_apply, hθ]
    exact congrArg φ (h2 u)


[LFC_SRC_STR_GEN] Full strGen with its local-invariant correction
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/GaloisCochains/StrGen.lean#L1-L279
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisCochains.GLinkQuot

open NumberField IsDedekindDomain Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal Module UpperTheory.V UpperTheory.V.Fr UpperTheory.V.GC

private theorem cocycle_coboundary_of_vanishing_finite_index {k Γ : Type} [Field k] [Group Γ] (κ : Γ →* kˣ) (c : Γ → k)
    (hc : ∀ s t, c (s * t) = c s + (κ s : k) * c t) (N : Subgroup Γ) [N.FiniteIndex]
    (hN : ∀ n ∈ N, c n = 0) (hm : (N.index : k) ≠ 0) :
    ∃ X : k, ∀ s, c s = (κ s : k) * X - X := by
  have hcn : ∀ a, ∀ n ∈ N, c (a * n) = c a := by
    intro a n hn
    rw [hc, hN n hn, mul_zero, add_zero]
  have hq : ∀ a b : Γ, (a : Γ ⧸ N) = b → c a = c b := by
    intro a b h
    rw [QuotientGroup.eq] at h
    have := hcn a _ h
    rw [mul_inv_cancel_left] at this
    exact this.symm
  haveI : Fintype (Γ ⧸ N) := Fintype.ofFinite _
  have hcard : (Fintype.card (Γ ⧸ N) : k) = N.index := by
    rw [Subgroup.index_eq_card, Nat.card_eq_fintype_card]
  refine ⟨-(N.index : k)⁻¹ * ∑ q : Γ ⧸ N, c q.out, fun s => ?_⟩
  have h1 : ∀ q : Γ ⧸ N, (κ s : k) * c q.out = c (s • q).out - c s := by
    intro q
    have : c (s * q.out) = c (s • q).out := by
      apply hq
      rw [QuotientGroup.out_eq']
      conv_rhs => rw [← QuotientGroup.out_eq' q]
      rfl
    rw [← this, hc]
    ring
  have key : ∑ q : Γ ⧸ N, (κ s : k) * c q.out = ∑ q : Γ ⧸ N, c q.out - N.index * c s := by
    simp_rw [h1]
    rw [Finset.sum_sub_distrib, Finset.sum_const, Finset.card_univ, nsmul_eq_mul, hcard]
    congr 1
    exact Equiv.sum_comp (MulAction.toPerm s) (fun q : Γ ⧸ N => c q.out)
  set S := ∑ q : Γ ⧸ N, c q.out
  have hS : (κ s : k) * S = S - N.index * c s := by
    rw [Finset.mul_sum]
    exact key
  have e : (κ s : k) * (-(N.index : k)⁻¹ * S) - -(N.index : k)⁻¹ * S
      = -(N.index : k)⁻¹ * ((κ s : k) * S - S) := by ring
  rw [e, hS]
  field_simp
  ring

private theorem rank_kummer_dual_le_clRk {F Ω : Type} [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω]
    [IsAlgClosure F Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (k : Type) [Field k] [CharP k l]
    (K₁ : Subgroup (G F Ω)) (hK : IsOpen (K₁ : Set (G F Ω))) :
    Module.rank k (Kum.XDual 1 K₁ k k) ≤ clRk l K₁ := by
  classical
  let b := Basis.ofVectorSpace (ZMod l) (Kum.XDual 1 K₁ (ZMod l) (ZMod l))
  let cst := ZMod.castHom (dvd_refl l) k
  let e : _ → (K₁ → k) := fun i u => cst ((b i : K₁ → ZMod l) u)
  have hT : ∀ x : Kum.XDual 1 K₁ (ZMod l) (ZMod l),
      (fun u => cst ((x : K₁ → ZMod l) u)) ∈ Submodule.span k (Set.range e) := by
    intro x
    have hx : x ∈ Submodule.span (ZMod l) (Set.range b) := by
      rw [b.span_eq]
      trivial
    induction hx using Submodule.span_induction with
    | mem y hy =>
      obtain ⟨i, rfl⟩ := hy
      exact Submodule.subset_span ⟨i, rfl⟩
    | zero =>
      convert Submodule.zero_mem (Submodule.span k (Set.range e)) using 1
      funext u
      simp
    | add x y _ _ hx hy =>
      convert Submodule.add_mem _ hx hy using 1
      funext u
      simp
    | smul a x _ hx =>
      convert Submodule.smul_mem _ (cst a) hx using 1
      funext u
      simp
  have hsp : Kum.XDual 1 K₁ k k ≤ Submodule.span k (Set.range e) := by
    intro φ hφ
    obtain ⟨hlc, hadd, hine, hdec⟩ := hφ
    haveI : CompactSpace K₁ :=
      isCompact_iff_compactSpace.mp (Subgroup.isClosed_of_isOpen K₁ hK).isCompact
    have hfin : (Set.range φ).Finite := hlc.range_finite
    letI : Module (ZMod l) k := Module.compHom k cst
    haveI : Module.Finite (ZMod l) (Submodule.span (ZMod l) (Set.range φ)) :=
      Module.Finite.span_of_finite _ hfin
    let β := Module.finBasis (ZMod l) (Submodule.span (ZMod l) (Set.range φ))
    have hmem : ∀ u, φ u ∈ Submodule.span (ZMod l) (Set.range φ) :=
      fun u => Submodule.subset_span ⟨u, rfl⟩
    let ψ : Fin _ → (K₁ → ZMod l) := fun j u => β.coord j ⟨φ u, hmem u⟩
    have hψ : ∀ j, ψ j ∈ Kum.XDual 1 K₁ (ZMod l) (ZMod l) := by
      intro j
      refine ⟨?_, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
      · have h0 : IsLocallyConstant
            (fun u => (⟨φ u, hmem u⟩ : Submodule.span (ZMod l) (Set.range φ))) :=
          (IsLocallyConstant.iff_eventually_eq _).mpr
            fun x => (hlc.eventually_eq x).mono fun y hy => Subtype.ext hy
        exact h0.comp (β.coord j)
      · intro a c
        show β.coord j ⟨φ (a * c), _⟩ = β.coord j ⟨φ a, _⟩ + β.coord j ⟨φ c, _⟩
        rw [← map_add]
        congr 1
        exact Subtype.ext (hadd a c)
      · intro A u hu
        show β.coord j ⟨φ u, _⟩ = 0
        rw [← map_zero (β.coord j)]
        congr 1
        exact Subtype.ext (hine A u hu)
      · intro A hA u hu
        show β.coord j ⟨φ u, _⟩ = 0
        rw [← map_zero (β.coord j)]
        congr 1
        exact Subtype.ext (hdec A hA u hu)
    have hrep : φ = ∑ j, (β j : k) • (fun u => cst (ψ j u)) := by
      funext u
      rw [Finset.sum_apply]
      have h2 : ((∑ j, β.repr ⟨φ u, hmem u⟩ j • β j : Submodule.span (ZMod l) (Set.range φ)) : k)
          = φ u := congrArg Subtype.val (β.sum_repr ⟨φ u, hmem u⟩)
      rw [← h2, Submodule.coe_sum]
      apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
      intro j _
      rw [Submodule.coe_smul]
      show cst _ * _ = _
      rw [mul_comm]
      rfl
    rw [hrep]
    apply Submodule.sum_mem
    intro j _
    apply Submodule.smul_mem
    exact hT ⟨ψ j, hψ j⟩
  calc Module.rank k (Kum.XDual 1 K₁ k k)
      ≤ Module.rank k (Submodule.span k (Set.range e)) := Submodule.rank_mono hsp
    _ ≤ Cardinal.mk (Set.range e) := rank_span_le _
    _ ≤ Cardinal.mk _ := Cardinal.mk_range_le
    _ = clRk l K₁ := b.mk_eq_rank''

theorem UpperTheory.V.GC.strGen :
    ∀ (F Ω : Type) [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] [IsAlgClosure F Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (k : Type) [Field k] [CharP k l] (U K₁ : Subgroup (G F Ω)) [K₁.Normal], K₁ ≤ U → IsOpen (K₁ : Set (G F Ω)) → ¬ l ∣ K₁.relIndex U → ∀ (Γ : Type) [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] (π : U →* Γ), Continuous π → Function.Surjective π →
    ∀ (V : Type) [Fintype V] (Gv : V → Type) [∀ v, Group (Gv v)] [∀ v, TopologicalSpace (Gv v)] (loc : ∀ v, Gv v →* Γ) (κ : Γ →* kˣ), (∀ u : U, u.1 ∈ K₁ → κ (π u) = 1) →
    (∀ A : ValuationSubring Ω, ∀ u : U, u.1 ∈ Ine (F := F) A → u.1 ∈ K₁ → π u = 1 ∨ ∃ v s g, π u = g * loc v s * g⁻¹) →
    Module.rank k (H1C loc (chRep κ) fun _ => ⊥) ≤ Cardinal.lift.{0} (clRk l K₁) + (Nat.card {v // ∀ s, κ (loc v s) = 1} : Cardinal) := by
  classical
  intro F Ω _ _ _ _ _ l _ k _ _ U K₁ _ hKU hK₁ hl Γ _ _ π hπc hπs V _ Gv _ _ loc κ hκ hIne
  have hρ : ∀ (s : Γ) (w : k), chRep κ s w = (κ s : k) * w := fun _ _ => rfl
  let Zs := Z1C loc (chRep κ) (fun _ : V => (⊥ : Submodule k k))
  let Bs := Submodule.comap Zs.subtype (continuousCoboundaries1 (chRep κ) (fun _ : V => (⊥ : Submodule k k)))
  have hc1 : ∀ z : Zs, ∀ s t, z.1.1 (s * t) = z.1.1 s + (κ s : k) * z.1.1 t := by
    intro z s t
    obtain ⟨_, h2, _⟩ := z.2
    rw [h2 s t, hρ]
  have hcl : ∀ z : Zs, ∀ v s, z.1.1 (loc v s) = (κ (loc v s) : k) * z.1.2 v - z.1.2 v := by
    intro z v s
    obtain ⟨_, _, h3⟩ := z.2
    have := h3 v s
    rw [Submodule.mem_bot, sub_eq_zero, hρ] at this
    exact this
  let Φ : Zs →ₗ[k] (K₁ → k) :=
    { toFun := fun z u => z.1.1 (π ⟨u.1, hKU u.2⟩)
      map_add' := fun _ _ => rfl
      map_smul' := fun _ _ => rfl }
  have hΦB : Bs ≤ LinearMap.ker Φ := by
    intro z hz
    obtain ⟨X, hX1, _⟩ := hz
    rw [LinearMap.mem_ker]
    funext u
    show z.1.1 (π ⟨u.1, hKU u.2⟩) = 0
    refine (hX1 _).trans ?_
    rw [hρ, hκ ⟨u.1, hKU u.2⟩ u.2, Units.val_one, one_mul, sub_self]
  let Φb := Bs.liftQ Φ hΦB
  have hrange : LinearMap.range Φb ≤ Kum.XDual 1 K₁ k k := by
    rw [Submodule.range_liftQ]
    rintro _ ⟨z, rfl⟩
    obtain ⟨hlcz, _, _⟩ := z.2
    refine ⟨?_, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
    · have hcont : Continuous fun u : K₁ => π ⟨u.1, hKU u.2⟩ :=
        hπc.comp (Continuous.subtype_mk continuous_subtype_val _)
      exact hlcz.comp_continuous hcont
    · intro a b
      show z.1.1 (π ⟨(a * b).1, hKU (a * b).2⟩)
        = z.1.1 (π ⟨a.1, hKU a.2⟩) + z.1.1 (π ⟨b.1, hKU b.2⟩)
      have e : π ⟨(a * b).1, hKU (a * b).2⟩ = π ⟨a.1, hKU a.2⟩ * π ⟨b.1, hKU b.2⟩ := by
        rw [← map_mul]
        rfl
      rw [e, hc1, hκ _ a.2, Units.val_one, one_mul]
    · intro A u hu
      show z.1.1 (π ⟨u.1, hKU u.2⟩) = 0
      have c1 : z.1.1 1 = 0 := by simpa using hc1 z 1 1
      have hk := hκ ⟨u.1, hKU u.2⟩ u.2
      rcases hIne A ⟨u.1, hKU u.2⟩ hu u.2 with h | ⟨v, s, g, h⟩
      · rw [h, c1]
      · rw [h] at hk ⊢
        simp only [map_mul, map_inv, mul_inv_cancel_comm] at hk
        have ch : z.1.1 (loc v s) = 0 := by
          rw [hcl z v s, hk, Units.val_one, one_mul, sub_self]
        have e3 := hc1 z g g⁻¹
        rw [mul_inv_cancel, c1] at e3
        rw [hc1, hc1, ch, map_mul, hk, mul_one, mul_zero, add_zero]
        exact e3.symm
    · intro A hA
      exfalso
      simp at hA
  let e0 : ({v // ∀ s, κ (loc v s) = 1} → k) → V → k :=
    fun y v => if h : ∀ s, κ (loc v s) = 1 then y ⟨v, h⟩ else 0
  have e0add : ∀ y y', e0 (y + y') = e0 y + e0 y' := by
    intro y y'
    funext v
    by_cases h : ∀ s, κ (loc v s) = 1 <;> simp [e0, h]
  have e0smul : ∀ (r : k) y, e0 (r • y) = r • e0 y := by
    intro r y
    funext v
    by_cases h : ∀ s, κ (loc v s) = 1 <;> simp [e0, h]
  have e0mem : ∀ y, (((0 : Γ → k), e0 y) : (Γ → k) × (V → k)) ∈ Zs := by
    intro y
    refine ⟨IsLocallyConstant.const (0 : k), ?_, ?_⟩
    · intro s t
      show (0 : k) = 0 + chRep κ s 0
      simp
    · intro v s
      rw [Submodule.mem_bot]
      show (0 : k) - (chRep κ (loc v s) (e0 y v) - e0 y v) = 0
      rw [hρ]
      by_cases h : ∀ s, κ (loc v s) = 1
      · rw [h s, Units.val_one, one_mul, sub_self, sub_zero]
      · rw [show e0 y v = 0 from dif_neg h, mul_zero, sub_zero, sub_zero]
  let ι : ({v // ∀ s, κ (loc v s) = 1} → k) →ₗ[k] Zs :=
    { toFun := fun y => ⟨((0 : Γ → k), e0 y), e0mem y⟩
      map_add' := fun y y' => Subtype.ext (Prod.ext (by simp) (e0add y y'))
      map_smul' := fun r y => Subtype.ext (Prod.ext (by simp) (e0smul r y)) }
  let ψ := Bs.mkQ ∘ₗ ι
  have hker : LinearMap.ker Φb ≤ LinearMap.range ψ := by
    intro q hq
    obtain ⟨z, rfl⟩ := Submodule.Quotient.mk_surjective Bs q
    rw [LinearMap.mem_ker, Submodule.liftQ_apply] at hq
    have hc0 : ∀ u : K₁, z.1.1 (π ⟨u.1, hKU u.2⟩) = 0 := fun u => congrFun hq u
    set N := (K₁.subgroupOf U).map π with hNdef
    have hdvd : N.index ∣ K₁.relIndex U := Subgroup.index_map_dvd _ hπs
    have hNl : ¬ l ∣ N.index := fun h => hl (h.trans hdvd)
    have hN0 : N.index ≠ 0 := fun h => hNl (h ▸ dvd_zero l)
    haveI : N.FiniteIndex := ⟨hN0⟩
    have hNk : (N.index : k) ≠ 0 := by
      rw [Ne, CharP.cast_eq_zero_iff k l]
      exact hNl
    have hNc : ∀ n ∈ N, z.1.1 n = 0 := by
      rintro _ ⟨u, hu, rfl⟩
      exact hc0 ⟨u.1, Subgroup.mem_subgroupOf.mp hu⟩
    obtain ⟨X, hX⟩ := cocycle_coboundary_of_vanishing_finite_index κ z.1.1 (hc1 z) N hNc hNk
    refine ⟨fun v => z.1.2 v.1 - X, ?_⟩
    show Bs.mkQ (ι _) = Bs.mkQ z
    rw [Submodule.mkQ_apply, Submodule.mkQ_apply, Submodule.Quotient.eq]
    refine ⟨-X, ?_, ?_⟩
    · intro s
      show (0 : k) - z.1.1 s = chRep κ s (-X) - -X
      rw [hρ, hX s]
      ring
    · intro v
      rw [Submodule.mem_bot]
      show e0 (fun v => z.1.2 v.1 - X) v - z.1.2 v - -X = 0
      by_cases h : ∀ s, κ (loc v s) = 1
      · rw [show e0 (fun v => z.1.2 v.1 - X) v = z.1.2 v - X from dif_pos h]
        ring
      · rw [show e0 (fun v => z.1.2 v.1 - X) v = 0 from dif_neg h]
        push_neg at h
        obtain ⟨s, hs⟩ := h
        have e := hcl z v s
        rw [hX] at e
        have hs' : (κ (loc v s) : k) ≠ 1 := by rwa [Ne, Units.val_eq_one]
        have hm : ((κ (loc v s) : k) - 1) * (z.1.2 v - X) = 0 := by linear_combination -e
        rcases mul_eq_zero.mp hm with h1 | h1
        · exact absurd (sub_eq_zero.mp h1) hs'
        · linear_combination -h1
  have h1 : Module.rank k (LinearMap.range Φb) ≤ Cardinal.lift.{0} (clRk l K₁) := by
    rw [Cardinal.lift_id]
    exact (Submodule.rank_mono hrange).trans (rank_kummer_dual_le_clRk l k K₁ hK₁)
  have h2 : Module.rank k (LinearMap.ker Φb)
      ≤ (Nat.card {v // ∀ s, κ (loc v s) = 1} : Cardinal) := by
    refine (Submodule.rank_mono hker).trans ((rank_range_le ψ).trans_eq ?_)
    rw [rank_fun', Nat.card_eq_fintype_card]
  calc Module.rank k (H1C loc (chRep κ) fun _ => ⊥)
      = Module.rank k (LinearMap.range Φb) + Module.rank k (LinearMap.ker Φb) :=
        (LinearMap.rank_range_add_rank_ker Φb).symm
    _ ≤ _ := add_le_add h1 h2

private theorem exists_conj {F Ω : Type} [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] [IsAlgClosure F Ω]
    {A₀ A : ValuationSubring Ω} {v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F)} (h₀ : IsPlaceOver A₀ v) (hA : IsPlaceOver A v) :
    ∃ u : G F Ω, ∀ x ∈ Ine (F := F) A, u⁻¹ * x * u ∈ Dec (F := F) A₀ := by
  obtain ⟨g, hg⟩ := exists_smul_eq_of_isPlaceOver (F := F) h₀ hA
  have h : IsLift (RingEquiv.refl F) g.toRingEquiv := fun x => g.commutes x
  have hm : ∀ x, g.toRingEquiv x ∈ A ↔ x ∈ A₀ := fun x => by
    show g • x ∈ A ↔ _
    rw [← hg]
    exact ValuationSubring.smul_mem_pointwise_smul_iff
  exact ⟨g, fun x hx => conjGal_mem_Dec h hm hx.1⟩

theorem UpperTheory.V.GC.b1p :
    ∀ (F Ω : Type) [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] [IsAlgClosure F Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (k : Type) [Field k] [CharP k l] (Φ : Fr.Frame l k) (S : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))) (K : Subgroup (G F Ω)) (π : G F Ω →* Φ.Γ) (ι : ∀ v, Φ.Gv v →* G F Ω) (A : Φ.V → ValuationSubring Ω) (pl : Φ.V → HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F)) (e : Φ.Inf ≃ InfinitePlace F) (c : Φ.Inf → G F Ω), FLink l Φ S K π ι A pl e c → (∀ v, ∃ g, Φ.Ψ (Φ.loc v g) ^ 2 ≠ 1) → ∀ C₀ : ℕ, clRk l (K ⊓ (cycMod F Ω l 1).ker) ≤ C₀ → Module.rank k (H1C (k := k) (W := k) Φ.loc (chRep (Φ.Ψ⁻¹ ^ 2 * Φ.delK k)) fun _ => ⊥) ≤ C₀ := by
  intro F Ω _ _ _ _ _ l hl k _ _ Φ S K π ι A pl e c L h3 C₀ hC
  haveI := L.g.normal
  have hω : IsOpen ((cycMod F Ω l 1).ker : Set (G F Ω)) :=
    (isOpen_discrete {(1 : (ZMod (l ^ 1))ˣ)}).preimage GlobalTheory.Published.PoitouTate.continuous_cycMod
  have hidx : ¬ l ∣ (K ⊓ (cycMod F Ω l 1).ker).relIndex ⊤ := by
    rw [Subgroup.relIndex_top_right]
    intro h
    have h1 : (K ⊓ (cycMod F Ω l 1).ker).index ∣ K.index * (cycMod F Ω l 1).ker.index := by
      rw [← Subgroup.relIndex_mul_index (inf_le_left : K ⊓ (cycMod F Ω l 1).ker ≤ K), mul_comm,
        Subgroup.inf_relIndex_left]
      exact Nat.mul_dvd_mul_left _ (Subgroup.relIndex_dvd_index_of_normal _ _)
    rcases (hl.out.dvd_mul).1 (h.trans h1) with h2 | h2
    · exact L.g.idx h2
    · rw [Subgroup.index_ker] at h2
      have h4 := h2.trans (Subgroup.card_subgroup_dvd_card _)
      haveI : NeZero (l ^ 1) := ⟨pow_ne_zero _ hl.out.ne_zero⟩
      rw [Nat.card_eq_fintype_card, ZMod.card_units_eq_totient, pow_one, Nat.totient_prime hl.out] at h4
      have h5 := hl.out.two_le
      have := Nat.le_of_dvd (by omega) h4
      omega
  have key := strGen F Ω l k ⊤ (K ⊓ (cycMod F Ω l 1).ker) le_top (L.g.isOpen.inter hω) hidx Φ.Γ
    (π.comp (Subgroup.subtype ⊤)) (L.g.cont.comp continuous_subtype_val)
    (fun y => by
      obtain ⟨x, hx⟩ := L.g.onto y
      exact ⟨⟨x, trivial⟩, hx⟩)
    Φ.V Φ.Gv Φ.loc (Φ.Ψ⁻¹ ^ 2 * Φ.delK k)
    (fun u hu => by
      have h1 := L.K_del _ hu.1
      have h2 := L.K_Ψ _ hu.1
      simp [Frame.delK, h1, h2])
    (fun B u hu hK => by
      obtain ⟨p1, p2, -⟩ := Kum.placeDict F Ω
      by_cases hB : B = ⊤
      · left
        rw [hB, p1, Subgroup.mem_bot] at hu
        simp [hu]
      obtain ⟨w, hw⟩ := p2 B hB
      by_cases hS : w ∈ S
      · right
        obtain ⟨v, rfl⟩ := (L.loc.mem w).2 (L.only w hS)
        obtain ⟨g, hg⟩ := exists_conj (L.loc.isOver v) hw
        have h1 := hg _ hu
        rw [← L.loc.range v] at h1
        obtain ⟨s, hs⟩ := h1
        refine ⟨v, s, π g, ?_⟩
        rw [L.loc_eq, hs]
        simp [mul_assoc]
      · left
        have : u.1 ∈ π.ker := by
          rw [L.g.ker]
          refine Subgroup.le_topologicalClosure _ (Subgroup.subset_normalClosure ?_)
          simp only [Set.mem_iUnion]
          exact ⟨w, hS, B, hw, hu, hK.1⟩
        exact this)
  haveI : IsEmpty {v // ∀ s, (Φ.Ψ⁻¹ ^ 2 * Φ.delK k) (Φ.loc v s) = 1} := ⟨fun ⟨v, hv⟩ => by
    obtain ⟨g, hg⟩ := h3 v
    have h1 : Φ.del (Φ.loc v g) = 1 := (Φ.loc0 v g).2
    have h2 := hv g
    apply hg
    simpa [Frame.delK, h1] using h2⟩
  rw [Nat.card_of_isEmpty, Nat.cast_zero, add_zero, Cardinal.lift_id] at key
  exact key.trans hC


[LFC_SRC_B1] Original b1 on doubled local family
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/GaloisCochains/PsiLocalPairEqLocal.lean#L1-L105
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisCochains.GLinkQuot

import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.SelmerTransport.Places

private theorem ine_conj {F Ω : Type} [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] (v : G F Ω)
    (B : ValuationSubring Ω) (g : G F Ω) (hg : g ∈ Ine (F := F) (v • B)) : v⁻¹ * g * v ∈ Ine (F := F) B := by
  have hl : IsLift (RingEquiv.refl F) v.toRingEquiv := fun y => v.commutes y
  have hA : ∀ x : Ω, v.toRingEquiv x ∈ v • B ↔ x ∈ B := fun x => by
    show v • x ∈ v • B ↔ x ∈ B
    exact ValuationSubring.smul_mem_pointwise_smul_iff
  have e : v⁻¹ * g * v = conjGal hl g := AlgEquiv.ext fun _ => rfl
  rw [e]
  exact conjGal_mem_Ine hl hA hg

private theorem not_dvd_ker_index {H : Type} [Group H] (l : ℕ) [hl : Fact l.Prime] (χ : H →* (ZMod (l ^ 1))ˣ) :
    ¬ l ∣ χ.ker.index := by
  intro h
  haveI : NeZero (l ^ 1) := ⟨pow_ne_zero _ hl.out.ne_zero⟩
  rw [Subgroup.index_ker] at h
  have h2 := h.trans (Subgroup.card_subgroup_dvd_card χ.range)
  rw [Nat.card_eq_fintype_card, ZMod.card_units_eq_totient, pow_one, Nat.totient_prime hl.out] at h2
  have h3 := hl.out.two_le
  have := Nat.le_of_dvd (by omega) h2
  omega

private theorem Psi_local_pair_eq_local {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {k : Type} [Field k] (Φ : Fr.Frame l k) (p : Φ.V × Bool)
    (s : Φ.Gv p.1) : Φ.Ψ (Φ.loc2 p s).1 = Φ.Ψ (Φ.loc p.1 s) := by
  obtain ⟨v, b⟩ := p
  cases b
  · rfl
  · show Φ.Ψ (Φ.c * Φ.loc v s * Φ.c⁻¹) = _
    rw [map_mul, map_mul, map_inv, mul_inv_cancel_comm]

theorem UpperTheory.V.GC.b1 :
    ∀ (F Ω : Type) [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] [IsAlgClosure F Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (k : Type) [Field k] [CharP k l] (Φ : Fr.Frame l k) (S : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))) (K : Subgroup (G F Ω)) (π : G F Ω →* Φ.Γ) (ι : ∀ v, Φ.Gv v →* G F Ω) (A : Φ.V → ValuationSubring Ω) (pl : Φ.V → HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F)) (e : Φ.Inf ≃ InfinitePlace F) (c : Φ.Inf → G F Ω), FLink l Φ S K π ι A pl e c → (∀ v, ∃ g, Φ.Ψ (Φ.loc v g) ^ 2 ≠ 1) → ∀ C₀ : ℕ, clRk l (K ⊓ (cycMod F Ω l 1).ker) ≤ C₀ → Module.rank k (H1C (k := k) (W := k) Φ.loc2 (chRep (Φ.Ψ⁻¹.comp Φ.del.ker.subtype)) fun _ => ⊥) ≤ C₀ := by
  intro F Ω _ _ _ _ _ l hl k _ _ Φ S K π ι A pl e c h hΨ C₀ hC
  haveI := h.g.normal
  let U : Subgroup (G F Ω) := Φ.del.ker.comap π
  let π' : U →* Φ.del.ker := π.subgroupComap Φ.del.ker
  have hπ' : ∀ u : U, (π' u).1 = π u.1 := fun _ => rfl
  have hKU : K ≤ U := fun g hg => h.K_del g hg
  have hω : IsOpen ((cycMod F Ω l 1).ker : Set (G F Ω)) :=
    by
      haveI : NeZero (l ^ 1) := ⟨pow_ne_zero _ hl.out.ne_zero⟩
      have e1 : ((cycMod F Ω l 1).ker : Set (G F Ω)) =
          (fun g => ((cycZ F Ω l g : ℤ_[l]ˣ) : ℤ_[l]) - 1) ⁻¹' Metric.closedBall 0 ((l : ℝ) ^ (-(1 : ℕ) : ℤ)) := by
        ext g
        rw [Set.mem_preimage, mem_closedBall_zero_iff, PadicInt.norm_le_pow_iff_mem_span_pow, ← PadicInt.ker_toZModPow,
          RingHom.mem_ker, map_sub, map_one, sub_eq_zero, SetLike.mem_coe, MonoidHom.mem_ker, Units.ext_iff]
        rfl
      rw [e1]
      refine (IsUltrametricDist.isOpen_closedBall _ (zpow_ne_zero _ (Nat.cast_ne_zero.2 hl.out.ne_zero))).preimage ?_
      exact (Units.continuous_val.comp (continuous_cycZ F Ω l)).sub continuous_const
  have hidx : ¬ l ∣ (K ⊓ (cycMod F Ω l 1).ker).relIndex U := by
    intro hd
    have h1 := hd.trans (Subgroup.relIndex_dvd_index_of_le (inf_le_left.trans hKU : K ⊓ (cycMod F Ω l 1).ker ≤ U))
    rw [← Subgroup.relIndex_mul_index (inf_le_left : K ⊓ (cycMod F Ω l 1).ker ≤ K), Subgroup.inf_relIndex_left] at h1
    rcases (Nat.Prime.dvd_mul hl.out).1 h1 with h2 | h2
    · exact not_dvd_ker_index l _ (h2.trans (Subgroup.relIndex_dvd_index_of_normal _ _))
    · exact h.g.idx h2
  have key := strGen F Ω l k U (K ⊓ (cycMod F Ω l 1).ker) (inf_le_left.trans hKU) (h.g.isOpen.inter hω) hidx
    Φ.del.ker π' ((h.g.cont.comp continuous_subtype_val).subtype_mk _)
    (π.subgroupComap_surjective_of_surjective _ h.g.onto) (Φ.V × Bool) (fun p => Φ.Gv p.1) Φ.loc2
    (Φ.Ψ⁻¹.comp Φ.del.ker.subtype) (fun u hu => by
      show (Φ.Ψ (π u.1))⁻¹ = 1
      rw [h.K_Ψ _ hu.1, inv_one]) (fun B u hu huK => by
      by_cases hB : B = ⊤
      · left
        rw [((Base.places F Ω l B).2.1 hB).1, Subgroup.mem_bot] at hu
        exact Subtype.ext (by rw [hπ', hu, map_one]; rfl)
      obtain ⟨w, hw⟩ := (Base.places F Ω l B).1 hB
      by_cases hS : w ∈ S
      · right
        obtain ⟨v, rfl⟩ := (h.loc.mem w).2 (h.only w hS)
        obtain ⟨g, rfl⟩ := exists_smul_eq_of_isPlaceOver (F := F) (h.loc.isOver v) hw
        have h1 := Ine_le_Dec _ (ine_conj g _ _ hu)
        rw [← h.loc.range v] at h1
        obtain ⟨s, hs⟩ := h1
        have h2 : π u.1 = π g * Φ.loc v s * (π g)⁻¹ := by
          rw [h.loc_eq, hs, ← map_inv, ← map_mul, ← map_mul]
          congr 1
          group
        rcases Int.units_eq_one_or (Φ.del (π g)) with h3 | h3
        · exact ⟨(v, false), s, ⟨π g, h3⟩, Subtype.ext h2⟩
        · refine ⟨(v, true), s, ⟨π g * Φ.c⁻¹, ?_⟩, Subtype.ext (h2.trans ?_)⟩
          · rw [MonoidHom.mem_ker, map_mul, map_inv, h3, h.del_c, mul_inv_cancel]
          · show _ = π g * Φ.c⁻¹ * (Φ.c * Φ.loc v s * Φ.c⁻¹) * (π g * Φ.c⁻¹)⁻¹
            group
      · left
        refine Subtype.ext ?_
        show π u.1 = 1
        rw [← MonoidHom.mem_ker, h.g.ker]
        refine Subgroup.le_topologicalClosure _ (Subgroup.subset_normalClosure ?_)
        simp only [Set.mem_iUnion]
        exact ⟨w, hS, B, hw, hu, huK.1⟩)
  haveI : IsEmpty {p : Φ.V × Bool // ∀ s, (Φ.Ψ⁻¹.comp Φ.del.ker.subtype) (Φ.loc2 p s) = 1} := ⟨fun p => by
    obtain ⟨s, hs⟩ := hΨ p.1.1
    have h1 : (Φ.Ψ (Φ.loc2 p.1 s).1)⁻¹ = 1 := p.2 s
    rw [Psi_local_pair_eq_local, inv_eq_one] at h1
    exact hs (by rw [h1, one_pow])⟩
  rw [Nat.card_of_isEmpty, Nat.cast_zero, add_zero, Cardinal.lift_id] at key
  exact key.trans hC
