private theorem cast_toZModPow_one (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (k : Type) [Field k] [CharP k l] (x : ℤ_[l]) :
    ZMod.castHom (dvd_pow_self l one_ne_zero) k (PadicInt.toZModPow 1 x) =
      ZMod.castHom (dvd_refl l) k (PadicInt.toZMod x) := by
  have hx : x - (PadicInt.zmodRepr x : ℤ_[l]) ∈ IsLocalRing.maximalIdeal ℤ_[l] := PadicInt.sub_zmodRepr_mem x
  have h1 : PadicInt.toZMod (x - (PadicInt.zmodRepr x : ℤ_[l])) = 0 := by
    rw [← RingHom.mem_ker, PadicInt.ker_toZMod]; exact hx
  have h2 : PadicInt.toZModPow 1 (x - (PadicInt.zmodRepr x : ℤ_[l])) = 0 := by
    rw [← RingHom.mem_ker, PadicInt.ker_toZModPow, pow_one, ← PadicInt.maximalIdeal_eq_span_p]; exact hx
  rw [map_sub, sub_eq_zero] at h1 h2
  rw [h1, h2]
  simp only [map_natCast]

theorem UpperTheory.V.GC.h1 :
    Hypotheses₂ → ∀ (F Ω : Type) [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] [IsAlgClosure F Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (k : Type) [Field k] [CharP k l] (Φ : Fr.Frame l k) (S : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))) (K : Subgroup (G F Ω)) (π : G F Ω →* Φ.Γ) (ι : ∀ v, Φ.Gv v →* G F Ω) (A : Φ.V → ValuationSubring Ω) (pl : Φ.V → HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F)) (e : Φ.Inf ≃ InfinitePlace F) (c : Φ.Inf → G F Ω), FLink l Φ S K π ι A pl e c → Finite k → (∀ v, ∃ s, Φ.om k (Φ.loc v s) ≠ 1) → ∀ C₀ : ℕ, clRk l (K ⊓ (cycMod F Ω l 1).ker) ≤ C₀ → Module.rank k (H1C (k := k) (W := k) Φ.loc (chRep (1 : Φ.Γ →* kˣ)) fun _ => ⊤) ≤ 1 + C₀ := by
  intro hP F Ω _ _ _ _ _ l _ k _ _ Φ S K π ι A pl e c hL hk hom C₀ hC
  have hS : ∀ v : S, ∃ u, pl u = v.1 := fun v => (hL.loc.mem v.1).2 (hL.only v.1 v.2)
  choose u hu using hS
  have hA : ∀ v : S, IsPlaceOver (A (u v)) v.1 := fun v => hu v ▸ hL.loc.isOver (u v)
  have hc : ∀ w : InfinitePlace F, IsCCAt w (c (e.symm w)) := fun w => by
    simpa using hL.cc (e.symm w)
  have h := character_H1_galois_quotient hP F Ω l hL.real S K Φ.Γ π hL.g k 1 (fun _ _ => rfl) (fun w => c (e.symm w)) hc
    (fun v => A (u v)) hA C₀ hC Φ.V Φ.Gv Φ.loc
  have hcyc : ∀ g, Φ.om k (π g) = cycF F Ω l k g := fun g => by
    apply Units.ext
    have := cast_toZModPow_one l k (cycZ F Ω l g : ℤ_[l])
    simp only [Frame.om, cycF, cycMod, MonoidHom.comp_apply, hL.eps, Units.coe_map]
    exact this.symm
  have hE : IsEmpty {v : S // ∀ g ∈ Dec (F := F) (A (u v)), (1 : Φ.Γ →* kˣ) (π g) = cycF F Ω l k g} := by
    refine ⟨fun ⟨v, hv⟩ => ?_⟩
    obtain ⟨s, hs⟩ := hom (u v)
    apply hs
    rw [hL.loc_eq, hcyc, ← hv _ (by rw [← hL.loc.range]; exact ⟨s, rfl⟩)]
    rfl
  have e1 : Nat.card {w : InfinitePlace F // (1 : Φ.Γ →* kˣ) (π (c (e.symm w))) = 1} = finrank ℚ F := by
    haveI := hL.real
    rw [Nat.card_congr (Equiv.subtypeUnivEquiv fun _ => MonoidHom.one_apply _), Nat.card_eq_fintype_card,
      InfinitePlace.card_eq_nrRealPlaces_add_nrComplexPlaces, IsTotallyReal.nrComplexPlaces_eq_zero, IsTotallyReal.finrank, add_zero]
  have e2 : Nat.card {_u : Unit // (1 : Φ.Γ →* kˣ) = 1} = 1 := by
    rw [Nat.card_congr (Equiv.subtypeUnivEquiv fun _ => rfl)]; simp
  rw [e1, e2, Nat.card_of_isEmpty, add_zero] at h
  push_cast at h
  exact (Cardinal.add_nat_le_add_nat_iff _).1 h

open scoped Pointwise in
private theorem ch_conj {F Ω : Type} [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] (v : G F Ω)
    (B : ValuationSubring Ω) (g : G F Ω) (hg : g ∈ Ine (F := F) (v • B)) : v⁻¹ * g * v ∈ Ine (F := F) B := by
  have hl : IsLift (RingEquiv.refl F) v.toRingEquiv := fun y => v.commutes y
  have hA : ∀ x : Ω, v.toRingEquiv x ∈ v • B ↔ x ∈ B := fun x => by
    show v • x ∈ v • B ↔ x ∈ B
    exact ValuationSubring.smul_mem_pointwise_smul_iff
  have e : v⁻¹ * g * v = conjGal hl g := AlgEquiv.ext fun _ => rfl
  rw [e]
  exact conjGal_mem_Ine hl hA hg

private theorem ch_idx {H : Type} [Group H] (l : ℕ) [hl : Fact l.Prime] (K : Subgroup H) (hK : ¬ l ∣ K.index)
    (χ : H →* (ZMod (l ^ 1))ˣ) : ¬ l ∣ (K ⊓ χ.ker).index := by
  intro h
  rw [← Subgroup.relIndex_mul_index (inf_le_left : K ⊓ χ.ker ≤ K), Subgroup.inf_relIndex_left, hl.out.dvd_mul] at h
  refine h.elim (fun h => ?_) hK
  replace h := h.trans (Subgroup.relIndex_dvd_index_of_normal _ _)
  haveI : NeZero (l ^ 1) := ⟨pow_ne_zero _ hl.out.ne_zero⟩
  rw [Subgroup.index_ker] at h
  have h2 := h.trans (Subgroup.card_subgroup_dvd_card χ.range)
  rw [Nat.card_eq_fintype_card, ZMod.card_units_eq_totient, pow_one, Nat.totient_prime hl.out] at h2
  have h3 := hl.out.two_le
  have := Nat.le_of_dvd (by omega) h2
  omega

private def chZero {H k : Type} [Group H] [Field k] (K : Subgroup H) (χ : H →* kˣ) (z : H → k)
    (hz : ∀ s t, z (s * t) = z s + χ s * z t) : Subgroup H where
  carrier := {g | g ∈ K ∧ z g = 0}
  one_mem' := ⟨K.one_mem, by simpa using hz 1 1⟩
  mul_mem' {a b} ha hb := ⟨K.mul_mem ha.1 hb.1, by rw [hz, ha.2, hb.2, mul_zero, add_zero]⟩
  inv_mem' {a} ha := ⟨K.inv_mem ha.1, by
    have h1 : z 1 = 0 := by simpa using hz 1 1
    have h2 := hz a⁻¹ a
    rw [inv_mul_cancel, h1, ha.2, mul_zero, add_zero] at h2
    exact h2.symm⟩

private theorem chZero_normal {H k : Type} [Group H] [Field k] (K : Subgroup H) [hK : K.Normal] (χ : H →* kˣ)
    (hχ : ∀ g ∈ K, χ g = 1) (z : H → k) (hz : ∀ s t, z (s * t) = z s + χ s * z t) : (chZero K χ z hz).Normal :=
  ⟨fun n hn g => ⟨hK.conj_mem n hn.1 g, by
    have h1 : z 1 = 0 := by simpa using hz 1 1
    have h2 := hz g g⁻¹
    rw [mul_inv_cancel, h1] at h2
    rw [hz, hz, hn.2, map_mul, hχ n hn.1, mul_one, mul_zero, add_zero, ← h2]⟩⟩

private theorem ch_card {k V : Type} [Field k] [Finite k] [AddCommGroup V] [Module k V] {n : ℕ} (h : Module.rank k V ≤ n) :
    Finite V ∧ Nat.card V ≤ Nat.card k ^ n := by
  haveI : Module.Finite k V := Module.rank_lt_aleph0_iff.1 (h.trans_lt Cardinal.natCast_lt_aleph0)
  refine ⟨Module.finite_of_finite k, ?_⟩
  rw [Module.natCard_eq_pow_finrank (K := k) (V := V)]
  exact Nat.pow_le_pow_right Nat.card_pos (Module.finrank_le_of_rank_le h)

private theorem ch_rank {k V : Type} [Field k] [Finite k] [AddCommGroup V] [Module k V] [Finite V] {n : ℕ}
    (h : Nat.card V ≤ Nat.card k ^ n) : Module.rank k V ≤ n := by
  haveI : Module.Finite k V := Module.Finite.of_finite
  rw [Module.natCard_eq_pow_finrank (K := k) (V := V)] at h
  rw [← Module.finrank_eq_rank]
  exact Nat.cast_le.2 ((Nat.pow_le_pow_iff_right Finite.one_lt_card).1 h)

private theorem ch_unr {F Ω : Type} [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] [IsAlgClosure F Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime]
    (k : Type) [Field k] [Finite k] [CharP k l] (K : Subgroup (G F Ω)) [K.Normal] (ho : IsOpen (K : Set (G F Ω)))
    (hi : ¬ l ∣ K.index) (χ : G F Ω →* kˣ) (hχ : ∀ g ∈ K, χ g = 1) (C₀ : ℕ) (hC : clRk l K ≤ C₀) (Y : Type)
    (f : Y → G F Ω → k) (h1 : ∀ y, IsLocallyConstant (f y)) (h2 : ∀ y s t, f y (s * t) = f y s + χ s * f y t)
    (h3 : ∀ y (A : ValuationSubring Ω), ∀ g ∈ Ine (F := F) A ⊓ K, f y g = 0)
    (h4 : ∀ a b m, (∀ g, f a g - f b g = χ g * m - m) → a = b) : Nat.card Y ≤ Nat.card k ^ C₀ := by
  let N : Subgroup (G F Ω) := Subgroup.normalClosure (⋃ A : ValuationSubring Ω, (Ine (F := F) A ⊓ K : Subgroup (G F Ω)))
  have hN : ∀ y, N ≤ chZero K χ (f y) (h2 y) := fun y => by
    haveI := chZero_normal K χ hχ (f y) (h2 y)
    exact Subgroup.normalClosure_le_normal (Set.iUnion_subset fun A g hg => ⟨hg.2, h3 y A g hg⟩)
  have hNK : N ≤ K := Subgroup.normalClosure_le_normal (Set.iUnion_subset fun A g hg => hg.2)
  let κ : G F Ω ⧸ N →* kˣ := QuotientGroup.lift N χ fun g hg => hχ g (hNK hg)
  let loc : ∀ _ : Empty, G F Ω ⧸ N →* G F Ω ⧸ N := fun _ => MonoidHom.id _
  have hr := strGen F Ω l k ⊤ K le_top ho (by rwa [Subgroup.relIndex_top_right]) (G F Ω ⧸ N)
    ((QuotientGroup.mk' N).comp (⊤ : Subgroup (G F Ω)).subtype) (QuotientGroup.continuous_mk.comp continuous_subtype_val)
    ((QuotientGroup.mk'_surjective N).comp fun g => ⟨⟨g, trivial⟩, rfl⟩) Empty (fun _ => G F Ω ⧸ N) loc κ
    (fun u hu => hχ u.1 hu) (fun A u ha hb => Or.inl ((QuotientGroup.eq_one_iff _).2
      (Subgroup.subset_normalClosure (Set.mem_iUnion.2 ⟨A, ha, hb⟩))))
  rw [Nat.card_of_isEmpty, Cardinal.lift_id, Nat.cast_zero, add_zero] at hr
  obtain ⟨hfin, hcard⟩ := ch_card (hr.trans hC)
  let z : Y → G F Ω ⧸ N → k := fun y => Quotient.lift (f y) fun a b hab => by
    have e := h2 y a (a⁻¹ * b)
    rw [(hN y (QuotientGroup.leftRel_apply.1 hab)).2, mul_inv_cancel_left, mul_zero, add_zero] at e
    exact e.symm
  have hz : ∀ y, (z y, (0 : Empty → k)) ∈ Z1C loc (chRep κ) fun _ => ⊥ := fun y =>
    ⟨fun s => (QuotientGroup.isQuotientMap_mk N).isOpen_preimage.1 (h1 y s), fun s t => by
      obtain ⟨s, rfl⟩ := QuotientGroup.mk_surjective s
      obtain ⟨t, rfl⟩ := QuotientGroup.mk_surjective t
      exact h2 y s t, fun v => v.elim⟩
  refine (Nat.card_le_card_of_injective (fun y => (Submodule.Quotient.mk ⟨_, hz y⟩ : H1C loc (chRep κ) fun _ => ⊥))
    fun a b hab => ?_).trans hcard
  obtain ⟨m, hm, -⟩ := (Submodule.Quotient.eq _).1 hab
  exact h4 a b m fun g => hm (QuotientGroup.mk g)

private theorem ch_trace (R k : Type) [Field R] [Field k] [Finite k] [Algebra R k] :
    ∃ τ : k ≃+ TDual R k, ∀ a m, τ a m = Algebra.trace R k (a * m) := by
  haveI : Finite R := Finite.of_injective _ (algebraMap R k).injective
  haveI : Module.Finite R k := Module.Finite.of_finite
  exact ⟨((Algebra.traceForm R k).toDual (traceForm_nondegenerate R k)).toAddEquiv, fun a m => rfl⟩

open scoped Classical in
private theorem ch_fix {k I T : Type} [Field k] [Finite k] (u : I → kˣ) (e : T → k) (he : Function.Injective e)
    (h : ∀ t i, u i * e t = e t) : Nat.card T ≤ Nat.card k ^ (if ∀ i, u i = 1 then 1 else 0) := by
  split_ifs with hu
  · rw [pow_one]
    exact Nat.card_le_card_of_injective e he
  · obtain ⟨i, hi⟩ := not_forall.1 hu
    have h0 : ∀ t, e t = 0 := fun t => by
      by_contra h'
      exact hi (Units.ext (mul_right_cancel₀ h' ((h t i).trans (one_mul _).symm)))
    haveI : Subsingleton T := ⟨fun a b => he ((h0 a).trans (h0 b).symm)⟩
    rw [pow_zero]
    exact Finite.card_le_one_iff_subsingleton.2 inferInstance

theorem UpperTheory.V.GC.character_H1_galois_quotient :
    Hypotheses₂ → ∀ (F Ω : Type) [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] [IsAlgClosure F Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime], IsTotallyReal F → ∀ (S : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))) (K : Subgroup (G F Ω)) (Γ : Type) [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [IsTopologicalGroup Γ] [CompactSpace Γ] [T2Space Γ] [TotallyDisconnectedSpace Γ] (π : G F Ω →* Γ), GLink l S K π → ∀ (k : Type) [Field k] [Finite k] [CharP k l] (κ : Γ →* kˣ), (∀ g ∈ (K ⊓ (cycMod F Ω l 1).ker), κ (π g) = 1) →
    ∀ c : InfinitePlace F → G F Ω, (∀ w, IsCCAt w (c w)) → ∀ (A : S → ValuationSubring Ω), (∀ v, IsPlaceOver (A v) v.1) → ∀ C₀ : ℕ, clRk l (K ⊓ (cycMod F Ω l 1).ker) ≤ C₀ →
    ∀ (V : Type) (Gv : V → Type) [∀ v, Group (Gv v)] [∀ v, TopologicalSpace (Gv v)] (loc : ∀ v, Gv v →* Γ),
    Module.rank k (H1C loc (chRep κ) fun _ => ⊤) + (Nat.card {w // κ (π (c w)) = 1} : Cardinal) ≤
      ((Nat.card {_u : Unit // κ = 1} + C₀ + finrank ℚ F + Nat.card {v : S // ∀ g ∈ Dec (F := F) (A v), κ (π g) = cycF F Ω l k g} : ℕ) : Cardinal) := by
  intro P F Ω _ _ _ _ _ l hl hF S K Γ _ _ _ _ _ _ π h k _ _ hch κ hκ c hc A hA C₀ hC V Gv _ _ loc
  classical
  obtain ⟨S', p, hSS, hp, hps, hπ, -⟩ := levelS F Ω l S K Γ π h
  have hπ' : ∀ g, p (toGS Ω _ g) = π g := fun g => DFunLike.congr_fun hπ g
  have hS' : ContainsAbove S' l := fun v hv => hSS (h.above v hv)
  obtain ⟨μ, hμ⟩ := GlobalTheory.Published.PoitouTate.exists_lift_cycMod (Ω := Ω) (r := 1) hS'
  have hμ' : ∀ g, μ (toGS Ω _ g) = cycMod F Ω l 1 g := fun g => DFunLike.congr_fun hμ g
  haveI := h.normal
  haveI : Fact (l ^ 1).Prime := ⟨by rw [pow_one]; exact hl.out⟩
  letI : Algebra (ZMod (l ^ 1)) k := @ZMod.algebra k _ (l ^ 1) (by rwa [pow_one])
  letI : TopologicalSpace k := ⊥
  haveI : DiscreteTopology k := ⟨rfl⟩
  have ho : IsOpen ((K ⊓ (cycMod F Ω l 1).ker : Subgroup (G F Ω)) : Set (G F Ω)) :=
    h.isOpen.inter ((isOpen_discrete {1}).preimage GlobalTheory.Published.PoitouTate.continuous_cycMod)
  have hi := ch_idx l K h.idx (cycMod F Ω l 1)
  let ω : GS Ω (S' : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))) →* kˣ := (Units.map (algebraMap (ZMod (l ^ 1)) k).toMonoidHom).comp μ
  have hω : ∀ g, ω (toGS Ω _ g) = cycF F Ω l k g := fun g => Units.ext (by
    show algebraMap (ZMod (l ^ 1)) k (μ (toGS Ω _ g)) = _
    rw [hμ']
    exact RingHom.congr_fun (Subsingleton.elim (algebraMap (ZMod (l ^ 1)) k)
      (ZMod.castHom (dvd_pow_self l one_ne_zero) k)) _)
  let κ' : GS Ω (S' : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))) →* kˣ := (κ.comp p)⁻¹ * ω
  have hκ' : ∀ g, κ' (toGS Ω _ g) = (κ (π g))⁻¹ * cycF F Ω l k g := fun g => by
    show (κ (p (toGS Ω _ g)))⁻¹ * ω (toGS Ω _ g) = _
    rw [hπ', hω]
  have hK1 : ∀ g ∈ K ⊓ (cycMod F Ω l 1).ker, κ' (toGS Ω _ g) = 1 := fun g hg => by
    rw [hκ', hκ g hg, cycF, MonoidHom.comp_apply, show cycMod F Ω l 1 g = 1 from hg.2, map_one, inv_one, mul_one]
  let ρ : Representation (ZMod (l ^ 1)) (GS Ω (S' : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F)))) k := Loc.resCoef (ZMod (l ^ 1)) (chRep κ')
  have hρa : ∀ g m, ρ g m = κ' g * m := fun _ _ => rfl
  have hρ : ContAction ρ := by
    have h1 : IsLocallyConstant fun g => κ' (toGS Ω _ g) := by
      rw [IsLocallyConstant.iff_exists_open]
      intro g
      refine ⟨(fun x => g⁻¹ * x) ⁻¹' (K ⊓ (cycMod F Ω l 1).ker : Subgroup (G F Ω)), ho.preimage (by fun_prop),
        by simp, fun x hx => ?_⟩
      have := hK1 _ hx
      rw [map_mul, map_mul, map_inv, map_inv, inv_mul_eq_one] at this
      exact this.symm
    have h2 : IsLocallyConstant κ' := fun s => (QuotientGroup.isQuotientMap_mk _).isOpen_preimage.1 (h1 s)
    exact (continuous_of_discreteTopology (f := fun q : k × k => q.1 * q.2)).comp
      (((h2.comp Units.val).continuous.comp continuous_fst).prodMk continuous_snd)
  obtain ⟨τ, hτ⟩ := ch_trace (ZMod (l ^ 1)) k
  have hτe : ∀ g a, twDual μ ρ g (τ a) = τ (κ (p g) * a) := fun g a => TDual.ext fun m => by
    rw [twDual_apply, hτ, hτ, hρa, ← smul_eq_mul, ← map_smul, Algebra.smul_def]
    congr 1
    have e : κ' g⁻¹ * ω g = κ (p g) := by
      show (κ (p g⁻¹))⁻¹ * ω g⁻¹ * ω g = _
      rw [map_inv, map_inv, map_inv, inv_inv, inv_mul_cancel_right]
    rw [← e, Units.val_mul]
    show (ω g : k) * _ = _
    ring
  let A' : S' → ValuationSubring Ω := fun v => if hv : v.1 ∈ S then A ⟨v.1, hv⟩ else placeOf Ω v.1
  have hA' : ∀ v, IsPlaceOver (A' v) v.1 := fun v => by
    by_cases hv : v.1 ∈ S
    · simp only [A', dif_pos hv]
      exact hA ⟨v.1, hv⟩
    · simp only [A', dif_neg hv]
      exact isPlaceOver_placeOf Ω v.1
  choose s hs using Submodule.Quotient.mk_surjective
    ((continuousCoboundaries1 (chRep κ) fun _ : V => (⊤ : Submodule k k)).comap (Z1C loc (chRep κ) fun _ => ⊤).subtype)
  let f : (H1C loc (chRep κ) fun _ => ⊤) → Z1 (twDual μ ρ) := fun x => ⟨fun g => τ ((s x).1.1 (p g)),
    (((s x).2.1.comp_continuous hp).comp τ).continuous, fun a b => by
      beta_reduce
      rw [map_mul p, (s x).2.2.1, map_add, hτe, chRep_apply]⟩
  obtain ⟨hfin, hle⟩ := gwTame P l h.odd S S' h.above hS' A' hA' _ ho hi k ρ hρ μ hμ
    (fun g hg => ⟨LinearMap.ext fun m => by rw [hρa, hK1 g hg, Units.val_one, one_mul]; rfl, (hμ' g).trans hg.2⟩) c hc _ f
    (fun a b hab => by
      obtain ⟨φ, hφ⟩ := (mem_coboundaries1 _ _).1 ((Submodule.Quotient.eq _).1 hab)
      obtain ⟨m, rfl⟩ := τ.surjective φ
      rw [← hs a, ← hs b]
      refine (Submodule.Quotient.eq _).2 ⟨m, fun g => ?_, fun _ => trivial⟩
      obtain ⟨g, rfl⟩ := hps g
      have e : τ ((s a).1.1 (p g)) - τ ((s b).1.1 (p g)) = _ := hφ g
      rw [hτe, ← map_sub, ← map_sub] at e
      exact τ.injective e)
    (fun x q hq g hg => by
      show τ ((s x).1.1 (p (toGS Ω _ g))) = 0
      have e : π g = 1 := by
        rw [← MonoidHom.mem_ker, h.ker]
        refine Subgroup.le_topologicalClosure _ (Subgroup.subset_normalClosure ?_)
        simp only [Set.mem_iUnion]
        exact ⟨q.1, hq, A' q, hA' q, hg.1, hg.2.1⟩
      have e1 : (s x).1.1 1 = 0 := by simpa using (s x).2.2.1 1 1
      rw [hπ', e, e1, map_zero])
  have b1 : Nat.card {y : H1 ρ // ∃ z : Z1 ρ, Submodule.Quotient.mk z = y ∧
      ∀ v : S', ∀ g ∈ Ine (F := F) (A' v) ⊓ (K ⊓ (cycMod F Ω l 1).ker), z.1 (toGS Ω _ g) = 0} ≤ Nat.card k ^ C₀ := by
    refine ch_unr l k _ ho hi (κ'.comp (toGS Ω _)) hK1 C₀ hC _ (fun y g => y.2.choose.1 (toGS Ω _ g))
      (fun y => ((IsLocallyConstant.iff_continuous _).2 y.2.choose.2.1).comp_continuous (continuous_toGS Ω _))
      (fun y s t => by rw [map_mul, y.2.choose.2.2]; rfl) (fun y B g hg => ?_) fun a b m hm => ?_
    · have hz : ∀ s t, y.2.choose.1 (toGS Ω _ (s * t)) = y.2.choose.1 (toGS Ω _ s) +
          (κ'.comp (toGS Ω _)) s * y.2.choose.1 (toGS Ω _ t) := fun s t => by rw [map_mul, y.2.choose.2.2]; rfl
      have h1 := (chZero (K ⊓ (cycMod F Ω l 1).ker) _ (fun g => y.2.choose.1 (toGS Ω _ g)) hz).one_mem
      obtain ⟨p1, p2, -⟩ := Base.places F Ω l B
      by_cases hB : B = ⊤
      · have e : g = 1 := Subgroup.mem_bot.1 ((p2 hB).1 ▸ hg.1)
        rw [e]
        exact h1.2
      obtain ⟨v, hv⟩ := p1 hB
      by_cases hvS : v ∈ S'
      · obtain ⟨σ, rfl⟩ := exists_smul_eq_of_isPlaceOver (hA' ⟨v, hvS⟩) hv
        have h2 : σ⁻¹ * g * σ ∈ K ⊓ (cycMod F Ω l 1).ker := Subgroup.Normal.conj_mem' inferInstance _ hg.2 _
        have h3 := (chZero_normal (K ⊓ (cycMod F Ω l 1).ker) (κ'.comp (toGS Ω _)) hK1
          (fun g => y.2.choose.1 (toGS Ω _ g)) hz).conj_mem (σ⁻¹ * g * σ)
          ⟨h2, y.2.choose_spec.2 ⟨v, hvS⟩ _ ⟨ch_conj σ _ g hg.1, h2⟩⟩ σ
        rw [show σ * (σ⁻¹ * g * σ) * σ⁻¹ = g by group] at h3
        exact h3.2
      · have e : toGS Ω (S' : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))) g = toGS Ω _ 1 := by
          rw [map_one]
          refine (QuotientGroup.eq_one_iff _).2 (Subgroup.le_topologicalClosure _ (Subgroup.subset_normalClosure ?_))
          simp only [Set.mem_iUnion]
          exact ⟨v, hvS, B, hv, hg.1⟩
        show y.2.choose.1 (toGS Ω _ g) = 0
        rw [e]
        exact h1.2
    · refine Subtype.ext (a.2.choose_spec.1.symm.trans (Eq.trans ?_ b.2.choose_spec.1))
      refine (Submodule.Quotient.eq _).2 ((mem_coboundaries1 _ _).2 ⟨m, fun g => ?_⟩)
      obtain ⟨g, rfl⟩ := QuotientGroup.mk_surjective g
      exact hm g
  have b2 : Nat.card (H0 (twDual μ ρ)) ≤ Nat.card k ^ Nat.card {_u : Unit // κ = 1} := by
    refine (ch_fix (fun g => κ (p g)) (fun t : H0 (twDual μ ρ) => τ.symm t.1)
      (τ.symm.injective.comp Subtype.val_injective) fun t g => τ.injective ?_).trans
      (Nat.pow_le_pow_right Nat.card_pos ?_)
    · rw [← hτe, τ.apply_symm_apply]
      exact t.2 g
    · split_ifs with h1
      · haveI : Nonempty {_u : Unit // κ = 1} := ⟨⟨(), MonoidHom.ext fun g => by
          obtain ⟨g, rfl⟩ := hps g
          exact h1 g⟩⟩
        exact Nat.card_pos
      · exact Nat.zero_le _
  have b3 : (∏ v : S', if v.1 ∈ S then Nat.card (H0 (ρ.comp (locFam S' A' v))) else 1) ≤
      Nat.card k ^ Nat.card {v : S // ∀ g ∈ Dec (F := F) (A v), κ (π g) = cycF F Ω l k g} := by
    let Q : S' → Prop := fun v => v.1 ∈ S ∧ ∀ g ∈ Dec (F := F) (A' v), κ (π g) = cycF F Ω l k g
    have h1 : Nat.card {v // Q v} ≤ Nat.card {v : S // ∀ g ∈ Dec (F := F) (A v), κ (π g) = cycF F Ω l k g} :=
      Nat.card_le_card_of_injective (fun v => ⟨⟨v.1.1, v.2.1⟩, by
        have := v.2.2
        simp only [A', dif_pos v.2.1] at this
        exact this⟩) fun a b hab => Subtype.ext (Subtype.ext (Subtype.mk.inj (Subtype.mk.inj hab)))
    refine le_trans ?_ (Nat.pow_le_pow_right Nat.card_pos h1)
    rw [Nat.card_eq_fintype_card (α := {v // Q v}), Fintype.card_subtype, Finset.card_filter,
      ← Finset.prod_pow_eq_pow_sum]
    refine Finset.prod_le_prod' fun v _ => ?_
    by_cases hv : v.1 ∈ S
    · rw [if_pos hv]
      refine (ch_fix (fun g : Dec (F := F) (A' v) => κ' (toGS Ω _ g.1)) (fun t : H0 (ρ.comp (locFam S' A' v)) => t.1)
        Subtype.val_injective fun t g => t.2 g).trans (Nat.pow_le_pow_right Nat.card_pos ?_)
      split_ifs with h2 h3
      · exact le_rfl
      · exact (h3 ⟨hv, fun g hg => inv_mul_eq_one.1 ((hκ' g).symm.trans (h2 ⟨g, hg⟩))⟩).elim
      all_goals exact Nat.zero_le _
    · rw [if_neg hv]
      exact Nat.one_le_pow _ _ Nat.card_pos
  have b4 : archH0Card S' ρ c ≤ Nat.card k ^ Nat.card {w // ¬ κ (π (c w)) = 1} := by
    rw [archH0Card, IsTotallyReal.nrComplexPlaces_eq_zero, pow_zero, mul_one, Nat.card_eq_fintype_card (α := {w // ¬ κ (π (c w)) = 1}),
      Fintype.card_subtype, Finset.card_filter, ← Finset.prod_pow_eq_pow_sum,
      Finset.filter_true_of_mem fun w _ => hF.isReal w]
    refine Finset.prod_le_prod' fun w _ => ?_
    refine (ch_fix (fun _ : Unit => κ' (toGS Ω _ (c w))) (fun t : {m : k // ρ (toGS Ω _ (c w)) m = m} => t.1)
      Subtype.val_injective fun t _ => t.2).trans (Nat.pow_le_pow_right Nat.card_pos ?_)
    by_cases h2 : ∀ _ : Unit, κ' (toGS Ω _ (c w)) = 1
    · rw [if_pos h2, if_pos]
      intro h3
      obtain ⟨j, hj, -⟩ := hc w
      have h4 : cycZ F Ω l (c w) = -1 := IsConjOm.cyc (E := F) (show IsConjOm (c w).toRingEquiv from ⟨j, hj⟩)
      have h5 := h2 ()
      rw [hκ', h3, inv_one, one_mul] at h5
      have h6 : (ZMod.castHom (dvd_pow_self l one_ne_zero) k)
          ((PadicInt.toZModPow 1 : ℤ_[l] →+* ZMod (l ^ 1)) (cycZ F Ω l (c w) : ℤ_[l]ˣ)) = 1 := congrArg Units.val h5
      rw [h4, Units.val_neg, Units.val_one, map_neg, map_one, map_neg, map_one] at h6
      have h7 : ((2 : ℕ) : k) = 0 := by
        push_cast
        linear_combination -h6
      exact h.odd ((Nat.prime_dvd_prime_iff_eq hl.out Nat.prime_two).1 ((@CharP.cast_eq_zero_iff k _ l hch 2).1 h7))
    · rw [if_neg h2]
      exact Nat.zero_le _
  have b5 : Nat.card {w // ¬ κ (π (c w)) = 1} + Nat.card {w // κ (π (c w)) = 1} = finrank ℚ F := by
    rw [Nat.card_eq_fintype_card, Nat.card_eq_fintype_card, Fintype.card_subtype_compl,
      Nat.sub_add_cancel (Fintype.card_subtype_le _), InfinitePlace.card_eq_nrRealPlaces_add_nrComplexPlaces,
      ← InfinitePlace.card_add_two_mul_card_eq_rank, IsTotallyReal.nrComplexPlaces_eq_zero]
  have b6 := hle.trans (Nat.mul_le_mul (Nat.mul_le_mul (Nat.mul_le_mul b1 b2) b3) b4)
  rw [← pow_add, ← pow_add, ← pow_add] at b6
  refine (add_le_add_left (ch_rank b6) _).trans ?_
  rw [← Nat.cast_add, Nat.cast_le]
  omega


[LFC_SRC_LEVEL_S] Exact finite ramification quotient and normal-closure kernel
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/GaloisCochains/LevelS.lean#L1-L87
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisCochains.GLinkQuot
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.SelmerTransport.RamFinite
import Development.AbelianVarietyTheory.Proofs.InertiaEigenvalues.LengthCyclotomicBlockCocyclesLeSha

open NumberField IsDedekindDomain Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal Module UpperTheory.V UpperTheory.V.Fr UpperTheory.V.GC

private theorem ine_le_open (F Ω : Type) [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] [IsAlgClosure F Ω]
    (K : Subgroup (G F Ω)) (hK : IsOpen (K : Set (G F Ω))) :
    ∃ T : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F)), ∀ v, v ∉ T → ∀ A : ValuationSubring Ω, IsPlaceOver A v →
      Ine (F := F) A ≤ K := by
  obtain ⟨E, hE, hEK⟩ := (krullTopology_mem_nhds_one_iff F Ω (K : Set (G F Ω))).1 (hK.mem_nhds K.one_mem)
  haveI : NumberField E := NumberField.of_module_finite F E
  haveI : IsAlgClosed Ω := IsAlgClosure.isAlgClosed F
  haveI : IsAlgClosure E Ω := ⟨inferInstance, Algebra.IsAlgebraic.tower_top (K := F) E⟩
  obtain ⟨T, hT⟩ := (Base.ramFinite F F E Ω).1
  refine ⟨T, fun v hv A hA g hg => hEK ?_⟩
  have h := hT v hv A hA (show g ∈ Base.galOf F F Ω ⊓ Ine (F := F) A from ⟨fun x => g.commutes x, hg⟩)
  exact (IntermediateField.mem_fixingSubgroup_iff E g).2 fun x hx => h ⟨x, hx⟩

theorem UpperTheory.V.GC.levelS :
    ∀ (F Ω : Type) [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] [IsAlgClosure F Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (S : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))) (K : Subgroup (G F Ω)) (Γ : Type) [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [IsTopologicalGroup Γ] [CompactSpace Γ] [T2Space Γ] [TotallyDisconnectedSpace Γ] (π : G F Ω →* Γ), GLink l S K π →
    ∃ (S' : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))) (p : GS Ω (S' : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))) →* Γ), S ⊆ S' ∧ Continuous p ∧ Function.Surjective p ∧ p.comp (toGS Ω _) = π ∧ ∀ A : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F) → ValuationSubring Ω, (∀ v, IsPlaceOver (A v) v) →
    p.ker = (Subgroup.normalClosure (⋃ q : {v // v ∈ S' ∧ v ∉ S}, (((Ine (F := F) (A q.1) ⊓ K).map (toGS Ω (S' : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F)))) : Subgroup _) : Set (GS Ω (S' : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))))))).topologicalClosure := by
  intro F Ω _ _ _ _ _ l _ S K Γ _ _ _ _ _ _ π h
  classical
  obtain ⟨T, hT⟩ := ine_le_open F Ω K h.isOpen
  have hgen : ∀ v ∉ S, ∀ B : ValuationSubring Ω, IsPlaceOver B v → ∀ g ∈ Ine (F := F) B, g ∈ K → π g = 1 := by
    intro v hv B hB g hg hgK
    rw [← MonoidHom.mem_ker, h.ker]
    refine Subgroup.le_topologicalClosure _ (Subgroup.subset_normalClosure ?_)
    simp only [Set.mem_iUnion]
    exact ⟨v, hv, B, hB, hg, hgK⟩
  have hcl : IsClosed (π.ker : Set (G F Ω)) := isClosed_singleton.preimage h.cont
  have hNS : NS Ω ((S ∪ T : Finset _) : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))) ≤ π.ker := by
    refine Subgroup.topologicalClosure_minimal _ (Subgroup.normalClosure_le_normal ?_) hcl
    intro g hg
    simp only [Set.mem_iUnion] at hg
    obtain ⟨v, hv, B, hB, hg⟩ := hg
    exact hgen v (fun h' => hv (Finset.mem_union_left _ h')) B hB g hg
      (hT v (fun h' => hv (Finset.mem_union_right _ h')) B hB hg)
  have hp : Continuous (QuotientGroup.lift _ π hNS) :=
    (QuotientGroup.isQuotientMap_mk _).continuous_iff.2 h.cont
  refine ⟨S ∪ T, QuotientGroup.lift _ π hNS, Finset.subset_union_left, hp, fun y => ?_, rfl, fun A hA => ?_⟩
  · obtain ⟨g, rfl⟩ := h.onto y
    exact ⟨toGS Ω _ g, rfl⟩
  generalize hN : (Subgroup.normalClosure (⋃ q : {v // v ∈ S ∪ T ∧ v ∉ S}, (((Ine (F := F) (A q.1) ⊓ K).map
    (toGS Ω ((S ∪ T : Finset _) : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F)))) : Subgroup _) :
      Set (GS Ω ((S ∪ T : Finset _) : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))))))).topologicalClosure = N
  haveI hNn : N.Normal := by
    rw [← hN]
    exact Subgroup.is_normal_topologicalClosure _
  have hNc : IsClosed (N : Set (GS Ω ((S ∪ T : Finset _) : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))))) := by
    rw [← hN]
    exact Subgroup.isClosed_topologicalClosure _
  apply le_antisymm
  · have key : π.ker ≤ N.comap (toGS Ω _) := by
      rw [h.ker]
      refine Subgroup.topologicalClosure_minimal _ (Subgroup.normalClosure_le_normal ?_)
        (hNc.preimage QuotientGroup.continuous_mk)
      intro g hg
      simp only [Set.mem_iUnion] at hg
      obtain ⟨v, hv, B, hB, hg, hgK⟩ := hg
      by_cases hvS : v ∈ S ∪ T
      · obtain ⟨u, rfl⟩ := exists_smul_eq_of_isPlaceOver (F := F) (hA v) hB
        have hm : u⁻¹ * g * u ∈ N.comap (toGS Ω _) := by
          rw [← hN]
          refine Subgroup.le_topologicalClosure _ (Subgroup.subset_normalClosure ?_)
          exact Set.mem_iUnion.2 ⟨⟨v, hvS, hv⟩,
            Subgroup.mem_map_of_mem _ ⟨GlobalTheory.Cut.ShaBound.conj_mem_Ine' hg, h.normal.conj_mem' g hgK u⟩⟩
        have := (hNn.comap (toGS Ω _)).conj_mem _ hm u
        simpa [mul_assoc] using this
      · have h1 : toGS Ω ((S ∪ T : Finset _) : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))) g = 1 := by
          refine (QuotientGroup.eq_one_iff g).2 ?_
          refine Subgroup.le_topologicalClosure _ (Subgroup.subset_normalClosure ?_)
          simp only [Set.mem_iUnion]
          exact ⟨v, hvS, B, hB, hg⟩
        show toGS Ω _ g ∈ N
        rw [h1]
        exact N.one_mem
    intro x hx
    obtain ⟨g, rfl⟩ := QuotientGroup.mk_surjective x
    exact key hx
  · rw [← hN]
    refine Subgroup.topologicalClosure_minimal _ (Subgroup.normalClosure_le_normal ?_) (isClosed_singleton.preimage hp)
    intro x hx
    obtain ⟨q, g, hg, rfl⟩ := Set.mem_iUnion.1 hx
    exact hgen q.1 q.2.2 _ (hA q.1) g hg.1 hg.2


[LFC_SRC_TAME_EQ] Equivariant inertia correction and extension of cochains
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/GaloisCochains/TameEq.lean#L1-L188
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisCochains.GLinkQuot
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisCochains.IsClosedOfClosedFiniteIndex
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisCochains.DecZhat
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisCochains.LocEuler
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.LevelCohomology.HcUrIndex
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.LevelCohomology.HcUrAbs
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.SelmerTransport.Places
import Mathlib.Topology.Algebra.ClopenNhdofOne

open NumberField IsDedekindDomain Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal Module UpperTheory.V UpperTheory.V.Fr UpperTheory.V.GC

private theorem tameEq_cochain {k Γ M : Type} [CommRing k] [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [IsTopologicalGroup Γ]
    [CompactSpace Γ] [TotallyDisconnectedSpace Γ] [AddCommGroup M] [Module k M] [Finite M] [TopologicalSpace M]
    [DiscreteTopology M] (ρ : Representation k Γ M) (hρ : ContAction ρ) (J : Subgroup Γ) [hJn : J.Normal]
    (hJ : ∀ j ∈ J, ρ j = 1) (f : J → M) (hf : IsLocallyConstant f) (hadd : ∀ a b, f (a * b) = f a + f b) :
    ∃ c : Γ → M, IsLocallyConstant c ∧ ∀ g (j : J), c (g * j) = c g + ρ g (f j) := by
  classical
  have f1 : f 1 = 0 := by
    have e := hadd 1 1
    rw [mul_one] at e
    exact left_eq_add.1 e
  obtain ⟨W, hWo, hW⟩ := isOpen_induced_iff.1 (hf {0})
  have hKo : IsOpen (⋂ m : M, (fun g : Γ => ρ g m) ⁻¹' {m}) :=
    isOpen_iInter_of_finite fun m =>
      (isOpen_discrete _).preimage (hρ.comp (continuous_id.prodMk continuous_const))
  have hW1 : (1 : Γ) ∈ W := by
    have : (1 : J) ∈ Subtype.val ⁻¹' W := by rw [hW]; exact f1
    exact this
  obtain ⟨U, hU⟩ := ProfiniteGrp.exist_openNormalSubgroup_sub_open_nhds_of_one (hWo.inter hKo)
    ⟨hW1, by simp⟩
  have hUρ : ∀ u ∈ U, ρ u = 1 := fun u hu => LinearMap.ext fun m => Set.mem_iInter.1 (hU hu).2 m
  have hUf : ∀ j : J, j.1 ∈ U → f j = 0 := fun j hj => by
    have : j ∈ Subtype.val ⁻¹' W := (hU hj).1
    rw [hW] at this
    exact this
  have hUn : U.toSubgroup.Normal := inferInstance
  let f' : Γ → M := fun g => if h : ∃ j : J, j.1⁻¹ * g ∈ U then f h.choose else 0
  have hf' : ∀ (g : Γ) (j : J), j.1⁻¹ * g ∈ U → f' g = f j := fun g j hj => by
    have h : ∃ j : J, j.1⁻¹ * g ∈ U := ⟨j, hj⟩
    have e : f' g = f h.choose := dif_pos h
    have h2 : (j⁻¹ * h.choose).1 ∈ U := by
      have := mul_mem hj (inv_mem h.choose_spec)
      simpa [mul_assoc] using this
    have e2 := hadd j (j⁻¹ * h.choose)
    rw [mul_inv_cancel_left, hUf _ h2, add_zero] at e2
    rw [e, e2]
  let N : Subgroup Γ := J ⊔ U.toSubgroup
  let r : Γ → Γ := fun g => (g : Γ ⧸ N).out
  have hr : ∀ g, ∃ j : J, j.1⁻¹ * ((r g)⁻¹ * g) ∈ U := fun g => by
    have h1 : (r g)⁻¹ * g ∈ ((J ⊔ U.toSubgroup : Subgroup Γ) : Set Γ) :=
      QuotientGroup.eq.1 (QuotientGroup.out_eq' (g : Γ ⧸ N))
    rw [Subgroup.normal_mul] at h1
    obtain ⟨j, hj, u, hu, e⟩ := h1
    refine ⟨⟨j, hj⟩, ?_⟩
    rw [← e]
    show j⁻¹ * (j * u) ∈ U
    rw [inv_mul_cancel_left]
    exact hu
  have hrN : ∀ g, ∀ n ∈ N, r (g * n) = r g := fun g n hn => by
    simp only [r, QuotientGroup.mk_mul_of_mem g hn]
  refine ⟨fun g => ρ (r g) (f' ((r g)⁻¹ * g)), ?_, fun g j => ?_⟩
  · rw [IsLocallyConstant.iff_exists_open]
    intro g
    refine ⟨(fun x => g⁻¹ * x) ⁻¹' U, U.isOpen.preimage (by fun_prop), by simp, fun x hx => ?_⟩
    have hx' : g⁻¹ * x ∈ U := hx
    have e : r x = r g := by
      have := hrN g _ (Subgroup.mem_sup_right (S := J) (T := U.toSubgroup) hx')
      rwa [mul_inv_cancel_left] at this
    obtain ⟨j, hj⟩ := hr g
    rw [e, hf' _ j hj, hf' _ j (by simpa [mul_assoc] using mul_mem hj hx')]
  · obtain ⟨i, hi⟩ := hr g
    have e : ρ g = ρ (r g) := by
      have h1 : g = r g * (i.1 * (i.1⁻¹ * ((r g)⁻¹ * g))) := by group
      conv_lhs => rw [h1]
      rw [map_mul, map_mul, hJ _ i.2, hUρ _ hi, mul_one, mul_one]
    have h2 : (i * j).1⁻¹ * ((r g)⁻¹ * (g * j)) ∈ U := by
      have h3 : j.1⁻¹ * (i.1⁻¹ * ((r g)⁻¹ * g)) * j.1⁻¹⁻¹ ∈ U := hUn.conj_mem _ hi j.1⁻¹
      simpa [mul_assoc] using h3
    show ρ (r (g * j)) (f' ((r (g * j))⁻¹ * (g * j))) = ρ (r g) (f' ((r g)⁻¹ * g)) + _
    rw [hrN g j (Subgroup.mem_sup_left j.2), hf' _ _ h2, hf' _ _ hi, hadd, map_add, e]

private theorem tameEq_ext {k Γ M : Type} [CommRing k] [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [IsTopologicalGroup Γ]
    [CompactSpace Γ] [TotallyDisconnectedSpace Γ] [AddCommGroup M] [Module k M] [Finite M] [TopologicalSpace M]
    [DiscreteTopology M] (ρ : Representation k Γ M) (hρ : ContAction ρ) (J : Subgroup Γ) [hJn : J.Normal]
    (hJ : ∀ j ∈ J, ρ j = 1)
    (h2 : ∀ z : Z2 ρ, (∀ s t, ∀ j ∈ J, ∀ j' ∈ J, z.1 (s * j, t * j') = z.1 (s, t)) → ∃ b : Γ → M, IsLocallyConstant b ∧
      (∀ s, ∀ j ∈ J, b (s * j) = b s) ∧ ∀ s t, z.1 (s, t) = ρ s (b t) - b (s * t) + b s)
    (f : J → M) (hf : IsLocallyConstant f) (hadd : ∀ a b, f (a * b) = f a + f b)
    (heq : ∀ (d : Γ) (j j' : J), j'.1 = d * j.1 * d⁻¹ → f j' = ρ d (f j)) :
    ∃ x : Z1 ρ, ∀ j : J, x.1 j = f j := by
  obtain ⟨c, hc, hcj⟩ := tameEq_cochain ρ hρ J hJ f hf hadd
  obtain ⟨b, hb, hbj, hbz⟩ := h2 ⟨d1 ρ c, coboundaries2_le_cocycles2 ρ hρ ⟨c, hc.continuous, rfl⟩⟩ (fun s t j hj j' hj' => by
    show ρ (s * j) (c (t * j')) - c (s * j * (t * j')) + c (s * j) = ρ s (c t) - c (s * t) + c s
    have hm : t⁻¹ * j * t ∈ J := by simpa using hJn.conj_mem j hj t⁻¹
    have e : s * j * (t * j') = s * t * ((⟨t⁻¹ * j * t, hm⟩ * ⟨j', hj'⟩ : J) : Γ) := by
      simp [mul_assoc]
    have e2 := heq t ⟨t⁻¹ * j * t, hm⟩ ⟨j, hj⟩ (by simp [mul_assoc])
    rw [e, hcj, hcj s ⟨j, hj⟩, hcj t ⟨j', hj'⟩, hadd, e2]
    simp only [map_mul, map_add, hJ j hj, mul_one, Module.End.mul_apply]
    abel)
  have hz : c - b ∈ Z1 ρ := ⟨hc.continuous.sub hb.continuous, fun s t => by
    have e : ρ s (c t) - c (s * t) + c s = _ := hbz s t
    simp only [Pi.sub_apply, map_sub]
    calc c (s * t) - b (s * t)
        = c s - b s + (ρ s (c t) - ρ s (b t)) -
          ((ρ s (c t) - c (s * t) + c s) - (ρ s (b t) - b (s * t) + b s)) := by abel
      _ = _ := by rw [e, sub_self, sub_zero]⟩
  refine ⟨⟨c - b, hz⟩, fun j => ?_⟩
  have e1 := hz.2 1 1
  have e2 := hcj 1 j
  have e3 := hbj 1 j j.2
  simp only [Pi.sub_apply, one_mul, map_one, Module.End.one_apply] at e1 e2 e3
  show c j - b j = f j
  rw [e2, e3, add_sub_right_comm, left_eq_add.1 e1, zero_add]

open Formalism.GaloisCohomology in
theorem UpperTheory.V.GC.tameEq :
    LocalFiniteness → LocalTateDuality → LocalEulerChar → ∀ (F Ω : Type) [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] [IsAlgClosure F Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F)) (A : ValuationSubring Ω), IsPlaceOver A v → ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 F) ∉ v.asIdeal → ∀ (K : Subgroup (G F Ω)) [K.Normal], IsOpen (K : Set (G F Ω)) → ¬ l ∣ K.index →
    ∀ (k : Type) [Field k] [Finite k] [CharP k l] (M : Type) [AddCommGroup M] [Module k M] [Module.Finite k M] [TopologicalSpace M] [DiscreteTopology M] (ρ : Representation k (G F Ω) M), ContAction ρ → (∀ g ∈ Ine (F := F) A ⊓ K, ρ g = 1) →
    Module.Finite k (eqHom ρ (Dec (F := F) A) (Ine (F := F) A ⊓ K)) ∧ finrank k (eqHom ρ (Dec (F := F) A) (Ine (F := F) A ⊓ K)) = finrank k (H2 (ρ.comp (Dec (F := F) A).subtype)) := by
  intro lf lt le F Ω _ _ _ _ _ l _ v A hA hl K _ hKo hKi k _ _ _ M _ _ _ _ _ ρ hρ hJ
  haveI : IsGalois F Ω := {}
  haveI : Finite M := Module.finite_of_finite k
  haveI : CompactSpace (Dec (F := F) A) := isCompact_iff_compactSpace.1 (isClosed_Dec A).isCompact
  let σ : Representation k (Dec (F := F) A) M := ρ.comp (Dec (F := F) A).subtype
  have hσ : ContAction σ := hρ.comp ((continuous_subtype_val.comp continuous_fst).prodMk continuous_snd)
  let I := (Ine (F := F) A).subgroupOf (Dec (F := F) A)
  let J := (Ine (F := F) A ⊓ K).subgroupOf (Dec (F := F) A)
  haveI hIn : I.Normal := inferInstance
  haveI hJn : J.Normal := ⟨fun n hn g => ⟨hIn.conj_mem n hn.1 g, ‹K.Normal›.conj_mem n.1 hn.2 g.1⟩⟩
  have hJI : J ≤ I := Subgroup.comap_mono inf_le_left
  have hidx : ¬ l ∣ J.relIndex I := by
    rw [Subgroup.relIndex_subgroupOf (Ine_le_Dec _), Subgroup.inf_relIndex_left]
    exact fun h => hKi (h.trans (Subgroup.relIndex_dvd_index_of_normal K _))
  have hJc : IsClosed (J : Set (Dec (F := F) A)) :=
    ((isClosed_Ine A).inter (K.isClosed_of_isOpen hKo)).preimage continuous_subtype_val
  have hJσ : ∀ j ∈ J, σ j = 1 := fun j hj => hJ j.1 hj
  obtain ⟨φ, -, h1, h2⟩ := decZhat F Ω v A hA
  obtain ⟨a1, -, -, a2, -⟩ := locEuler lf lt le F Ω l v A hA k M σ hσ
  have a3 := a2 hl
  have hur : finrank k (H1ur σ J) = finrank k (H0 σ) := by
    rw [X.urIndex l σ hσ I J hJI hidx, X.urAbs σ hσ I φ h1 h2]
  let e : (Ine (F := F) A ⊓ K : Subgroup (G F Ω)) → Dec (F := F) A := fun j => ⟨j.1, Ine_le_Dec A j.2.1⟩
  have he : Continuous e := continuous_subtype_val.subtype_mk _
  let p : Z1 σ →ₗ[k] eqHom ρ (Dec (F := F) A) (Ine (F := F) A ⊓ K) :=
    { toFun := fun z => ⟨fun j => z.1 (e j), (IsLocallyConstant.iff_continuous _).2 (z.2.1.comp he), fun a b => by
          have := z.2.2 (e a) (e b)
          rw [hJσ (e a) a.2] at this
          exact this, fun d hd j j' h => by
          have h3 := z.2.2 (e j') ⟨d, hd⟩
          have h4 := z.2.2 ⟨d, hd⟩ (e j)
          have h5 : e j' * ⟨d, hd⟩ = ⟨d, hd⟩ * e j := Subtype.ext (by simp [e, h])
          rw [hJσ (e j') j'.2, h5, h4, add_comm] at h3
          exact (add_right_cancel h3).symm⟩
      map_add' := fun _ _ => rfl
      map_smul' := fun _ _ => rfl }
  have hp : ∀ z, p z = 0 ↔ ∀ j ∈ J, z.1 j = 0 := fun z =>
    ⟨fun h j hj => congrFun (congrArg Subtype.val h) ⟨j.1, hj⟩, fun h => Subtype.ext (funext fun j => h (e j) j.2)⟩
  let q : H1 σ →ₗ[k] eqHom ρ (Dec (F := F) A) (Ine (F := F) A ⊓ K) :=
    Submodule.liftQ _ p fun z hz => (hp z).2 fun j hj => by
      obtain ⟨m, hm⟩ := (mem_coboundaries1 _ _).1 hz
      have : z.1 j = σ j m - m := hm j
      rw [this, hJσ j hj]
      simp
  have hk : LinearMap.ker q = H1ur σ J := by
    ext x
    obtain ⟨z, rfl⟩ := Submodule.Quotient.mk_surjective _ x
    refine (hp z).trans (Iff.trans ?_ (Submodule.Quotient.mk_eq_zero _).symm)
    refine Iff.trans ?_ (mem_coboundaries1 _ _).symm
    constructor
    · exact fun h => ⟨0, fun j => (h j.1 j.2).trans (by simp)⟩
    · rintro ⟨m, hm⟩ j hj
      have : z.1 j = σ j m - m := hm ⟨j, hj⟩
      rw [this, hJσ j hj]
      simp
  have hq : Function.Surjective q := by
    intro f
    let e' : J → (Ine (F := F) A ⊓ K : Subgroup (G F Ω)) := fun j => ⟨j.1.1, j.2⟩
    have he' : Continuous e' := (continuous_subtype_val.comp continuous_subtype_val).subtype_mk _
    obtain ⟨x, hx⟩ := tameEq_ext σ hσ J hJσ (H2_unramified_inflation l _ k M σ hσ I J φ hJI hJc hidx hJσ h1 h2)
      (fun j => f.1 (e' j)) (f.2.1.comp_continuous he') (fun a b => f.2.2.1 (e' a) (e' b))
      (fun d j j' h => f.2.2.2 d.1 d.2 (e' j) (e' j') (congrArg Subtype.val h))
    exact ⟨Submodule.Quotient.mk x, Subtype.ext (funext fun j => hx ⟨e j, j.2⟩)⟩
  refine ⟨Module.Finite.of_surjective q hq, ?_⟩
  have a4 := q.finrank_range_add_finrank_ker
  rw [LinearMap.range_eq_top.2 hq, finrank_top, hk, hur, a3] at a4
  show _ = finrank k (H2 σ)
  omega


[LFC_SRC_INFL_KER] Actual second-degree inflation defect estimate
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/GaloisCochains/InflKer.lean#L1-L206

open NumberField IsDedekindDomain Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal Module UpperTheory.V UpperTheory.V.Fr UpperTheory.V.GC

namespace UpperTheory.V.GC

private theorem inflKer_lin {k W P H : Type} [Field k] [AddCommGroup W] [Module k W] [AddCommGroup P] [Module k P]
    [AddCommGroup H] [Module k H] [Module.Finite k P] (r : W →ₗ[k] P) (τ : W →ₗ[k] H)
    (h : LinearMap.ker r ≤ LinearMap.ker τ) (U : Submodule k P) (hU : U ≤ (LinearMap.ker τ).map r) :
    Module.Finite k (LinearMap.range τ) ∧ finrank k (LinearMap.range τ) + finrank k U ≤ finrank k P := by
  let K := (LinearMap.ker τ).map r
  let s : W →ₗ[k] P ⧸ K := K.mkQ.comp r
  have hs : LinearMap.ker s = LinearMap.ker τ := by
    rw [LinearMap.ker_comp, Submodule.ker_mkQ, Submodule.comap_map_eq, sup_eq_left.2 h]
  have e : LinearMap.range s ≃ₗ[k] LinearMap.range τ :=
    s.quotKerEquivRange.symm.trans ((Submodule.quotEquivOfEq _ _ hs).trans τ.quotKerEquivRange)
  refine ⟨Module.Finite.equiv e, ?_⟩
  rw [← e.finrank_eq, ← K.finrank_quotient_add_finrank]
  exact add_le_add (Submodule.finrank_le _) (Submodule.finrank_mono hU)

section

variable {Γ' Γ k M : Type} [Group Γ'] [TopologicalSpace Γ'] [IsTopologicalGroup Γ'] [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ]
  [Field k] [AddCommGroup M] [Module k M] [TopologicalSpace M] [DiscreteTopology M]
  (p : Γ' →* Γ) (hp : Continuous p) (ρ : Representation k Γ M)

private def inflW : Submodule k (Z2 ρ × (Γ' → M)) where
  carrier := {x | Continuous x.2 ∧ x.2 1 = 0 ∧ ∀ g h, ρ (p g) (x.2 h) - x.2 (g * h) + x.2 g = x.1.1 (p g, p h)}
  zero_mem' := ⟨continuous_const, rfl, by simp⟩
  add_mem' := by
    rintro x y ⟨a1, a2, a3⟩ ⟨b1, b2, b3⟩
    refine ⟨a1.add b1, by simp [a2, b2], fun g h => ?_⟩
    simp only [Prod.snd_add, Prod.fst_add, Pi.add_apply, map_add, Submodule.coe_add, ← a3, ← b3]
    abel
  smul_mem' := by
    rintro c x ⟨a1, a2, a3⟩
    refine ⟨a1.const_smul c, by simp [a2], fun g h => ?_⟩
    simp only [Prod.smul_snd, Prod.smul_fst, Pi.smul_apply, map_smul, Submodule.coe_smul, ← a3, smul_add, smul_sub]

omit [IsTopologicalGroup Γ'] in
private theorem inflW_mul (x : inflW p ρ) (n : Γ') (hn : p n = 1) (g : Γ') :
    x.1.2 (n * g) = x.1.2 n + x.1.2 g ∧ x.1.2 (g * n) = x.1.2 g + ρ (p g) (x.1.2 n) := by
  obtain ⟨⟨z, β⟩, -, h1, h⟩ := x
  dsimp only at h1 h ⊢
  have a := h 1 g
  have b := h g 1
  have c := h n g
  have d := h g n
  simp only [h1, hn, map_one, one_mul, mul_one, map_zero, Module.End.one_apply] at a b c d
  rw [← a] at c
  rw [← b] at d
  constructor
  · linear_combination (norm := abel1) -c
  · linear_combination (norm := abel1) -d

omit [IsTopologicalGroup Γ'] in
private theorem inflW_conj (x : inflW p ρ) (n : Γ') (hn : p n = 1) (g : Γ') :
    x.1.2 (g * n * g⁻¹) = ρ (p g) (x.1.2 n) := by
  have a := (inflW_mul p ρ x (g * n * g⁻¹) (by simp [hn]) g).1
  have b := (inflW_mul p ρ x n hn g).2
  rw [inv_mul_cancel_right, b, add_comm] at a
  exact (add_right_cancel a).symm

private theorem inflW_desc [CompactSpace Γ'] [T2Space Γ] (hp : Continuous p) (hs : Function.Surjective p)
    (S : Set Γ') (hS : p.ker = (Subgroup.normalClosure S).topologicalClosure) (x : inflW p ρ)
    (hx : ∀ s ∈ S, x.1.2 s = 0) : ∃ b : Γ → M, Continuous b ∧ ∀ g, b (p g) = x.1.2 g := by
  let N : Subgroup Γ' :=
    { carrier := {n | p n = 1 ∧ x.1.2 n = 0}
      one_mem' := ⟨map_one _, x.2.2.1⟩
      mul_mem' := by
        rintro a b ⟨a1, a2⟩ ⟨b1, b2⟩
        exact ⟨by simp [a1, b1], by rw [(inflW_mul p ρ x a a1 b).1, a2, b2, add_zero]⟩
      inv_mem' := by
        rintro a ⟨a1, a2⟩
        refine ⟨by simp [a1], ?_⟩
        have := (inflW_mul p ρ x a a1 a⁻¹).1
        rw [mul_inv_cancel, x.2.2.1, a2, zero_add] at this
        exact this.symm }
  haveI : N.Normal := ⟨fun n hn g => ⟨by simp [hn.1], by rw [inflW_conj p ρ x n hn.1, hn.2, map_zero]⟩⟩
  have hc : IsClosed (N : Set Γ') :=
    (isClosed_eq hp continuous_const).inter (isClosed_eq x.2.1 continuous_const)
  have hN : p.ker ≤ N := by
    rw [hS]
    refine Subgroup.topologicalClosure_minimal _ (Subgroup.normalClosure_le_normal fun s h => ⟨?_, hx s h⟩) hc
    have : s ∈ p.ker := hS ▸ Subgroup.le_topologicalClosure _ (Subgroup.subset_normalClosure h)
    exact this
  have hb : ∀ g, x.1.2 (Function.surjInv hs (p g)) = x.1.2 g := fun g => by
    have h1 : p (g⁻¹ * Function.surjInv hs (p g)) = 1 := by simp [Function.surjInv_eq hs]
    have := (inflW_mul p ρ x _ h1 g).2
    rw [mul_inv_cancel_left, (hN h1).2, map_zero, add_zero] at this
    exact this
  refine ⟨fun γ => x.1.2 (Function.surjInv hs γ), ?_, hb⟩
  rw [(hp.isClosedMap.isQuotientMap hp hs).continuous_iff]
  exact x.2.1.congr fun g => (hb g).symm

private def inflR {T : Type} (Dq Jq : T → Subgroup Γ') (hJ : ∀ q, Jq q ≤ p.ker) :
    inflW p ρ →ₗ[k] ∀ q, eqHom (ρ.comp p) (Dq q) (Jq q) where
  toFun x q := ⟨fun j => x.1.2 j, (IsLocallyConstant.iff_continuous _).2 (x.2.1.comp continuous_subtype_val),
    fun a b => (inflW_mul p ρ x a (hJ q a.2) b).1, fun d _ j j' h => by
      dsimp only
      rw [h]
      exact inflW_conj p ρ x j (hJ q j.2) d⟩
  map_add' _ _ := rfl
  map_smul' _ _ := rfl

private def inflJ : Z1 (ρ.comp p) →ₗ[k] inflW p ρ where
  toFun f := ⟨(0, f.1), f.2.1, by simpa using f.2.2 1 1, fun g h => by
    dsimp only
    rw [f.2.2]
    simp⟩
  map_add' _ _ := by ext <;> simp
  map_smul' _ _ := by ext <;> simp

omit [IsTopologicalGroup Γ'] in
private theorem inflW_range :
    LinearMap.range ((Submodule.mkQ _ : Z2 ρ →ₗ[k] H2 ρ) ∘ₗ LinearMap.fst _ _ _ ∘ₗ (inflW p ρ).subtype) =
      LinearMap.ker (resH2 ρ p hp) := by
  apply le_antisymm
  · rintro _ ⟨x, rfl⟩
    refine (Submodule.Quotient.mk_eq_zero _).2 ((separated_dvr_second_cohomology_mem _ _).2 ⟨x.1.2, x.2.1, ?_⟩)
    funext q
    exact x.2.2.2 q.1 q.2
  · intro y hy
    obtain ⟨z, rfl⟩ := Submodule.Quotient.mk_surjective _ y
    obtain ⟨β, hβ, he⟩ := (separated_dvr_second_cohomology_mem _ _).1 ((Submodule.Quotient.mk_eq_zero _).1 hy)
    have he' : ∀ g h, ρ (p g) (β h) - β (g * h) + β g = z.1 (p g, p h) := fun g h => congrFun he (g, h)
    let c : Z2 ρ := ⟨fun q => ρ q.1 (β 1), ?_, fun g h k => by simp⟩
    · refine ⟨⟨(z - c, fun g => β g - β 1), hβ.sub continuous_const, sub_self _, fun g h => ?_⟩, ?_⟩
      · simp only [c, Submodule.coe_sub, Pi.sub_apply, ← he', map_sub]
        abel
      · refine (Submodule.Quotient.eq _).2 ((separated_dvr_second_cohomology_mem _ _).2 ⟨fun _ => -β 1, continuous_const, ?_⟩)
        funext q
        simp [c, d1]
    · have h1 : β 1 = z.1 (1, 1) := by simpa using he' 1 1
      have h2 : ∀ g, ρ g (z.1 (1, 1)) = z.1 (g, 1) := fun g => by
        have := z.2.2 g 1 1
        simp only [mul_one, sub_add_cancel] at this
        exact sub_eq_zero.1 this
      simp only [h1, h2]
      exact z.2.1.comp (continuous_fst.prodMk continuous_const)

end

end UpperTheory.V.GC

theorem UpperTheory.V.GC.inflKer :
    ∀ (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (Γ' : Type) [Group Γ'] [TopologicalSpace Γ'] [IsTopologicalGroup Γ'] [CompactSpace Γ'] [T2Space Γ'] [TotallyDisconnectedSpace Γ'] (Γ : Type) [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [IsTopologicalGroup Γ] [CompactSpace Γ] [T2Space Γ] [TotallyDisconnectedSpace Γ] (p : Γ' →* Γ) (hp : Continuous p), Function.Surjective p →
    ∀ (k : Type) [Field k] [Finite k] [CharP k l] (M : Type) [AddCommGroup M] [Module k M] [Module.Finite k M] [TopologicalSpace M] [DiscreteTopology M] (ρ : Representation k Γ M), ContAction ρ → ∀ (T : Type) [Fintype T] (Dq Jq : T → Subgroup Γ'), p.ker = (Subgroup.normalClosure (⋃ q, (Jq q : Set Γ'))).topologicalClosure →
    (∀ q, Module.Finite k (eqHom (ρ.comp p) (Dq q) (Jq q))) → Module.Finite k (H1 (ρ.comp p)) →
    Module.Finite k (H1 ρ) ∧ Module.Finite k (LinearMap.ker (resH2 ρ p hp)) ∧
    finrank k (LinearMap.ker (resH2 ρ p hp)) + finrank k (H1 (ρ.comp p)) ≤ finrank k (H1 ρ) + ∑ q, finrank k (eqHom (ρ.comp p) (Dq q) (Jq q)) := by
  intro l _ Γ' _ _ _ _ _ _ Γ _ _ _ _ _ _ p hp hs k _ _ _ M _ _ _ _ _ ρ _ T _ Dq Jq hker hfin hH
  have hJ : ∀ q, Jq q ≤ p.ker := fun q s h =>
    hker ▸ Subgroup.le_topologicalClosure _ (Subgroup.subset_normalClosure (Set.mem_iUnion.2 ⟨q, h⟩))
  let r := inflR p ρ Dq Jq hJ
  have hd : ∀ x, r x = 0 → ∃ b : Γ → M, Continuous b ∧ ∀ g, b (p g) = x.1.2 g := fun x hx =>
    inflW_desc p ρ hp hs _ hker x fun s h => by
      obtain ⟨q, hq⟩ := Set.mem_iUnion.1 h
      exact congrFun (congrArg Subtype.val (congrFun hx q)) ⟨s, hq⟩
  have hinj : Function.Injective (resH1 ρ p hp) := by
    rw [injective_iff_map_eq_zero]
    intro y hy
    obtain ⟨z, rfl⟩ := Submodule.Quotient.mk_surjective _ y
    obtain ⟨m, hm⟩ := (mem_coboundaries1 _ _).1 ((Submodule.Quotient.mk_eq_zero _).1 hy)
    refine (Submodule.Quotient.mk_eq_zero _).2 ((mem_coboundaries1 _ _).2 ⟨m, fun g => ?_⟩)
    obtain ⟨g, rfl⟩ := hs g
    exact hm g
  haveI hF : Module.Finite k (H1 ρ) := Module.Finite.of_injective _ hinj
  let r1 : H1 (ρ.comp p) →ₗ[k] ∀ q, eqHom (ρ.comp p) (Dq q) (Jq q) :=
    Submodule.liftQ _ (r.comp (inflJ p ρ)) fun f hf => by
      obtain ⟨m, hm⟩ := (mem_coboundaries1 _ _).1 hf
      funext q
      refine Subtype.ext (funext fun j => (hm j).trans ?_)
      have : p j = 1 := hJ q j.2
      simp [this]
  have hk1 : LinearMap.ker r1 ≤ LinearMap.range (resH1 ρ p hp) := by
    intro y hy
    obtain ⟨z, rfl⟩ := Submodule.Quotient.mk_surjective _ y
    obtain ⟨b, hb, he⟩ := hd (inflJ p ρ z) hy
    refine ⟨Submodule.Quotient.mk ⟨b, hb, fun g h => ?_⟩, congrArg _ (Subtype.ext (funext he))⟩
    obtain ⟨g, rfl⟩ := hs g
    obtain ⟨h, rfl⟩ := hs h
    rw [← map_mul, he, he, he]
    exact z.2.2 g h
  obtain ⟨h1, h2⟩ := inflKer_lin r
    ((Submodule.mkQ _ : Z2 ρ →ₗ[k] H2 ρ) ∘ₗ LinearMap.fst _ _ _ ∘ₗ (inflW p ρ).subtype) (fun x hx => by
      obtain ⟨b, hb, he⟩ := hd x hx
      refine (Submodule.Quotient.mk_eq_zero _).2 ((separated_dvr_second_cohomology_mem _ _).2 ⟨b, hb, ?_⟩)
      funext ⟨g, h⟩
      obtain ⟨g, rfl⟩ := hs g
      obtain ⟨h, rfl⟩ := hs h
      rw [d1_apply, ← map_mul, he, he, he]
      exact x.2.2.2 g h) (LinearMap.range r1) (by
      rintro _ ⟨y, rfl⟩
      obtain ⟨z, rfl⟩ := Submodule.Quotient.mk_surjective _ y
      exact ⟨inflJ p ρ z, (Submodule.mkQ _).map_zero, rfl⟩)
  rw [inflW_range p hp ρ] at h1 h2
  refine ⟨hF, h1, ?_⟩
  rw [Module.finrank_pi_fintype] at h2
  have h3 := r1.finrank_range_add_finrank_ker
  have h4 := (Submodule.finrank_mono hk1).trans (LinearMap.finrank_range_le _)
  have h5 : finrank k (H1 (ρ.comp p)) ≤ finrank k (LinearMap.range r1) + finrank k (H1 ρ) :=
    h3.symm.le.trans (Nat.add_le_add_left h4 _)
  refine (Nat.add_le_add_left h5 _).trans ?_
  rw [← add_assoc, add_comm]
  exact Nat.add_le_add_left h2 _


[LFC_SRC_LOC_EULER] Local finite-module Euler and duality identities
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/GaloisCochains/LocEuler.lean#L1-L121
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisCochains.GLinkQuot

open NumberField IsDedekindDomain Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal Module UpperTheory.V UpperTheory.V.Fr UpperTheory.V.GC

private noncomputable def subquotEquiv {R R' X : Type*} [CommRing R] [CommRing R'] [AddCommGroup X]
    [Module R X] [Module R' X] (Z B : Submodule R X) (Z' B' : Submodule R' X)
    (hZ : ∀ x, x ∈ Z ↔ x ∈ Z') (hB : ∀ x, x ∈ B ↔ x ∈ B') :
    (Z ⧸ B.comap Z.subtype) ≃ (Z' ⧸ B'.comap Z'.subtype) :=
  Quotient.congr (Equiv.subtypeEquivRight hZ) fun a b => by
    rw [Submodule.quotientRel_def, Submodule.quotientRel_def]
    exact hB _

private theorem card_H0dual {R k Γ Γ' M : Type} [Field R] [Field k] [Finite k] [Algebra R k] [Group Γ] [Group Γ']
    [AddCommGroup M] [Module R M] [Module k M] [IsScalarTower R k M] [Finite M]
    (ρ : Representation k Γ M) (ρ' : Representation R Γ' M) (e : Γ' ≃* Γ) (h : ∀ g m, ρ' g m = ρ (e g) m)
    (χ : Γ →* kˣ) (χ' : Γ' →* Rˣ) (hχ : ∀ g, (χ (e g) : k) = algebraMap R k (χ' g)) :
    Nat.card (Loc.H0dual χ ρ) = Nat.card (Loc.H0dual χ' ρ') := by
  haveI : Finite R := Finite.of_injective _ (algebraMap R k).injective
  let T : Module.Dual k M →+ Module.Dual R M :=
    { toFun := fun f => (Algebra.trace R k).comp (f.restrictScalars R)
      map_zero' := by ext; simp
      map_add' := fun f f' => by ext; simp }
  have hT : ∀ f m, T f m = Algebra.trace R k (f m) := fun _ _ => rfl
  have hinj : Function.Injective T := by
    rw [injective_iff_map_eq_zero]
    intro f hf
    ext m
    refine (traceForm_nondegenerate R k).1 (f m) fun a => ?_
    have := hT f (a • m)
    rw [hf, f.map_smul, smul_eq_mul, mul_comm] at this
    exact this.symm
  haveI : Finite (Module.Dual R M) := DFunLike.finite _
  have hbij : Function.Bijective T := hinj.bijective_of_nat_card_le (by
    rw [Module.natCard_eq_pow_finrank (K := R) (V := Dual R M), Module.natCard_eq_pow_finrank (K := k) (V := Dual k M),
      Subspace.dual_finrank_eq, Subspace.dual_finrank_eq, ← Module.natCard_eq_pow_finrank,
      ← Module.natCard_eq_pow_finrank])
  refine Nat.card_congr ((Equiv.ofBijective _ hbij).subtypeEquiv fun f => ?_)
  rw [Equiv.ofBijective_apply]
  constructor
  · intro hf g x
    rw [hT, hT, h, hf, hχ, ← Algebra.smul_def, map_smul, smul_eq_mul]
  · intro hf g x
    obtain ⟨g, rfl⟩ := e.surjective g
    have : f.comp (ρ (e g)) = (χ (e g) : k) • f := hinj (LinearMap.ext fun y => by
      have := hf g y
      rw [hT, hT, h] at this
      rw [hT, hT, LinearMap.comp_apply, this, LinearMap.smul_apply, smul_eq_mul, hχ, ← Algebra.smul_def,
        map_smul, smul_eq_mul])
    exact LinearMap.congr_fun this x

private theorem card_twDual {R Γ M : Type} [CommRing R] [Group Γ] [AddCommGroup M] [Module R M]
    (χ : Γ →* Rˣ) (ρ : Representation R Γ M) :
    Nat.card (H0 ((twDual χ ρ).comp (MonoidHom.id Γ))) = Nat.card (Loc.H0dual χ ρ) := by
  refine Nat.card_congr (Equiv.subtypeEquivRight (α := TDual R M) fun y => ?_)
  constructor
  · intro hy g m
    have e := congrArg (fun φ : TDual R M => φ (ρ g m)) (hy g)
    simp only [MonoidHom.comp_apply, MonoidHom.id_apply, twDual_apply] at e
    rw [← Module.End.mul_apply, ← map_mul, inv_mul_cancel, map_one, Module.End.one_apply] at e
    exact e.symm
  · intro hy g
    refine TDual.ext fun m => ?_
    simp only [MonoidHom.comp_apply, MonoidHom.id_apply, twDual_apply]
    have := hy g (ρ g⁻¹ m)
    rw [← Module.End.mul_apply, ← map_mul, mul_inv_cancel, map_one, Module.End.one_apply] at this
    exact this.symm

theorem UpperTheory.V.GC.locEuler :
    LocalFiniteness → LocalTateDuality → LocalEulerChar → ∀ (F Ω : Type) [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] [IsAlgClosure F Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F)) (A : ValuationSubring Ω), IsPlaceOver A v → ∀ (k : Type) [Field k] [Finite k] [CharP k l] (M : Type) [AddCommGroup M] [Module k M] [Module.Finite k M] [TopologicalSpace M] [DiscreteTopology M] (ρ : Representation k (Dec (F := F) A) M), ContAction ρ →
    Module.Finite k (H1 ρ) ∧ Module.Finite k (H2 ρ) ∧ (((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 F) ∈ v.asIdeal → finrank k (H1 ρ) = finrank k (H0 ρ) + finrank k (H2 ρ) + locDeg v * finrank k M) ∧
    (((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 F) ∉ v.asIdeal → finrank k (H1 ρ) = finrank k (H0 ρ) + finrank k (H2 ρ)) ∧ finrank k (H2 ρ) = finrank k (Loc.H0dual ((cycF F Ω l k).comp (Dec (F := F) A).subtype) ρ) := by
  intro hF hT hE0 F Ω _ _ _ _ _ l _ w A hAw k _ _ hc M _ _ _ _ _ ρ hρ
  have hl : l.Prime := Fact.out
  haveI : Fact (l ^ 1).Prime := ⟨by rwa [pow_one]⟩
  haveI : CharP k (l ^ 1) := by rwa [pow_one]
  letI : Algebra (ZMod (l ^ 1)) k := ZMod.algebra k (l ^ 1)
  letI : Module (ZMod (l ^ 1)) M := Module.compHom M (algebraMap (ZMod (l ^ 1)) k)
  haveI : IsScalarTower (ZMod (l ^ 1)) k M := IsScalarTower.of_algebraMap_smul fun _ _ => rfl
  haveI : Finite M := Module.finite_of_finite k
  let ρ' : Representation (ZMod (l ^ 1)) ↥(Dec (F := F) A) M := Loc.resCoef (ZMod (l ^ 1)) ρ
  have h : ∀ g m, ρ' g m = ρ (MulEquiv.refl _ g) m := fun _ _ => rfl
  have hρ' : ContAction ρ' := hρ
  have e0 : H0 ρ ≃ H0 ρ' := Equiv.subtypeEquivRight fun _ => Iff.rfl
  have e1 : H1 ρ ≃ H1 ρ' := subquotEquiv _ _ _ _ (fun _ => Iff.rfl) (fun _ => Iff.rfl)
  have e2 : H2 ρ ≃ H2 ρ' := subquotEquiv _ _ _ _ (fun _ => Iff.rfl) (fun _ => Iff.rfl)
  obtain ⟨-, f1, f2, -⟩ := hF F Ω l 1 M A ρ' w one_pos hAw hρ'
  obtain ⟨⟨inv⟩, -, -, -, hb⟩ := hT F Ω l 1 M A ρ' w one_pos hAw hρ'
  have hE := hE0 F Ω l 1 M A ρ' w one_pos hAw hρ'
  haveI : Finite (H1 ρ) := Finite.of_equiv _ e1.symm
  haveI : Finite (H2 ρ) := Finite.of_equiv _ e2.symm
  have hk : 1 < Nat.card k := Finite.one_lt_card
  refine ⟨Module.Finite.of_finite, Module.Finite.of_finite, fun hw => ?_, fun hw => ?_, ?_⟩
  · apply Nat.pow_right_injective hk
    beta_reduce
    rw [pow_add, pow_add, mul_comm (locDeg w), pow_mul, ← Module.natCard_eq_pow_finrank, ← Module.natCard_eq_pow_finrank,
      ← Module.natCard_eq_pow_finrank, ← Module.natCard_eq_pow_finrank, Nat.card_congr e0, Nat.card_congr e1,
      Nat.card_congr e2]
    exact hE.1 hw
  · apply Nat.pow_right_injective hk
    beta_reduce
    rw [pow_add, ← Module.natCard_eq_pow_finrank, ← Module.natCard_eq_pow_finrank,
      ← Module.natCard_eq_pow_finrank, Nat.card_congr e0, Nat.card_congr e1, Nat.card_congr e2]
    exact hE.2 hw
  · apply Nat.pow_right_injective hk
    beta_reduce
    let χ := (cycMod F Ω l 1).comp (Dec (F := F) A).subtype
    haveI : Finite (Module.Dual k M) := DFunLike.finite _
    haveI : Finite (TDual (ZMod (l ^ 1)) M) := (DFunLike.finite _ : Finite (M →ₗ[ZMod (l ^ 1)] ZMod (l ^ 1)))
    let B : Type := H0 ((twDual χ ρ').comp (MonoidHom.id _))
    let E : H2 ρ' ≃ Module.Dual (ZMod (l ^ 1)) B :=
      (Equiv.ofBijective _ hb).trans (LinearEquiv.congrRight inv).toEquiv
    haveI : Finite B := Subtype.finite
    haveI := hc
    have hχ : ∀ g, ((((cycF F Ω l k).comp (Dec (F := F) A).subtype) (MulEquiv.refl _ g) : kˣ) : k) =
        algebraMap (ZMod (l ^ 1)) k (χ g) := fun g =>
      RingHom.congr_fun (Subsingleton.elim (ZMod.castHom (dvd_pow_self l one_ne_zero) k)
        (algebraMap (ZMod (l ^ 1)) k)) _
    rw [← Module.natCard_eq_pow_finrank, ← Module.natCard_eq_pow_finrank, Nat.card_congr e2,
      card_H0dual ρ ρ' (MulEquiv.refl _) h _ χ hχ,
      ← card_twDual, Nat.card_congr E, Module.natCard_eq_pow_finrank (K := ZMod (l ^ 1)),
      Subspace.dual_finrank_eq, ← Module.natCard_eq_pow_finrank]


[LFC_SRC_GLOB_EULER] Global finite-module Euler identity
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/GaloisCochains/GlobEuler.lean#L1-L57
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisCochains.GLinkQuot

open NumberField IsDedekindDomain Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal Module UpperTheory.V UpperTheory.V.Fr UpperTheory.V.GC

private noncomputable def subquotEquiv {R R' X : Type*} [CommRing R] [CommRing R'] [AddCommGroup X]
    [Module R X] [Module R' X] (Z B : Submodule R X) (Z' B' : Submodule R' X)
    (hZ : ∀ x, x ∈ Z ↔ x ∈ Z') (hB : ∀ x, x ∈ B ↔ x ∈ B') :
    (Z ⧸ B.comap Z.subtype) ≃ (Z' ⧸ B'.comap Z'.subtype) :=
  Quotient.congr (Equiv.subtypeEquivRight hZ) fun a b => by
    rw [Submodule.quotientRel_def, Submodule.quotientRel_def]
    exact hB _

theorem UpperTheory.V.GC.globEuler :
    GlobalFiniteness → GlobalEulerChar → ∀ (F Ω : Type) [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] [IsAlgClosure F Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime], l ≠ 2 → IsTotallyReal F → ∀ (S : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))), ContainsAbove S l → ∀ (k : Type) [Field k] [Finite k] [CharP k l] (M : Type) [AddCommGroup M] [Module k M] [Module.Finite k M] [TopologicalSpace M] [DiscreteTopology M] (ρ : Representation k (GS Ω (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F)))) M), ContAction ρ →
    ∀ c : InfinitePlace F → G F Ω, (∀ w, IsCCAt w (c w)) → Module.Finite k (H1 ρ) ∧ Module.Finite k (H2 ρ) ∧
    finrank k (H1 ρ) + ∑ w, finrank k (LinearMap.ker (ρ (toGS Ω _ (c w)) - 1)) = finrank k (H0 ρ) + finrank k (H2 ρ) + finrank ℚ F * finrank k M := by
  classical
  intro hF hE F Ω _ _ _ _ _ l _ hl hR S hS k _ _ _ M _ _ _ _ _ ρ hρ c hc
  haveI : Finite M := Module.finite_of_finite k
  haveI : CharP k (l ^ 1) := by rw [pow_one]; infer_instance
  letI : Module (ZMod (l ^ 1)) M := Module.compHom _ (ZMod.castHom (dvd_refl _) k)
  let ρ' : Representation (ZMod (l ^ 1)) (GS Ω (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F)))) M :=
    { toFun := fun g =>
        { toFun := ρ g
          map_add' := map_add (ρ g)
          map_smul' := fun c m => map_smul (ρ g) (ZMod.castHom (dvd_refl _) k c) m }
      map_one' := LinearMap.ext fun m => show ρ 1 m = m by rw [map_one]; rfl
      map_mul' := fun g h => LinearMap.ext fun m => show ρ (g * h) m = ρ g (ρ h m) by
        rw [map_mul]; rfl }
  have e0 : H0 ρ ≃ H0 ρ' := Equiv.subtypeEquivRight fun _ => Iff.rfl
  have e1 : H1 ρ ≃ H1 ρ' := subquotEquiv _ _ _ _ (fun _ => Iff.rfl) (fun _ => Iff.rfl)
  have e2 : H2 ρ ≃ H2 ρ' := subquotEquiv _ _ _ _ (fun _ => Iff.rfl) (fun _ => Iff.rfl)
  have ec : ∀ w, Nat.card {m : M // ρ' (toGS Ω _ (c w)) m = m} =
      Nat.card k ^ finrank k (LinearMap.ker (ρ (toGS Ω _ (c w)) - 1)) := fun w => by
    rw [← Module.natCard_eq_pow_finrank]
    refine Nat.card_congr (Equiv.subtypeEquivRight fun m => ?_)
    rw [LinearMap.mem_ker, LinearMap.sub_apply, Module.End.one_apply, sub_eq_zero]
    exact Iff.rfl
  obtain ⟨-, f1, f2, -⟩ := hF F Ω l 1 M S ρ' one_pos hS hρ
  have h := hE F Ω l 1 M S ρ' one_pos hS hρ c fun w _ => hc w
  have hr : ∀ w : InfinitePlace F, w.IsReal := fun w => IsTotallyReal.isReal w
  rw [archH0Card, IsTotallyReal.nrComplexPlaces_eq_zero, pow_zero, mul_one,
    Finset.filter_true_of_mem fun w _ => hr w, Finset.prod_congr rfl fun w _ => ec w,
    Finset.prod_pow_eq_pow_sum] at h
  haveI : Finite (H1 ρ) := Finite.of_equiv _ e1.symm
  haveI : Finite (H2 ρ) := Finite.of_equiv _ e2.symm
  haveI : Module.Finite k (H1 ρ) := Module.Finite.of_finite
  haveI : Module.Finite k (H2 ρ) := Module.Finite.of_finite
  have hk : 1 < Nat.card k := Finite.one_lt_card
  refine ⟨inferInstance, inferInstance, ?_⟩
  apply Nat.pow_right_injective hk
  beta_reduce
  rw [pow_add, pow_add, pow_add, mul_comm (finrank ℚ F), pow_mul, ← Module.natCard_eq_pow_finrank (V := H1 ρ),
    ← Module.natCard_eq_pow_finrank (V := H0 ρ), ← Module.natCard_eq_pow_finrank (V := H2 ρ),
    ← Module.natCard_eq_pow_finrank (V := M),
    Nat.card_congr e0, Nat.card_congr e1, Nat.card_congr e2]
  exact h.symm


[LFC_SRC_PT_TAIL] Normalized Poitou-Tate tail and coefficient trace
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/GaloisCochains/PtTail.lean#L1-L176
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisCochains.GLinkQuot

open NumberField IsDedekindDomain Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal Module UpperTheory.V UpperTheory.V.Fr UpperTheory.V.GC

section SlopeTestRemainingChoices
open UpperTheory.V.Loc GlobalTheory.Published GlobalTheory.Published.PoitouTate

section Transport

variable {R R' Γ M : Type} [CommRing R] [CommRing R'] [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ]
  [AddCommGroup M] [Module R M] [Module R' M] [TopologicalSpace M] [DiscreteTopology M]
  (ρ : Representation R Γ M) (ρ' : Representation R' Γ M) (h : ∀ g m, ρ' g m = ρ g m)

include h in
private theorem mem_Z2_iff (f : Γ × Γ → M) : f ∈ Z2 ρ ↔ f ∈ Z2 ρ' := by
  simp only [mem_cocycles2, h]

include h in
private theorem mem_B2_iff (f : Γ × Γ → M) : f ∈ B2 ρ ↔ f ∈ B2 ρ' := by
  have : ∀ c, d1 ρ c = d1 ρ' c := fun c => funext fun ⟨g, g'⟩ => by simp only [d1_apply, h]
  simp only [separated_dvr_second_cohomology_mem, this]

private def h2EquivOfRepEq : H2 ρ ≃ H2 ρ' :=
  Quotient.congr (Equiv.subtypeEquivRight (mem_Z2_iff ρ ρ' h)) fun a b => by
    rw [Submodule.quotientRel_def, Submodule.quotientRel_def, Submodule.mem_comap, Submodule.mem_comap]
    exact mem_B2_iff ρ ρ' h _

include h in
private theorem nonempty_sha2_equiv_sha2 {I : Type} {D : I → Type} [∀ i, Group (D i)] [∀ i, TopologicalSpace (D i)]
    (φ : ∀ i, D i →* Γ) (hφ : ∀ i, Continuous (φ i)) :
    Nonempty (Sha2 ρ φ hφ ≃ Sha2 ρ' φ hφ) := by
  refine ⟨(h2EquivOfRepEq ρ ρ' h).subtypeEquiv fun x => ?_⟩
  induction x using Submodule.Quotient.induction_on with
  | H z =>
    simp only [Sha2, Submodule.mem_iInf, LinearMap.mem_ker]
    refine forall_congr' fun i => ?_
    change Submodule.Quotient.mk (restrictCocycles2 ρ (φ i) (hφ i) z) = 0 ↔
      Submodule.Quotient.mk (restrictCocycles2 ρ' (φ i) (hφ i) ⟨z.1, (mem_Z2_iff ρ ρ' h _).1 z.2⟩) = 0
    rw [Submodule.Quotient.mk_eq_zero, Submodule.Quotient.mk_eq_zero, Submodule.mem_comap, Submodule.mem_comap]
    exact mem_B2_iff (ρ.comp (φ i)) (ρ'.comp (φ i)) (fun g m => h _ m) _

end Transport

private theorem card_ker_range {R A B : Type} [Ring R] [AddCommGroup A] [Module R A] [AddCommGroup B] [Module R B]
    (f : A →ₗ[R] B) : Nat.card A = Nat.card (LinearMap.ker f) * Nat.card (LinearMap.range f) := by
  rw [Submodule.card_eq_card_quotient_mul_card (LinearMap.ker f), Nat.card_congr f.quotKerEquivRange.toEquiv]

private theorem card_exact {R A B C : Type} [Ring R] [AddCommGroup A] [Module R A] [AddCommGroup B] [Module R B]
    [AddCommGroup C] [Module R C] (f : A →ₗ[R] B) (g : B →ₗ[R] C) (h : Function.Exact f g)
    (hg : Function.Surjective g) :
    Nat.card (LinearMap.ker f) * Nat.card B = Nat.card A * Nat.card C := by
  rw [card_ker_range f, card_ker_range g, LinearMap.exact_iff.1 h, LinearMap.range_eq_top.2 hg, mul_assoc,
    Nat.card_congr (Submodule.topEquiv (R := R) (M := C)).toEquiv]

private theorem card_H0dual {R k Γ Γ' M : Type} [Field R] [Field k] [Finite k] [Algebra R k] [Group Γ] [Group Γ']
    [AddCommGroup M] [Module R M] [Module k M] [IsScalarTower R k M] [Finite M]
    (ρ : Representation k Γ M) (ρ' : Representation R Γ' M) (e : Γ' ≃* Γ) (h : ∀ g m, ρ' g m = ρ (e g) m)
    (χ : Γ →* kˣ) (χ' : Γ' →* Rˣ) (hχ : ∀ g, (χ (e g) : k) = algebraMap R k (χ' g)) :
    Nat.card (H0dual χ ρ) = Nat.card (H0dual χ' ρ') := by
  haveI : Finite R := Finite.of_injective _ (algebraMap R k).injective
  let T : Module.Dual k M →+ Module.Dual R M :=
    { toFun := fun f => (Algebra.trace R k).comp (f.restrictScalars R)
      map_zero' := by ext; simp
      map_add' := fun f f' => by ext; simp }
  have hT : ∀ f m, T f m = Algebra.trace R k (f m) := fun _ _ => rfl
  have hinj : Function.Injective T := by
    rw [injective_iff_map_eq_zero]
    intro f hf
    ext m
    refine (traceForm_nondegenerate R k).1 (f m) fun a => ?_
    have := hT f (a • m)
    rw [hf, f.map_smul, smul_eq_mul, mul_comm] at this
    exact this.symm
  haveI : Finite (Module.Dual R M) := DFunLike.finite _
  have hbij : Function.Bijective T := hinj.bijective_of_nat_card_le (by
    rw [Module.natCard_eq_pow_finrank (K := R) (V := Dual R M), Module.natCard_eq_pow_finrank (K := k) (V := Dual k M),
      Subspace.dual_finrank_eq, Subspace.dual_finrank_eq, ← Module.natCard_eq_pow_finrank,
      ← Module.natCard_eq_pow_finrank])
  refine Nat.card_congr ((Equiv.ofBijective _ hbij).subtypeEquiv fun f => ?_)
  rw [Equiv.ofBijective_apply]
  constructor
  · intro hf g x
    rw [hT, hT, h, hf, hχ, ← Algebra.smul_def, map_smul, smul_eq_mul]
  · intro hf g x
    obtain ⟨g, rfl⟩ := e.surjective g
    have : f.comp (ρ (e g)) = (χ (e g) : k) • f := hinj (LinearMap.ext fun y => by
      have := hf g y
      rw [hT, hT, h] at this
      rw [hT, hT, LinearMap.comp_apply, this, LinearMap.smul_apply, smul_eq_mul, hχ, ← Algebra.smul_def,
        map_smul, smul_eq_mul])
    exact LinearMap.congr_fun this x

private theorem card_twDual {R Γ Γ' M : Type} [CommRing R] [Group Γ] [Group Γ'] [AddCommGroup M] [Module R M]
    (χ : Γ →* Rˣ) (ρ : Representation R Γ M) (π : Γ' →* Γ) (hπ : Function.Surjective π) :
    Nat.card (H0 (twDual χ ρ)) = Nat.card (H0dual (χ.comp π) (ρ.comp π)) := by
  refine Nat.card_congr (Equiv.subtypeEquivRight (α := TDual R M) fun y => ?_)
  constructor
  · intro hy g m
    have e := congrArg (fun φ : TDual R M => φ (ρ (π g) m)) (hy (π g))
    simp only [twDual_apply] at e
    rw [← Module.End.mul_apply, ← map_mul, inv_mul_cancel, map_one, Module.End.one_apply] at e
    exact e.symm
  · intro hy g
    obtain ⟨g, rfl⟩ := hπ g
    refine TDual.ext fun m => ?_
    simp only [twDual_apply]
    have := hy g (ρ (π g)⁻¹ m)
    simp only [MonoidHom.comp_apply] at this
    rw [← Module.End.mul_apply, ← map_mul, mul_inv_cancel, map_one, Module.End.one_apply] at this
    exact this.symm

private theorem card_eq_card_pow_finrank (k V : Type) [Field k] [AddCommGroup V] [Module k V] [Finite V] :
    Nat.card V = Nat.card k ^ finrank k V := by
  haveI : Module.Finite k V := Module.Finite.of_finite
  exact Module.natCard_eq_pow_finrank

end SlopeTestRemainingChoices

open UpperTheory.V.Loc GlobalTheory.Published GlobalTheory.Published.PoitouTate in
theorem UpperTheory.V.GC.ptTail :
    LocalFiniteness → GlobalFiniteness → GlobalTheory.Published.PoitouTateNormalised → ∀ (F Ω : Type) [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] [IsAlgClosure F Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime], l ≠ 2 → ∀ (S : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))), ContainsAbove S l → ∀ (A : S → ValuationSubring Ω), (∀ v, IsPlaceOver (A v) v.1) → ∀ (k : Type) [Field k] [Finite k] [CharP k l] (M : Type) [AddCommGroup M] [Module k M] [Module.Finite k M] [TopologicalSpace M] [DiscreteTopology M] (ρ : Representation k (GS Ω (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F)))) M), ContAction ρ →
    finrank k (Sha2 ρ (fun v => locHom (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))) (A v)) fun v => continuous_locHom _ (A v)) + ∑ v, finrank k (H2 (ρ.comp (locHom (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))) (A v)))) = finrank k (H2 ρ) + finrank k (Loc.H0dual (cycF F Ω l k) (ρ.comp (toGS Ω _))) := by
  intro hL _ hP F Ω _ _ _ _ _ l _ h2 S hS A hA k _ _ _ M _ _ _ _ _ ρ hρ
  have hl : l.Prime := Fact.out
  let χ := cycF F Ω l k
  let c : ZMod (l ^ 1) →+* k := ZMod.castHom (dvd_pow_self l one_ne_zero) k
  haveI : Fact (l ^ 1).Prime := ⟨by rwa [pow_one]⟩
  haveI : CharP k (l ^ 1) := by rwa [pow_one]
  letI : Algebra (ZMod (l ^ 1)) k := ZMod.algebra k (l ^ 1)
  letI : Module (ZMod (l ^ 1)) M := Module.compHom M (algebraMap (ZMod (l ^ 1)) k)
  haveI : IsScalarTower (ZMod (l ^ 1)) k M := IsScalarTower.of_algebraMap_smul fun _ _ => rfl
  haveI : Finite M := Module.finite_of_finite k
  let ρ' : Representation (ZMod (l ^ 1)) _ M := resCoef (ZMod (l ^ 1)) ρ
  have h : ∀ g m, ρ' g m = ρ g m := fun _ _ => rfl
  have hρ' : ContAction ρ' := hρ
  obtain ⟨ζ, hζ⟩ := exists_primitiveRoot_pow F Ω l 1
  obtain ⟨μ, hμ⟩ := exists_lift_cycMod (Ω := Ω) (r := 1) hS
  obtain ⟨inv, -, hN⟩ := hP F Ω l 1 (Or.inl h2) one_pos ζ hζ
  obtain ⟨⟨-, -, -, -, -, -, -, -, -, hex, hsurj⟩, ⟨⟨-, hfs, -⟩, -⟩, -⟩ := hN S hS A hA μ hμ M ρ' hρ'
  have hc := card_exact _ _ hex hsurj
  have hker : LinearMap.ker (loc2 S A ρ') = Sha2 ρ' (locFam S A) (continuous_locFam S A) := LinearMap.ker_pi _
  haveI f2 : ∀ v : S, Finite (H2 (ρ'.comp (locFam S A v))) := fun v => by
    obtain ⟨-, -, f2, -⟩ := hL F Ω l 1 M (A v) (ρ'.comp (locFam S A v)) v.1 one_pos (hA v)
      (hρ'.comp' (continuous_locFam S A v))
    exact f2
  haveI : Finite (H2 ρ') := by
    apply Nat.finite_of_card_ne_zero
    rw [card_ker_range (loc2 S A ρ'), hker]
    exact mul_ne_zero Nat.card_pos.ne' Nat.card_pos.ne'
  obtain ⟨eS⟩ := nonempty_sha2_equiv_sha2 ρ ρ' h (locFam S A) (continuous_locFam S A)
  have e2 := h2EquivOfRepEq ρ ρ' h
  have e3 : ∀ v : S, H2 (ρ.comp (locFam S A v)) ≃ H2 (ρ'.comp (locFam S A v)) := fun v =>
    h2EquivOfRepEq _ _ fun g m => h _ m
  haveI : Finite (H2 ρ) := Finite.of_equiv _ e2.symm
  haveI : ∀ v : S, Finite (H2 (ρ.comp (locFam S A v))) := fun v => Finite.of_equiv _ (e3 v).symm
  haveI : Finite (Module.Dual k M) := DFunLike.finite _
  haveI : Finite (TDual (ZMod (l ^ 1)) M) := (DFunLike.finite _ : Finite (M →ₗ[ZMod (l ^ 1)] ZMod (l ^ 1)))
  haveI : Finite (H0 (twDual μ ρ')) := Subtype.finite
  have hk : 1 < Nat.card k := Finite.one_lt_card
  have hd : Nat.card (Module.Dual (ZMod (l ^ 1)) (H0 (twDual μ ρ'))) =
      Nat.card (H0dual χ (ρ.comp (toGS Ω _))) := by
    rw [Module.natCard_eq_pow_finrank (K := ZMod (l ^ 1)), Subspace.dual_finrank_eq,
      ← Module.natCard_eq_pow_finrank, card_twDual μ ρ' (toGS Ω _) (QuotientGroup.mk'_surjective _), hμ]
    refine (card_H0dual (ρ.comp (toGS Ω _)) (ρ'.comp (toGS Ω _)) (MulEquiv.refl (G F Ω)) (fun _ _ => rfl) χ
      (cycMod F Ω l 1) fun g => ?_).symm
    exact RingHom.congr_fun (Subsingleton.elim c (algebraMap _ k)) _
  change finrank k (Sha2 ρ (locFam S A) (continuous_locFam S A)) +
    ∑ v, finrank k (H2 (ρ.comp (locFam S A v))) = _
  apply Nat.pow_right_injective hk
  beta_reduce
  rw [pow_add, pow_add, ← Finset.prod_pow_eq_pow_sum]
  haveI : Finite (Sha2 ρ (locFam S A) (continuous_locFam S A)) := Subtype.finite
  haveI : Finite (H0dual χ (ρ.comp (toGS Ω (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F)))))) := Subtype.finite
  rw [← card_eq_card_pow_finrank, ← card_eq_card_pow_finrank, ← card_eq_card_pow_finrank, Finset.prod_congr rfl fun v _ => (card_eq_card_pow_finrank k _).symm]
  rw [Nat.card_congr eS, Nat.card_congr e2, ← hd, ← hc, hker, Nat.card_pi]
  exact congrArg _ (Finset.prod_congr rfl fun v _ => Nat.card_congr (e3 v))


[LFC_SRC_H2_EQ] Full quotient global H2 inequality
