section SlopeTestRemainingChoices

private theorem hg_rank {k V W : Type} [Field k] [AddCommGroup V] [Module k V] [AddCommGroup W] [Module k W]
    (f : V →ₗ[k] W) [Module.Finite k (LinearMap.ker f)] [Module.Finite k W] (U : Submodule k V) (U' : Submodule k W)
    (h : ∀ x ∈ U, f x ∈ U') :
    Module.Finite k V ∧ finrank k U ≤ finrank k (LinearMap.ker f) + finrank k U' := by
  haveI : Module.Finite k V := by
    haveI := Module.Finite.equiv f.quotKerEquivRange.symm
    exact Module.Finite.of_submodule_quotient (LinearMap.ker f)
  refine ⟨this, ?_⟩
  have e := LinearMap.finrank_range_add_finrank_ker (f.domRestrict U)
  have a : finrank k (LinearMap.range (f.domRestrict U)) ≤ finrank k U' := Submodule.finrank_mono (by
    rintro _ ⟨x, rfl⟩
    exact h x.1 x.2)
  have b : finrank k (LinearMap.ker (f.domRestrict U)) ≤ finrank k (LinearMap.ker f) := by
    rw [← Submodule.finrank_map_subtype_eq]
    refine Submodule.finrank_mono ?_
    rintro _ ⟨x, hx, rfl⟩
    exact hx
  omega

private theorem hg_eqHom {k Γ Γ' M : Type} [Field k] [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [Group Γ'] [TopologicalSpace Γ']
    [AddCommGroup M] [Module k M] (ρ : Representation k Γ' M) (t : Γ →* Γ') (ht : Continuous t) (D J : Subgroup Γ)
    [Module.Finite k (eqHom (ρ.comp t) D J)] :
    Module.Finite k (eqHom ρ (D.map t) (J.map t)) ∧
      finrank k (eqHom ρ (D.map t) (J.map t)) ≤ finrank k (eqHom (ρ.comp t) D J) := by
  let u : J → J.map t := fun j => ⟨t j.1, j.1, j.2, rfl⟩
  have hu : Continuous u := (ht.comp continuous_subtype_val).subtype_mk _
  let φ : eqHom ρ (D.map t) (J.map t) →ₗ[k] eqHom (ρ.comp t) D J :=
    { toFun := fun f => ⟨fun j => f.1 (u j), f.2.1.comp_continuous hu, fun a b => by
          rw [← f.2.2.1]
          exact congrArg f.1 (Subtype.ext (map_mul t a.1 b.1)),
        fun d hd j j' h => f.2.2.2 (t d) ⟨d, hd, rfl⟩ (u j) (u j') (by
          change t j'.1 = t d * t j.1 * (t d)⁻¹
          rw [h, map_mul, map_mul, map_inv])⟩
      map_add' := fun _ _ => rfl
      map_smul' := fun _ _ => rfl }
  have hφ : Function.Injective φ := by
    intro f g h
    refine Subtype.ext (funext ?_)
    rintro ⟨_, j, hj, rfl⟩
    exact congrFun (congrArg Subtype.val h) ⟨j, hj⟩
  exact ⟨Module.Finite.of_injective φ hφ, LinearMap.finrank_le_finrank_of_injective hφ⟩

private theorem hg_loc {F Ω : Type} [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] [IsAlgClosure F Ω] (l : ℕ)
    [Fact l.Prime] {v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F)} {A : ValuationSubring Ω} (hA : IsPlaceOver A v)
    (K : Subgroup (G F Ω)) [K.Normal] (hK : IsOpen (K : Set (G F Ω))) (hl : ¬ l ∣ K.index)
    {Γ : Type} [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] (π : Dec (F := F) A →* Γ) (hπ : Continuous π)
    (h1 : ∀ g : Dec (F := F) A, g.1 ∈ Ine (F := F) A ⊓ K → π g = 1)
    {k : Type} [Field k] [Finite k] [CharP k l] {M : Type} [AddCommGroup M] [Module k M] [Module.Finite k M]
    [TopologicalSpace M] [DiscreteTopology M] (ρ : Representation k Γ M) (hρ : ContAction ρ) (x : H2 ρ) :
    resH2 ρ π hπ x = 0 := by
  haveI : CharZero Ω := charZero_of_injective_algebraMap (algebraMap F Ω).injective
  haveI : IsGalois F Ω := {}
  haveI : CompactSpace (Dec (F := F) A) := isCompact_iff_compactSpace.1 (isClosed_Dec A).isCompact
  obtain ⟨φ, -, h2, h3⟩ := decZhat F Ω v A hA
  obtain ⟨z, rfl⟩ := Submodule.Quotient.mk_surjective _ x
  haveI : ((Ine (F := F) A ⊓ K).subgroupOf (Dec (F := F) A)).Normal :=
    inferInstanceAs ((Ine (F := F) A).subgroupOf (Dec (F := F) A) ⊓ K.subgroupOf _).Normal
  obtain ⟨b, hb, -, hz⟩ := H2_unramified_inflation l (Dec (F := F) A) k M (ρ.comp π) (hρ.comp (hπ.prodMap continuous_id))
    ((Ine (F := F) A).subgroupOf (Dec (F := F) A)) ((Ine (F := F) A ⊓ K).subgroupOf (Dec (F := F) A)) φ
    (Subgroup.comap_mono inf_le_left)
    (((isClosed_Ine A).inter (K.isClosed_of_isOpen hK)).preimage continuous_subtype_val)
    (by
      rw [Subgroup.relIndex_subgroupOf (Ine_le_Dec _), Subgroup.inf_relIndex_left]
      exact fun h => hl (h.trans (Subgroup.relIndex_dvd_index_of_normal _ _)))
    (fun j hj => by rw [MonoidHom.comp_apply, h1 j hj, map_one]) h2 h3 (restrictCocycles2 ρ π hπ z)
    (fun s t j hj j' hj' => by simp only [restrictCocycles2_apply, map_mul, h1 j hj, h1 j' hj', mul_one])
  change Submodule.Quotient.mk (restrictCocycles2 ρ π hπ z) = 0
  rw [Submodule.Quotient.mk_eq_zero]
  exact (separated_dvr_second_cohomology_mem _ _).2 ⟨b, hb.continuous, funext fun p => (hz p.1 p.2).symm⟩

end SlopeTestRemainingChoices

theorem UpperTheory.V.GC.H2_galois_quotient_eq :
    Hypotheses₂ → ∀ (F Ω : Type) [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] [IsAlgClosure F Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime], IsTotallyReal F → ∀ (S : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))) (K : Subgroup (G F Ω)) (Γ : Type) [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [IsTopologicalGroup Γ] [CompactSpace Γ] [T2Space Γ] [TotallyDisconnectedSpace Γ] (π : G F Ω →* Γ) (h : GLink l S K π) (k : Type) [Field k] [Finite k] [CharP k l] (M : Type) [AddCommGroup M] [Module k M] [Module.Finite k M] [TopologicalSpace M] [DiscreteTopology M] (ρ : Representation k Γ M), ContAction ρ →
    ∀ c : InfinitePlace F → G F Ω, (∀ w, IsCCAt w (c w)) → ∀ (A : S → ValuationSubring Ω), (∀ v, IsPlaceOver (A v) v.1) → Module.Finite k (H1 ρ) ∧ Module.Finite k (H2 ρ) ∧
    finrank k (Sha2 ρ (fun v => π.comp (Dec (F := F) (A v)).subtype) fun v => h.cont.comp continuous_subtype_val) + ∑ v, finrank k (H2 (ρ.comp (π.comp (Dec (F := F) (A v)).subtype))) + finrank k (H0 ρ) + finrank ℚ F * finrank k M ≤
      finrank k (H1 ρ) + ∑ w, finrank k (LinearMap.ker (ρ (π (c w)) - 1)) + finrank k (Loc.H0dual (cycF F Ω l k) (ρ.comp π)) := by
  intro P F Ω _ _ _ _ _ l _ hF S K Γ _ _ _ _ _ _ π h k _ _ _ M _ _ _ _ _ ρ hρ c hc A hA
  classical
  haveI : CharZero Ω := charZero_of_injective_algebraMap (algebraMap F Ω).injective
  haveI : IsGalois F Ω := {}
  haveI := h.normal
  obtain ⟨A', hA', rfl⟩ : ∃ A' : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F) → ValuationSubring Ω, (∀ v, IsPlaceOver (A' v) v) ∧
      A = fun v : S => A' v.1 := by
    refine ⟨fun v => if hv : v ∈ S then A ⟨v, hv⟩ else (exists_isPlaceOver Ω v).choose, fun v => ?_, funext fun v => ?_⟩
    · by_cases hv : v ∈ S
      · simpa only [dif_pos hv] using hA ⟨v, hv⟩
      · simpa only [dif_neg hv] using (exists_isPlaceOver Ω v).choose_spec
    · simp only [dif_pos v.2]
  obtain ⟨S', p, hSS, hp, hps, rfl, hker⟩ := levelS F Ω l S K Γ π h
  haveI hcl := isClosed_NS Ω (S' : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F)))
  haveI : TotallyDisconnectedSpace (GS Ω (S' : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F)))) :=
    (AlgebraTools.totallyDisconnected_quot _ _ hcl).1
  have hS' : ContainsAbove S' l := fun v hv => hSS (h.above v hv)
  have hρ' : ContAction (ρ.comp p) := hρ.comp (hp.prodMap continuous_id)
  have hkill : ∀ q, q ∉ S → ∀ g ∈ Ine (F := F) (A' q) ⊓ K, p (toGS Ω _ g) = 1 := by
    intro q hq g hg
    have : g ∈ (p.comp (toGS Ω _)).ker := by
      rw [h.ker]
      refine Subgroup.le_topologicalClosure _ (Subgroup.subset_normalClosure ?_)
      simp only [Set.mem_iUnion]
      exact ⟨q, hq, A' q, hA' q, hg⟩
    exact this
  let n : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F) → ℕ := fun v =>
    finrank k (H2 ((ρ.comp p).comp (locHom (S' : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))) (A' v))))
  have hq : ∀ q : {v // v ∈ S' ∧ v ∉ S},
      Module.Finite k (eqHom (ρ.comp p) ((Dec (F := F) (A' q.1)).map (toGS Ω _)) ((Ine (F := F) (A' q.1) ⊓ K).map (toGS Ω _))) ∧
      finrank k (eqHom (ρ.comp p) ((Dec (F := F) (A' q.1)).map (toGS Ω _)) ((Ine (F := F) (A' q.1) ⊓ K).map (toGS Ω _))) ≤
        n q.1 := by
    intro q
    obtain ⟨a1, a2⟩ := tameEq P.lf P.lt P.le F Ω l q.1 (A' q.1) (hA' _) (fun hv => q.2.2 (h.above _ hv)) K h.isOpen h.idx
      k M ((ρ.comp p).comp (toGS Ω _)) (hρ'.comp ((continuous_toGS Ω _).prodMap continuous_id))
      (fun g hg => by rw [MonoidHom.comp_apply, MonoidHom.comp_apply, hkill _ q.2.2 g hg, map_one])
    obtain ⟨b1, b2⟩ := hg_eqHom (ρ.comp p) (toGS Ω _) (continuous_toGS Ω _) (Dec (F := F) (A' q.1))
      (Ine (F := F) (A' q.1) ⊓ K)
    exact ⟨b1, b2.trans a2.le⟩
  obtain ⟨g1, g2, g3⟩ := globEuler P.gf P.ge F Ω l h.odd hF S' hS' k M (ρ.comp p) hρ' c hc
  have tail := ptTail P.lf P.gf P.pt F Ω l h.odd S' hS' (fun v => A' v.1) (fun v => hA' v.1) k M (ρ.comp p) hρ'
  letI : Fintype {v // v ∈ S' ∧ v ∉ S} := Fintype.subtype (S' \ S) fun _ => Finset.mem_sdiff
  obtain ⟨i1, i2, i3⟩ := inflKer l _ Γ p hp hps k M ρ hρ {v // v ∈ S' ∧ v ∉ S}
    (fun q => (Dec (F := F) (A' q.1)).map (toGS Ω _)) (fun q => (Ine (F := F) (A' q.1) ⊓ K).map (toGS Ω _))
    (hker A' hA') (fun q => (hq q).1) g1
  obtain ⟨r1, r2⟩ := hg_rank (resH2 ρ p hp)
    (Sha2 ρ (fun v : S => (p.comp (toGS Ω _)).comp (Dec (F := F) (A' v.1)).subtype) fun v => h.cont.comp continuous_subtype_val)
    (Sha2 (ρ.comp p) (fun v : S' => locHom (S' : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))) (A' v.1)) fun v => continuous_locHom _ (A' v.1))
    (by
      intro x hx
      simp only [Sha2, Submodule.mem_iInf, LinearMap.mem_ker] at hx ⊢
      intro v
      have e : resH2 (ρ.comp p) (locHom (S' : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))) (A' v.1)) (continuous_locHom _ _) (resH2 ρ p hp x) =
          resH2 ρ ((p.comp (toGS Ω _)).comp (Dec (F := F) (A' v.1)).subtype) (h.cont.comp continuous_subtype_val) x := by
        obtain ⟨z, rfl⟩ := Submodule.Quotient.mk_surjective _ x
        rfl
      refine e.trans ?_
      by_cases hv : v.1 ∈ S
      · exact hx ⟨v.1, hv⟩
      · exact hg_loc l (hA' v.1) K h.isOpen h.idx _ _ (fun g hg => hkill _ hv g.1 hg) ρ hρ x)
  refine ⟨i1, r1, ?_⟩
  have e0 : H0 (ρ.comp p) = H0 ρ := by
    ext m
    exact ⟨fun hm g => by obtain ⟨g, rfl⟩ := hps g; exact hm g, fun hm g => hm (p g)⟩
  rw [e0] at g3
  have s1 : ∑ v : S', n v.1 = ∑ v : S, n v.1 + ∑ q : {v // v ∈ S' ∧ v ∉ S}, n q.1 := by
    rw [Finset.sum_coe_sort S' n, Finset.sum_coe_sort S n, ← Finset.sum_sdiff hSS, add_comm,
      Finset.sum_subtype (S' \ S) (p := fun v => v ∈ S' ∧ v ∉ S) (fun _ => Finset.mem_sdiff) n]
  have s2 := Finset.sum_le_sum (s := Finset.univ) fun q _ => (hq q).2
  change _ + ∑ v : S', n v.1 = _ at tail
  beta_reduce
  change _ + ∑ v : S, n v.1 + _ + _ ≤ _ + ∑ w, finrank k (LinearMap.ker ((ρ.comp p) (toGS Ω _ (c w)) - 1)) +
    finrank k (Loc.H0dual (cycF F Ω l k) ((ρ.comp p).comp (toGS Ω _)))
  omega


[LFC_SRC_H2_SURJ] Local coefficient quotient inequality
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/GaloisCochains/H2GaloisQuotientSurjective.lean#L1-L78

private theorem finrank_iInf_ker_add_sum_le_of_surjective {k V : Type} [Field k] [AddCommGroup V] [Module k V] {ι : Type} [Fintype ι]
    (W U : ι → Type) [∀ i, AddCommGroup (W i)] [∀ i, Module k (W i)] [∀ i, AddCommGroup (U i)]
    [∀ i, Module k (U i)] (hV : Module.Finite k V) (hW : ∀ i, Module.Finite k (W i))
    (r : ∀ i, V →ₗ[k] W i) (f : ∀ i, W i →ₗ[k] U i) (hf : ∀ i, Function.Surjective (f i))
    (p : ∀ i, V →ₗ[k] U i) (hp : ∀ i, p i = (f i).comp (r i)) :
    finrank k (⨅ i, LinearMap.ker (p i) : Submodule k V) + ∑ i, finrank k (U i) ≤
      finrank k (⨅ i, LinearMap.ker (r i) : Submodule k V) + ∑ i, finrank k (W i) := by
  classical
  haveI := hV
  haveI := hW
  obtain rfl : p = fun i => (f i).comp (r i) := funext hp
  set X : Submodule k V := ⨅ i, LinearMap.ker ((f i).comp (r i)) with hX
  set Y : Submodule k V := ⨅ i, LinearMap.ker (r i) with hY
  have hmem : ∀ x : X, ∀ i, r i x ∈ LinearMap.ker (f i) := fun x i => by
    have := (Submodule.mem_iInf _).mp x.2 i
    simpa using this
  let g : X →ₗ[k] (∀ i, LinearMap.ker (f i)) :=
    LinearMap.pi fun i => ((r i).comp X.subtype).codRestrict (LinearMap.ker (f i)) (fun x => hmem x i)
  have h1 := LinearMap.finrank_range_add_finrank_ker g
  have h2 : finrank k (LinearMap.range g) ≤ ∑ i, finrank k (LinearMap.ker (f i)) := by
    calc _ ≤ finrank k (∀ i, LinearMap.ker (f i)) := Submodule.finrank_le _
      _ = _ := Module.finrank_pi_fintype k
  have h3 : finrank k (LinearMap.ker g) ≤ finrank k Y := by
    let e : LinearMap.ker g →ₗ[k] Y := (X.subtype.comp (LinearMap.ker g).subtype).codRestrict Y (fun x => by
      have hx := LinearMap.mem_ker.mp x.2
      refine (Submodule.mem_iInf _).mpr fun i => ?_
      have := congrArg Subtype.val (congrFun hx i)
      exact this)
    refine LinearMap.finrank_le_finrank_of_injective (f := e) ?_
    intro a b hab
    have := congrArg Subtype.val hab
    exact Subtype.ext (Subtype.ext this)
  have h4 : ∀ i, finrank k (LinearMap.ker (f i)) + finrank k (U i) = finrank k (W i) := by
    intro i
    have := LinearMap.finrank_range_add_finrank_ker (f i)
    rw [LinearMap.range_eq_top.mpr (hf i), finrank_top] at this
    omega
  have h5 : ∑ i, finrank k (LinearMap.ker (f i)) + ∑ i, finrank k (U i) = ∑ i, finrank k (W i) := by
    rw [← Finset.sum_add_distrib]
    exact Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i _ => h4 i
  omega

theorem UpperTheory.V.GC.H2_galois_quotient_surjective :
    Hypotheses₂ → ∀ (F Ω : Type) [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] [IsAlgClosure F Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime], IsTotallyReal F → ∀ (S : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))) (K : Subgroup (G F Ω)) (Γ : Type) [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [IsTopologicalGroup Γ] [CompactSpace Γ] [T2Space Γ] [TotallyDisconnectedSpace Γ] (π : G F Ω →* Γ) (h : GLink l S K π) (k : Type) [Field k] [Finite k] [CharP k l] (M : Type) [AddCommGroup M] [Module k M] [Module.Finite k M] [TopologicalSpace M] [DiscreteTopology M] (ρ : Representation k Γ M), ContAction ρ →
    ∀ c : InfinitePlace F → G F Ω, (∀ w, IsCCAt w (c w)) → ∀ (A : S → ValuationSubring Ω), (∀ v, IsPlaceOver (A v) v.1) → Loc.H0dual (cycF F Ω l k) (ρ.comp π) = ⊥ →
    ∀ (Q : S → Type) [∀ v, AddCommGroup (Q v)] [∀ v, Module k (Q v)] [∀ v, TopologicalSpace (Q v)] [∀ v, DiscreteTopology (Q v)] (σ : ∀ v, Representation k (Dec (F := F) (A v)) (Q v)) (q : ∀ v, M →ₗ[k] Q v)
    (hq : ∀ v s m, q v (ρ ((π.comp (Dec (F := F) (A v)).subtype) s) m) = σ v s (q v m)), (∀ v, Function.Surjective (q v)) →
    finrank k (⨅ v, LinearMap.ker (compatiblePairH2 ρ (σ v) (π.comp (Dec (F := F) (A v)).subtype) (h.cont.comp continuous_subtype_val) (q v) continuous_of_discreteTopology (hq v)) : Submodule k (H2 ρ)) +
      ∑ v, finrank k (H2 (σ v)) + finrank k (H0 ρ) + finrank ℚ F * finrank k M ≤ finrank k (H1 ρ) + ∑ w, finrank k (LinearMap.ker (ρ (π (c w)) - 1)) := by
  intro P F Ω _ _ _ _ _ l _ hF S K Γ _ _ _ _ _ _ π h k _ _ _ M _ _ _ _ _ ρ hρ c hc A hA hdual Q _ _ _ _ σ q hq hsurj
  obtain ⟨_, hfin2, hG⟩ := H2_galois_quotient_eq P F Ω l hF S K Γ π h k M ρ hρ c hc A hA
  rw [hdual, finrank_bot, add_zero] at hG
  have hcv : ∀ v : S, Continuous (π.comp (Dec (F := F) (A v)).subtype) :=
    fun v => h.cont.comp continuous_subtype_val
  have hρv : ∀ v : S, ReciprocityTheory.Gal.ContAction (ρ.comp (π.comp (Dec (F := F) (A v)).subtype)) :=
    fun v => hρ.comp (((hcv v).comp continuous_fst).prodMk continuous_snd)
  have hfinv : ∀ v : S, Module.Finite k (H2 (ρ.comp (π.comp (Dec (F := F) (A v)).subtype))) :=
    fun v => (locEuler P.lf P.lt P.le F Ω l v.1 (A v) (hA v) k M _ (hρv v)).2.1
  have hontov : ∀ v : S, Function.Surjective (mapH2 (ρ.comp (π.comp (Dec (F := F) (A v)).subtype)) (σ v) (q v)
      continuous_of_discreteTopology (hq v)) :=
    fun v => local_second_cohomology_surjective_at_place P.lf F Ω l v.1 (A v) (hA v) k M (Q v) _ (σ v) (hρv v) (q v) (hq v) (hsurj v)
  have key := finrank_iInf_ker_add_sum_le_of_surjective (k := k) (V := H2 ρ)
    (fun v : S => H2 (ρ.comp (π.comp (Dec (F := F) (A v)).subtype))) (fun v => H2 (σ v)) hfin2 hfinv
    (fun v => resH2 ρ _ (hcv v))
    (fun v => mapH2 _ (σ v) (q v) continuous_of_discreteTopology (hq v)) hontov
    (fun v => compatiblePairH2 ρ (σ v) (π.comp (Dec (F := F) (A v)).subtype) (h.cont.comp continuous_subtype_val)
      (q v) continuous_of_discreteTopology (hq v))
    (fun v => Submodule.linearMap_qext _ (LinearMap.ext fun f => rfl))
  unfold Sha2 at hG
  beta_reduce at hG key
  omega


[LFC_SRC_H2_RIGHT] Source local H2 right-exactness; natural Tate-duality proof used here
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/GaloisCochains/H2RightExactModL.lean#L1-L117
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisCochains.GLinkQuot
import Development.AbelianVarietyTheory.PublishedTheorems.GaloisCohomology₂.CohDimTwoLocalOfLocalFiniteness

variable {k G M : Type} [CommRing k] [Group G] [AddCommGroup M] [Module k M]

private theorem dn_two (ρ : Representation k G M) (b : (Fin 2 → G) → M) (x y z : G) :
    dn ρ 2 b ![x, y, z] = ρ x (b ![y, z]) - b ![x * y, z] + b ![x, y * z] - b ![x, y] := by
  have e0 : (fun i : Fin 2 => (![x, y, z] : Fin 3 → G) i.succ) = ![y, z] := by
    funext i; fin_cases i <;> rfl
  have e1 : Fin.contractNth 0 (· * ·) (![x, y, z] : Fin 3 → G) = ![x * y, z] := by
    funext i; fin_cases i <;> rfl
  have e2 : Fin.contractNth 1 (· * ·) (![x, y, z] : Fin 3 → G) = ![x, y * z] := by
    funext i; fin_cases i <;> rfl
  have e3 : Fin.contractNth 2 (· * ·) (![x, y, z] : Fin 3 → G) = ![x, y] := by
    funext i; fin_cases i <;> rfl
  change ρ _ (b _) + Finset.univ.sum (fun j : Fin 3 =>
      (-1 : k) ^ ((j : ℕ) + 1) • b (Fin.contractNth j (· * ·) ![x, y, z])) = _
  rw [Fin.sum_univ_three, e0, e1, e2, e3]
  have p3 : (-1 : k) ^ 3 = -1 := by ring
  simp [p3]
  abel

private theorem dn_three (ρ : Representation k G M) (b : (Fin 3 → G) → M) (x y z w : G) :
    dn ρ 3 b ![x, y, z, w] = ρ x (b ![y, z, w]) - b ![x * y, z, w] + b ![x, y * z, w]
      - b ![x, y, z * w] + b ![x, y, z] := by
  have e0 : (fun i : Fin 3 => (![x, y, z, w] : Fin 4 → G) i.succ) = ![y, z, w] := by
    funext i; fin_cases i <;> rfl
  have e1 : Fin.contractNth 0 (· * ·) (![x, y, z, w] : Fin 4 → G) = ![x * y, z, w] := by
    funext i; fin_cases i <;> rfl
  have e2 : Fin.contractNth 1 (· * ·) (![x, y, z, w] : Fin 4 → G) = ![x, y * z, w] := by
    funext i; fin_cases i <;> rfl
  have e3 : Fin.contractNth 2 (· * ·) (![x, y, z, w] : Fin 4 → G) = ![x, y, z * w] := by
    funext i; fin_cases i <;> rfl
  have e4 : Fin.contractNth 3 (· * ·) (![x, y, z, w] : Fin 4 → G) = ![x, y, z] := by
    funext i; fin_cases i <;> rfl
  change ρ _ (b _) + Finset.univ.sum (fun j : Fin 4 =>
      (-1 : k) ^ ((j : ℕ) + 1) • b (Fin.contractNth j (· * ·) ![x, y, z, w])) = _
  rw [Fin.sum_univ_four, e0, e1, e2, e3, e4]
  have p3 : (-1 : k) ^ 3 = -1 := by ring
  have p4 : (-1 : k) ^ 4 = 1 := by ring
  simp [p3, p4]
  abel

private theorem H2_map_surjective_of_H3_subsingleton [TopologicalSpace G] [ContinuousMul G] [TopologicalSpace M] [DiscreteTopology M]
    {Q N : Type} [AddCommGroup Q] [Module k Q] [TopologicalSpace Q] [DiscreteTopology Q]
    [AddCommGroup N] [Module k N] [TopologicalSpace N] [DiscreteTopology N]
    (ρ : Representation k G M) (σ : Representation k G Q) (τ : Representation k G N) (hc : ContAction ρ)
    (q : M →ₗ[k] Q) (hq : ∀ s m, q (ρ s m) = σ s (q m)) (hs : Function.Surjective q)
    (i : N →ₗ[k] M) (hi : ∀ s m, i (τ s m) = ρ s (i m)) (hinj : Function.Injective i)
    (hex : ∀ m, q m = 0 ↔ ∃ n, i n = m) (h3 : Subsingleton (Hn τ 3)) :
    Function.Surjective (mapH2 ρ σ q continuous_of_discreteTopology hq) := by
  classical
  intro cl
  obtain ⟨f, rfl⟩ := Submodule.Quotient.mk_surjective _ cl
  obtain ⟨sec, hsec⟩ := hs.hasRightInverse
  let Φ : G × G → M := fun p => sec (f.1 p)
  have hΦ : Continuous Φ := continuous_of_discreteTopology.comp f.2.1
  have hqΦ : ∀ p, q (Φ p) = f.1 p := fun p => hsec _
  let c : (Fin 3 → G) → M := fun g =>
    ρ (g 0) (Φ (g 1, g 2)) - Φ (g 0 * g 1, g 2) + Φ (g 0, g 1 * g 2) - Φ (g 0, g 1)
  have hcc : Continuous c := by
    have h0 : Continuous fun g : Fin 3 → G => ρ (g 0) (Φ (g 1, g 2)) :=
      hc.comp (f := fun g : Fin 3 → G => (g 0, Φ (g 1, g 2))) (by fun_prop)
    exact ((h0.sub (by fun_prop)).add (by fun_prop)).sub (by fun_prop)
  have hqc : ∀ g, q (c g) = 0 := fun g => by
    simp only [c, map_sub, map_add, hq, hqΦ]
    exact f.2.2 _ _ _
  let r : M → N := Function.invFun i
  have hr : ∀ g, i (r (c g)) = c g := fun g => Function.invFun_eq ((hex _).1 (hqc g))
  obtain ⟨b, hb, hbc⟩ := (GlobalTheory.Published.subsingleton_Hn_succ_iff τ 2).1 h3 (fun g => r (c g))
    ((continuous_of_discreteTopology (f := r)).comp hcc) (by
      funext g
      obtain ⟨x, y, z, w, rfl⟩ : ∃ x y z w, g = ![x, y, z, w] :=
        ⟨g 0, g 1, g 2, g 3, by funext j; fin_cases j <;> rfl⟩
      apply hinj
      rw [dn_three]
      simp only [map_add, map_sub, hi, hr, Pi.zero_apply, map_zero]
      simp [c, mul_assoc]
      abel)
  have hb' : Continuous fun p : G × G => i (b ![p.1, p.2]) :=
    continuous_of_discreteTopology.comp (hb.comp (by fun_prop))
  refine ⟨Submodule.Quotient.mk ⟨fun p => Φ p - i (b ![p.1, p.2]), hΦ.sub hb', fun x y z => ?_⟩, ?_⟩
  · have e := congrArg i (congrFun hbc ![x, y, z])
    rw [dn_two] at e
    simp only [map_add, map_sub, hi, hr] at e
    simp only [c, Matrix.cons_val_zero, Matrix.cons_val_one, Matrix.cons_val_two, Matrix.tail_cons, Matrix.head_cons] at e
    simp only [map_sub]
    rw [← sub_eq_zero.2 e.symm]
    abel
  · change Submodule.Quotient.mk _ = _
    congr 1
    apply Subtype.ext
    funext p
    change q (Φ p - i (b ![p.1, p.2])) = _
    rw [map_sub, hqΦ, (hex _).2 ⟨_, rfl⟩, sub_zero]

end

theorem UpperTheory.V.GC.local_second_cohomology_surjective_at_place :
    LocalFiniteness → ∀ (F Ω : Type) [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] [IsAlgClosure F Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F)) (A : ValuationSubring Ω), IsPlaceOver A v → ∀ (k : Type) [Field k] [Finite k] [CharP k l] (M : Type) [AddCommGroup M] [Module k M] [Module.Finite k M] [TopologicalSpace M] [DiscreteTopology M] (Q : Type) [AddCommGroup Q] [Module k Q] [TopologicalSpace Q] [DiscreteTopology Q]
    (ρ : Representation k (Dec (F := F) A) M) (σ : Representation k (Dec (F := F) A) Q), ContAction ρ → ∀ (q : M →ₗ[k] Q) (hq : ∀ s m, q (ρ s m) = σ s (q m)), Function.Surjective q →
    Function.Surjective (mapH2 ρ σ q continuous_of_discreteTopology hq) := by
  intro hLF F Ω _ _ _ _ _ l _ v A hA k _ _ _ M _ _ _ _ _ Q _ _ _ _ ρ σ hc q hq hs
  haveI : Finite M := Module.finite_of_finite k
  have hst : ∀ g, LinearMap.ker q ≤ (LinearMap.ker q).comap (ρ g) := fun g m hm => by
    rw [Submodule.mem_comap, LinearMap.mem_ker, hq, LinearMap.mem_ker.1 hm, map_zero]
  let τ := ρ.subrepresentation (LinearMap.ker q) hst
  have hτ : ContAction τ :=
    Continuous.subtype_mk (hc.comp (continuous_fst.prodMk (continuous_subtype_val.comp continuous_snd))) _
  refine H2_map_surjective_of_H3_subsingleton ρ σ τ hc q hq hs (LinearMap.ker q).subtype (fun _ _ => rfl) Subtype.val_injective
    (fun m => ⟨fun h => ⟨⟨m, h⟩, rfl⟩, by rintro ⟨n, rfl⟩; exact n.2⟩) ?_
  refine (GlobalTheory.Published.cohDimTwoLocal_of_localFiniteness hLF).of_nsmul l 1 (fun m => ?_) τ hA hτ 3 le_rfl
  rw [pow_one, ← Nat.cast_smul_eq_nsmul k, CharP.cast_eq_zero, zero_smul]


[LFC_SRC_CONE] Literal cone maps and dimension identity
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/GaloisCochains/ConeCount.lean#L1-L371
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisCochains.GLinkQuot

open NumberField IsDedekindDomain Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal Module UpperTheory.V UpperTheory.V.Fr UpperTheory.V.GC

noncomputable section

namespace UpperTheory.V.GC.ConeCnt

section Lin

variable {k A W L O S : Type} [Field k] [AddCommGroup A] [Module k A] [AddCommGroup W] [Module k W]
  [AddCommGroup L] [Module k L] [AddCommGroup O] [Module k O] [AddCommGroup S] [Module k S]

private theorem count [Module.Finite k A] [Module.Finite k L] [Module.Finite k S]
    (f : A →ₗ[k] L) (π : W →ₗ[k] L) (hπ : Function.Surjective π) (g : W →ₗ[k] O) (h : O →ₗ[k] S)
    (h1 : LinearMap.ker g = (LinearMap.range f).comap π) (h2 : LinearMap.ker h = LinearMap.range g) :
    Module.Finite k O ∧ finrank k O + finrank k A =
      finrank k (LinearMap.range h) + finrank k L + finrank k (LinearMap.ker f) := by
  have e1 : LinearMap.ker ((LinearMap.range f).mkQ ∘ₗ π) = LinearMap.ker g := by
    rw [LinearMap.ker_comp, Submodule.ker_mkQ, h1]
  have hs : Function.Surjective ((LinearMap.range f).mkQ ∘ₗ π) :=
    (Submodule.mkQ_surjective _).comp hπ
  let e : LinearMap.range g ≃ₗ[k] L ⧸ LinearMap.range f :=
    g.quotKerEquivRange.symm.trans ((Submodule.quotEquivOfEq _ _ e1.symm).trans
      (LinearMap.quotKerEquivOfSurjective _ hs))
  haveI : Module.Finite k (LinearMap.range g) := Module.Finite.equiv e.symm
  have a5 : finrank k (LinearMap.ker h) = finrank k (LinearMap.range g) := by rw [h2]
  haveI : Module.Finite k (LinearMap.ker h) := by rw [h2]; infer_instance
  haveI : Module.Finite k O := by
    apply Module.rank_lt_aleph0_iff.mp
    rw [← LinearMap.rank_range_add_rank_ker h]
    exact Cardinal.add_lt_aleph0 (Module.rank_lt_aleph0 k _) (Module.rank_lt_aleph0 k _)
  refine ⟨inferInstance, ?_⟩
  have a1 := LinearMap.finrank_range_add_finrank_ker f
  have a2 := LinearMap.finrank_range_add_finrank_ker h
  have a3 := Submodule.finrank_quotient_add_finrank (LinearMap.range f)
  have a4 := e.finrank_eq
  omega

end Lin

variable {k Γ M V : Type} [Field k] [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [AddCommGroup M] [Module k M]
  [TopologicalSpace M] [DiscreteTopology M] {Gv : V → Type} [∀ v, Group (Gv v)] [∀ v, TopologicalSpace (Gv v)]
  (loc : ∀ v, Gv v →* Γ) (hloc : ∀ v, Continuous (loc v)) (ρ : Representation k Γ M)
  {Q : V → Type} [∀ v, AddCommGroup (Q v)] [∀ v, Module k (Q v)] [∀ v, TopologicalSpace (Q v)]
  [∀ v, DiscreteTopology (Q v)] (σ : ∀ v, Representation k (Gv v) (Q v)) (q : ∀ v, M →ₗ[k] Q v)
  (hq : ∀ v s m, q v (ρ (loc v s) m) = σ v s (q v m))
  (r : ∀ v, Q v →ₗ[k] M) (hr : ∀ v y, q v (r v y) = y)

private theorem first_differential_eq {G N : Type} [Group G] [AddCommGroup N] [Module k N] (τ : Representation k G N) (c : G → N) :
    d1 τ c = Alg.dOne τ c := by
  funext p
  show τ p.1 (c p.2) - c (p.1 * p.2) + c p.1 = c p.1 + τ p.1 (c p.2) - c (p.1 * p.2)
  abel

private def f0 : H0 ρ →ₗ[k] (∀ v, H0 (σ v)) where
  toFun x := fun v => ⟨q v x.1, fun g => by rw [← hq, x.2]⟩
  map_add' _ _ := funext fun v => Subtype.ext (map_add _ _ _)
  map_smul' _ _ := funext fun v => Subtype.ext (map_smul _ _ _)

private def f1 : H1 ρ →ₗ[k] (∀ v, H1 (σ v)) :=
  LinearMap.pi fun v => pairH1DecompositionMap ρ (σ v) (loc v) (hloc v) (q v) continuous_of_discreteTopology (hq v)

private def g1Z : (∀ v, H0 (σ v)) →ₗ[k] Z1C loc ρ (fun v => LinearMap.ker (q v)) where
  toFun y := ⟨(0, fun v => r v (y v).1), IsLocallyConstant.const 0, by simp, fun v s => by
    have := (y v).2 s
    simp [hq, hr, this]⟩
  map_add' _ _ := Subtype.ext (Prod.ext (by simp) (funext fun v => by simp))
  map_smul' _ _ := Subtype.ext (Prod.ext (by simp) (funext fun v => by simp))

private def g1 : (∀ v, H0 (σ v)) →ₗ[k] H1C loc ρ (fun v => LinearMap.ker (q v)) :=
  Submodule.mkQ _ ∘ₗ g1Z loc ρ σ q hq r hr

private def h1Z : Z1C loc ρ (fun v => LinearMap.ker (q v)) →ₗ[k] Z1 ρ where
  toFun x := ⟨x.1.1, x.2.1.continuous, x.2.2.1⟩
  map_add' _ _ := rfl
  map_smul' _ _ := rfl

private def h1 : H1C loc ρ (fun v => LinearMap.ker (q v)) →ₗ[k] H1 ρ :=
  Submodule.mapQ _ _ (h1Z loc ρ q) fun x hx => by
    obtain ⟨X, a1, -⟩ := hx
    exact (mem_coboundaries1 _ _).2 ⟨X, a1⟩

private theorem ker_g1_eq_range_f0 : LinearMap.ker (g1 loc ρ σ q hq r hr) =
    (LinearMap.range (f0 loc ρ σ q hq)).comap LinearMap.id := by
  rw [Submodule.comap_id]
  ext y
  rw [LinearMap.mem_ker, LinearMap.mem_range]
  constructor
  · intro hy
    have hy' : g1Z loc ρ σ q hq r hr y ∈ Submodule.comap (Z1C loc ρ fun v => LinearMap.ker (q v)).subtype
        (continuousCoboundaries1 ρ fun v => LinearMap.ker (q v)) :=
      (Submodule.Quotient.mk_eq_zero _).1 hy
    obtain ⟨X, h1, h2⟩ := hy'
    refine ⟨⟨X, fun g => ?_⟩, ?_⟩
    · have : ρ g X - X = 0 := (h1 g).symm
      exact sub_eq_zero.1 this
    · funext v
      apply Subtype.ext
      have := h2 v
      change r v (y v).1 - X ∈ LinearMap.ker (q v) at this
      rw [LinearMap.mem_ker, map_sub, hr] at this
      exact (sub_eq_zero.1 this).symm
  · rintro ⟨x, rfl⟩
    apply (Submodule.Quotient.mk_eq_zero _).2
    refine ⟨x.1, fun s => ?_, fun v => ?_⟩
    · exact (sub_eq_zero.2 (x.2 s)).symm
    · show r v (q v x.1) - x.1 ∈ LinearMap.ker (q v)
      rw [LinearMap.mem_ker, map_sub, hr, sub_self]

private theorem ker_h1_eq_range_g1 : LinearMap.ker (h1 loc ρ q) = LinearMap.range (g1 loc ρ σ q hq r hr) := by
  ext c
  rw [LinearMap.mem_ker, LinearMap.mem_range]
  constructor
  · intro hc
    obtain ⟨x, rfl⟩ := Submodule.Quotient.mk_surjective _ c
    have hx : h1Z loc ρ q x ∈ Submodule.comap (Z1 ρ).subtype (B1 ρ) :=
      (Submodule.Quotient.mk_eq_zero _).1 hc
    obtain ⟨m, hm⟩ := (mem_coboundaries1 _ _).1 hx
    have hm' : ∀ s, x.1.1 s = ρ s m - m := hm
    have key : ∀ v (s : Gv v), σ v s (q v (x.1.2 v - m)) = q v (x.1.2 v - m) := by
      intro v s
      have h3 := x.2.2.2 v s
      rw [LinearMap.mem_ker] at h3
      rw [← hq, ← sub_eq_zero, ← map_sub]
      have e : ρ (loc v s) (x.1.2 v - m) - (x.1.2 v - m) =
          -(x.1.1 (loc v s) - (ρ (loc v s) (x.1.2 v) - x.1.2 v)) := by
        rw [hm', map_sub]; abel
      rw [e, map_neg, h3, neg_zero]
    refine ⟨fun v => ⟨q v (x.1.2 v - m), key v⟩, ?_⟩
    show Submodule.Quotient.mk (g1Z loc ρ σ q hq r hr _) = Submodule.Quotient.mk x
    rw [Submodule.Quotient.eq]
    refine ⟨-m, fun s => ?_, fun v => ?_⟩
    · show (0 : M) - x.1.1 s = ρ s (-m) - -m
      rw [hm' s, map_neg]; abel
    · show r v (q v (x.1.2 v - m)) - x.1.2 v - -m ∈ LinearMap.ker (q v)
      rw [LinearMap.mem_ker, map_sub, map_sub, hr, map_neg, map_sub]; abel
  · rintro ⟨y, rfl⟩
    show Submodule.Quotient.mk (h1Z loc ρ q (g1Z loc ρ σ q hq r hr y)) = 0
    exact (Submodule.Quotient.mk_eq_zero _).2 ((mem_coboundaries1 _ _).2 ⟨0, fun g => by
      show (0 : M) = ρ g 0 - 0
      simp⟩)

include hr in
private theorem range_h1_eq_ker_f1 : LinearMap.range (h1 loc ρ q) = LinearMap.ker (f1 loc hloc ρ σ q hq) := by
  ext c
  rw [LinearMap.mem_range, LinearMap.mem_ker]
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨c', rfl⟩
    obtain ⟨x, rfl⟩ := Submodule.Quotient.mk_surjective _ c'
    funext v
    show (Submodule.Quotient.mk (compatiblePairCocycles1 ρ (σ v) (loc v) (hloc v) (q v) continuous_of_discreteTopology (hq v)
      (h1Z loc ρ q x)) : H1 (σ v)) = 0
    apply (Submodule.Quotient.mk_eq_zero _).2
    refine (mem_coboundaries1 _ _).2 ⟨q v (x.1.2 v), fun s => ?_⟩
    have h3 := x.2.2.2 v s
    rw [LinearMap.mem_ker, map_sub, map_sub, hq] at h3
    show q v (x.1.1 (loc v s)) = σ v s (q v (x.1.2 v)) - q v (x.1.2 v)
    exact sub_eq_zero.1 h3
  · intro hc
    obtain ⟨z, rfl⟩ := Submodule.Quotient.mk_surjective _ c
    have hv : ∀ v, ∃ m, ∀ s : Gv v, q v (z.1 (loc v s)) = σ v s m - m := by
      intro v
      have h0 : (Submodule.Quotient.mk (compatiblePairCocycles1 ρ (σ v) (loc v) (hloc v) (q v) continuous_of_discreteTopology (hq v)
          z) : H1 (σ v)) = 0 := congrFun hc v
      have h' : compatiblePairCocycles1 ρ (σ v) (loc v) (hloc v) (q v) continuous_of_discreteTopology (hq v) z ∈
          Submodule.comap (Z1 (σ v)).subtype (B1 (σ v)) := (Submodule.Quotient.mk_eq_zero _).1 h0
      exact (mem_coboundaries1 _ _).1 h'
    choose m hm using hv
    refine ⟨Submodule.Quotient.mk ⟨(z.1, fun v => r v (m v)), (IsLocallyConstant.iff_continuous _).2 z.2.1,
      z.2.2, fun v s => ?_⟩, rfl⟩
    show z.1 (loc v s) - (ρ (loc v s) (r v (m v)) - r v (m v)) ∈ LinearMap.ker (q v)
    rw [LinearMap.mem_ker, map_sub, map_sub, hq, hr, hm, sub_self]

private def kerF0EquivInvariants : LinearMap.ker (f0 loc ρ σ q hq) ≃ₗ[k] coneH0 ρ (fun v => LinearMap.ker (q v)) where
  toFun x := ⟨x.1.1, x.1.2, fun v => congrArg Subtype.val (congrFun x.2 v)⟩
  map_add' _ _ := rfl
  map_smul' _ _ := rfl
  invFun x := ⟨⟨x.1, x.2.1⟩, funext fun v => Subtype.ext (x.2.2 v)⟩
  left_inv _ := rfl
  right_inv _ := rfl

private def h2Z : continuousCocycles2 loc ρ (fun v => LinearMap.ker (q v)) →ₗ[k] Z2 ρ where
  toFun x := ⟨x.1.1, x.2.1.continuous, fun s t u => by
    have := x.2.2.2.1 s t u
    rw [sub_add_eq_add_sub, this]; abel⟩
  map_add' _ _ := rfl
  map_smul' _ _ := rfl

private def h2 : coneH2 loc ρ (fun v => LinearMap.ker (q v)) →ₗ[k] H2 ρ :=
  Submodule.mapQ _ _ (h2Z loc ρ q) fun x hx => by
    obtain ⟨h, y, a1, a3, -⟩ := hx
    exact (separated_dvr_second_cohomology_mem _ _).2 ⟨h, a1.continuous, by rw [first_differential_eq]; exact a3.symm⟩

private def g2Z : (∀ v, Z1 (σ v)) →ₗ[k] continuousCocycles2 loc ρ (fun v => LinearMap.ker (q v)) where
  toFun a := ⟨(0, fun v s => r v ((a v).1 s)), IsLocallyConstant.const 0,
    fun v => ((IsLocallyConstant.iff_continuous _).2 (a v).2.1).comp (r v), by simp,
    fun v s t => by
      change q v _ = 0
      simp only [Alg.dOne, (a v).2.2 s t, Pi.zero_apply, map_sub, map_add, MonoidHom.comp_apply, hq, hr, map_zero,
        sub_self]⟩
  map_add' _ _ := Subtype.ext (Prod.ext (by simp) (funext fun v => funext fun s => by simp))
  map_smul' _ _ := Subtype.ext (Prod.ext (by simp) (funext fun v => funext fun s => by simp))

private def g2 : (∀ v, Z1 (σ v)) →ₗ[k] coneH2 loc ρ (fun v => LinearMap.ker (q v)) :=
  Submodule.mkQ _ ∘ₗ g2Z loc ρ σ q hq r hr

private def pL : (∀ v, Z1 (σ v)) →ₗ[k] (∀ v, H1 (σ v)) where
  toFun a := fun v => Submodule.Quotient.mk (a v)
  map_add' _ _ := rfl
  map_smul' _ _ := rfl

private theorem pL_surj : Function.Surjective (pL σ) := by
  intro a
  choose a' ha using fun v => Submodule.Quotient.mk_surjective _ (a v)
  exact ⟨a', funext ha⟩

include hr in
private theorem range_h2_eq_inf_ker_pairing : LinearMap.range (h2 loc ρ q) =
    ⨅ v, LinearMap.ker (compatiblePairH2 ρ (σ v) (loc v) (hloc v) (q v) continuous_of_discreteTopology (hq v)) := by
  ext c
  rw [LinearMap.mem_range, Submodule.mem_iInf]
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨c', rfl⟩ v
    obtain ⟨x, rfl⟩ := Submodule.Quotient.mk_surjective _ c'
    rw [LinearMap.mem_ker]
    show (Submodule.Quotient.mk (compatiblePairCocycles2 ρ (σ v) (loc v) (hloc v) (q v) continuous_of_discreteTopology (hq v)
      (h2Z loc ρ q x)) : H2 (σ v)) = 0
    apply (Submodule.Quotient.mk_eq_zero _).2
    refine (separated_dvr_second_cohomology_mem _ _).2 ⟨fun s => q v (x.1.2 v s), ((x.2.2.1 v).comp (q v)).continuous, ?_⟩
    rw [first_differential_eq]
    funext p
    have h4 := x.2.2.2.2 v p.1 p.2
    rw [LinearMap.mem_ker, map_sub, sub_eq_zero] at h4
    show _ = q v (x.1.1 (loc v p.1, loc v p.2))
    rw [h4]
    simp only [Alg.dOne, map_sub, map_add, MonoidHom.comp_apply, hq]
  · intro hc
    obtain ⟨z, rfl⟩ := Submodule.Quotient.mk_surjective _ c
    have hv : ∀ v, ∃ cv : Gv v → Q v, Continuous cv ∧
        d1 (σ v) cv = fun p => q v (z.1 (loc v p.1, loc v p.2)) := by
      intro v
      have h0 : (Submodule.Quotient.mk (compatiblePairCocycles2 ρ (σ v) (loc v) (hloc v) (q v) continuous_of_discreteTopology (hq v)
          z) : H2 (σ v)) = 0 := LinearMap.mem_ker.1 (hc v)
      have h' : compatiblePairCocycles2 ρ (σ v) (loc v) (hloc v) (q v) continuous_of_discreteTopology (hq v) z ∈
          Submodule.comap (Z2 (σ v)).subtype (B2 (σ v)) := (Submodule.Quotient.mk_eq_zero _).1 h0
      exact (separated_dvr_second_cohomology_mem _ _).1 h'
    choose cv hcv hd using hv
    refine ⟨Submodule.Quotient.mk ⟨(z.1, fun v s => r v (cv v s)), (IsLocallyConstant.iff_continuous _).2 z.2.1,
      fun v => ((IsLocallyConstant.iff_continuous _).2 (hcv v)).comp (r v), fun s t u => ?_, fun v s t => ?_⟩, rfl⟩
    · have := z.2.2 s t u
      show ρ s (z.1 (t, u)) + z.1 (s, t * u) = z.1 (s * t, u) + z.1 (s, t)
      rw [← sub_eq_zero, ← this]; abel
    · show z.1 (loc v s, loc v t) - Alg.dOne (ρ.comp (loc v)) (fun s => r v (cv v s)) (s, t) ∈ LinearMap.ker (q v)
      have e := congrFun (hd v) (s, t)
      rw [first_differential_eq] at e
      rw [LinearMap.mem_ker, map_sub, ← e]
      simp only [Alg.dOne, map_sub, map_add, MonoidHom.comp_apply, hq, hr, sub_self]

include hloc in
private theorem ker_h2_eq_range_g2 : LinearMap.ker (h2 loc ρ q) = LinearMap.range (g2 loc ρ σ q hq r hr) := by
  ext c
  rw [LinearMap.mem_ker, LinearMap.mem_range]
  constructor
  · intro hc
    obtain ⟨x, rfl⟩ := Submodule.Quotient.mk_surjective _ c
    have hx : h2Z loc ρ q x ∈ Submodule.comap (Z2 ρ).subtype (B2 ρ) :=
      (Submodule.Quotient.mk_eq_zero _).1 hc
    obtain ⟨β, hβ, hd⟩ := (separated_dvr_second_cohomology_mem _ _).1 hx
    rw [first_differential_eq] at hd
    have hd' : x.1.1 = Alg.dOne ρ β := hd.symm
    have hcoc : ∀ v (s t : Gv v), q v (x.1.2 v (s * t) - β (loc v (s * t))) =
        q v (x.1.2 v s - β (loc v s)) + σ v s (q v (x.1.2 v t - β (loc v t))) := by
      intro v s t
      have h4 := x.2.2.2.2 v s t
      rw [hd', LinearMap.mem_ker] at h4
      simp only [Alg.dOne, MonoidHom.comp_apply, map_mul, map_sub, map_add, hq] at h4 ⊢
      rw [← sub_eq_zero, ← h4]; abel
    refine ⟨fun v => ⟨fun s => q v (x.1.2 v s - β (loc v s)), ?_, hcoc v⟩, ?_⟩
    · exact (continuous_of_discreteTopology (f := q v)).comp
        ((x.2.2.1 v).continuous.sub (hβ.comp (hloc v)))
    · show Submodule.Quotient.mk (g2Z loc ρ σ q hq r hr _) = Submodule.Quotient.mk x
      rw [Submodule.Quotient.eq]
      refine ⟨-β, fun _ => 0, (IsLocallyConstant.iff_continuous _).2 hβ.neg, ?_, fun v s => ?_⟩
      · show (0 : Γ × Γ → M) - x.1.1 = Alg.dOne ρ (-β)
        rw [hd']
        funext p
        simp only [Alg.dOne, Pi.sub_apply, Pi.zero_apply, Pi.neg_apply, map_neg]
        abel
      · show r v (q v (x.1.2 v s - β (loc v s))) - x.1.2 v s - ((-β) (loc v s) - (ρ (loc v s) 0 - 0)) ∈
          LinearMap.ker (q v)
        rw [LinearMap.mem_ker]
        simp only [map_sub, hr, Pi.neg_apply, map_neg, map_zero, sub_zero]
        abel
  · rintro ⟨a, rfl⟩
    show Submodule.Quotient.mk (h2Z loc ρ q (g2Z loc ρ σ q hq r hr a)) = 0
    apply (Submodule.Quotient.mk_eq_zero _).2
    exact (separated_dvr_second_cohomology_mem _ _).2 ⟨0, continuous_const, by rw [map_zero]; rfl⟩

private theorem ker_g2_eq_comap_range_f1 : LinearMap.ker (g2 loc ρ σ q hq r hr) =
    (LinearMap.range (f1 loc hloc ρ σ q hq)).comap (pL σ) := by
  ext a
  rw [LinearMap.mem_ker, Submodule.mem_comap, LinearMap.mem_range]
  constructor
  · intro ha
    have ha' : g2Z loc ρ σ q hq r hr a ∈ Submodule.comap (continuousCocycles2 loc ρ fun v => LinearMap.ker (q v)).subtype
        (continuousCoboundaries2 loc ρ fun v => LinearMap.ker (q v)) := (Submodule.Quotient.mk_eq_zero _).1 ha
    obtain ⟨β, Y, hβ, h0, hY⟩ := ha'
    have hz : β ∈ Z1 ρ := by
      refine ⟨hβ.continuous, fun s t => ?_⟩
      have e : (0 : M) = β s + ρ s (β t) - β (s * t) := congrFun h0 (s, t)
      rw [eq_sub_iff_add_eq, zero_add] at e
      exact e
    refine ⟨Submodule.Quotient.mk ⟨β, hz⟩, ?_⟩
    funext v
    show (Submodule.Quotient.mk (compatiblePairCocycles1 ρ (σ v) (loc v) (hloc v) (q v) continuous_of_discreteTopology (hq v)
      ⟨β, hz⟩) : H1 (σ v)) = Submodule.Quotient.mk (a v)
    rw [Submodule.Quotient.eq]
    refine (mem_coboundaries1 _ _).2 ⟨q v (Y v), fun s => ?_⟩
    have h3 : r v ((a v).1 s) - (β (loc v s) - (ρ (loc v s) (Y v) - Y v)) ∈ LinearMap.ker (q v) := hY v s
    rw [LinearMap.mem_ker, map_sub, map_sub, map_sub, hr, hq, sub_eq_zero] at h3
    show q v (β (loc v s)) - (a v).1 s = σ v s (q v (Y v)) - q v (Y v)
    rw [h3]; abel
  · rintro ⟨c, hc⟩
    obtain ⟨z, rfl⟩ := Submodule.Quotient.mk_surjective _ c
    have hv : ∀ v, ∃ m, ∀ s, q v (z.1 (loc v s)) - (a v).1 s = σ v s m - m := by
      intro v
      have h1' : (Submodule.Quotient.mk (compatiblePairCocycles1 ρ (σ v) (loc v) (hloc v) (q v) continuous_of_discreteTopology (hq v)
          z) : H1 (σ v)) = Submodule.Quotient.mk (a v) := congrFun hc v
      rw [Submodule.Quotient.eq] at h1'
      exact (mem_coboundaries1 _ _).1 h1'
    choose m hm using hv
    show Submodule.Quotient.mk (g2Z loc ρ σ q hq r hr a) = 0
    apply (Submodule.Quotient.mk_eq_zero _).2
    refine ⟨z.1, fun v => r v (m v), (IsLocallyConstant.iff_continuous _).2 z.2.1, ?_, fun v s => ?_⟩
    · funext p
      show (0 : M) = z.1 p.1 + ρ p.1 (z.1 p.2) - z.1 (p.1 * p.2)
      rw [z.2.2, sub_self]
    · show r v ((a v).1 s) - (z.1 (loc v s) - (ρ (loc v s) (r v (m v)) - r v (m v))) ∈ LinearMap.ker (q v)
      rw [LinearMap.mem_ker]
      simp only [map_sub, hq, hr]
      rw [← hm v s]; abel

end UpperTheory.V.GC.ConeCnt

end

open UpperTheory.V.GC.ConeCnt in
theorem UpperTheory.V.GC.coneCount :
    ∀ (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (Γ : Type) [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [IsTopologicalGroup Γ] [CompactSpace Γ] [T2Space Γ] [TotallyDisconnectedSpace Γ] (V : Type) [Fintype V] (Gv : V → Type) [∀ v, Group (Gv v)] [∀ v, TopologicalSpace (Gv v)] [∀ v, IsTopologicalGroup (Gv v)] [∀ v, CompactSpace (Gv v)] [∀ v, T2Space (Gv v)] [∀ v, TotallyDisconnectedSpace (Gv v)] (loc : ∀ v, Gv v →* Γ) (hloc : ∀ v, Continuous (loc v)) (k : Type) [Field k] [Finite k] [CharP k l] (M : Type) [AddCommGroup M] [Module k M] [Module.Finite k M] [TopologicalSpace M] [DiscreteTopology M] (ρ : Representation k Γ M), ContAction ρ →
    ∀ (Q : V → Type) [∀ v, AddCommGroup (Q v)] [∀ v, Module k (Q v)] [∀ v, TopologicalSpace (Q v)] [∀ v, DiscreteTopology (Q v)] (σ : ∀ v, Representation k (Gv v) (Q v)) (q : ∀ v, M →ₗ[k] Q v)
    (hq : ∀ v s m, q v (ρ (loc v s) m) = σ v s (q v m)), (∀ v, Function.Surjective (q v)) → Module.Finite k (H1 ρ) → Module.Finite k (H2 ρ) → (∀ v, Module.Finite k (H1 (σ v))) →
    Module.Finite k (H1C loc ρ fun v => LinearMap.ker (q v)) ∧ Module.Finite k (coneH2 loc ρ fun v => LinearMap.ker (q v)) ∧
    finrank k (coneH2 loc ρ fun v => LinearMap.ker (q v)) + finrank k (coneH0 ρ fun v => LinearMap.ker (q v)) + finrank k (H1 ρ) + ∑ v, finrank k (H0 (σ v)) =
      finrank k (H1C loc ρ fun v => LinearMap.ker (q v)) + finrank k (H0 ρ) + ∑ v, finrank k (H1 (σ v)) +
      finrank k (⨅ v, LinearMap.ker (compatiblePairH2 ρ (σ v) (loc v) (hloc v) (q v) continuous_of_discreteTopology (hq v)) : Submodule k (H2 ρ)) := by
  intro l _ Γ _ _ _ _ _ _ V _ Gv _ _ _ _ _ _ loc hloc k _ _ _ M _ _ _ _ _ ρ _ Q _ _ _ _ σ q hq hqs hH1 hH2 hH1v
  obtain ⟨r, hr⟩ : ∃ r : ∀ v, Q v →ₗ[k] M, ∀ v y, q v (r v y) = y := by
    choose r hr using fun v => (q v).exists_rightInverse_of_surjective (LinearMap.range_eq_top.2 (hqs v))
    exact ⟨r, fun v y => LinearMap.congr_fun (hr v) y⟩
  haveI : ∀ v, Module.Finite k (Q v) := fun v => Module.Finite.of_surjective (q v) (hqs v)
  obtain ⟨fin1, e1⟩ := count (f0 loc ρ σ q hq) LinearMap.id Function.surjective_id (g1 loc ρ σ q hq r hr)
    (h1 loc ρ q) (ker_g1_eq_range_f0 loc ρ σ q hq r hr) (ker_h1_eq_range_g1 loc ρ σ q hq r hr)
  obtain ⟨fin2, e2⟩ := count (f1 loc hloc ρ σ q hq) (pL σ) (pL_surj σ) (g2 loc ρ σ q hq r hr)
    (h2 loc ρ q) (ker_g2_eq_comap_range_f1 loc hloc ρ σ q hq r hr) (ker_h2_eq_range_g2 loc hloc ρ σ q hq r hr)
  refine ⟨fin1, fin2, ?_⟩
  rw [range_h1_eq_ker_f1 loc hloc ρ σ q hq r hr] at e1
  rw [range_h2_eq_inf_ker_pairing loc hloc ρ σ q hq r hr] at e2
  rw [(kerF0EquivInvariants loc ρ σ q hq).finrank_eq, Module.finrank_pi_fintype] at e1
  rw [Module.finrank_pi_fintype] at e2
  omega


[LFC_SRC_EC] Full GC.ec

private theorem cast_toZModPow_one (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (k : Type) [Field k] [CharP k l] (x : ℤ_[l]) :
    ZMod.castHom (dvd_pow_self l one_ne_zero) k (PadicInt.toZModPow 1 x) =
      ZMod.castHom (dvd_refl l) k (PadicInt.toZMod x) := by
  have hx : x - (PadicInt.zmodRepr x : ℤ_[l]) ∈ IsLocalRing.maximalIdeal ℤ_[l] := PadicInt.sub_zmodRepr_mem x
  have h1 : PadicInt.toZMod (x - (PadicInt.zmodRepr x : ℤ_[l])) = 0 := by
    rw [← RingHom.mem_ker, PadicInt.ker_toZMod]; exact hx
  have h2 : PadicInt.toZModPow 1 (x - (PadicInt.zmodRepr x : ℤ_[l])) = 0 := by
    rw [← RingHom.mem_ker, PadicInt.ker_toZModPow, pow_one, ← PadicInt.maximalIdeal_eq_span_p]; exact hx
  rw [map_sub, sub_eq_zero] at h1 h2
  rw [h1, h2]
  simp only [map_natCast]

private theorem sum_deg (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (F : Type) [Field F] [NumberField F] {V : Type} [Fintype V]
    (pl : V → HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F)) (hi : Function.Injective pl) (hm : ∀ v, ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 F) ∈ (pl v).asIdeal) :
    ∑ v, locDeg (pl v) ≤ finrank ℚ F := by
  classical
  have hl : l.Prime := Fact.out
  have hmax : (Ideal.span {(l : ℤ)}).IsMaximal :=
    PrincipalIdealRing.isMaximal_of_irreducible (Nat.prime_iff_prime_int.1 hl).irreducible
  have hsum := Ideal.sum_ramification_inertia_eq_finrank (Ideal.span {(l : ℤ)}) (𝓞 F)
  rw [RingOfIntegers.rank] at hsum
  have hover : ∀ v, (pl v).asIdeal ∈ (Ideal.span {(l : ℤ)}).primesOver (𝓞 F) := fun v => by
    refine ⟨(pl v).isPrime, ⟨hmax.eq_of_le (Ideal.comap_ne_top _ (pl v).isPrime.ne_top) ?_⟩⟩
    rw [Ideal.span_singleton_le_iff_mem, Ideal.mem_comap]
    simpa using hm v
  let e : V → (Ideal.span {(l : ℤ)}).primesOver (𝓞 F) := fun v => ⟨(pl v).asIdeal, hover v⟩
  have he : Function.Injective e := fun a b h => hi (HeightOneSpectrum.ext (congrArg Subtype.val h))
  rw [← hsum]
  refine le_trans (le_of_eq ?_) (Finset.sum_le_sum_of_subset (Finset.subset_univ (Finset.univ.map ⟨e, he⟩)))
  rw [Finset.sum_map]
  rfl

section

variable {k G H M : Type} [CommRing k] [Group G] [TopologicalSpace G] [Group H] [TopologicalSpace H]
  [AddCommGroup M] [Module k M] [TopologicalSpace M] [DiscreteTopology M]

private theorem resH1_surjective_of_isomorphism (τ : Representation k H M) (j : G →* H) (hj : Continuous j) (r : H →* G) (hr : Continuous r)
    (h1 : ∀ x, j (r x) = x) (h2 : ∀ x, r (j x) = x) : Function.Surjective (resH1 τ j hj) := by
  intro x
  obtain ⟨z, rfl⟩ := Submodule.Quotient.mk_surjective _ x
  refine ⟨Submodule.Quotient.mk ⟨z.1 ∘ r, z.2.1.comp hr, fun g h => ?_⟩, ?_⟩
  · have := z.2.2 (r g) (r h)
    simp only [MonoidHom.comp_apply, h1] at this
    simp only [Function.comp_apply, map_mul, this]
  · refine congrArg Submodule.Quotient.mk (Subtype.ext (funext fun g => ?_))
    change z.1 (r (j g)) = z.1 g
    rw [h2]

private theorem ker_le {Γ W : Type} [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [AddCommGroup W] [Module k W] [TopologicalSpace W]
    [DiscreteTopology W] (ρ : Representation k Γ W) (τ : Representation k H M) (j : G →* H) (r : H →* G)
    (hr : Continuous r) (h1 : ∀ x, j (r x) = x) (a : G →* Γ) (ha : Continuous a) (b : H →* Γ) (hb : Continuous b)
    (hab : ∀ x, a x = b (j x)) (q : W →ₗ[k] M) (hq : Continuous q)
    (ea : ∀ s m, q (ρ (a s) m) = (τ.comp j) s (q m)) (eb : ∀ s m, q (ρ (b s) m) = τ s (q m)) :
    LinearMap.ker (compatiblePairH2 ρ (τ.comp j) a ha q hq ea) ≤ LinearMap.ker (compatiblePairH2 ρ τ b hb q hq eb) := by
  intro x hx
  obtain ⟨z, rfl⟩ := Submodule.Quotient.mk_surjective _ x
  obtain ⟨c, hc, hz⟩ := (separated_dvr_second_cohomology_mem _ _).1 ((Submodule.Quotient.mk_eq_zero _).1 hx)
  refine (Submodule.Quotient.mk_eq_zero _).2 ((separated_dvr_second_cohomology_mem _ _).2 ⟨c ∘ r, hc.comp hr, funext fun p => ?_⟩)
  have := congrFun hz (r p.1, r p.2)
  change τ (j (r p.1)) (c (r p.2)) - c (r p.1 * r p.2) + c (r p.1) = q (z.1 (a (r p.1), a (r p.2))) at this
  rw [hab, hab, h1, h1, ← map_mul] at this
  exact this

private theorem contAct [IsTopologicalGroup G] (ρ : Representation k G M) (h : IsOpen (ρ.ker : Set G)) :
    ContAction ρ := by
  refine continuous_prod_of_discrete_right.2 fun m => ?_
  refine IsLocallyConstant.continuous ((IsLocallyConstant.iff_exists_open _).2 fun g => ?_)
  refine ⟨(fun x => g⁻¹ * x) ⁻¹' (ρ.ker : Set G), h.preimage (by fun_prop), by simp, fun x hx => ?_⟩
  have hx' : ρ (g⁻¹ * x) = 1 := hx
  have : ρ x = ρ g := by rw [← mul_inv_cancel_left g x, map_mul, hx', mul_one]
  simp only [this]

end

theorem UpperTheory.V.GC.ec :
    Hypotheses₂ → ∀ (F Ω : Type) [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] [IsAlgClosure F Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (k : Type) [Field k] [CharP k l] (Φ : Fr.Frame l k) (S : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))) (K : Subgroup (G F Ω)) (π : G F Ω →* Φ.Γ) (ι : ∀ v, Φ.Gv v →* G F Ω) (A : Φ.V → ValuationSubring Ω) (pl : Φ.V → HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F)) (e : Φ.Inf ≃ InfinitePlace F) (c : Φ.Inf → G F Ω), FLink l Φ S K π ι A pl e c → Φ.EC := by
  intro hP F Ω _ _ _ _ _ l _ k _ _ Φ S K π ι A pl e c hL E W _ _ _ _ _ _ ρ b hop hb hdual
  classical
  letI : TopologicalSpace W := ⊥
  haveI : DiscreteTopology W := ⟨rfl⟩
  letI : ∀ v, TopologicalSpace (W ⧸ b v) := fun _ => ⊥
  haveI : ∀ v, DiscreteTopology (W ⧸ b v) := fun _ => ⟨rfl⟩
  have hρ : ContAction ρ := contAct ρ hop
  have hpl : ∀ v, ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 F) ∈ (pl v).asIdeal := fun v => (hL.loc.mem _).1 ⟨v, rfl⟩
  let u0 : Φ.V → S := fun v => ⟨pl v, hL.g.above _ (hpl v)⟩
  have hu0 : Function.Bijective u0 := ⟨fun x y h => hL.loc.inj (congrArg Subtype.val h), fun w => by
    obtain ⟨v, hv⟩ := (hL.loc.mem w.1).2 (hL.only w.1 w.2)
    exact ⟨v, Subtype.ext hv⟩⟩
  let u : S ≃ Φ.V := (Equiv.ofBijective u0 hu0).symm
  have hu : ∀ w : S, pl (u w) = w.1 := fun w => congrArg Subtype.val (u.symm_apply_apply w)
  have hA : ∀ w : S, IsPlaceOver (A (u w)) w.1 := fun w => hu w ▸ hL.loc.isOver (u w)
  have hc : ∀ w : InfinitePlace F, IsCCAt w (c (e.symm w)) := fun w => by
    simpa using hL.cc (e.symm w)
  have hcyc : ∀ g, Φ.om E (π g) = cycF F Ω l E g := fun g => by
    apply Units.ext
    have := cast_toZModPow_one l E (cycZ F Ω l g : ℤ_[l])
    simp only [Frame.om, cycF, cycMod, MonoidHom.comp_apply, hL.eps, Units.coe_map]
    exact this.symm
  have hD : Loc.H0dual (cycF F Ω l E) (ρ.comp π) = ⊥ := by
    refine (Submodule.eq_bot_iff _).2 fun f hf => hdual f fun g X => ?_
    obtain ⟨g, rfl⟩ := hL.g.onto g
    rw [hcyc]
    exact hf g X
  let j : ∀ v, Φ.Gv v →* Dec (F := F) (A v) := fun v =>
    (ι v).codRestrict _ fun s => by rw [← hL.loc.range]; exact ⟨s, rfl⟩
  have hjc : ∀ v, Continuous (j v) := fun v => (hL.loc.cont v).subtype_mk _
  have hjb : ∀ v, Function.Bijective (j v) := fun v =>
    ⟨fun x y h => hL.loc.ι_inj v (congrArg Subtype.val h), fun x => by
      have hx : x.1 ∈ (ι v).range := by rw [hL.loc.range]; exact x.2
      obtain ⟨s, hs⟩ := hx
      exact ⟨s, Subtype.ext hs⟩⟩
  let r : ∀ v, Dec (F := F) (A v) →* Φ.Gv v := fun v => (MulEquiv.ofBijective (j v) (hjb v)).symm.toMonoidHom
  have hrc : ∀ v, Continuous (r v) := fun v =>
    Continuous.continuous_symm_of_equiv_compact_to_t2 (f := (MulEquiv.ofBijective (j v) (hjb v)).toEquiv) (hjc v)
  have h1 : ∀ v x, j v (r v x) = x := fun v => (MulEquiv.ofBijective (j v) (hjb v)).apply_symm_apply
  have h2 : ∀ v x, r v (j v x) = x := fun v => (MulEquiv.ofBijective (j v) (hjb v)).symm_apply_apply
  let d : ∀ v, Dec (F := F) (A v) →* Φ.Γ := fun v => π.comp (Dec (F := F) (A v)).subtype
  have hdc : ∀ v, Continuous (d v) := fun v => hL.g.cont.comp continuous_subtype_val
  have hdj : ∀ v s, Φ.loc v s = d v (j v s) := hL.loc_eq
  have hlc : ∀ v, Continuous (Φ.loc v) := fun v => ((hdc v).comp (hjc v)).congr fun s => (hdj v s).symm
  let τ : ∀ v, Representation E (Dec (F := F) (A v)) (W ⧸ b v) := fun v =>
    Representation.quotient (ρ.comp (d v)) (b v) fun g X hX => by
      have := hb v (r v g) X hX
      rw [hdj, h1] at this
      exact this
  have hτq : ∀ v s m, (b v).mkQ (ρ (d v s) m) = τ v s ((b v).mkQ m) := fun _ _ _ => rfl
  have hσq : ∀ v s m, (b v).mkQ (ρ (Φ.loc v s) m) = ((τ v).comp (j v)) s ((b v).mkQ m) := fun v s m => by
    rw [hdj]; rfl
  have hτ : ∀ v, ContAction (τ v) := fun v => by
    refine continuous_prod_of_discrete_right.2 fun m => ?_
    obtain ⟨m, rfl⟩ := (b v).mkQ_surjective m
    exact (continuous_of_discreteTopology (f := (b v).mkQ)).comp (hρ.comp ((hdc v).prodMk continuous_const))
  have hE := fun v => locEuler hP.lf hP.lt hP.le F Ω l (pl v) (A v) (hL.loc.isOver v) E (W ⧸ b v) (τ v) (hτ v)
  have hon := fun v => resH1_surjective_of_isomorphism (τ v) (j v) (hjc v) (r v) (hrc v) (h1 v) (h2 v)
  obtain ⟨f1, f2, -⟩ := H2_galois_quotient_eq hP F Ω l hL.real S K Φ.Γ π hL.g E W ρ hρ _ hc _ hA
  have hO := H2_galois_quotient_surjective hP F Ω l hL.real S K Φ.Γ π hL.g E W ρ hρ _ hc _ hA hD (fun w => W ⧸ b (u w))
    (fun w => τ (u w)) (fun w => (b (u w)).mkQ) (fun w => hτq (u w)) fun w => (b (u w)).mkQ_surjective
  have hC := coneCount l Φ.Γ Φ.V Φ.Gv Φ.loc hlc E W ρ hρ (fun v => W ⧸ b v) (fun v => (τ v).comp (j v))
    (fun v => (b v).mkQ) hσq (fun v => (b v).mkQ_surjective) f1 f2
    fun v => haveI := (hE v).1; Module.Finite.of_surjective _ (hon v)
  have hbq : (fun v => LinearMap.ker (b v).mkQ) = b := funext fun v => Submodule.ker_mkQ _
  rw [hbq] at hC
  obtain ⟨-, g2, hC⟩ := hC
  refine ⟨g2, ?_⟩
  have hK := Submodule.finrank_mono (s := ⨅ v, LinearMap.ker (compatiblePairH2 ρ ((τ v).comp (j v)) (Φ.loc v) (hlc v) (b v).mkQ
      continuous_of_discreteTopology (hσq v)))
    (t := ⨅ w : S, LinearMap.ker (compatiblePairH2 ρ (τ (u w)) (π.comp (Dec (F := F) (A (u w))).subtype) (hdc (u w)) (b (u w)).mkQ
      continuous_of_discreteTopology (hτq (u w))))
    (le_iInf fun w => (iInf_le _ (u w)).trans
      (ker_le ρ _ (j _) (r _) (hrc _) (h1 _) _ _ _ _ (hdj _) _ _ _ _))
  have s2 : ∑ w : S, finrank E (H2 (τ (u w))) = ∑ v, finrank E (H2 (τ v)) :=
    u.sum_comp fun v => finrank E (H2 (τ v))
  have sR : ∑ w : InfinitePlace F, finrank E (LinearMap.ker (ρ (π (c (e.symm w))) - 1)) =
      ∑ w, finrank E (LinearMap.ker (ρ (Φ.cw w) - 1)) := by
    rw [e.symm.sum_comp fun w => finrank E (LinearMap.ker (ρ (π (c w)) - 1))]
    exact Finset.sum_congr rfl fun w _ => by rw [hL.cw]
  have hloc : ∀ v, finrank E (H1 ((τ v).comp (j v))) + Φ.dv v * finrank E (b v) ≤
      finrank E (H0 ((τ v).comp (j v))) + finrank E (H2 (τ v)) + locDeg (pl v) * finrank E W := fun v => by
    haveI := (hE v).1
    have a1 := LinearMap.finrank_le_finrank_of_surjective (hon v)
    have a2 := (hE v).2.2.1 (hpl v)
    have a3 : H0 ((τ v).comp (j v)) = H0 (τ v) := le_antisymm
      (fun m hm g => by have := hm (r v g); rwa [MonoidHom.comp_apply, h1] at this) fun m hm g => hm (j v g)
    rw [a3, hL.dv, ← (b v).finrank_quotient_add_finrank, mul_add]
    omega
  have hsum := Finset.sum_le_sum fun v (_ : v ∈ Finset.univ) => hloc v
  rw [Finset.sum_add_distrib, Finset.sum_add_distrib, Finset.sum_add_distrib, ← Finset.sum_mul] at hsum
  have hdeg := Nat.mul_le_mul_right (finrank E W) (sum_deg l F pl hL.loc.inj hpl)
  rw [s2, sR] at hO
  omega

open NumberField Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal IsLocalRing UpperTheory.V.HV UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.V.Pr UpperTheory.V.Fr UpperTheory.V

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.clrk_tr :
    ∀ (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (F : Type) [Field F] [NumberField F] [Algebra F Ω] (U : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)), U ≤ galOf ℚ F Ω → GC.clRk l (U.comap (galEmb ℚ F Ω)) = GC.clRk l U := by
  intro Ω _ _ _ l _ F _ _ _ U hU
  haveI : Module.IsTorsionFree F Ω := DivisionSemiring.to_moduleIsTorsionFree
  haveI : IsAlgClosure F Ω := ⟨IsAlgClosure.isAlgClosed ℚ, Algebra.IsAlgebraic.tower_top (K := ℚ) F⟩
  have hemb := (galEmbTop ℚ F Ω).1
  have hfam := (galEmbFam ℚ F Ω l).1
  let e : U.comap (galEmb ℚ F Ω) ≃ U :=
    { toFun := fun u => ⟨galEmb ℚ F Ω u.1, u.2⟩
      invFun := fun u => ⟨Kum.toGal F hU u, u.2⟩
      left_inv := fun _ => rfl
      right_inv := fun _ => rfl }
  have hc : Continuous e := (hemb.continuous.comp continuous_subtype_val).subtype_mk _
  have hc' : Continuous e.symm := (hemb.continuous_iff.2 continuous_subtype_val).subtype_mk _
  have hI : ∀ (A : ValuationSubring Ω) (σ : G F Ω), galEmb ℚ F Ω σ ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) A ↔ σ ∈ Ine (F := F) A :=
    fun A σ => SetLike.ext_iff.1 (hfam A).2 σ
  have hD : ∀ (A : ValuationSubring Ω) (σ : G F Ω), galEmb ℚ F Ω σ ∈ Dec (F := ℚ) A ↔ σ ∈ Dec (F := F) A :=
    fun A σ => SetLike.ext_iff.1 (hfam A).1 σ
  have key : (Kum.XDual 1 U (ZMod l) (ZMod l)).map (LinearEquiv.funCongrLeft (ZMod l) (ZMod l) e).toLinearMap =
      Kum.XDual 1 (U.comap (galEmb ℚ F Ω)) (ZMod l) (ZMod l) := by
    ext φ
    constructor
    · rintro ⟨ψ, ⟨h1, h2, h3, h4⟩, rfl⟩
      exact ⟨h1.comp_continuous hc, fun a b => h2 (e a) (e b), fun A u h => h3 A (e u) ((hI A _).2 h),
        fun A hA u h => h4 A hA (e u) ((hD A _).2 h)⟩
    · rintro ⟨h1, h2, h3, h4⟩
      exact ⟨φ ∘ e.symm, ⟨h1.comp_continuous hc', fun a b => h2 (e.symm a) (e.symm b),
        fun A u h => h3 A (e.symm u) ((hI A _).1 h), fun A hA u h => h4 A hA (e.symm u) ((hD A _).1 h)⟩, rfl⟩
  unfold GC.clRk
  rw [← key]
  exact ((LinearEquiv.funCongrLeft (ZMod l) (ZMod l) e).submoduleMap _).rank_eq.symm

open NumberField Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal IsLocalRing UpperTheory.V.HV UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.V.Pr UpperTheory.V.Fr UpperTheory.V

private theorem cycMod_emb (F Ω : Type) [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] [Algebra F Ω]
    (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (g : G F Ω) : cycMod ℚ Ω l 1 (galEmb ℚ F Ω g) = cycMod F Ω l 1 g := rfl

private theorem one_of_cast (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (k : Type) [Field k] [CharP k l] (x : ℤ_[l])
    (h : ZMod.castHom (dvd_refl l) k (PadicInt.toZMod x) = 1) : PadicInt.toZModPow 1 x = 1 := by
  have h1 : x - 1 ∈ RingHom.ker (PadicInt.toZMod : ℤ_[l] →+* ZMod l) := by
    rw [RingHom.mem_ker, map_sub, map_one, sub_eq_zero]
    exact (ZMod.castHom (dvd_refl l) k).injective (h.trans (map_one _).symm)
  rw [PadicInt.ker_toZMod, PadicInt.maximalIdeal_eq_span_p, ← pow_one (l : ℤ_[l]), ← PadicInt.ker_toZModPow,
    RingHom.mem_ker, map_sub, map_one, sub_eq_zero] at h1
  exact h1

private theorem rk_link {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (L : Type) [Field L]
    [NumberField L] [IsCMField L] [Algebra L Ω] (A A' : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)) :
    GC.clRk l ((A ⊓ A').comap (galEmb ℚ (W.Lp L) Ω) ⊓ (cycMod (W.Lp L) Ω l 1).ker) =
      GC.clRk l (upl Ω L ⊓ A ⊓ A' ⊓ (cycMod ℚ Ω l 1).ker) := by
  have hU : upl Ω L ⊓ A ⊓ A' ⊓ (cycMod ℚ Ω l 1).ker ≤ galOf ℚ (W.Lp L) Ω :=
    fun g hg x => hg.1.1.1 x.1 x.2
  rw [← clrk_tr Ω l (W.Lp L) _ hU]
  congr 1
  ext g
  simp only [Subgroup.mem_inf, Subgroup.mem_comap, MonoidHom.mem_ker]
  rw [cycMod_emb]
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨⟨ha, ha'⟩, hc⟩
    exact ⟨⟨⟨fun x hx => galEmb_mem ℚ (W.Lp L) Ω g ⟨x, hx⟩, ha⟩, ha'⟩, hc⟩
  · rintro ⟨⟨⟨_, ha⟩, ha'⟩, hc⟩
    exact ⟨⟨ha, ha'⟩, hc⟩

private theorem ok_link (P : GC.Hypotheses₂) {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type}
    [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [CharP (ResidueField O) l] {η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ} {Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ} {σ : G ℚ Ω}
    {B : ValuationSubring Ω} {M : ℕ} {Hb : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)} (hG : Gen l η Ψ σ B M Hb) (m C₀ : ℕ) (L : Type) [Field L]
    [NumberField L] [IsCMField L] [Algebra L Ω] (g₁ : G ℚ Ω) (Φ : Frame l (ResidueField O))
    (hU : UFacts l η Ψ M m (upl Ω L)) (hL : Linked l η Ψ σ g₁ B M L Φ) (hg : barChar O Ψ g₁ ^ 2 ≠ 1)
    (hS : ∀ a : ℕ, a = 1 ∨ a = 2 → StrictCl l η Ψ ((barChar O Ψ)⁻¹ ^ a * etaO O η) (upl Ω L))
    (hC : GC.clRk l (upl Ω L ⊓ η.ker ⊓ (barChar O Ψ).ker ⊓ (cycMod ℚ Ω l 1).ker) ≤ C₀) (hm : 3 * C₀ + 7 < l ^ m) :
    Φ.Ok C₀ ∧ Φ.Big C₀ := by
  haveI : IsAlgClosure (W.Lp L) Ω :=
    ⟨IsAlgClosure.isAlgClosed ℚ, Algebra.IsAlgebraic.tower_top (K := ℚ) (W.Lp L)⟩
  have hb := ok_basic l η Ψ σ B M Hb hG m L g₁ Φ hU hL hg
  obtain ⟨h_del_open, h_csq, h_delc, h_loc, h_eps, h_Ψopen, h_Ψc, h_g0, h_card, h_cw, h_delcw,
    h_f2a, h_f2b, h_f2c, h_f3, h_f5, h_ΨN, h_dv, h_ne⟩ := hb
  have hf4 := frobenius_galoisField_four_compatibility l η Ψ σ B M Hb hG m L g₁ Φ hU hL 1 (Or.inl rfl) (hS 1 (Or.inl rfl))
  have hf4' := frobenius_galoisField_four_compatibility l η Ψ σ B M Hb hG m L g₁ Φ hU hL 2 (Or.inr rfl) (hS 2 (Or.inr rfl))
  rw [pow_one] at hf4
  obtain ⟨k, S, π, ι, pl, e, c, hF, hπ, hι⟩ := hL
  have hrk : GC.clRk l (((η.ker ⊓ (barChar O Ψ).ker).comap (galEmb ℚ (W.Lp L) Ω)) ⊓
      (cycMod (W.Lp L) Ω l 1).ker) ≤ C₀ := by
    rw [rk_link l L η.ker (barChar O Ψ).ker]
    exact hC
  have hom : ∀ v : Φ.V, ∃ s, (Φ.om (ResidueField O)) ((Φ.loc v) s) ≠ 1 := by
    intro v
    obtain ⟨g, hg'⟩ := k.mu v
    refine ⟨g, fun h => hg' ?_⟩
    rw [k.j_loc] at h
    simp only [Frame.om, MonoidHom.comp_apply, k.eps] at h
    have h1 := congrArg Units.val h
    simp only [Units.coe_map, Units.val_one, MonoidHom.coe_coe, RingHom.coe_comp,
      Function.comp_apply] at h1
    apply Units.ext
    simp only [cycMod, MonoidHom.comp_apply, Units.coe_map, MonoidHom.coe_coe, Units.val_one]
    exact one_of_cast l (ResidueField O) _ h1
  have hfin : Finite (ResidueField O) := hG.fin
  have hok : Φ.Ok C₀ :=
    { l_odd := hG.odd
      fin := hfin
      del_open := h_del_open
      c_sq := h_csq
      del_c := h_delc
      loc_cont := h_loc
      eps_cont := h_eps
      Ψ_open := h_Ψopen
      Ψ_c := h_Ψc
      baseSubgroup_spec := h_g0
      phi := GC.phi _ Ω l _ Φ S _ π ι k.pl pl e c hF
      card_inf := h_card
      cw_sq := h_cw
      del_cw := h_delcw
      f2a := h_f2a
      f2b := h_f2b
      f2c := h_f2c
      f3 := h_f3
      f4 := hf4
      f4' := hf4'
      f5 := h_f5
      Ψ_N := h_ΨN
      gen := GC.gen P _ Ω l _ Φ S _ π ι k.pl pl e c hF hom
      cl := GC.cl _ Ω l _ Φ S _ π ι k.pl pl e c hF
      ec := GC.ec P _ Ω l _ Φ S _ π ι k.pl pl e c hF
      b1p := GC.b1p _ Ω l _ Φ S _ π ι k.pl pl e c hF h_f3 C₀ hrk
      b1 := GC.b1 _ Ω l _ Φ S _ π ι k.pl pl e c hF h_f3 C₀ hrk
      h1 := GC.h1 P _ Ω l _ Φ S _ π ι k.pl pl e c hF hfin hom C₀ hrk }
  refine ⟨hok, ?_, ?_⟩
  · obtain ⟨v⟩ := h_ne
    have h1 : Φ.dv v ≤ Φ.d := by
      unfold Frame.d
      exact Finset.single_le_sum (fun _ _ => Nat.zero_le _) (Finset.mem_univ v)
    have := h_dv v
    omega
  · intro v
    have := h_dv v
    omega

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.frame_ex :
    GC.Hypotheses₂ → ∀ {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [CharP (ResidueField O) l] (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ) (σ : G ℚ Ω) (B : ValuationSubring Ω) (M : ℕ) (Hb : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)), Gen l η Ψ σ B M Hb → ∀ Q : ℕ, Q ≠ 0 → HasFrameW l η Ψ σ B M Q := by
  intro P Ω _ _ _ l _ O _ _ _ η Ψ σ B M Hb hG Q hQ
  obtain ⟨H₀, h0⟩ := exists_twistCharacter_continuousCohomology l η Ψ σ B M Hb hG
  obtain ⟨w, hw, hwQ⟩ := auxw_ex l η Ψ σ B M Hb hG Q hQ
  obtain ⟨C₀, hC₀⟩ := b0 l η Ψ σ B M Hb hG H₀ w h0 hw
  have hl : l.Prime := Fact.out
  set m := 3 * C₀ + 7 with hm_def
  have hm : 3 * C₀ + 7 < l ^ m := Nat.lt_pow_self hl.one_lt
  have hU := ufacts l η Ψ σ B M Hb hG H₀ w h0 hw m
  obtain ⟨L, iF, iN, iC, iA, hab, hup, hgal⟩ := field_ex l η Ψ σ B M Hb hG m _ hU
  have hM : B.valuation (M : Ω) < 1 := by
    obtain ⟨t, ht⟩ := hG.l_dvd
    rw [ht, Nat.cast_mul, map_mul]
    have hle : B.valuation (t : Ω) ≤ 1 := B.valuation_le_one ⟨(t : Ω), natCast_mem B t⟩
    exact lt_of_le_of_lt (mul_le_of_le_one_right' hle) hG.over_l
  obtain ⟨s, ⟨hsD, hsU⟩, hs2⟩ := hU.f3 B hG.over_l
  have hsη : s ∈ η.ker := hU.split B hM ⟨hsD, hsU⟩
  have hs : s ∈ galOf ℚ L Ω := by
    rw [hgal]
    exact ⟨hsU, hsη⟩
  rw [← hup] at hU
  obtain ⟨Φ, hL⟩ := link_ex l η Ψ σ B M Hb hG m L s hU hab (by rw [hgal, hup]) hs
  have hS : ∀ a : ℕ, a = 1 ∨ a = 2 → StrictCl l η Ψ ((barChar O Ψ)⁻¹ ^ a * etaO O η) (upl Ω L) := by
    intro a ha
    rw [hup]
    exact strict P l η Ψ σ B M Hb hG H₀ w h0 hw m a ha
  have hC : GC.clRk l (upl Ω L ⊓ η.ker ⊓ (barChar O Ψ).ker ⊓ (cycMod ℚ Ω l 1).ker) ≤ C₀ := by
    rw [hup]
    exact hC₀ m
  obtain ⟨hok, hbig⟩ := ok_link P l hG m C₀ L s Φ hU hL hs2 hS hC hm
  refine ⟨L, iF, iN, iC, iA, s, Φ, C₀, w, hL, hok, hbig, hw, hwQ, ?_⟩
  rw [hup]
  intro x hx
  exact hx.1.1.2


[LFC_SRC_SEED] Original coefficient-DVR seed_cl, inherited CAF7

private theorem km_mono {Ω C : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [Monoid C] {m n : ℕ} (h : m ∣ n) {χ : G ℚ Ω →* C}
    (hχ : Af.KillsMu m χ) : Af.KillsMu n χ := fun s hs => hχ s fun ζ hζ => hs ζ (by
  obtain ⟨k, rfl⟩ := h
  rw [pow_mul, hζ, one_pow])

private theorem split_of {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {N w : ℕ} (hw : w ≡ 1 [MOD N])
    {χ : G ℚ Ω →* (Qbar l)ˣ} (hχ : Af.KillsMu N χ) : Sd.SplitAt N w χ :=
  ⟨hχ, fun s hs => hχ s fun ζ hζ => by
    rw [hs ζ hζ, pow_eq_pow_mod w hζ, hw, ← pow_eq_pow_mod 1 hζ, pow_one]⟩

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.seed_cl :
    ∀ (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) [CharP (ResidueField c.O) l] (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* c.Oˣ) (σ : G ℚ Ω) (B : ValuationSubring Ω) (M : ℕ) (Hb : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)), Gen l η Ψ σ B M Hb → ∃ Q : ℕ, Q ≠ 0 ∧ ∀ (L : Type) [Field L] [NumberField L] [IsCMField L] [Algebra L Ω] (w : ℕ), IsAbelianGalois ℚ L → galOf ℚ L Ω = upl Ω L ⊓ η.ker → FrobeniusWitnessPrime l η Ψ M w → w ≡ 1 [MOD Q] → upl Ω L ≤ Hth Ω l w → (∃ s ∈ galOf ℚ L Ω, barChar c.O Ψ s ^ 2 ≠ 1) → SeedClause c η Ψ σ L := by
  intro Ω _ _ _ l _ c _ η Ψ σ B M Hb hG
  have hlp : l.Prime := Fact.out
  obtain ⟨a, hηa, hΨa⟩ := hG.cyc
  obtain ⟨j, hj⟩ := hG.Ψ_l
  obtain ⟨D, δ, hD, -, hδ, hηδ⟩ := hG.quad
  obtain ⟨K, iK, nK, aK, hK2, hKc, hKη⟩ := sc_quad Ω η D δ hD hδ hηδ
  obtain ⟨T, hT, hTa⟩ := sc_teich Ω l c hG.fin
  have hM : M ^ a * l ≠ 0 := mul_ne_zero (pow_ne_zero _ hG.M_ne) hlp.ne_zero
  obtain ⟨r, N, hlN, hN⟩ := Nat.exists_eq_pow_mul_and_not_dvd hM l hlp.one_lt.ne'
  have hN0 : N ≠ 0 := by
    rintro rfl
    exact hM (by rw [hN, mul_zero])
  let φ : G ℚ Ω →* (ResidueField c.O)ˣ := barChar c.O Ψ * (omO Ω l c.O ^ j)⁻¹
  let χ' : G ℚ Ω →* (Qbar l)ˣ := T.comp φ
  let η' : G ℚ Ω →* (Qbar l)ˣ := (Units.map (Int.castRingHom (Qbar l)).toMonoidHom).comp η
  have hφ : ∀ s, φ s = barChar c.O Ψ s * ((omO Ω l c.O) s ^ j)⁻¹ := fun s => rfl
  have hχ : ∀ s, χ' s = T (φ s) := fun s => rfl
  have hη'ker : η'.ker = η.ker := by
    ext s
    simp only [MonoidHom.mem_ker]
    exact map_eq_one_iff _ (Units.map_injective (f := (Int.castRingHom (Qbar l)).toMonoidHom) Int.cast_injective)
  have hφK : Af.KillsMu (M ^ a * l) φ := by
    intro s hs
    have h1 : barChar c.O Ψ s = 1 := km_mono (dvd_mul_right _ _) hΨa s hs
    have h2 : omO Ω l c.O s = 1 := km_mono (dvd_mul_left _ _) (sc_om Ω l c.O).1 s hs
    rw [hφ, h1, h2, one_pow, inv_one, mul_one]
  have hχK : Af.KillsMu (M ^ a * l) χ' := by
    intro s hs
    rw [hχ, hφK s hs, map_one]
  have hη'K : Af.KillsMu (M ^ a * l) η' := by
    intro s hs
    have h1 : η s = 1 := km_mono (dvd_mul_right _ _) hηa s hs
    show Units.map _ (η s) = 1
    rw [h1, map_one]
  have hχN : Af.KillsMu N χ' := by
    refine sc_strip Ω l _ χ' r N hN0 hlN (hN ▸ hχK) B hG.over_l ?_
    intro s hs
    rw [MonoidHom.mem_ker, hχ, hφ, hj B hG.over_l s hs, mul_inv_cancel, map_one]
  have hη'N : Af.KillsMu N η' := by
    refine sc_strip Ω l _ η' r N hN0 hlN (hN ▸ hη'K) B hG.over_l ?_
    rw [hη'ker]
    exact hG.η_l B hG.over_l
  have hT1 : T (-1) = -1 := by
    have hne : (-1 : (ResidueField c.O)ˣ) ≠ 1 := by
      intro h
      have h' : (-1 : ResidueField c.O) = 1 := by
        simpa using congrArg (fun u : (ResidueField c.O)ˣ => (u : ResidueField c.O)) h
      rw [neg_one_eq_one_iff, ringChar.eq (ResidueField c.O) l] at h'
      exact hG.odd h'
    have hsq : ((T (-1) : (Qbar l)ˣ) : Qbar l) * (T (-1) : Qbar l) = 1 := by
      rw [← Units.val_mul, ← map_mul, neg_one_mul, neg_neg, map_one, Units.val_one]
    rcases mul_self_eq_one_iff.1 hsq with h | h
    · exact absurd (hT (by rw [map_one]; exact Units.ext h)) hne
    · exact Units.ext (by rw [h, Units.val_neg, Units.val_one])
  have happ : ∀ s, (GlobalTheory.GalOfCusp.cycBar ℚ Ω l ^ j * χ') s =
      (GlobalTheory.GalOfCusp.cycBar ℚ Ω l) s ^ j * T ((Units.map ↑(residue c.O)) (Ψ s) * ((omO Ω l c.O) s ^ j)⁻¹) :=
    fun s => rfl
  have hord : ¬ l ∣ orderOf χ' := by
    classical
    haveI := Fintype.ofFinite (ResidueField c.O)
    have hpow : χ' ^ Fintype.card (ResidueField c.O)ˣ = 1 := MonoidHom.ext fun s => by
      show (T (φ s)) ^ Fintype.card (ResidueField c.O)ˣ = 1
      rw [← map_pow, pow_card_eq_one, map_one]
    intro hd
    have hd2 := hd.trans (orderOf_dvd_of_pow_eq_one hpow)
    obtain ⟨n, -, hcard⟩ := FiniteField.card (ResidueField c.O) l
    rw [Fintype.card_units, hcard] at hd2
    have hl1 : l ∣ l ^ (n : ℕ) := dvd_pow_self l n.ne_zero
    have hpos : 1 ≤ l ^ (n : ℕ) := Nat.one_le_pow _ _ hlp.pos
    have h3 : l ∣ (l ^ (n : ℕ) - 1) + 1 := by
      rw [Nat.sub_add_cancel hpos]
      exact hl1
    exact hlp.one_lt.ne' (Nat.dvd_one.1 ((Nat.dvd_add_right hd2).1 h3))
  refine ⟨N, hN0, ?_⟩
  intro L _ _ _ _ w hLab hLgal hw hwQ hLth hs0
  obtain ⟨s0, hs0L, hs0⟩ := hs0
  obtain ⟨θ, hθker, hθ1, hθl, hθw⟩ := sc_theta Ω l w hw.prime hw.one
  have hwN : ¬ w ∣ N := by
    intro hd
    have hd' : w ∣ M ^ a * l := hN ▸ dvd_mul_of_dvd_right hd _
    rcases (Nat.Prime.dvd_mul hw.prime).1 hd' with h | h
    · exact hw.cop (hw.prime.dvd_of_dvd_pow h)
    · have hwl := (Nat.prime_dvd_prime_iff_eq hw.prime hlp).1 h
      have h1 : w % l = 1 % l := hw.one
      rw [hwl, Nat.mod_self, Nat.mod_eq_of_lt hlp.one_lt] at h1
      exact zero_ne_one h1
  have hLK : galOf ℚ L Ω ≤ galOf ℚ K Ω := by
    rw [hKη, hLgal]
    exact inf_le_right
  refine ⟨K, iK, nK, aK, N, w, χ', η', θ, j, ?_, ?_, hord, fun s => rfl, fun s => happ s ▸ hTa (Ψ s) j s, ?_⟩
  · refine ⟨hG.cc, ⟨hG.odd, Nat.pos_of_ne_zero hN0, hlN, hw.prime, hwN, hθ1, hθl, hθw⟩, hK2, hKc, hLab, hLK,
      by rw [hη'ker, hKη], split_of hwQ hη'N, split_of hwQ hχN, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
    · rw [hθker, hLgal]
      exact inf_le_left.trans hLth
    · rw [hχ, hφ, hG.even, (sc_om Ω l c.O).2 σ hG.cc, one_mul, ← inv_pow, inv_neg_one, map_pow, hT1]
    · refine ⟨s0, hs0L, fun hc => hs0 ?_⟩
      exact sc_cong l c (Ψ s0) _ (happ s0 ▸ hTa (Ψ s0) j s0) hc
  · intro heq
    apply hG.g4
    intro s hs
    rw [Subgroup.mem_inf, MonoidHom.mem_ker, MonoidHom.mem_ker] at hs
    have h1 : χ' s = 1 := by
      rw [hχ, hφ, hs.1, hs.2, one_pow, inv_one, mul_one, map_one]
    rw [heq] at h1
    rw [← hη'ker]
    exact h1
  · exact sc_disc Ω l K hK2 (fun B' hB' => hKη ▸ hG.η_l B' hB')

import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.FrobeniusSplitPrimeDatum
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.DatFacts
